1b1(dkar chag): @##oM swasti si s+t+waM/bla ma dang mgon po kun 'byed rgyal po la phyag 'tshal lo//thog ma'i ston pa kun 'byed
1b2(dkar chag): rgyal po las//rdo rje sems dpa' gsang bdag gnyis kyis zhus/ches sgo brgyad khri bzhi stong don bsdus pa//thog mar nges pa don gyis
1b3(dkar chag): rgyud rnams bsungs//khyad par 'phags pa gsang sngags rgyud rnams dang //mched du 'phags pa theg pa rims pa dgu//mdo sngags spyi dang bye
1b4(dkar chag): breg gang yang gsungs//gcig rgyud gsungs pa'i dam chos yang brtse 'di//snod ldan rnams kyis rgyud pa ma chad par//snod min rnams la
1b5(dkar chag): ma spel bka' brgya btabs//snod min rnams la spel bar gyur pa'i tshe//yi dam lha dang mkha'agro chos skyong dang //bzhi bdag lha klu mthu brtsal can
2a1(dkar chag): rnams kyis//bka' las rgyung ba drug pos myur dru gcod/dge ba 'di yis la khyab sems can kun//sdig dang sgrub pa'i tshogs rnams byang gyur te/slang dus la
2a2(dkar chag): rim pa bgrod par shog/samar thugs sum tu bzang pos papa shog/kr-i shes/sheg pa'i grangs no ki/nyis rgya dru bu'i bdun bzhugs//gnyis ky-i nagasu pastsu/
2a3(dkar chag): bzhi bcu bcu//de/sum brgya re gnyis bcun// sum brgyad lnga bzhun//che/nyis brgya re gsum bcu//ji/nyis brgya re gcig nu///sum brgya badris bzhun//laM sum brgya nyer lnga bzhun//gri sum brgya rje lnga bzhugs/
2a4(dkar chag): de/nyis brgya brgyad cu bzhun//ni/sum brgya nyer lnga bzhugs/pi nyis brgya gya lnga bzhugs//pha sum brgya zhe drug bzhugs/khaM/brgya re gnyis bzhugs//khi/'og nyis brgya lnga bzhugs//me/nyis brgya don gcig
2a5(dkar chag): bzhugasata tsi/sum brgya nyer brgyad bzhugs//tshe/nyis brgya zhe bzhi bzhags/hri nyis brgya zhe gsum bzhugs//wi/sum brgya bcu bdun bzhugs/zhe/sum brgya drug bzhugs//pre/nyis brgya gsuM bzhugs
2a6(dkar chag): nyis brgyad dgu bzhugs/yi/nyis brgya zhe gnyis bzhu/r-i nyis brgya gya lnga bzhugs/li/nyes brgya go brgyad bzhugs//nyis brgya go dgu bzhugs//si/sum brgya nyi shu bzhug so//
2b1(dkar chag): te/nyis brgya phya gsum bzhugs/e nyis brgya go gcig bzhugs/ki nyis brgya don lnga bzhug so///mchog gsum tha ma ngas pa'i thugs
2b2(dkar chag): rje dang //yi dam tshe dpag med kyi byin brlabs yis//bar chad kun sel chos skyengs mthu brtsal dang //dgos 'dod 'byung ba'i nor lha gter bdag rnams//khyad par snying rgyang zab
2b3(dkar chag): mo'i byin brlabs kyis//sbyin pa'i bdag po 'phrin las rgyas gyur cig/sku tshe bstan zhing zhabs par bstan par shog/'phrin las 'dod dgu'i char rgyun 'bab par shog/
2b4(dkar chag): g.yangs chags tshe yis bum pa 'dzin par shog/mi nor dar zhing rgyas pa'i dngos grub bstsol///'dres mdzad nas kyis zhabs pade bstan pa dang //kha mnyam
2b5(dkar chag): rigs drug sems can ma lus kun//rnams mkhyen rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas thob par shog/bkra shis dpal 'bar 'dzam gling brgyan du byon/
2b6(dkar chag): @#//oM sa sti se ste/mtshan brjod par dka' ba'i yangs brtse 'di//chos pog mar nges pa don gyis rgyud //mdo sngags dang rgyud sde bye brag kun//sdom byed par 'phags pa'i rgya gar yul/
3a1(dkar chag): @/chos phung brgyad khri bzhi stong don gsungs pa//khyad par ched du kyi yog rnams gsum dang //skyeng rdzogs zung 'jug pa'i man ngag dang //lha rnams kun gyi sgrub thabs so sor gsal/spyan ras gzigs kyis zhing
3a2(dkar chag): khams bod yul 'dir//rdo sprul pa'i rgyal po khri bsrong sde btsan 'khrungs//bong khams skye 'gro bde la dgod pa'i phyir//gtsug lag khang gi sa bcad mdzad pa la/lha srin sde brgyad sdug rngub che ba yis//gtsug lag khang gis sa spyang ma gter bas
3a3(dkar chag): mkhan po bud+d+he sa twa spyan drangs pa'i//ma thub rgyal po thugs khral mdzad pa la//mkhan po lung bstan mngal skyes yin pas lha srin mi thul bas//rngus skyes sprul ba'i sku gcig gdan drangs gsungs//rgyal po'i zhal nas mi drongs gsung pa la//sngon gyi
3a4(dkar chag): smon lam yod pas gdan drongs bsungs//spyan 'dren btang bas u rgyan rje dang 'jal//gu ru'i zhal ba zhes bod la pheb par mdzad//'phebs lam lho srin sdug rtsub dam la btags//bod 'bangs bsod nams che bas rje 'brangs 'jal//g+hu ru'i
3a5(dkar chag): /thugs rjes gtsug lag khang rnams bzhengs//rgyal po thugs dgongs legs par mdzad pa la//sku rten 'grub kyang gsungs rten ma 'grub pas//slob dpon rje la bsol ba btab pa yis//g+hu ru'i thugs rjes mdo sngags bong du bsgyur//
3a6(dkar chag): bai ro tsa na la sogs lo ts+tsha bas//bong khams bstan pa nyi mor bsal ba yis//u rgyan thugs rjes bod khams bstan pa la/snyigs ma'i dus su gsang sngags bstan pa 'phel//dar zhing rgyas pa dbus gtsang zal
3a7(dkar chag): mo'i slang //dam chos rin chen kun gyis yang snying 'di//chu bo lo hi ta yis byang phyogs su//khyad du 'phags pa'i zang zangs lha brag dkar//sangs rgyas gnyis pa'i zhabs bcags gnas kyis ri//de yis
3b1(dkar chag): shar phyogs skye 'khra ri bo'i 'dabs//tshe sngon tshogs bsags dkon mchog gsum la mos//'dod dgu'i bsam don longs spyod 'char ltar babs//ser sna'i 'dus grol sbyin pa'i gter kha mched//
3b2(dkar chag): dad pa'i sten thebo sku gsung thugs la mos//nor rdzas gang yod dkon mchog gsum la 'phul//dka' las khur zhing mdo sngags kun la 'bad//sku tshe snga mi'i smon la mthu nus kyis//rang gzhan skye 'gro yongs kyi don bsam pa'i//'bad pa'i
3b3(dkar chag): 'bras bu dus 'dir bsam pa 'grub//'di phyi gnyis kyi don chen 'grub pa e ma ho////zhes ched du brjod pa bdag rgya dpon rab brtan gyis//rje spyir sangs rgyas kyi bstan pa dar phyir dang //'dzam+bu gling gi skyes chen dam pa rgyal ba yab
3b4(dkar chag): sa kyi u mdzad pa'i//bstan 'dzin gyis skyes chen rnams kyis sku tsheri zhabs pad bstan phyir dang //'phags pa'i dge 'dun rnams kyi chos spyod phel phyir dang //skye 'gro sen rnams la sde skyid 'byung ba'i phyir//mtha' dmag la sogs khrag
3b5(dkar chag): long zhi bar shog/char chu dus phebs lo phyug legs pa'i phyir//mi nang phyug nad thams cad zhi bar shog/rang gzhan sems can thams cad kyi don dang /'grel thogs rnams dang khyad par du drin can pha mas gtso byas sangs rgyas thob pa'i phyir/
3b6(dkar chag): Sh+yang du 'phags pa'i snying mo rgyud 'bum bzhengs// rgyal ba'i bka' 'gyur yongasu rdzogs pa gzhengs//yang khyang du 'phags pa 'bum rgyas 'bring bsdus gsum dang /ma Ni bka' 'bum/pad+ma bka' thang /mi la'i rnam 'gur/mdo mangs//
3b7(dkar chag): thos grol/gser gyis bris pa'i dri med bshags rgyud dang mna' bshags bzhengs//tog dang bzung chen gral lnga dang bshags rgyud sgra rgod gnyis dang mna' bshags dang rdo rje gcod pa bcas gzhengs//khyad du 'phags pa'i ma
4a1(dkar chag): dud bskor ma phyed gnyis yod pa dang /lha khang bstan bcas bzhengs//gzhan yang dge ba bsam las 'das pa bsgrubs lags pa'i//bod khams skye 'gro yongs kyi mig thos yang 'phel du gyur cig/'dag ste/
4a2(dkar chag): dge ba'i rtsa ba 'di'i mthu dang nus pa la brten nas/sems can thams cad rang rig pa ye shes kyi dbang thob par gyur cig/snang ba rgyas theb par gyur cig/mtshan ma rang sar 'dzin par gyur cig/bsting bsal rgyud la skye bar gyur cig/
4a3(dkar chag): myur du rnam mkhyen sangs rgyas kyis go 'phang thob par shog/khyad par du yang bdag rgya dpon rab rten gyi rje gnas gsum gyi bskor ba dang /mchod rten la sku kar tshar gnyis/'bri gung la ma Ni dung gcig btsugs//gzhan yang dge ba che
4a4(dkar chag): grwa chung gsum bsam las 'das pa bsgrub pa'i don gyi 'gro ba rigs drug la gnas pa'i sems can dbug chags rnams bde ba can du skye bar shog/rigs rus khung btsun rmug po ldong nas chad//sbyin pa'i bdag po rgya dpon
4a5(dkar chag): rab brten bstod/no bo'i mchog btso rgya dpon srid dar bstod//tshwa bo'i mchog btso dge slong blo bzang bstan 'phel+la/tshwa bo'i mchog gyur yis khas grub bstod//yum gyi mchog gyur a ma khra lcug bstod //lha mo dang tshul 'dra ba'i
4a6(dkar chag): a ma dpal 'dren bstod//bu mo'i mchog gyur a ne bde legs dpal 'joms bstod//bu mo'i mchog gyir bsod nams dpal 'dren bstod//khongs rnams thams cad bka' gros chos la mos//bdag gi dge bcu chos la
4a7(dkar chag): sbyad pa yis//sngon gyur phyir mi ldog pa'i sa thob shog/bstan pa'i dpal gyur bla ma'i zhabs par bstan//bstan 'dzin skyes bus sa steng yong la khyab//bstan pa'i sbyin bdag mnga' thang 'byor pa rgyas//bstan pa yun ring gnas pa'i bkris shog/
2a1(rnying rgyud): #/yas khang na/nyid kyi rang bzhin dang /nyid kyi ngo bo dang /nyid kyi thugs rje ye shes mngon du phyung ba'i 'kho 'di lta ste/
2a2(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin gyi 'khor chos kyi sku zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /nyid kyi ngo bo'i 'khor longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku sa zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /nyid kyi ngo bo'i
2a3(rnying rgyud): 'khor longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sgra zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /nyid kyi dpo 'khor longs spyod rdzogs pa'i me zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /ting/
2b1(rnying rgyud): 'khor dang /gzhan yang thugs rje ye shes mngon du phyung ba'i sprul pa'i 'khor 'di lta ste/'dod pa'i khams kyi sems can zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /gzugs kyi khams kyi
2b2(rnying rgyud): sems can zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /gzugs med pa'i khams kyi sems can zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /gzhan yang nyid kyi rang bzhin la lta ba'i 'khor rnal 'byor pa
2b3(rnying rgyud): bzhi 'di lta ste/shin tu rnal 'byor zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /yongs su rnal 'byor zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /rnal 'byor chen po zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /sems dpa'i rnal
2b4(rnying rgyud): 'byor zhes bya ba'i 'khor dang /nyid kyi rang bzhin dang /nyid kyi ngo bo dang /nyid kyi thugs rje'i rang bzhin 'byed pa mi mnga' bas thabs cig go//gzhan yang nyid kyi rang bzhin rtog
2b5(rnying rgyud): pa'i 'khor 'di lta ste/sngar 'das pa'i sangs rgyas nyid la gnas pa'i 'khor dang /da ltar bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas nyid kyi don mdzad pa'i 'khor dang /slang nas 'byon pa'i
3a1(rnying rgyud): @sangs rgyas nyid las byung ba'i 'khor dang /'byed pa mi mnga' ba'i rang bzhin gyis thabs cig par bzhug so//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des/
3a2(rnying rgyud): 'khor thams cad nyid kyi rang bzhin du byin gyis brlabs pa'i phyir/'khor thams cad nyid kyi sems nyid kyi thugs la thim par mdzad do//rang byung gi ye shes gsal bar
3a3(rnying rgyud): mdzad do//de nas thams cad chos nyid du ldan par bya ba'i phyir/thams cad bsdus nas thig le chen po cig tu bzhug so//de nas sems dpa' rdo rje de nyid kyis/rang bzhin thig le
3a4(rnying rgyud): chen po gcig tu bzhugs pa de las byung nas/byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po'i spyan sngar/dga' ba'i sems kyis dngas pa'i mdangs phyung ste 'khod pas/byang chub kyi sems
3a5(rnying rgyud): kun byed rgyal pos/sems dpa' rdo rje la smras pa/sems dpa' rdo rje e ma ho//dga' ba'i sems skyes e ma ho//dang ba'i mdangs phyung e ma ho//nga las byung ba e ma ho//
3b1(rnying rgyud): ces gsungs pas/de nas sems dpa' rdo rjes zhus pa/kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags/ston pa nyid kyang thig kyang thig le spros med dam/'khor rnams kun kyang thig le spros
3b2(rnying rgyud): med dam/bstan pa kun kyang thig le spros med dam/dus dang gnas kyang thig le spros med dam/thams cad kun kyang thig le'i rang bzhin no/ston pa'i ston pas ci zhig ston par mdzad/
3b3(rnying rgyud): 'khor du 'khor ba'i don ni ci la 'khor/'khor la bstan pa ci zhig ston par byed/dus dang gnas ni gcig pa ji lta bu//zhes zhuso//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des/sems
3b4(rnying rgyud): dpa' rdo rje de la 'di skad ces gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po/yid sgra la chug cig/ngas don sgrar bshad kyis/kye sems dpa' chen po/sems nyid kun byed rgyal po de nyid ni/chos rnams
3b5(rnying rgyud): thams cad kun gyi snying po ste/snying po ma spros ye nas thig le yin/thig le ye nas spros pa med pa'i don/ston pa dang ni bstan pa 'khor dus gnas/nga las byung ba ye nas thig le ste/
3b6(rnying rgyud): nga'i rang bzhin thig le bstan pa yin/zhes gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las/gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed
4a1(rnying rgyud): rgyal po des/chos thams cad nyid las byung ba'i ting nge 'dzin zhes bya ba la gnas par mdzad do//de nas 'khor gyi rang bzhin las sems dpa' rdo rje langs nas/ston pa byang chub
4a2(rnying rgyud): kyi sems kun byed rgyal po'i zhal du mdangs phyung ste/'khod nas zhus pa/kye ston pa'i ston byed rgyal po lags/khyod kyi 'khor du bdag 'dus 'khod pa de/bdag ni 'tshal ba'i don ni ci zhig mchis//
4a3(rnying rgyud): bdag 'tshal don ni khyod kyis bstan du gsol/zhes zhus so//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des/nyid kyi rang bzhin dang /nyid kyi ngo bo dang /nyid kyi thugs rje las/chos thams cad
4a4(rnying rgyud): bkod pa ni/nyid kyi rang byung gi ye shes chen po gcig las/rang byung ye shes chen po lnga byung ba ni/'di lta ste/zhe sdang rang byung ye shes chen po dang /'dod chags rang byung ye shes chen po dang /
4a5(rnying rgyud): gti mug rang byung ye shes chen po dang /phrag dog rang byung ye shes chen po dang /nga rgyal rang byung ye shes chen po dang /rang byung gi ye shes lnga po des/rgyan gyi rgyu chen po lnga ni phyung /'jig cing
4a6(rnying rgyud): rten pa'i khams chen po gsum ni bkod de/rgyan gyi rgyu'i lus sa zhes bya ba'i lus dang /rgyan gyi rgyu'i lus chu zhes bya ba'i lus dang /rgyan gyi rgyu'i lus me zhes bya ba'i lus dang /rgyan gyi rgyu'i lus rlung
4b1(rnying rgyud): zhes bya ba'i lus dang /rgyan gyi rgyu'i lus na mkha' zhes bya ba'i lus la gcig tu lus thams cad blangs/ye shes lnga'i rigs so sor phye ste/rang byung ye shes zhe sdang gi rigs dang /rang byung ye shes 'dod chags kyi rigs dang /rang byung ye
4b2(rnying rgyud): shes gti mug gi rigs dang /rang byung ye shes phrag dog gi rigs dang /rang byung ye shes nga rgyal gyi rigs dang /rang byung ye shes lnga'i rigs lus rgyan gyis lus can gyi chos nyid bkod do//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po de nyid kyang /
4b3(rnying rgyud): nyid kyi rang bzhin chos nyid bkod pa de bzhin du bcug so//de nas sems dpa' rdo rje de/byang chub sems dpa' kun byed rgyal po'i spyan sder yang 'dug nas/kye ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//nyid kyi thugs cig rang byung ye shes las/2
4b4(rnying rgyud): byung ye shes rnam pa lnga byung nas//de las rgyan gyi rgyu lnga byung nas ni//ye shes lnga'i lus lha blangs pa 'di//kun byed rgyal po dgos pa ci zhig che//zhes nus so//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des/slar gsungs pa/
4b5(rnying rgyud): sems dpa' chen mo dgis pa ni//kun byed rgyal ba byed mkhan pa las ni//ma gtogs pa'i byed mkhan gzhan med do//chom nyid byed pa 'ang ni dalas med//ston pa gsum yang 'god pa nga las med//khyod choms rnams 'ad
4b6(rnying rgyud): nga las med//de bzhin chos nyid dgod pa nga las med//sems dpa' rdo rje khyod la nga yis ni//nga'i rang bzhin khyod la bstan par bya//nga la rang bzhin rnam pa gsum yod de//nga'i rang bzhin byang chub sems yino//byang ba dag gi rang bzhin
4b7(rnying rgyud): bstan pa ni//phun sum tshogs pa rnam gsum chos nyid byang //chub pa dag gi rang bzhin bstan pa ni//dgos pa tshogs pa rnam pa gsum gyis ni//nam mkha' bzhin du kun la khyab par gnas//sems zhes bya ba'i rang bzhin bstan pa ni//mi 'gag ma lus/
5a1(rnying rgyud): kun byed rgyal po ste//kun byas byang chub sems su ma lus byas//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//chos nyid mngon du phyung nas bstan pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o//
5a2(rnying rgyud): de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po nges//chos thams cad kyi sngon rol du byed pa po dbyung ba 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po yid na dbang la chug cig/ni dbang sgra la rtogs shig/kye nga ni kun byed rgyal po ste//nga ni kun byed byang chub
5a3(rnying rgyud): sems cang med pa'i sngon rol na//chos rnams 'byung ba'i snying po med//nga med pa'i sngon rol na//chos kun byed pa'i rgyal po med//nga med pa'i sngon rol na//ston pa bya ba ye nas med//nga med pa'i sngon rol na//bstan pa bya ba ye nas med//nga med pa
5a4(rnying rgyud): sngon rol ma/'khor zhes bya ba ye nas med//sems dpa' rdo rje rtog ma skye//sems dpa' chen po khyod nyid kyang //nga'i pa yin//zhes gsung so//de nas sems dpa' rdo rjes//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po la zhus pa/
5a5(rnying rgyud): kye kun byed rgyal po byang chub sems//khyod ni nas chen po yin//ston pa dang ni bstan pa dang //'khor la sogs pa'i chos rnams kun//byed mkhan khyod kyis byas pas na/ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//byed mkhan khyod kyis byas
5a6(rnying rgyud): par snang //ston pa gcig gam rnam pa du ru snang //bstan pa gcig gam rnam pa du ru yod/'khor rnams gcig gam rnam pa du ru yod//zhes gsungaso//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos sngar gsungs pa//kye rang byung ye shes kun
5a7(rnying rgyud): gyi ston par gcig/nga'i rang bzhin rnam gsum snang ba la//ston pa'i grangs ni rnam pa gsum du byung //nga ni de bzhin nyid du gcig pa las//chos rnams kun gyi chos nyid de bzhin nyid//nga nyid byang chub sems las gzhan med do//nga las byung
5b1(rnying rgyud): ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//'khor la bstan pa rnam pa gsum du bstam/nga'i ngo bo gcig tu phyung nas ni//nga'i rang bzhin gcig tu bstan pa'i phyir//kun byed nga'i 'khor du kun kyang gcig/nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyi/
5b2(rnying rgyud): 'khor gyi bye brag rnam pa gsum du yod ces gsung so//sems dpa' rdo rjes zhus pa//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//khyod las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum la//khyod sar 'gro ba'i lam ni yod dam med//med na khyod kyi
5b3(rnying rgyud): sa ru phyin mi phyin//yod na bgrod pas phyin nam ma bgrod phyin//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum la//nga'i sa ru bgrod pa'i lam med de//nga'i rang byung ye shes bstan pa yin//sku gsum ston pa
5b4(rnying rgyud): de'i 'khor rnams la//rnam par thar pa'i lam ni rnam lnga ste//dus gsum sangs rgyas kun gyi lam du bstan//rang byung ye shes lnga'i lam lnga ni//'dod chags dang ni zhe sdang gti mug dang //nga rgyal dang ni phrag dog rnam lnga ste//rang
5b5(rnying rgyud): byung ye shes rnam lnga kun gyi lam//ces gsung so//ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//rang byung ye shes lam lnga bstan pa ste//rang byung zhes ni ji ltar byung lags//ye shes bya ba'i gtan tshigs ci la gdags//lam
5b6(rnying rgyud): zhes bya ba'i don ni gang la bgyi//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug nga rgyal dang //phrag dog la sogs rnam pa lnga po de//lam lnga yin pa'i don de bshad du gsol//zhus so//sems dpa' chen po//rang byung ye shes rnam pa lnga lam
5b7(rnying rgyud): nis rang byung rgyu rkyen med par byung ba yin//ye shes 'gag pa med par gsal ba yin//lam ni rgyu rkyen med pas mi dgos//mi bgrod rang byung lnga la lam lngar bshad//'dod pa chos rnams bdag tu 'dod pa yin//chags
6a1(rnying rgyud): pa bdag tu 'dod pa de la chags//zhe ba skye ba med pa'i rang bzhin la//sdang ba cho 'phrul sna tshogs de la sdang //gti mug mi rtog pa'i ting 'dzin gyis//chos nyid mnyam pa nyid la dbye bsal med//nga ni ma n+yo
6a2(rnying rgyud): pa'i byang chub sems//de bzhin nyid du ye nas rgyal bar byed//thams cad de bzhin nyid du gcig pa las//yin min sgro skur 'debs pa phrag dog byed//bgrod du med pa'i lam lngar bstan pa yin//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon
6a3(rnying rgyud): cig/ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//rang byung ye shes rnam lnga 'di las byung //'dod ba'i khams kyang rang byung ye shes yin//gzugs kyi khams kyang rang byung ye shes yin//gzugs med khams kyang rang
6a4(rnying rgyud): byung ye shes yin//kun byed rgyal po nga yis byas pa'i phyir//ngas byas thams cad ye shes ngo bor bshad//khams gsum sems can rgyud drug thams cad kun//rang byung ye shes min par ngas ma bshad//de las ma gtogs//
6a5(rnying rgyud): pa'i byang chub sems//sangs rgyas kun gyi mes po ngas ma bshad//kun byed rgyal po nga'i rang bzhin la//chos nyid rnam pa gsum du bstan pa ste//ma skyes pa'i chos nyid dngos mi snang //de ni gzugs med khams su bstan pa yin///
6a6(rnying rgyud): skye ba'i cho 'phrul gzugs su snang ba ni//de ni gzugs kyi khams su bstan pa yin//thugs rje ye shes ngo bo mngon phyung nas//bde sdug las de thugs rje zad bya'i phyir//'dod pa'i khams de thugs rjes phyung ba yin//zhes gsung
6a7(rnying rgyud): so byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//chos thams cad kun byed rgyal pos byas pa las//ji ltar byung ba'i khungs bstan po'i le'u ste/gsum pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po nges/
6b1(rnying rgyud): nyid rang bzhin mtshan gyi chos nyid bshad pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po rdo rje khyod nyon cig/nga ni ye nas rang byung ye shes te//nga ni ye nas chos kun snying po yin//nga ni kun byed rgyal po byang chub sems//nga'i mtshan ni
6b2(rnying rgyud): sems dpas rtogs par gyis//nga'i mtshan ni sems dpas rtogs gyur nas//chos rnams thams cad ma lus rtogs 'gyur gyis//nga zhes bya ba snying po ste//chos rnams kun gyi snying po yin//rang byung zhes ni bya ba ni//rgyu rkyen med/
6b3(rnying rgyud): pa'i snying po bas//rtsakun las 'das pa yin//ye shes zhes ni bya ba ni//'gag pa med cing ma bsgribs pas//chos kun ma lus ston par byed//nga ni byang chub sems la bya//ye ldes bya ba'i don 'di ni//theg ma
6b4(rnying rgyud): nyid nas gnas pa'i don//chos kun zhes ni bya ba ni//ston pa kun kyang chos nyid la//bstan pa kun kyang chos nyid yin//'khor gnas dus kun 'ang chos nyid yin//chos nyid ma yin cig kyang med//snying po bya ba'i chos nyid ni//thams cad
6b5(rnying rgyud): 'byung ba'i snying po ste//byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin la//ston pa gsum yang de las byung //bstan pa gsum yang de las byung //'khor gnas dus kyang de las byung //thams cad 'byung ba'i snying por bshad//nga ni snying po sems
6b6(rnying rgyud): nyid kyis//chos rnams ma lus byed de//kun zhes bya ba'i chos rnams kun/chos kun+da gang dang gang zhe na//ston pa dang ni bstan pa dang //'khor gnas dus ni thams cad kun/byed ces bya ba mkhan po ste//ston pa dang ni bstan pa dang /
6b7(rnying rgyud): 'khor gnas dus ni kun byed pas//rang byung ye shes mkhan po yin//rgyal po zhes ni bya ba yin//snying po rang byung ye shes te//byed pa'i mkhan po kun las rgyal//chos rnams byed mkhan kun las rgyal//byang ba'i don ni 'di lta ste//
7a1(rnying rgyud): snying po byang chub sems de ni//rang byung ye nas rnam dag pas//kun byed rgyal pos byas pa kun//kun bzang nyid du rnam dag pas//byang zhes bya bar bshad pa yin//chub ces bya ba 'di lta ste/
7a2(rnying rgyud): snying po rang byung ye shes nges//snang ba dang ni srid pa dang //snod dang bcud kyis bsdus pa dang //dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad dang //khams gsum rgyud drug sems can dang //de bzhin nyid ni thams cad la/kuH
7a3(rnying rgyud): la chub ste khyab par gnas//des na chub ces bya bar bshad//sems kyi don ni 'di lta ste//snying po rang byung ye shes nges//snang srid snod bcud thams cad la//'jug cing dbang sgyur gsal bar gcong //des na sems
7a4(rnying rgyud): zhes bya bar bshad//rgyu rkyen med pa'i snying po des//kun la dbang bsgyur thams cad byed//kye sems dpa' chen po//nga'i rang bzhin rtogs gyur na/ston pa kun kyang rtogs 'gyur ba//bstan pa kun kyang rtogs par 'gyur//
7a5(rnying rgyud): 'khor gyi bsam pa rig 'gyur la//dus gnas kun kyang cig 'gyur rig/chos rnams thams cad nga yin pas//nga'i rang bzhin byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//nyid kyi mtshan bshad pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o///
7a6(rnying rgyud): de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//sems dpa' rdo rje la nyid kyi rang bzhin//bstan pa'i mdo 'dus pa'i don 'di bstan to//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga'i rang bzhin 'di lta ste//yod ni gcig las
7a7(rnying rgyud): med pa ste//bstan ni rnam pa gnyi su bstan//byung ni theg pa dgu ru byung //'dus ni rdzogs pa chen por 'dus//yin ni byang chub sems yin te//gnas ni chos nyid dbyings na'o///gsal ni rig pa'i mkha' la gsal//khyab ni snod
7a8(rnying rgyud): 'di dag ma/chos rnams thang rig 'gyur bas//khya byed rtsol sgrub kun las 'das//mi rtsol lhun gyis grub par 'gyur//ces gsung so//
7b1(rnying rgyud): bcud la khyab//byung ni snang srid kun tu byung //bstan du mtshan ma'i dngos po med//mthong du dmigs pa'i yul dang bral//shes su tshig tu brjod du med//rgyu las ma byung snying po 'di//sgros kyis btags pa kun dang bral//don de nges par
7b2(rnying rgyud): rtogs 'dod na//dpe ni nam mkha' lta bur brtag//don ni chos nyid skye ba med//brtags ni sems nyid 'gags pa med//nam mkha' lta bu'i chos nyid la//nam mkha' lta bu'i dpe yis mtshon//dmigs su med pa'i chos nyid la//dmigs
7b3(rnying rgyud): su med par mtshon te bstan//tshig tu brjod du med pa la//brjod du med pa'i tshig brjod de//dmigs med don gyi ngo bor bstan//bsdus te bstan pa'i don 'di ni//don gyi ngo bo 'grel pa ste//'di yis nga don rtogs par gyis//'di yis nga
7b4(rnying rgyud): don rtogs par byed//'di yis nga don ma rtogs na//don gyi sgra tshig ci bstan kyang //nga dang phrad par mi 'gyur te//nga las gol la nga bsgribs pas//chos kyi snying po thob mi 'gyur//zhes gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed
7b5(rnying rgyud): rgyal po las/don 'grel gyi le'u ste lnga pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des/chos thams cad nyid kyi rang bzhin la rdzogs pa de bstan pa'i tshig 'di gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po
7b6(rnying rgyud): tshig 'di nyon cig/kun byed rgyal po nga yis ni//nga yis chos rnams dang por bkod//nga yis chos rnams mngon du phyung //nga'i ngo bo khyod la bstan//nga'i rang bzhin bstan nas ni//tshig dang smras kyang don mtshon na//ngo bo bstan
7b7(rnying rgyud): pas khyod kyis mthong //rang bzhin bstan pa a de rig/tshig tu brjod pas don sgrar thos//ces gsungs nas cang mo gsung bar//chos thams cad kyi ngo bor bzhugs pas//sems dpa' rdo rje 'khor gyi nang nas langs nas/byang
8a1(rnying rgyud): chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po'i zhal du mangs phyung ste 'khod pas/kun byed rgyal pos/kye sems dpa' chen po//khyod nga la mdangs kyis 'khong pa de/khyod kyi don ci 'dod pa go sol cig ces gsung so//de nas song rdo rjes zhus pa/
8a2(rnying rgyud): kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//chos rnams thang khyod kyi rang bzhin na//khyod kyis ston pa'i bstan pa 'khor bkong ci zhig lags//phun sum tshogs pa'i ngos po ci zhig lags ces zhuso//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos 'di skad ces gsung
8a3(rnying rgyud): so//kye senga' chen po nyon cig/chos rnams phun sum tshogs par bkod pa ni//nga ni phun sum tshogs pa'i rang bzhin te//nga'i rang bzhin khyod la bstan par bya//nga'i rang bzhin spros bral mi 'byed pas//chos dbyings gnas 'di nga yis byas pa yin//byang chub nyid las de na
8a4(rnying rgyud): gzhan mi bzhugs//nga'i rang bzhin ma bsgribs kun khyab pas//gsal ba'i nam mkha' ye shes gzhal yas khang //rang byung ye shes nyid las gzhan mi bzhugs//nga ni thad 'byung ba'i snying po bas//chen po lnga dang khams gsum rgyud drug rnams//nga'i sku gsung thugs las gzhan med pas///
8a5(rnying rgyud): nga'i rang bzhin bkod pa yin//dus gsum sangs rgyas khams gsum sems can yang //nga'i rang bzhin khyed la bstan pa yin//nga'i ngo bo ma skyes spros bral phyir//mi gnas spyod yul kun las 'das pa yin//mi snang ting 'dzin bsgom pa'i yul las 'das//nga'i rang bzhin khyed
8a6(rnying rgyud): la mi snang yang //nga'i ngo bo khyed la snang byas pas//khams gsum chen po lnga dang rgyud drug rnams//snang ba'i rang bzhin byang chub sems lnga las//rang byung snying po ye shes lnga byung bas//ye shes lnga'i yul lnga gsal phyung bas//'dod pa lnga byung chags pa lnga byung nas//chags lnga so sor
8a7(rnying rgyud): smin pa'i 'bras bu ni/'gro ba drug gi khyad par so sor snang //'di ltar snang ba nga yis khyed la bstan pa yin//sku gsum theg pa rnam pa drug bstan pa//nga yis bstan na khyed kyis ma mthong ste//de re nga'i rang bzhin re re la/de re nga'i ngo bo re re ste//sku re dang ni gsung re thugs rje dang //kun gyis mthong ba'i ngo ba
8b1(rnying rgyud): ri roro//de yang nga'i rang bzhin bstan pa yin//yang dag kun gyi ngo bo byang chub ngas//spros med dbyis kyi ngo bo nga yin la//sangs rgyas kun gyi gnas chen nga yin pas//rom pa khams gsum 'gro drug nga las shar//bde ba sdug bsngal las kyi khyad par te//yang dag ma nor nga'i/
8b2(rnying rgyud): thugs rje bstan//rgyu dang 'bras bu theg pa can rnams la//kun byed nga'i lung 'di bstan mi bya/nga'i lung 'di nges par bstan byas na//dge sdig las la rgyu 'bras yong zer nas//yang dag nga la sgo dang skur btab pas//yang dag nga dang yun du phrang mi 'gyur//nga ni ston pa kun byed byang chub
8b3(rnying rgyud): sems /byang chub sems ni kun byed rgyal po ste//dus gsum sangs rgyas byang chub sems kyis byas//khams gsum sems can byang chub sems kyis byas//snang srid snod bcung byang chub sems kyis byas//rgyu 'bras dpe don gtan tshigs mthun par byas//rgyu'i dus na 'byung chen
8b4(rnying rgyud): lnga byas la//'bras bu'i dus na khams gsum son byas//dpe dus na kun dpe' na mkha' byed//don gyi dus na kun don skye med byed//rtags kyi dus na kun byed byang chub sems//gtan tshigs dus na rang byung ye shes ston//rgyu 'bras dpe don gtan tshigs mthun par byas//byang chub sems
8b5(rnying rgyud): kyis kun byas gcig ma bya//kun byas byang chub nyid kyi rang bzhin byas//gcig ma byas pa nyid la bya mi dgos//kun byed pa'i rang bzhin ma brtags par//nga yis byas pa'i chos la nges brtags pas//brkam zhing chags pa'i dbang gis snang ba grub//mi rtag sgyu ma'i rang
8b6(rnying rgyud): /bzhin 'jig par 'gyur//cha med dmus long lta bu'i rang bzhin no//kun byed rgyal pos byas par mi che bar//dbang po drug la yul drug snang ba la//sdug bsngal mthong nas dbang po 'gegs par byed//des ni kun byed byas par mi tsha bas//skye 'gag med pa'i 'bras bu de
8b7(rnying rgyud): la med/nges pa gcig la bden pa gnyis byung na//kun byed nga yis byas pa'i chos nyid la//don dam kun rdzob yin min gnyis byung bas//bden pa gnyis kyis rgyu 'bras yongs mi 'da'//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i 'bras bu de la med//med par tshol ba'i dag pa ting 'dzin dang ga
9a1(rnying rgyud): 'phrul yan lag bsgom mngar bas//blang dor med pa'i 'bras bu thob pa med//don cig ting 'dzin rnam gsum bsgom byed pas//bsnyen bsgrub rnam bzhi'i rim par bsgom byed pas//sgrub 'das pa'i 'bras bu de la med//lhun gyis grub pa'i bya byed kun dang brag
9a2(rnying rgyud): skye med mtshon med gdags med de brjod pas//sgro skur bral ba'i brjod med de rig byed//chos kun byang chub sems yin de de ba na//kun dpe na mkha' lta bu'i rang bzhin du//byang chub sems kyi don yang de bzhi no//nam mkha' rlung chus me rnam pa lnga//byang chub sems las rman
9a3(rnying rgyud): byang mngon sangs rgyas//srid gsum lam lnga 'gro drug snang ba yang //rnam smin las kyis mi bskyod mngon sangs rgyas//khams gsum ye nas byang chub sku gsung thugs//de bas snang srid snod bcud thams cad kun//nam mkha'i ngang na mi gnas med pa bzhin//byang chub sems
9a4(rnying rgyud): kye klong chen yul che bas//sangs rgyas sems can snod bcud kun kyang gnas//thams cad rnam par dag pa'i chos nyid la//gnyi su med de sgro skur kun dang bral//gang gis med pa'i snying po'i sems ma mthong //bskal pa du mar don dang phrad mi 'gyur//chos kun rtsa ba byang chub seMsu
9a5(rnying rgyud): gcig sangs rgyas sems can snang srid snod bcud kun//thams cad 'byung ba'i byang chub snying po la//gcig du med la bgrangs na brjod mi lang //sangs rgyas sku gsung sems can lus ngag kyang //byang chub sems yin ye nas gzung 'dzin bral//gzung 'dzin bral ba/
9a6(rnying rgyud): gang gis grub pa de//des ni kun grub chos kyi rtsa ba grub//byang chub sems kyi mdo rtsa gcig pu la//sems shing rtog la dga' bas rnam par bgrangs//gnyis dang grangs las 'das la nga yis bstan//ji bzhin rang bzhin chos nyid sems gcig la//sems la rang bzhin
9a7(rnying rgyud): rnam pa gsum snang bas//rang bzhin gsum ni bkod pa'i mchog gsum ste//de ni phun sum tshogs pa'i chos nyid //gnyis med gcig gi rang bzhin kun 'brel pas//rnal ma gcig la 'byor pa rnam pa bzhi//rnal 'byor rnam pa bzhi'i yul du bstan//sems nyid gcig
9b1(rnying rgyud): la che ba lngar bstan pa//sems kyi rang bzhin ston lnga ldan pas//rang bzhin lnga la che lngar ming du btags//nges pa gcig la yin min smos byung bas//go pa sgri ba the tsom rtog pa bsam yas ni/kun byed rtsol sgrub rtog 'das sel bar byed//yod med sgro skur the tsom bsal ba'i phyis
9b2(rnying rgyud): de bzhin nyid kyi byang chub mi rtog pas//ma nor nges pa'i lung nges gtan la 'bebs//de nyid/yid ches mngon du bstan pa'i phyir//la dor yid ches pa'i lung bcu yis//rtsol sgrub byar med don bstan de yid ches//med par bden pa'i tshig dgu smras pa ni//snying po gcig la mthog/
9b3(rnying rgyud): ba'i yul dgu byung //rang re mthong ba'i bden pa'i tshig dgu smra//kun byed nga la thams cad rdzogs pa'i phyir//byang chub sems kyi don 'di che bar gyis//chos kun rtsa ba kun byed byang chub sems//ji ltar snang ba nga'i ngo yin//ji ltar byung ba nga'i cho 'phrul yin//sgra tshig ji ltar byung ba thaMd
9b4(rnying rgyud): kun//nga'i don nyid tshig sgrar phyung ba yin//sangs rgyas sku dang ye shes ston dang //sems can lus dang bag chags la sogs pa//snang srid snod bcud bsdus pa thams cad kun/byang chub sems kyi ngo ye nas yin//sems las ma gtogs pa'i chos rnams ni//nga las
9b5(rnying rgyud): byung ba'i sangs rgyas lngar 'das la//kun byed rgyal po nga yis lung ma bstan//da ltar bzhugs dang slad kyis byon pa la//kun byed rgyal po byang chub lung mi ston zhes gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//rtsa ba gcig pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o//
9b6(rnying rgyud): /de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//nyid kyis byas pa las byung ba'i chos rdor dbye ba'i chos 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po sems ni ji bzhin do ste//ji bzhin gnyis med bde ba chen po la/rnam pa kun ldan rnam pa yongs kyis med//spros bral nam para' bzhin/
9b7(rnying rgyud): du ye gnas la//dmigs pa'i yul 'das gcig ces bgrang du med//byang chub sems la bgrang zhing mtshon du med//sems kyis byas pa'i chos la rnam grangs byung //sems kyis byas pa gang dang gang zhe na//snang sring bsnod bcung sangs rgyas sems can rnams//sos/
11a1(rnying rgyud): kyi rang bzhin ngo de las byas//bas na sa mngon du byung ste bstan pa ni//chen po lnga dang rig drug snang ba dang //de'i don mdzad gzugs sku rnam gnyis ni//sems kyi rang bzhin dag tu phyung nas bgrangs ces bka' stsal to///de nas sede rdo rjes
11a2(rnying rgyud): zhus pa//kye kun byed rgyal po e ma ho//kun tu bzang po e ma ho//byang chub sems khyod e ma ho//dang por byang chub sems langs nas//rang dang chos rnams ma shes nas//kun mkhyen khyed kyi 'khor du 'dus//zhu ba'i dmigs ni mi tshabs//kun mkhyen khyod kyis bka' stsal pa//chos
11a3(rnying rgyud): rnams gcig tu 'dus par ni//byang chub sems mdo bka' stsal na//gcig la gnyi su bstan pa ni//rnam grangs don 'di ji ltar lags ces zhuso//de nas byang chub sems byed rgyal pos bka' stsal pa//kye da ni ya nas lhun gyis grub//nga ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//lhun
11a4(rnying rgyud): gyis grub pa'i snying po yin//nga ni thugs rje'i ngo ste//gang la dga' ba'i dusu ni//gang dga' ba'i don bya ba'i phyir//nga yis byas pa'i don bstan gyis//sems dpa' rdo rje khyod nyon cig/gcig la dga' ba de la ni//brjod med snying po gcig tu bstan//chos rnams gcig tu 'dus pa/
11a5(rnying rgyud): phyir/byang chub sems kyi ngo ste//sems kyi ngo kun byed pas//mtshan ma grangs la dga' ba la//kun byed rgyal pos byas pa'i//sna tshogs brjod kyis mi lang bstan//de yang nga'i rang bzhin bstan//gcig na ngi'i snying po ste//gnyis byung nga yis byas pa ste//byas pa'i chos la bgrang
11a6(rnying rgyud): pa yin//grangs kyi mdo ni gcig dang gnyis//grangs kyi mthara' ni brjod med//brjod med chos kyi snying po yin//brjod med snying po gcig tu bstan//gcig ni kun pad byang chub sems//byas pa'i chos ni rnam pa gnyis//rgyu dang 'bras bu rnam gnyis las//don dam kun rdzob rnam gnyis
11a7(rnying rgyud): kyis//ngapa don rtags dang gtan tshigs gsum//des lung drang lung gnyisu bshad//rgyu mi 'byung chen rnam lnga la//'bras bu khams gsum sems can yin//khams gsum sems can rgyu byas nas//'bras bu sangs rgyas ming btags te/rgyu 'bras dpe don de ru bstan/rgyu ni khams dasu
11b1(rnying rgyud): sems can la//'bras bu sangs rgyas lung bstan pa//de ni drang ba'i lung du bshad//rnam pa gnyisu dbye bar bya//rgyu ni 'byung chen rnam lnga yin//byas pa'i chos ni thams cad sems ni mngon du phyung ba ste//de bzhin nyid du ma rtogs par//ma rig log rtog gzung 'dzin gyis//
11b2(rnying rgyud): yul drug chos la nges brtags pas//'jig pa drug la rten pa drug//'jig rten zhesu bshad pa yin//yul drug chags pa drug rten nas//chags drug sdug bsngal yang la//mya ngan mya ngan zhesu bshad/mya ngan mya ngan 'das zhes pa//yul drug chos la mi rtog pas//skye drug gas
11b3(rnying rgyud): su mi skye ste//sdug bsngal drug las 'das pa'i phyir//mya ngan 'das zhes de la bya//ji bzhin shar po byang chub las//ma brtsal rang bzhin shar ba'i//'byung ba chen po lnga po yang //rang gi mtshan nyid rang 'dzin la//thams cad snying po byang chub kyang //dmigasu med pa'i ngo 'dzin//
11b4(rnying rgyud): mtshan nyid mtshan nyid cesu bshad//rang bzhin shar ba'i chen po lngas//chos kun bskyed pa'i snying po des//de yis bskyed pa'i seMn rnams//rang byung yin par mi rtog par//spyod pa'i lam de rgyur bltas nas//rgyu las 'bras bu 'byung 'dod pas//rgyu zhes ming du btags pa yin//
11b5(rnying rgyud): 'bras bu gang thob theg par bshad//spong 'gegs sbyong ba rnamasu bstan//'bras bu zhesu btags pa ni//snying po'i rgyu las 'bras bur bshad//snying po'i rang bzhin de la ni//skye 'jig med pa'i rang bzhin la//rdo rje zhesu bshad pa yin//rdo rje de sgrub phyi ba gnyis//sku dang gsung thugs
11b6(rnying rgyud): ye shes ni//blang dor bas pa'i sgo nas ni//chu zla bzhin du grub par 'dod//blang dor med pa'i nang pa ni//rim pa gsum dang snying po yi//bdag nyid dbyings dang ye shes te//gang la'ang mi rten pa med 'di//kun gyi snying por gyur pas na//bya ba med pa'i 'phrin las 'das//bya med bde ba spong byed pa/
11b7(rnying rgyud): mi rtsol bde ba'i lung bstan bkod//bskal pa gsum dang mi che bdun//zla drug lo gcig zla bcu drug rtsol bar byed pa'i theg pa can//byar med do 'di bstan pas//mi rtsol lhun grub pade la gnas//'di la ma rdzogs gang yang med//gcig rdzogs gnyis rdzogs kun rdzogs pas//byad
12a1(rnying rgyud): phun sum tshogs par bde//gcig rdzogs byang chub semasu rdzogs//gnyis rdzogs sems kyis byas pa rdzogs//kun rdzogs phun sum tshogs par rdzogs//gcig la rdzogs pa'i lung 'di yis//sangs rgyas dgongs pa 'di yis ni//sangs rgyas dgongs pa 'di la
12a2(rnying rgyud): gnas/gnyis la rdzogs pa'i don 'di yis//cir yang sems kyi sprul par rdzogs//kun rdzogs pa'i don 'di yis//thams cad phun sum tshogs par byed//byar med 'di la gang gnas pa//lus ni gang zag lha mi yang //dgongs pa chos nyid sangs rgyas yin//'di yis sen don byas na//
12a3(rnying rgyud): rtsol sgrub mi byed bde bar 'gyur zhes gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//rnam grangs bsdus pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//byas pa'i chos thams cad de bzhin nyid gcig pa las//de bzhin nyid las rang bzhin rnam/
12a4(rnying rgyud): ba gsum snang bar gyur pa ste//saur snang ba 'di gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po//nga'i de bzhin nyid gcig ni//phun sum tshogs pa gsum du snang //de bzhin ma skyes rang bzhin ni//de bzhin nyid de yul du snang //yul de kun gyi de bzhin nyid//de la kun kyang brtags byas nas//rang bzhin ni
12a5(rnying rgyud): lta ba/ma bsgribs rang byung ye shes ni//rgyu 'bras ston pa sku gsum snang //la la rgyu'i ston par snang //la la 'brel pa'i ston par snang //la la 'bras bu'i ston par snang //thugs rje mngon du phyung ba'i//rang byung ye shes rnam lnga las/byung ba'i sangs rgyas sems can rnams/
12a6(rnying rgyud): bstan pa'i yul dang 'khor du snang //de lta bu'i 'o bar tshogs la//rgyu 'bras ston pa sku gsum gyis//de bzhin nyid kyi yul gcig las//'khor gyi lta ba sau las//la la spong 'gegs yul du bstan//le lar sbyong zhing bgrod par bstan//la lar byin brlabs dag par bstan/
12a7(rnying rgyud): la lang thung dor gyis thug la lar bang rgyud dag pa ste/cho la ning sgrub tu meng bar bstan/nga ltar ston pa'i sku gsum gyes/rgyung nga 'bras la dgongs nas bsdus/rgyu dang 'bras bu rnam gnyis kyang /de bzhin nyid la g.yas pa med/kun byed nga yis lung bsta'o/de na
12b1(rnying rgyud): nyid ni 'di lta ste//kun byed nga yang de bzhin nyid//ngas byas kun kyang de bzhin nyid//yul drug nga yis byas pa yin//dbang drug nga yi rig pa yin//rnam par shes pa'i tshogs rnams ni//nga yi rang byung ye shes yin//'byung chen lnga yang rgyu lnga ste//kun gyi rgyu lnga de bzhin nyid//khaMs gsuM/
12b2(rnying rgyud): rgyud drug phyung ba yang //de bzhin nyid kyi ngor bshad//de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin te//ji gnyi su med pa de//rang bzhin gcig tu gnas pa la/rnal du bzhag pa bzhi phyung ba/rna khyor rnam pa bzhi las byung //kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/kun gyi snying po byang chub sems 'di ni//chos rnam
12b3(rnying rgyud): kun gyi rnal ma'i nga ste//ji bzhin de bzhin nyi du ye nas gnas//shin tu rna khyor yongasu rnal 'byor dang //rnal 'byor chen po sems dpa'i rnal 'byor bzhis//snying po'i don gyi rnal mo byar med la//bltas pas mthong ba'i yul ni rnam pa bzhi//sems dpa'i rnal 'byor yul dang dbang pos
12b4(rnying rgyud): mthong //byang chub lnga dang cho 'phrul rnam pa bzhi//byin gyis brlabs nas sems dpa'i lha gnyis lha//snying po don gyi rnal ma mthong ba med//ma bcos de bzhin nyi sems dpa' zhog/don gyi snying po rnal ma byar med la//rnal 'byor chen po rnams kyis mthong ba ni//ye nas /
12b5(rnying rgyud): rgyud dag pa'i dkyil 'khor la//rgyu las 'bras bu lha'i dkyil 'khor de//bsnyen bsgrub bzhi yis yan lag rdzogs byas nas//'phro 'du bdag nyid lhun gyis grub par blta//don gyi snying po rnal ma mthong ba med//bcos de bzhin nyid chen po zhog/don gyi snying po rnal ma byar me
12b6(rnying rgyud): laT yo su rnal 'byor rnams kyis mthong ba ni//dbyings ni snying po rnal ma'i rgyur mthong la//ye shes chen po rnal ma'i 'bras bur mthong //snying po gcig la rgyu 'bras gnyis mthong bas//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i rnal ma mthong ba med//ma bcos de bzhin yong su rnal 'byor nos/
12b7(rnying rgyud): don gyi snying po rnal ma byar med la/shin tu rnal 'byor rdzogs chen lha mthong ba//byang chub sems dpa' kun gyi snying por mthong //de nyid ma bcos don gyi rnal mar mthong //lta ba ye nas bsgom du med par bzhag/dam tshig ye nas bsrung du med par gzhag phrin las
13a1(rnying rgyud): ye nas brtsal du med par gzhag/gzhag pas de bzhin nyid la rnal du 'byor//rnal 'byor rnam bzhi lta ba'i bye brag ni//yul cig re re la yang bzhir dbye ba//sems dpa'i rnal 'byor bye brag bzhir dbye ba//yul dang dbang po rnam par mi rtog sems//des
13a2(rnying rgyud): na sems dpa'i rnal 'byor zhesu bshad//byang chub byin rlabs cho 'phrul 'byin pa ni//de ni rnal 'byor chen po zhesu bshad//phyag rgya chen po bzhi dang ldan pa ni//yongasu rnal 'byor zhesu bshad pa yin//ye shes dam tshig rang bzhin med lta bas//shin tu rnal 'byor dgongs pa sad
13a3(rnying rgyud): bzhen/blang dor med pa'i rnal 'byor de ma mthong //rnal 'byor chen po bye brag bzhir dbye ba//dang po ting 'dzin rnam gsum sems dpa' ni//sems dpa'i rnal 'byor zhesu bshad pa yin//rang rgyud dag pas ye shes lha byed pas//rnal 'byor chen po zhesu bshad pa yin//
13a4(rnying rgyud): bsnyen bsgrub rnam bzhi yongasu rdzogs pa ni//yongasu rnal 'byor zhesu bshad pa yin//thams cad bdag nyid chen por lta ba ni//shin tu rnal 'byor zhesu bshad pa yin//rtsol sgrub 'das pa'i rnal ma mthong ba min//yongasu rnal 'byor bye brag bzhir lta ba//ting 'dzin rim pas bdag bskur/
13a5(rnying rgyud): mi bskyed par//chos nyid rang bzhin dag par sems dpa'i phyir//sems dpa'i rnal 'byor zhesu bshad pa yin//yab yum thabs dang shes rab mi ltos par//snying po tsam gyis dkyil 'khor rdzogs pa'i phyir//rnal 'byor chen po'i dgongs pa bshad pa yin//sna
13a6(rnying rgyud): la rang bzhin med pa'i chos nyid//spyod pas yong su rnal 'byor nyi du bshad//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//rgyu dbyings 'bras bu ye shes lta ba'i phyir//shin tu rnal 'byor nyi du bshad pa yin//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i rnal ma mthong ba min//shin tu rna khyor byi/
13a7(rnying rgyud): brag rnam bzhi ni//blang dor med pa'i byang chub sems de ni//sems dpa'i rnal 'byor zhesu bshad pa yin//rtsol sgrub 'das pa'i byang chub sems de ni//rnal 'byor chen po zhesu bshad pa yin//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i byang chub sems de ni//yong su rnal 'byor zhes/
13b1(rnying rgyud): bag pa yin/min chen sgo dang skur ba 'das pa'i chos//shin tu rnal 'byor zhe su gri ba kang //yongs rnal 'byor la sogs gsum pa la//'a byung ye shes me dang mi ldan pas/lam la bgrod seMngs la sbyong bar byed/dam tshig tshul bsrung lta ba /
13b2(rnying rgyud): bas/gom pa drag dal lha spyod yod ma yin//de bas shin tu rnal 'byor lta spyod ni//ye nas kun byed nyid kyis byos pa'i phyir//lam la mi bgrod sa la sbyong mi byed//dam tshig mi srung lta ba bsgom mi byed//byang chub chen po'i lam las kun byung phyir//byang chub//
13b3(rnying rgyud): nyid las byang chub nyid mi bgrod//bgrongs byang chub nyid las med pa'i phyir//byang chub nyid la byang chub nyid mi sbyong //dam tshig rang bzhin byang chub nyid yin pas//byang chub nyid la byang chub nyid mi srung //bsgom pa'i rang bzhin byang chub nyid yin pas//byang chub
13b4(rnying rgyud): nyid kyis nyid la bsgom du med//lta ba'i yul ni byang chub nyid yin pas//byang chub nyid la nyid kyis bltar maud phyir//shin tu rnal 'byor lha sbyod byar med 'di//rang byung ye shes gom pa drag dal can//kun byed rgyal po nga'i gom pa yin//rgyu 'bras
13b5(rnying rgyud): theg pa can gyi spyod yul min//zhes gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//kye rang bzhin las chos nyid kyi yul kun la bkod pa'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po de
13b6(rnying rgyud): rgyu 'bras kyi theg pa bgrod la ma nor bas//rdzogs pa chen po las go lau sa dang sgrib par gyur pa 'di gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po/byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin 'di nyid ni//chos kun ma lus pa'i snying po ste//ma skyes rnam par dag
13b7(rnying rgyud): bas sgrib pa med//bgrod pa'i lam dang bral bas gol sa med//ye nas lhun gyis grub pas btsal du med//chos kun snying po byang chub sems gcig la//gcig la rnam par bgrangs pas gol sgrib byung //bgro du med pa 'gro/
15a1(rnying rgyud): pas gol sa byung //dmigs med bltas pas ma mthong sgrib pa byung //de bzhin nyi du thams cad gcig pa la/rgyu spyang 'bras bu phyin par 'dod pa ni//pha rol phyin bcus bcu thob 'dod pa//skal pa gsum gyi bar du grol nas bskri/
15a2(rnying rgyud): phyi nang rtog pa dag pa rnam gsum gyis//byang chub lnga dang cho 'phrul yan lag gis//dam tshig sdom pa tshul bzhin dag na yang //mi tshe bdun gyi bar du grol zhing bsgribs//rgyu la spyod cing 'bras bu lta ba ni//lha spyod sau gnyisu bltas pa yis//gnyis
15a3(rnying rgyud): med la mi tshe gsum bsgribs gol//snying po gcig la blang dor byed pa de//gcig tu ma mthong gnyisu mthong bas na//blang dor ma bral bar du grol zhing bsgribs//rang byung gcig la yin pa gsum bsgoms pas//rtsol bral yul las gol zhes lung
15a4(rnying rgyud): ston cig/ji ltar snang zhing srid pa'i chos rnams la//snying po de bzhin nyi du ma rtogs par//dbyings dang ye shes rgyu 'bras bltas pa'i phyir//rgyu'i sgros dang 'bras bu'i skur yin//sgro skur gnyis la yid ches bar du sgribs//med pa'i 'bras bu 'dod/
15a5(rnying rgyud): pa rnam drug ni//rgyu la bden pa gnyis kyis thob byed dang //dag gsum spyad pas 'bras bu thob byed dang //lha spyod sos thob par 'dod pa dang //blang dor spyad pas thob par 'dod pa dang //rim pa gsum bsgoms thob par 'dod pa dang //rgyu 'bras dbyings nyid
15a6(rnying rgyud): ye shes pa ltas pa yis//thob par 'dod pa'i ngos thob rnam pa drug go la sa drug dang sgrib pa drug drug ste//byang chub sems la ko la sgrib smon yin pas//des thob drug gi skyon ni gsal bar gsungs//rtog dpyong gnyis kyang gol sa sgrub pa
15a7(rnying rgyud): gzung 'dzin gnyis kyang grol sa sgrib par bshad//lha skyong gnyis kyang gol sa sgrib par bshad//lha spyod blang dor rtsol sgrub rgyu 'bras rnams//mi rtsol bde ba chel kun gol ba sgribs//byang chub sems ni namakha' lta bu ste//rnamapa' lta bu'i sems
15b1(rnying rgyud): nyid chos nyid la//lta ba bsgom med dam tshig bsrung du med//rdzogs chen byang chub sems kyi lta ba'o//'phrin las brtsal med ye shes sgrib pa med//sa la sbyang med bgrod pa'i lam yang med//phra ba chos med gnyis med 'brel ba med//seMs wa
15b2(rnying rgyud): des lung gtan la 'bebs pa med//sgo skur 'das pas man ngag la zlar med//rdzogs chen byang chub sems kyi lta ba'o//lta med gcig la bsgom med drug bsgoms pas//la gol la sgribs lta ba'i gol sgrib byung //bsrung med cig la sngo ba
15b3(rnying rgyud): /drug bsrungs pas//la gol la sgribs dam tshig gol sgrib byang //brtsol med lam la 'phrin las drug brtsal bas//la gol la sgribs 'phrin las gol sgrib byung //sgrib med cig la yas drug byung bas//la gol la sgribs ye shes gol sgrib byung //sbrang
15b4(rnying rgyud): med cig la spangs thob drug byung bas//la gol la sgribs sa'i gol sgrib byung //bgrod med cig la lam bgrod drug byung bas//la gol la sgribs lam gyi gol sgrib byung //'dzin med gcig la 'dzin byed drug byung bas//la gol la sgribs chos kyi

15b6(rnying rgyud): gol la sgribs man ngag gol sgrib byung //kun gyi snying po byang chub sems 'di ni//ye nas rang gi rang bzhin lhan grub pas//rang bzhin bcu yis brtsal zhing bsgrub mi dgos//nga'i rang bzhin kun dpe na mkha' 'dra//nam mkha' dag la kun kyang rtsol
15b7(rnying rgyud): mi byed//na mkha' dag la kun kyang brtsal bya ru//na mkhas kun byas rtsol sgrub kun las 'das//de bzhin kun byed snying po byang chub sems//nga'i spyod yul kun las 'das pa'i phyir//nga la ltar med bsgom du ye nas med//de bzhin rang bzhin bcu
16a1(rnying rgyud): @po des kyang ni//'das pa ngal bya ru yong ma yin//rgyu dang 'bras bu'i theg pa can rnams kyis//rang bzhin bcu yis ngal brtsal byas na//nga dang nga'i rang bzhin mthong 'dod pas//mkha' la gom bo ra sa la ltung ba bzhin//rang bzhin bcu pos sgrob
16a2(rnying rgyud): bas ltung bar 'gyur//nga'i rang bzhin nges par khungs bstam pa//nga ni spyod yul kun las 'das pa'i phyir//lta ba bsgom du med pa de bzhin du//rang bzhin bcu la de bzhin don med kyis//rang bzhin bcu la don yod ma rtog shig/nga ni bltas pas mthong ba'i yul
16a3(rnying rgyud): med pas//lta bar ma byed de bzhin nyid du zhog/spros dral ma skyes bral ba'i rgyu med pas//sdom pa dang ni tshul khrims bsrung mi dgos//snying po ye nas lhun gyis grub pa'i phyir//rtsol ba dang ni bsgrub pa bya mi dgos//rang byung ye shes sgrib pa med pa'i phyir//rig pa'i yis
16a4(rnying rgyud): gsal bar bskyed med//thams cad nga'i sa ru gnas pa'i phyir//sbyangs nas bgrod pa'i sa ni yod ma yin//nga ni kun la khyab par gnas pa'i phyir//nga la bgrod par bya ba'i lam yod min//da ni yi nas gzung 'dzin bral ba'i phyir//phra ba zhes ni ming du gdag su med//nga'i
16a5(rnying rgyud): gzugs kyis kun la khyab pa'i phyir//gnyis zhes bya ba ye nas yod ma yin//nga ni ye nas rang byung ye shes pas//nga la gzhan gyis gtan la dbab tu med//da ni byang chub snying po bas//gsang ba'i man ngag gzhan na yod ma yin//sgro dang skur pa kun las 'das pa'i phyir/
16a6(rnying rgyud): chos rnams kun gyi la ni nga yis zla//nga med pa'i yul na gzhan med pas//lta ba bsgom du med par la yang zla//nga las gzhan du bsrung du med pa'i phyir//dam tshig bsrung du med par la yang zla//nga las gzhan du brtsal du med pa'i phyir//'phrin las bya ru med par la yang /
16a7(rnying rgyud): zla/kha las gzhan du gnas pa med pa'i phyir//sa la sbyang du med par la yang zla//nga la sgrib pa ye nas med pa'i phyir//rang byung ye shes yin par la yang zla//nga ni ma skyes chos nyid yin pa'i phyir//phra ba chos nyid yin par la yang zla//nga las gzhan du bgro du med pa'i phyir/
16b1(rnying rgyud): la bgro med par la yang zla//sangs rgyas sems can snang srid snod bcud kun//snying po byang chub nga las byung ba'i phyir//ye nas gnyi su med par la yang zla//rang byung ye shes gtan la 'bebs pa'i phyir//lung chen thog 'bebs yin par la yang zla//chos rnams tha~Md
16b2(rnying rgyud): nga las med pa'i phyir//kun byed nga ni kun gyi la zla'o////nga ma shes sgrib pa'i ngor bshad//nga las gzhan brtsal gol sa byung ba yin//las dang shes bya rnam pa gnyisu bstan//ma mthong ba ni las kyi sgrib pa yin//ma shes pa ni shes bya'i sgrib pa yin//ji ltar
16b3(rnying rgyud): snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//snying po byang chub sems kyi ngo bas//ma rig ma shes sgrib gnyis ngor bshad//brtsal zhing sgrub pa gol sar ngas bshad//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad ni//rang yin gzhan min de la rang
16b4(rnying rgyud): rtog na dede ni rang gis rang la bsgribs pa ste//rang gi rang bzhin de yis shes pa min//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//sems dpa' nyid yin nyid las gzhan min phyir//mi rtog mnyam pa nyi du bde bar gnos//de la bde ba'i don ni 'di lta ste//rang bzhin chen
16b5(rnying rgyud): bcu la brtsal mi dgos//rang bzhin chen po bcu la mo brtsal bas//gol sa sgrib pa drug drug bcu po'i//sum brgya drug cu gol sgrib mtha' yas med//kye soda' chen po//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//rang bzhin snying po byang chub mngon phyung gis//'di ma yin gyi
16b6(rnying rgyud): chos ni gzhan ma bshad//'di la ma rtog shig par nga lung ston//sems dpa' khyed kyi 'khor gyi tshogs rnams la//ngas bstan bzhin du tshogs la lung ston cig ces gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las/gol sgrib skyon sel gyi le'u ste dgu pa'o//
16b7(rnying rgyud): de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//nyid kyi rang bzhin phun sum tshogs pa 'di gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/kun byedang las thams cad 'tshogs pa'i phyir//de phyir phun sum tshogs zhes bya bar bshad//kun byed nga'i rang bzhin rnam gsum las//
17b1(rnying rgyud): 'chil bal mas 'deb bye mam kyi milams rdzuchu kyi 'phem sa kyi ma byar la ma ma bya 'as yi la yi mabyachi 'gyi bya phyi 'dri gyu par dri tha ma sa
18a1(rnying rgyud): / rje ngod pa gsus/'a sdus pa da zhes bstan pa yis mang sngal yang bgang rnams pacu gcis por dbye ba da du pa sas dug gsus sas las 'dus chupa sang gdus/sems la tu skyong sa sdig gcag
