18a7(rnying rgyud): /'di lta ste/'og min chos kyi dbyings kyi pho brang ste/yang dag don gyi 'og min de ru bshang //ston pa long spyod rdzogs pa'i gnas bstan pa//'jig rten mtshan ma kun gyi steng gyur pa//gnas kyi 'og min khang bu brtsegs pa'i//gzhal yas khang de gnas kyi 'og min pa
18b1(rnying rgyud): ston pa sprul pa'i sku'i gnas bstan pa//sprul pa'i sku ni rabs bdun shAkya thub//gnas ni brgod phung po'i gnas bstan te/yang dag thugs rje'i sprul pa nges med gnas ces gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga las byung ba ston pa sku gsum gyi//du
18b2(rnying rgyud): ba'i zhing khams khyad par bstan pa ni//chos sku ston pa'i 'dul ba'i zhing khams mi chos kyi dbyings kyi mtha' yis klas pa dang //na mkha'i khams kyis ji ltar khyab pa de//chos kyi sku'i 'dul ba'i zhing khams yin//long spyod rdzogs sku'i 'dul ba'i zhing las
18b3(rnying rgyud): ni/nga las byung ba'i gzugs sgra dri ro reg/ji ltar 'dod pa'i long spyod spyod pas 'dul//sprul pa'i sku'i 'dul ba'i zhing khams ni//'dzam+bu gling ni bye ba phrag brgya las//rang byung ye shes thugs rjes rgyud drug 'dul//de ni mthun pa'i bye brag bstan pa yin//nga las byung ba'i
18b4(rnying rgyud): ston pa sku gsum ste//rgyu mthun 'bras bu mthun la rang bzhin gcig/rgya ni byang chub semasu kun mthun la//'bras bu sangs rgyas yin par kun kyang mthun//rang bzhin chos nyid do gcig par bstan//'dul ba'i dus lo snga pi phye ba med//ces gsungaso//kye sems dpa'
18b5(rnying rgyud): chen po yang nyon cig/kun byed rgyal po nga las byung ba'i//sku gsum ston de'i bshad lugs ni//ston pa chos skus byin gyis brlabs kyis bshad//long spyod rdzogs pas rang gi ngos bshad//sprul pa'i sku yis tshig dang don bsdebs nas//gang zag rna ba'i dbang pos tshig don
18b6(rnying rgyud): ston//chos sku'i byin gyi brlabs kyi bshad pa ni//kun byed nga'i rang bzhin bstan pa ste//skye ba med las skye ba'i cho 'phrul du//ji ltar snang srid snod bcud thams cad kun//r-i thugs rje cho 'phral phyung ba ste//rang bzhin byin gyis brlabs kyi bshad ces bya//long spyod rdzog sku
18b7(rnying rgyud): rang gi ngo ston//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//gzugs sgra dri ro reg bya la sogs pa'o//'dod po'i long spyod thams cad rdzogs par ston//rang gi ngos bshad ces nga yis bstan//sbrul sku'i tshig dang yi ge bsdebs bshad pa//ji ltar snang ba'i tshe/
19a1(rnying rgyud): rnams thams cad kun//ston pa'i ngor ston pa ma rig ste//ston pa zhes pa'i khyad par 'dod sprul pas//don rnams tshig dang yi ger slar brjod pas//tshig gi sgra des don rnams go byas nas//don de tshig sgra de yis bshad pa yin//kye se 'a chen po
19a2(rnying rgyud): 'di nyon cig/kun byed dang ni sku gsum ngo ste//mi rtog mnyam pa nyi du gnas pa de//spos bral chos kyi sku'i ngo yin//rang bzhin 'byung ba'i skye ba'i cho 'phrul de//'dod pa 'byung ba'i long spyod rdzogs sku yin//zugs rje ji ltar 'dul ba'i don mdzad pa//de ni sprul pa'i sku'i ngo yin//
19a3(rnying rgyud): kun byed rgyal po'i rang bzhin rnam gsum gyis//gang la gang gis 'dul ba'i skur ston mdzad//bstan pa'i rang bzhin brjo med pa'i don//gang la gang 'tsham de yis don mdzad pa//de ni bstan pa phun sum tshogs par gsungs//'khor gyi rang bzhin gang la gang 'dus nas/
19a4(rnying rgyud): de la 'dus nas de'i gdung 'tsho ba 'dzin//de la phun sum tshogs pa'i 'khor zhes bya//nga ni ston pa kun byed byang chub sems//kun byed cig la rnam pa gsum bgrangs pa//kun byed nga dang 'a'i rang bzhin dang //nga yis byas pa'i chos dang rnam pa gsum//da ni ston pa kun byed kun
19a5(rnying rgyud): gyis mos//nga 'i rang bzhin rnam pa bcur bstan pa//de ni bstan pa kun gyi snying por bshang /kun bang nga yos byis pa'i chos rnams kun//dus gsum gcig na bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas dang /khams gsum gnas pa'i 'gro ba thams cad kun/dbye ba med pas 'khor gyir/
19a6(rnying rgyud): par byas//kun byed cig gis phun sum tshogs byas pas//sems kyi dgos pa kun byas phun sum tshogs//rgyu sung gang gis 'dul ba'i ston sku la/nyid dang mthun pa'i gnas 'khor phun sum tshogs//ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po ngas/nga'i rang bzhin rnam pa gsum
19a7(rnying rgyud): bstan pa'i tsho gyi dang ba ston pa sku gsum shor /ma skyes gzung 'dzin bral ba'i logs te/'khor gyi dang po ston pacos sru shang /smye ba'i 'phrul kun pang rol pa de/dod skyong sangs spyong rdzongs pa'i sa ju shar/sna tshogs ji ltar 'dod pa byung ngas me/de 'a
19a8(rnying rgyud): lag kyi grol co la gsu na pa'i 'bu tsu rgo lab gang //stor ba'i mabazhin ni ga gsu star te/Sho sku yis sku dang thu rje'i sgra
19b1(rnying rgyud): bar kun kyang tshi ma byed pa//de ni sna tshogs sbrul sku zhesu bshad//sku gsum ston pa grags phyogs mthong ba dang //sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' bzod thob gsum//gang 'thang ston pa'i sku la de 'dus nas//de dang 'thun pa'i gnas dang don ston bzhugs//bstan pa'i rang ni
19b2(rnying rgyud): yang ni de 'dra ste//sku gsum gang gis 'khor la pa//gang la gang gis 'dul ba'i 'khor rnams la//sangs rgyas lha mi 'jig rten las dag la//bstan pa 'dul phyir phun sum tshogs zhes bshad//mi gnas chos sku dbyings na bzhugs pa sI//dus gsum
19b3(rnying rgyud): sangs rgyas khams gsum sems can la//spros med byang chub sems ni chos skus bstan//ye shes 'og min long sbyod bzhugs pa yis//sa bcu pa'i byang chub sems dpa' la//byang chub sems de sku dang ye shes bshad//thugs rje sna tshogs nges med sprul pa sri/
19b4(rnying rgyud): bzod thob gang na 'dus pa'i 'khor de la//ma nges gang gis 'dul ba'i don yang bstan//smon lam las bzang rkyeni gyes bar mchod//nga'i thugs rje sems dpa'i dus la bab//ngas bstan 'khor gyi nang na khyod sdud dang //gnyis med don nyid nga yis bstan pa gsum
19b5(rnying rgyud): /tshogs pas dus gnas rang bzhin de la tshang //phun sum tshogs pa rnam pa gsum po de//ye nas dkon pa'i dkon mchog gsum du bstan//kun byed nga'i sku gsung thugasu bshad//ston pa dkon pa'i sangs rgyas dkon mchog ni//chos sku longs spyod sku dang sbrul sku ste/
19b6(rnying rgyud): bstan pa dam chos dkon pa'i gsung mchog ni//brjod med pa'i kun byed gsung mchog gsum//'khor gyi dge 'dun thugs mchog de la ni//gang la gang dgos gang 'tsham ston par byed//da ni ston pa kun byed byang chub sems//sku gsum byed pa'i ston pa ngas byas
19b7(rnying rgyud): nas/bstan pa dam chos gsum du bkod pa ni//phyi dang nang dang gsang ba gsum du bkod//de la rnam pa gsum gsum dgur grags pa//sbrul skus thugs rjes ji ltar 'dul gsum sgrogs//long spyod pa byed phyi nang gsum du sgrogs//chos skus gsang ba'i mngon pa/
20a1(rnying rgyud): gsum bsgrogs pa/phyi dang nang dang gsang ba gsum po de//lus dang ngag dang yid dang sku gsung thugs//de las 'phros pas rnam pa dgur byung ba//la dgu lung dgu lam dgu bgrod pa yis//theg gcig kun ba byang chub sa la bgrod//kun bdade ba byed
20a2(rnying rgyud): med par brtsol sgrub 'das//bgrod pa med par byang chub sa la phyin//ces gsungs//byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po las//phun sum tshogs pa shin tu bkod pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o////de nas byang chub kyo sems kun byeng rgyal pos//chos tha~Md
20a3(rnying rgyud): kyi rtsa ba bsdus pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/chos thams cad kyi rtsa ba dang rang bzhin ni 'di lta ste//nga ni chos kun rang bzhin yin pa ste//nga'i rang bzhin las ni chos gzhan med//ston pa sku gsum nga'i rang bzhin yin//dus gsum sang+yas nga'i rang
20a4(rnying rgyud): bzhin yin//byang chub sems dpa' nga'i rang bzhin yin//rnal 'byor rnam bzhi nga'iri rang bzhin yin//'dod khams gzugs khams gzugs med khams gsum yang //kun byed nga'i rang bzhin bstan pa yin//'byung chen lnga yang nga'i rang bzhin yin//rgyud drug seMn nga'i//
20a5(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin yin//snang ba thams cad nga'i rang bzhin yin//srid pa thams cad nga'i rang bzhin yin//snod bcud bsdus pa nga'i rang bzhin yin//rang bzhin las ni gzhan med pa//chos rnams kun gyi rtsa ba nga la 'das//la ma 'dus pa ni gcig kyang med//ma skyes pa dang skye
20a6(rnying rgyud): ba'i cho 'phrul dang //thugs rje'i rang bzhin rnam pa gsum byung ba//de ni ston pa gsum gyi rang bzhin yin//snga ma da ltar phyi ma dus gsum yang //nga nyid gcig tu gnas pa'i rang bzhin la//sangs rgyas thams cad gcig tu gnas pa'i phyir//de yang nga'i rang bzhin bstan
20a7(rnying rgyud): ba yin//nga nyid gzung 'dzin bral bas byang ba dang //na mkha' bzhin du kun la khyab pa dang //chos rnams kun gyi snying po byed pa'i phyir//byang chub sems kyang nga'i rang bzhin yin//kun byedang ni gcig tu gnas pa la//nga'i rang bzhin de bzhin rnal ma la//rnam pani 'byor pa rnalam//
20b1(rnying rgyud): mye 'i bu rjes yang byung /cu les rdo lnga bstan pan//gr-i sa drug so snang ba yag/kun n+ya pa'i rang bzhi bstan pa yis/ na rgyal pa ba bzhin pas/
20b2(rnying rgyud): gcig tu 'byor/des nang ri rang bzhin yin par bshad/kun bye ba'i lus dag yid gsum las/'byung yang nga'i rang bzhin de las 'byung /'dus kyang ba rang bzhin de ru 'dus//min yang pa'i ngo ba yin pas//khams gsum pa'i rang bzhin yin par bshad/
20b3(rnying rgyud): byed nga'i rang bzhin rnam lnga ni//nga ri ngo rnam pa phyung ba ste//nam khor rlung chus me snang ba wa//kun byed nga'i rang bzhin tas //snang sring snod bcud bsdus pa thams cad kun/thams cad ngas byas nga las byung ba'i phyir//ngar ma 'dus pa'i chos ni gcig kyang med/9/
20b4(rnying rgyud): byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum yang //kun byed nga'i rang bzhin rnam gsum ston//snga ma da ltar phyi ma dus gsum gyi//rnam gsum gcig tu gnas pa'i sangs rgyas kyang //snga phyi med par kun byed rang bzhin gan+da//gzung 'dzin thal ba'i byang chub sems dpa' yang //kun byed 'i
20b5(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin sems dpar byed//rnal 'byor rnam bzhi rnal mar 'jog pa yang //kun byed pa las rnal du 'jog par byed/kun byeng rgyal pos thams cad byas pa bzhin//thams cad kun byed rgyal po nyid du 'dus//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po las//
20b6(rnying rgyud): chos thams cad kyi rtsa ba byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po nyid kho nar 'dus pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po nga yis//chos thams cad kyi khungs kyi mdo 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po//nga ni kun gyis
20b7(rnying rgyud): khungs yin pa ste//'di ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//thams cad nga las byung bar shes par gyis//chos kyi spyi khungs chen po 'di nyon la//chos rnams thams cad 'di ltar lung ston cig/kun byed rgyal po byang chub sems nga ni//lung chen kun gyi rgyal po nga yin/
20b8(rnying rgyud): pas/ston pa gsum yang dang po rang las byung //lung zhes bya ba ston pa'i bka' la gdags//man ngag kun gyi rtsa ba yin pas//sems las ma gtogs pa'i chos nyid ni//dus gsum sangs rgyas khams gsum san la//kun byed nga yis yang dag bstan pa ste/
21a1(rnying rgyud): rgyud dang mdo sde kun gyi spyi 'grel yin//rgyud dang mdo sde ston pa'i ston pa yang //kun byed nga'i rang bzhin phyung ba yin//ston pa dang ni bstan pa gnyis ka 'grel//kun byeng rgyal po byang chub sems nga ni//bstan pa kun gyi yang rtse yin par bshad//
21a2(rnying rgyud): nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//bstan pa'i 'dul ba mdo sde mngon pa dang //tan+ta sau'i sde ni 'bum sde dang //bskyed rdzogs gsang ba la sogs rtsol sgrub can//kun kyang rtsol 'das pa ngal bgrod//rtsol sgrub nyid kyis 'das pa nga mi mthong //des na bstan pa
21a3(rnying rgyud): kun gyi yang rtse yin par bshad//kun byed rgyal po byang chub sems nga ni//theg pa kun gyi snying yin par bshad//ston pa gsum gyi gnas pa'i theg pa gsum//nges par gnas pa'i theg pa gcig las med//gcig ni kun theg byang chub sems kyis//de ni theg pa kun//
21a4(rnying rgyud): gyi snying khur bshad//kye nga ni ma rig po'i mun bsal nas//ye shes chen po'i snang ba mched par byed//de yang ji lta bu zhes bstan zhe na//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//byang chub de bzhin nyi du ma rig ste//ma rig rtog pa'i mun pas 'thibs pa la//ji ltar
21a5(rnying rgyud): snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//kun dang byang chub sems kyis byas pa'i phyir//kun byed byang chub semasu rig byas nas//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//so sor rtog pa'i mun pa bsal nas kyang //rang byung ye shes chen po'i snang ba mched//
21a6(rnying rgyud): na ma rig mun sel snang ba mched ces bya//kye sems dpa' chen po//rnam par rtog pa'i dra ba dral nas ni//nyon mongs pa'i phreng thag gcod par byed//ji ltar snang ba'i chos 'di thams cad la//rgyu dang 'bras bu'i theg pa can rnams kyis//la las dug
21a7(rnying rgyud): mthong spong ba'i rtog pa byed//la las sems chags pa'i yul mthong nas//bden gnyis ma chags pa'i rtog pa byed//la las rlom dag bya ba'i yul mthong nas//dag pa gsum dang byin brlabs cho 'phrul gyis//rje dang khol po'i tshul
21b1(rnying rgyud): du rtog par byed//la las rang rgyud dag pa'i rang bzhin la//bsnyen bsgrub rnam bzhis sgrub 'dod rtog byed pa//thams cad kun byed byang chub sems nga yis//sems las gzhan ma yin rtog bral nas ni//sor rtog pa'i nyon mongs phreng thag gcod//ces
21b2(rnying rgyud): gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po//kun byed rgyal po byang chub sems nga ni//khyod kyis nga'i rang bzhin 'di rtogs la//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//nga las gzhan med pa ru lung ston cig/khyod kyis nga'i lung 'di bstan 'gyur na//khyod la
21b3(rnying rgyud): 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs thams cad kyis//kun byed nga'i rang bzhin rtogs gyur na//kun kyang nga'i rang bzhin nyid du 'gyur//kun kyang nga'i rang bzhin gyur pa na//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad la//kun kyang spong 'gegs bden gnyis spyod mi
21b4(rnying rgyud): byed bu gtsang sbras dag gsum byin gyis rlob mi byed//ting 'dzin sems bskyed rtsol dang sgrub mi byed//thams cad kun byed nga ru byas zin pas//kun byed da ru thams cad mnyam pa nyid//mnyam nyid da yis mnyam nyid bya mi dgos//mnyam nyid bya yi dogos pa
21b5(rnying rgyud): sngar lung bstan//zhes gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po la so//nges par lung khungs bstan pa'i le'u ste bcus pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po des//lung thams cad kyi rgyal po yin pa'i phyir//chos
21b6(rnying rgyud): ste lung bshad pa'i bshad lugs 'di gsungs//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga ni chos kyi spyi lung yin pa'i gtan tshigs don gyi bshad lugs rnam lnga ni//spyi don rnam pa lnga yis bshad pa ste//lo rgyus don gyi bshad lugs
21b7(rnying rgyud): bstan pa dang //rtsa ba'i don gyi bshad lugs bstan pa dang //yo ga'i don gyis bshad lugs bstan pa dang //dgos pa'i don gyi bshad lugs bstan pa dang //tshig gi don gyi bshad lugs bstan pa'o//dang po yid ches pa'i khungs bstan phyir//lo
22a1(rnying rgyud): rgyas don gyi bshadang lugs bstan par bya//chos kun rtsab sems 'dus pa'i phyir//de nas rtsa ba'i don de bstan par bya//theg pa'i khyad par sor dbye ba'i phyir/yo ga'i don gyi rtsa ba bstan par bya//rdol sgrub bya mi dgos pa bstan pa'i
22a2(rnying rgyud): phyir/dges ched don gyi dgos pa de ru bstan/mi rtogs don de rtogs par bya ba'i phyir//tshi go gi don de sgra ru brjod par bya//kye sems dpa' chen po//lo rgyus don gyi dgos pa bstan pa ni//dang por yid ches pa'i khungs bstan phyir//lo rgyus don gyi
22a3(rnying rgyud): bgang lugs bstan pa ni//rang bzhin byin gyis brlabs kyis bshad lugs dang /nga gi ngo bstan pa'i bshad lugs dang //don gyi sgra tshig bsdebs pa'i bsha zug so//kun byed ba'i rang bzhin rnam gsum las//nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis/
22a4(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin dang ni ngo sgra tshig gis//ji ltar byung ba'i khungs nges yid kyang ches//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/rdza ba don gyi dgos pa bstan pa ni//snang srid snod bcud bsdus pa thams cad dang //sangs rgyas sems can bsdus pa thams cad kus/
22a5(rnying rgyud): byed byang chub nga yis byas pa'i phyir//thams cad byang chub sems las ma rtogs pa//gzhan zhes bya ba ye nas med pa'i phyir//kun kyang byang chub semasu bstan pa'i phyir//rtse ba'i don gyi dgos ched bstan pa yin//kye sems dpa' cheno nyon cig/yo/
22a6(rnying rgyud): don gyi dgos ched bstan pa ni//kun byed nga las byung ba'i sku gsum gyis//long spyod sku dang chos sku bstan pa ni//tan+ta la sogs yo ga sde rnams las//sa'i khyad par bye brag yod kyi kyang //rgyu 'bras 'das pa nga yis bstan pa ni//rdzogs chen a ti/
22a7(rnying rgyud): lo ga'i don 'di ma//theg pa an 'byed rdzogs pa'i lung zhes bstan/des na yo ga'i don du gyi dgas pa bstan pa yim/dgas cher don gyi dgos pa bstan pa ni/bskal par dpag tu med pa'i sngon rib nag/kun byed byang chub pa 'i nga pa'i//las dang na ma
22b1(rnying rgyud): shin tun lhor ga/lta ba bsgom med dam tshig bsrung du med//'phrin las brtul med lam la bas grong med//sa la spyad med rgyu dang 'bras bu med//don dam dang ni kun rdzob rnam gnyis med//bsgom zhing bsgrub tu med par mthong ba dang //sems bskyed med ni
22b2(rnying rgyud): gnyen po med mthong bas//kun byed sems kyi rang bzhin mthong ba'i//de lta bu'i dgos ched bstan pa yin//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig tshig gi don gyi dgos pa bstan pa ni//kun byed byang chub sems pa de bzhin nyid//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams bstan pa ni//ku/
22b3(rnying rgyud): khyed de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin to//tshig dang yi ge sgra ru ma bsgrags na//gang zag yid kyi dbang po rig pa yis//de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin gab mi snang //de na/tshig dang yi ge sgra ru bsgrags//des na tshig gi don gyi dgos par bshad//kye sems dpa' chen po/gang dang /
22b4(rnying rgyud): gang gi lo rgyus bshad pa ni//bston pastan pa 'khor gyi lo rgyus te//ston pa kun byed rang bzhin rnam gsum las//'khor gyi rang bzhin ston pa gsum byung //sku gsum ston pa'i bshad lugs rnam gsum ni//ston pa bstan pa gsum gyi lo rgyus yin///
22b5(rnying rgyud): gang dang gang gi rtsa ba bshad pa ni//nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//ji skad bstan pa'i chos rnams thams cad kun//rtsub byang chub semasu bstan pa'i phyir//ston pa dang ni bstan pa 'khor rnams kyi//rtsa ba don gyi gtan tshigs bstan pa yin//'i rang
22b6(rnying rgyud): bzhin de bzhin nyid gcig//rnal 'byor rnam bzhi yo ga'i bye brag ni//lta ba spyod ba dam tshig bsgrub pa dang //mi mthun khyad par so'i bye brag ste//shin tu rnal 'byor rtsol sgrub 'das pas 'byed//des na yo ga'i don gyi gtan tshigs bstan//dgos byed don gyi nges/
22b7(rnying rgyud): /tshig bstan pa ni/dgos ni khams gsum sems can thams cad kun/rang sems de bzhin nyi du mthong gyur na//sangs rgyas kyi sa la mi gnas te//da lta nyid nas shin tu na myor thog//las med skal ba chung ba rnams la ni//ma gab mngon du byung yang mi go sta/
23a1(rnying rgyud): gang nig nor bu rin chen 'dod ba de//shing la byi ngor byas pas mi 'gyur zhin//las bzang skal ldan shin tu rnal 'byor la//lta ba dam tshig su lum dang //sems bskyed rgyu 'bras bsgom bsgrub gnyen po med//don dam kun rdzob rnam gnyis med/
23a2(rnying rgyud): mthong ba//des na sems kyi de bzhin nyid mthong bas//de lta bu la dgos pa'i dgos ched yin//zhes gsung so//tshig gi don gyi dgos pa'i nges tshig ni//med pa'i don bcu med pa'i sgra dang tshig/tshig tu brjod pas bya med don mthong nas//bya mi dgos pa'i kun byed de thob na//
23a3(rnying rgyud): tshig gis dgos pa bstan pa yin zhes bya//zhes gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las/sems kyi bshad lugs kyi le'u ste bcu gsum pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//nyid kyi gsang ba dam pa'i gdam ngag 'di gsung
23a4(rnying rgyud): so//kye sems dpa' chen po//kun byed nga ni kun tu gsang ba'o//las byung ba'i sku gsum ston pa la//nga'i rang bzhin rnam gsum mi bstan gsang //nga las gnas pa'i dus gsum sangs rgyas la//nga'i rang bzhin sku gsum ston pa la//nga'i rang bzhin mi bstan gsang bar bya/
23a5(rnying rgyud): nga la 'das pa'i 'khor tshogs thams cad la//nga'i rang bzhin mi bstan gsang bar bya//nga yis byas pa'i khams gsum sems can la//nga yi rang bzhin mi bstan gsang bar bya//nga'i rang bzhin ma gsang bstan byas na//sku gsum ston pa nga las 'byung mi 'gyur//sku
23a6(rnying rgyud): sum ston pa nga las ma byung na//bstan gsum theg gsum phun sum tshogs mi 'gyur//bstan gsum theg gsum phun sum tshogs na//sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun dkon mchog gsum//bla med byang chub kun gyis rig pa med//nga las byung ba'i dus gsum sa sa
23a7(rnying rgyud): la nga yi rang bzhin ma gsangs bstan gyur na//sku gsum ston gsum med pa'i skyon du 'gyur//nga la 'dus pa'i dus gsum 'khor ba'i tshogs rnams la//nga'i rang bzhin ma gsang bstan 'gyur na//ston gsum theg pa'i khyad par byed mi 'gyur//nga yis byas pa'i khams
23b1(rnying rgyud): gsum sems can la//nga ri rang bzhin thugs rje bstan 'gyur na//ston pa dus kyi bstan pa gnas med 'gyur//ston pa gsum gyi bstan pa gnas med na//kun byedang yis byas pa'i chos rnams la//phun sum tshogs pa zhes ni su yis 'dogs//des na kun byeng rgyal po yis
23b2(rnying rgyud): nga'i rang bzhin nga yis phyung nas ni//nga la nga'i rang bzhin nga yis bstan//nga las byung ba'i ston pa'i 'khor rnams la//bstan pa'i lung ni kun byedangs ma phog/shin tu rnal 'byor kun bye dang yin pas//de la nga'i rang bzhin bstan par bya//zhes gsung so//byang chub kyi sems
23b3(rnying rgyud): kun byed rgyal po las//skal med la gsang ba'i le'u ste/bcu bzhi pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal pos//nyid kyi lung chen po mngon du byung ba'i lung 'di bstan to//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/kun byed rgyal po nga yis ni//mngon du phyung ba gsum
23b4(rnying rgyud): stan te//nga'i rang bzhin rnam gsum ngas bstan pa//don rnams ston pa'i ston pa dang //don rig 'dod pa'i 'khor rnams dang //ma nor nges pa'i dodon rnams gsum//kun byed nga yis mngon du phyung //don ston ston pa mngon phyung ba ni//chos kyi sku dang long spyod rdzogs//sprul pa'i sku dang
23b5(rnying rgyud): rnam pa gsum//'khor gyi dang po ston pa sku gsum ste//don ston pa'i ston pa de//gzung 'dzin bral ba'i rang bzhin dang //nga'i ngo bstan pa dang //thugs rje'i rang bzhin don mdzad gsum//rang bzhin gsum las mngon du phyung //ston pa gsum yang mngon phyung ba'i//don rig 'dod pa mngon
23b6(rnying rgyud): byung ba/ston pa gsum gyi 'khor gsum ste//de bstan pa'i bye brag ni/dus gsum bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas dang /khams gsum gnas pa'i sems can kun/mi rtog mnyam nyid gnas pa ste/kun bye dang mis mngon phyubar khor/dung pos yul gyi rnam pa la/nyon mongos mi skyen
23b7(rnying rgyud): mi rtog/rang gi byang chub sems nyid la//chos dang chos nyid rtog byed pa//dang po sa nas bcu par bsgribs//sa bcu byang chub sems dpa' dang //long spyod 'dod yon rnam pa lngar//mthong ba'i ston 'khor rnam gnyis kyang //nga yis ngo mngon phyung yin//de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin la//
24a1(rnying rgyud): @rig pa yid kyi dbang po nges//brtags pas mthong ba'i shes pa de//la la rtag pa nyi mthong //la las stong pa chad par mthong //rgyu 'bras log par mthong ba yi//mu stegs rnam bzhi byin brlabs nas//rgyu dang 'bras bu lung bstan pa'i//sdig spong sams
24a2(rnying rgyud): dge rnam bzhi ni//bskal pa chen po gsum bar du//gnas pa'i 'khor dang ston pa yang //nga yis mngon du phyung ba yin//ye shes rig pa'i yul can la//yang dag nges par mngon du byung //kye sems dpa' chen po//mngon du phyung ba zhes bya ba//gang gis gang zhig mngon phyung ba ni///
24a3(rnying rgyud): gang gis nga'i mngon du phyung //gang zhig mngon du phyung ba ni//sems nyid de nyid mngon du phyung //gang gi don du mngon phyung na//yul la sor lta ba yi//'khor gyi don du mngon phyung yin//la la spong ba'i don du phyung //la la 'gags pa'i don du phyung //la la sbyong ba'i don du phyung
24a4(rnying rgyud): la la dag byed don du phyung //la la blang dor don du phyung //la la bdag nyid don du phyung //la las spang ba'i don du mthong //la las dgag pa'i yul du mthong //la las sbyang ba'i yul du mthong //la las dag byed yul du mthong //la las blang dor yul du mthong //la las//
24a5(rnying rgyud): dbang bsdu'i yul du mthong //rgyu dang 'bras bu'i ston pa yi//'khor gyi tshogs kyis de ltar mthong //sems dpa' chen po/nga yi rang bzhin mngon phyung la//nges par lung bston cig/nga ni khyod kyi ngo ste//nga'i rang bzhin phyung ba ste//khyed kyi rang bzhin phyung ba des//rang gi rang bzhin
24a6(rnying rgyud): shes par gyis//rang gi ri shes par gyis//rgyu 'bras 'khor la lung ston cig/ces gsung so//de nas sems dpa' rdo rje des//rgyu 'bras kyi 'khor la 'di skad ces gsung so//kye rgyu dang 'bras bu'i ston 'khor rnams//ston par ston pa kun byed kyis//kun byas phun sum tsho/
24a7(rnying rgyud): par byas//phun sum tshogs pa mngon byung ba//nga yi rang bzhin mngon phyung ba//rang gi rang bzhin yin par rtogs//rang gi ngo yin par rtogs//de las gzhan yin ma rtog par//rang nyid yin par kun tu rtogs//rang nyid yin par ma rtogs te//yul der du mi rtogs par//
24b1(rnying rgyud): yul de dgra dang che brtags nas//rang gi rang bzhin de spangs na//bskal pa brgya phrag yun ring su//chos nyid rang gi sems mi mthong //rang sems chos nyid ma mthong na//bskal pa brgya phrag yun ring su//rang gi byang chub de spong 'gyur//byar med pade chen de mi thob//yul de rang du/
24b2(rnying rgyud): ma rtogs par//yul de rkyen gyis dgrar brtags nas//rang gi rang bzhin de bkag na//bskal pa brgya phrag yun ring su//chos nyid rang gi sems mi mthong //rang sems chos nyid ma mthong na//bskal pa brgya phrag yun ring su//rang gi byang chub de 'gags pas//byar med bde//
24b3(rnying rgyud): chen de mi thob//yul de rang du ma rtogs par//yul de sbyang ba'i yul mthong nas//rang gi rang bzhin skyong byed na//bskal pa chen po gsum bar du//chos nyid rang gi sems mi mthong //rang sems chos nyid ma mthong ste//rang sems sbyang med gdeng dang bral//yul de byang //
24b4(rnying rgyud): chub rang dag la//gzung 'dzin dri ma yod mthong nas//phyi nang rtog pa dag gsum gyis//khrus dang gtsang sbra dag byed pa//dag pa'i rang sems ma mthong bas//rang sems dag pa de dang bral//mi'i skye ba tshe bdun du//rang sems chos nyid de mi mthong //blang dor med pa'i byang chub/
24b5(rnying rgyud): la/blang dang dor ba'i yul mthong nas//rang byung nyid la blang ngor byas//rang gis rang la blang doR+Ya//bar ma ched kyi tshe gsum du//rang sems gnyis med de mi mthong //lhun gyis grub pa'i byang chub la//brtsal zhing sgrub pa'i yul mthong nas//sems bskyed ting 'dzin
24b6(rnying rgyud): rnam gsum gyis//thams cad dbang sdud bdag tu byed//rang gi byang chub brtsal med la//brtsal bas sgrub par 'dod pa'i phyir//khacu drug zla bas tshe bsgrubs nas//stong dang brgya drug lo yis ni//cha lugs longs sku bzung nas ni//rang sems lhun grub bzod rtog byed//rang
24b7(rnying rgyud): sems chos nyid mngon phyung 'di//rgyu dang 'bras bur rtog byed pa'i//rjel sgrub can la phan med kyang //las dang skal bar ldan pa'i//dgos ched don du byung ba yin zhes sems dpa' rdo rjes gsung so////de nas kun byed rgyal po nyid kyis lung bstan pa//kye song/
25a1(rnying rgyud): /chen po//chos nyid mngon du phyung ba rang sems yin//rgyu dang 'bras bu theg pa can rnams kyis//rang sems mngon du phyung ba ma rtogs te//spong zhing 'gegs pas bskul ba yun ring gnas//sbyong zhing bgrod pas bskal pa gsum du gnas//dag skye
25a2(rnying rgyud): khrus dang gtsang sbras mi tshe bdun//byin brlabs blang dor can ni mi tshe gsum//dbang sdud bdag 'dod lo stong brgya drug tu//long sku ma nges de sri rang sems rtog/nam rtog dus na mi rtsol bde chen thob//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/rgyu 'bras theg chen bzhin du ma
25a3(rnying rgyud): yin gyis//kun bye dang yi lung 'di nges par zung //ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad la//rang sems chos nyid ye nas gcig yin pas//rgyu 'bras lugs bzhin 'di la rtog ma byed//rang sems de bzhin nyid la brtag bya ru//rang gi byang chub ngo snang ba yin///
25a4(rnying rgyud): 'di la ma gtogs sos ming btags pa//kun rdzob snang la don dam mi snang 'dogs//don dam kun rdzob gnyis med bden pa gcig/bden pa yang dag nges pa yod ma yin//yang dag 'dogs pa nga yang 'khrul pa yin//bde ba 'dod pa chags pa'i nad yin pas//'dod /
25a5(rnying rgyud): ba med pas bde ba len par byed//sangs rgyas bsgrubs pas grub pa ma yin te//ma brtsal rang bzhin gnas pas lhun gyis grub//ma brtsal rang bzhin gnas la mi rtog zhog/sangs rgyas don gyi bla dgas ming 'di la//sangs rgyas yang dag yod par nga mi ston//ga
25a6(rnying rgyud): gis sangs rgyas yod par ma'ong ba ni/chos nyid du byings las sangs rgyas mi rnyed de//sangs rgyas ma lta ra sems kyad meng rtogs/brtags na med de rang sems ye nas gas pa/ su m-i snang dang bzhi kum la khyub/yin pa do spong ba 'di yin te/'di med snyam pa spyi
25a7(rnying rgyud): 'bras theg can la//slong 'gegs sbyong ba byin brlabs sna tshogs kyis//rang sems chos nyid par med nga las gol//rang gam bzhag nas gzhan ma rgyang nas tshol/bar med bde por rtsol ba dang du len/tshe chad pa'i nang du de las med//ma yengs ting 'dzin 'dzin pa'i rti/
25b1(rnying rgyud): /phur yin//ye nas yin la yengs pa'i shor ba med//ma yengs ting 'dzin re ba'i skus mkhan des//rgyu 'bras theg chen drang ba'i lung bstan yin//ye nas yin la yengs med shor med de//rtsol sgrub kun gyi gnyen po 'joms byed de//rgyu 'bras ston pa'i ston pa 'khor rnaMs
25b2(rnying rgyud): la//kun byed nga'i lung 'di bstan gyur na//rgyu las 'bras bu 'byung zhes sgro skur 'dabs//sangs rgyas 'dod pa'i rnal 'byor ting 'dzin can//ting 'dzin 'dod pas ma bcos rnal 'byor spangs//ma bcos rnal ma kun gyi chos nyid yin//chos nyid las ni sangs gud
25b3(rnying rgyud): na med//sangs rgyas ming du btags pa bla dgas yin//chos nyid bya ba gzhan med rang sems te//rang sems ma bcos chos kyi sku ru bshad//ma bcos pa la ye nas skye med pas//skye med don la btsal zhing bsgrub tu med//brtsal zhing bsgrubs pas byar med 'grub/
25b4(rnying rgyud): mi 'gyur//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/chos kyi sku dang longs spyod rdzogs sku dang //sprul pa'i sku dang ston pa rnam pa gsum//theg gcig theg gnyis theg pa rnam gsum gyis//ston pa kun byed rgyal po nga yis phyung //nga ni ston pa kun gyi 'byung gnas yin/
25b5(rnying rgyud): nga'i rang bzhin rnam pa gsum las byung //spros bral skye med dga' ba'i 'khor rnams la//nga yi rang bzhin gzung 'dzin bral ba las//mi rtog mnyam pa nyid kyi chos sku phyung //chos sku'i 'khor ni phye ba med pa ste//ma skyes mnyam pa nyid kyi don ston pa//de ni lhag bla'i
25b6(rnying rgyud): chosu bshad//nga'i rang bzhin kun tu snang ba las//'dod spong dag pa'i 'khor gyi tshogs rnams la//nga yis nga long spyod rdzogs pa phyung //dau de la lta spong rdzogs byed pa//de ni thun mongs min pa'i theg pa ste//nga'i rol pa'i r-i phyung ba yin//nga'i rang bzhin thugs rjer
25b7(rnying rgyud): byung ba la/ji ltar 'dub bar sprul nas don mdzad pa'i//sprul pa'i sku dang 'khor tshogs rnams bzhi la/rgyu 'bras 'tshams pa'i thugs sde phyung ba yom/kye sems dpa' chen po//mdor na mngon du byung ba'i lung zhes bya//nga mi rang bzhim 'di ltar bstan pa la/'ayod
26a1(rnying rgyud): @#/phyung ba'i lung zhes bstan pa yin//kun byed nga'i rang bzhin gcig pu la//snang ba'i yul ni rnam pa gsum snang la//de las byung ba'i chos ni 'di lta ste//sangs rgyas thams cad snang srid snod bcud kun//kun byed pa'i rang bzhin mngon phyung yin//'di las gzhan ste/
26a2(rnying rgyud): pa ru rtog byed pa//kun byed pa dang phrad pa'i skal med pas//nga'i 'khor gyi dang po sku gsum gyis//rim pas sbyangs pa'i rgyu 'bras theg 'khor yin//kye sems dpa' chen po//nga yis mngon du phyung ba 'di//gzhan ma mngon tu phyung ba min//nga nyid mngon du phyung ba yin//chos nyid mngon du
26a3(rnying rgyud): phyung ba yin//rang sems mngon du phyung ba yin//ma nor mngon du phyung ba yin//nges pa mngon du phyung ba yin//de bzhin mngon du phyung ba yin//kye sems dpa' chen po/nga'i rang bzhin mngon du phyung ba 'dis//che ba lnga'i sangs rgyas 'di lta ste/nga'i ngo mngon du phyung ba yin//snang srid snod bcu dang //
26a4(rnying rgyud): yi chos nyid 'di//kun la snang bas mngon du phyung ba yin//bya mi dgos pas ye nas sangs rgyas yin//rtsol sgrub med pas ye nas che bar bshad//mngon par sangs rgyas zhesu bshad pa yin//nga yi rang bzhin mngon du phyung ba 'di//snying por gyur pas kun gyi bdag nyid yin//bya mi dgos
26a5(rnying rgyud): pas sangs rgyas yin//rtsol sgrub med pas ye nas che bar bshad//bdag nyid chen po sangs rgyas che bar bshad//ma skyes spros bral mngon du phyung ba 'di//ye nas gzung 'dzin bral bas chos kyi dbyings//ba mi dgos pas ye nas sangs rgyas yin//rtsol sgrub mi dgos ye nas che bar
26a6(rnying rgyud): bshad/chos dbyings sangs rgyas che ba yin par bshad//nga yis nges par mngon du phyung ba ni//dpe don rtags ni rnam pa gsum bstan te//chos nyid don la nam mkha'i dpe bstan te//byang chub sems kyi rtags kyis nges pa ru//the tshom za ba rnams la nges pa ru//dpe don rtags kyis de
26a7(rnying rgyud): yin sangs rgyas bstan//nga'i rang bzhin de bzhin nyid 'di ni//su la mngon du phyung yang mi snang bas//de ni ma nor ba'i rang bzhin la//bzhin ni bcos pa med pa'i rang bzhin te//nyid na ngo nyid la btags pa yin//de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin de nyid la//dus gsum sangs rgyas yod pa'i sgros mi 'dogro//
26b1(rnying rgyud): khams gsum sems can med pa'i skur mi 'debs//rtog dpyong bsam pa cir yang dgos med pas//sangs rgyas med pa'i che b.rang yis pashang //ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//rang bzhin rnam pa gsum mngon du byung ba'i leru skye bco lnga pa'o//de nas
26b2(rnying rgyud): byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//sems dpa' rdo rje la gdam ngag phog pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' rdo rje chen po 'di nyon cig/da ni ya nas kun byed rgyal po yin//ston pa bstan pa 'khor dus ngas byas nas//ston pa bstan pa yang ni nga yis byed//bstan pa nga yi rang bzhin de /
26b3(rnying rgyud): la/'khor yang nga yi ngo bo de pyang nas//dus gnas nga yi rang bzhin de ru ni//kun byed nga yi rang bzhin bstan pa las//nga tshe chos ni gcig kyang bstan pa med//sems dpa' chen po rdo rje khyod nyid kyang //kun bad nga yi rang bzhin bstan pa'i phyir//khyod kyang nga las nga yis phyung ba yin//kun byed
26b4(rnying rgyud): nga ni chos kyi snying por zhog/kun byed nga ni ye nas byas zin gyis//da gdong bya dgos kun la lung ma ston//da gdod bya dgos 'khor la lung bstan na//'khor rnams rtsol ba'i nad kyis zin par 'gyur//rang byung ye shes med pa'i skyon du 'gyur//byang chub sems la 'chos pa'i skyon du 'gyur te
26b5(rnying rgyud): nyid la 'chos pa'i skyon du 'gyur//skyon gyis ston khyadadu gsod par 'gyur//brdzun gyis bden pa bslus pas bslad bas na//min pa'i rtsol bas byar med yin ma bsgrub//sems dpa' khyod kyis kun byed nga rig gis//nga yis byas pa'i chos rnams thams cad kun//ye nas rang byu
26b6(rnying rgyud): ye shes de bzhin nyid//ston gsum nga yi rang byung ye shes yin//bstan pa gsum yang nga'i de bzhin nyid//'khor rnams kun kyang sems dpa' rdo rje'i tshul//dus gnas phun sum tshogs pa thams cad kun//kun bad rgyal po nga yi byang chub sems//ces gsungaso//byang chub
26b7(rnying rgyud): kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//sems dpa' rdo rje la gdam ngag phog pa'i le'u ste bcu drug pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po ngas/nyid kyi sku gdung 'di thung cig par gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po 'di rung shig spu gdu gros /
27a1(rnying rgyud): //'chang na//rgyal ba'i yang mes kun byed nga dang mnyam zhes gsung so//de nas sems dpa' rdo rjes zhus pa/kye rus gsum sangs rgyas kun gyi yang mes po//ston pa'i ston pa kun byeng rgyal po las//sku gdung ring bsrel rtag tu zung cig pa//sku ni rgyal
27a2(rnying rgyud): bgang dang gang gi sku//gdung ni sangs rgyas gang dang gang gi gdung //ring bsrel zhes ni ji lta bu la bgyi zhes zhuso//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/sku ni nga sras rgyal ba gsum gyi sku//gdung ni dus gsum rgyal ba nga yi sems//dus med rtag tu sems dpa' 'di 'chang na/du
27a3(rnying rgyud): gsum sangs rgyas kun gyi mchod pa'i rten//sku gdung ring srel zhes ni de la bya//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun bang rgyal po lags//sku gdung ring srel de ltar lags na yang //de la dus gsum sangs rgyas ji ltar mchod//mchod pa la ni ston ci zhig mchis ces zhuso//kye seda' chen po
27a4(rnying rgyud): khod nyon cig/nga yi sku gdung ring srel de la ni//dus gsum sangs rgyas rtag tu sems ltas mchod//de'i ston mi 'bral de thob nas//chos rnams kun bed+ma rgyal por nus par 'gyur//zhes gsung so//bang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//sku gdung gtad pa'i le'u ste bcu bdun
27a5(rnying rgyud): ba'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//chos thams cad kyi chos nyid nga yin pa'i lung 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/snang srid snod bcud thams cad kun//snang ni nga'i ngor snang //dag ni tshos nyid dbyingasu dag/'dul ba rnam pa sna tshogs ston//
27a6(rnying rgyud): sku gsum sna tshogs byung nas ni//theg pa gsum gyi ngo yang ston//rgyu 'bras can ni nges tshim byed//'das pa'i yul la mi ltos pas//rgyu la mi sgrub 'bras mi 'dod//'dod pa med pa'i dgongs pa des//rang bzhin lhun gyis grub pa ste//ye nas yin la bya mi dgos//don rnams ji
27a7(rnying rgyud): bzhin bskyod med pas//ji bzhin pa la bsgrub mi dgos//chos kun rang bzhin lhun grub phyir//dus gsum sangs rgyas grub pas kyang //rtsol zhing bsgrub pa'i lung mi 'dod//brtsal na gang brtsal ting 'dzin des//mi 'grub ting 'dzin gan du che//kye sems dpa' chen po//nga ni ston pa
27b1(rnying rgyud): thams cad kyis//bya ba'i 'phrin las sna tshogs dang //bsam pa'i ting 'dzin rim pa dang //'phro ba'i 'od zer sna tshogs dang //blta b.ri yul ni cir dmigs kyang //ji bzhin pa la g.yos pa med//bya ba'i las kyis grub pa med//bsam pa'i ting 'dzin yal bar 'gyur//'phro ba'i 'od zer
27b2(rnying rgyud): zhi gyur na blta yul gang yang mthong ba med//des na brtsal ba'i las ma byed//kye sems dpa' chen po/yul las brtsal bas grub pa'i lung de ni/rgyu dang 'bras bu'i theg pa can ston par byed//rgyu dang 'bras bu'i theg chen chos nyid ni//chos dang chos nyid rnam pa gnyis phye nas//chos
27b3(rnying rgyud): kyis chos nyid tshol ba rnyed lung med ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//brtsal bas mi 'grub pa'i le'u ste bco brgyad pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun bye rgyal po des//ma brtsal bas grub pa'i le'u 'di gsung so//kye sadel
27b4(rnying rgyud): chen po nyon cig/nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//bstan pa'i theg pa rnam gsum bstan pa ni//ston pa gsum gyis mi rtsol lung ma bstan//kun ba dang yis thig gi cag bstan pa ni//brtsal bas grub pa'i lung du ngas ma bshad//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/kun pad byang chub da'i
27b5(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin las//ma brtsal rang bzhin lhun gyis grub pa ni//rgyal ba kun gyi snying po sku gsum ste//nga yi rang bzhin ma bcos chos sku grub//nga yi do ma btsos long spyod rdzogs//nga yi do thugs rje mngon phyung sprul sku gsum//brtsal nas grub pa'i 'bras bu bstan pa min//tsos gsung so//
27b6(rnying rgyud): kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/sku gsum kun byedang ru bsdus pa ste//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//rang bzhin thugs rje ma bcos gsum//sku gsum nga yis de bzhin nyi du bstan//ngad nga'i de bzhin nyid las ni//sangs rgyas zhes bya'i ston smos kyang med//
27b7(rnying rgyud): zhes bya'i skyon gyis skur pa med//mi rtog mnyam pa nyid de bzhin gnas//'di las ma gtogs pa ni gzhan med pas//sangs rgyas rnams kyis 'di las lhag pa'i//kun byed nga las nges lung mnos pa med//byed mkhan kun byed rgyal po nga yis kyang //mi rtog mnyam nyid de
28a1(rnying rgyud): @bzhin nyid 'di la//nyid la nyid kyis bstan pa rdul tsam med ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po las//ma brtsal lhun gyis grub pa'i le'u ste bcu dgu pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po das//chos thams cad
28a2(rnying rgyud): kyi byung khungs nyid las byung ba 'di gsungaso//kye chos kun sdud pa'i rdo rje nyon//kun byed rgyal po byang chub sems//nga ni chos kun snying po ste//de ni chos kun sa bon yin//nga ni chos kun rgyu yang yin//nga ni chos kun sdong po ste//nga ni chos kun gzhi ma yin//nga ni
28a3(rnying rgyud): chos kyi rtsa ba ste//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//'dus pa'i phyir na snying por bshad//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//skye ba'i phyir na sa bon yin//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//'byung ba'i phyir na rgyu yang yin//ji ltar snang ba'i chos
28a4(rnying rgyud): rnams kyi//yan lag 'byung bas sdong po yin//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//ngal gnas pas gzhi ma yin//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//nga yin pas na rtsa bar bshad//k+She sems dpa' chen po rdo rje nyon cig kuni byed bya chu
28a5(rnying rgyud): sems nga ni//nga'i rang bzhin ma bcos rnam gsum yis//ming tsam sku gsum byung tshul bstan//nges par sku gsum ston pa yang //de bzhin nyid las g.yos pa med//byed byang chub sems nga ni//ma skyes gzung 'dzin bral ba la//chos sku/
28a6(rnying rgyud): skye med ming tsam byung //nges par chos sku ming tsam ste//de bzhin nyid las g.yos pa med//kun byed byang chub sems nga ni//ngi ming tsam mngon phyung ba//lo skyod rdzogs sku ming tsam tshu la//ngas par long spyod rdzogs sku ste//de bzhin nyid las g.yos pa ste/kun
28a7(rnying rgyud): byeng byang chub sems pa ni//thugs rje sbrul sku mngon phyung ba//zugs rje ma bcos rang bzhin las//ming tsam sprul sku tshul byung ba//nges par sprul pa'i sku de yong //de bzhini nyid las g.yos pa med//de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin las//sku gsum g.yos pa med/
28b1(rnying rgyud): pa bzhin//sku gsum bstan pa chos rnams dang //sku gsum 'dus pa 'khor rnams dang //chos rnams la yang g.yos pa med//ma g.yos pa'i chos nyid la//rgyu dang 'bras bur btags byas nas//rtsol dang sgrub par byas nas ni//rgyu dang 'bras bu 'byung 'dod pa/
28b2(rnying rgyud): byed nga las byas pa yi/rgyu 'bras 'das las gol ba yin//nes gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po khyad nyon cig/ las byung ba'i sku gsum ston pa dang //nga las byung ba'i dus gsum sangs rgyas dang //nga las byung ba'i 'khor gnas dus la sogs//ku
28b3(rnying rgyud): kyang nga'i byang chub sems yin na//sems dpa' rdo rje de la rtog mi rtog/sems dpar rdo rje de la ma rtog shig/nga ni ye nas kun byed byang chub sems//nga yis byas pa kun kyang byang chub sems//nga yi rang bzhin ye nas rnam dag byang //nga yis kun la ye nas kha/
28b4(rnying rgyud): chub nga yi rang bzhin gsal bas ye nas sems//nga ni na mkha' lta bu'i rang bzhin gyis//ye nas yin pa'i byang chub sems kyis mi//'jig rten sgyu mar snang ba'i chos bzhin du//sku gsum ston pa dus gsum sangs rgyas rnams//bu dang tsha bo 'khor gyi
28b5(rnying rgyud): tshul bzung nas//ma nor bden pa'i gdam ngag phog pa bzhin//byang chub sems ni nam mkha' bzhin bstan nas//kun byed byung tshul na mkha' bzhin du bstan//zhes gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//chos thams cad byang chub kyi sems kun
28b6(rnying rgyud): byed las byung ba'i khungs kyi le'u ste nyi shu pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//chos thams cad kyi khungs kyi rgyal po yin pa'i phyir//nyid las byung ba'i tshigs 'di gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po rdo rje khyod nyon cig/
28b7(rnying rgyud): nga ni chos rnams kun byed rgyal po ste//sku gsum ston pa'i pha ma nga yin la//dus gsum sangs rgyas kun gyi mes po yin//'khor gnas dus kyi ston pa'i sgron ma byed//kun byed nga'i rang bzhin rnam gsum las//sku gsum ston par las byung ba ni//sku gsum 'i/
29a1(rnying rgyud): bshad lugs rnam gsum gyis//chos kyi bshad lugs rnam pa gsum yang bstan//de yang kun byed nga yis bstan pa yin//kun byed rgyal po nga yi rang bzhin 'di//ston pa kun las khyad par 'phags pa'i phyir//gtan tshigs don gyi bshad/
29a2(rnying rgyud): lugs rnam pa lngas//ma nor nges pa'i gtan tshigs khungs bstan//kye sems dpa' chen po/kun byed rgyal po nga ni byang chub sems//byang chub sems ni ye nas rdzogs pa che//rdzogs pa chen po ye nas rgyu 'bras 'das//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i bshad lugs
29a3(rnying rgyud): /bstan pa dang //kun byed byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin las//sku gsum ston pa byung ba'i bshad lugaso dang //rnam pa gnyis kyi bshad lu gos bstan pa dang //kun byed byang chub sems kyi bshad lugs ni//byang chub sems ni rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i phyir//gtan tshi//
29a4(rnying rgyud): don gyi bshad lugs lnga yis bshad//sku gsum ston pa gsum gyi bshad lugs ni//phun sum tshogs pa rnam pa lnga nang du//chos kyi sku ni byin gyi brlabs kyis bshad//longs spyod rdzogs pa rang gi ngo ston//sprul pa'i sku ni don rnam tshig tu sdeb//sku gsuM
29a5(rnying rgyud): ston pa'i bshad lugs rnam gsum ste//nga yi rang bzhin rnam gsum bshad lugs yin//kun byed nga ni rgya 'bras 'das pa ste//rgyu 'bras 'das pa spyod yul kun las 'das//kun las 'das pa'i chos nyid byang chub sems//byang chub sems ni kun gyi snying po
29a6(rnying rgyud): nasnying po yin pas yid ches khungs kyang bstan//byang chub sems ni chos kun rtsa ba ste//rtsa ba yin pas thams cad don yang 'dus//byang chub sems ni khyad par kun las 'phags//khyad par 'phags pas theg pa'i shan yang 'byed//byang chub sems rtogs
29a7(rnying rgyud): chos nyid kun sangs rgyas//des na skal ldan dgos ched bstan pa yin//sems kyi rang bzhin tshig tu brjod med//ma brjod sems kyi rang bzhin mi snang bas//mi snang mtshon du med ces ma brjod na//kun kyang rang sems ma mthong rtsol sgrub byed//des na brjod med cikaM/
29b1(rnying rgyud): don yang bshad//des na gtan tshigs don gyi bshad lugs lngas//kun byed rgyal po byang chub sems kyi don//ma nor nges par gtan la 'thengs par byed//rgyu 'bras med par gtan la phab pa yin//thams cad byang chub sems yin yid kyang ches//byang chub sems yin ye nas med
29b2(rnying rgyud): ches pas//sems la brtsal ba med par yid kyang ches//brtsal du med par ye nas yid ches pas/mi rtsol bde ba chen po'i ngang la gnas//kun byed nga'i rang bzhin de ltar gnas/kun byed nga las sangs rgyas gzhan medo//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal
29b3(rnying rgyud): po las/gtan tshigs bshad lugs kyi le'u ste/nyi shu rtsa gcig pa'o//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po des//sems rnal du gzhag pa'i phyir//bya med rdzogs pa'i lung khungs chen po 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' rdo rje khyod nyon cig kyi bya med
29b4(rnying rgyud): /yul ni mi gnas na mkha' 'dra//mi gnas dmigs pa'i yul med mi rtog chos kyi lam//bsngo ba'i cha shas 'phra mo'i yul las byung ba ste//rtog sgom chos kyi sku ni khyad par don med pas//rang byung ye shes mi rtog kun tu ji bzhin gnas//bya bral yul la mi gan+da
29b5(rnying rgyud): gnyen pos bcasu med//yan lag chos kyi dbang gis snying po'i yul tshol zhing //rnam pa cir yang mi rtog thabs kyi de la rol//snying po rang bzhin 'byung bas chos sku gzhan na med//rdul phran gcig las gses shing phyogs cha kun dang bral//mi gnas
29b6(rnying rgyud): don nyid legs pa'i ye shes rang byung ste//mngon sum kun tu mi rtog yangs pa'i snying po la//rnam dag lam der zhugs nas mnyam pa'i rgyal po thob//mi 'gyur 'gyur ba med pas chags pa'i gnas med//de bzhin gzung ba'i yul med sems kyi gnas kyang me
29b7(rnying rgyud): mngon sum thob par 'dod nas rgyu la rtag tu bsgom//bsgom chags bde ba de las mnyam pa'i don mi 'byung //sku gcig kun tu khyab pas bsnan pa'i chos med de//mtha' la thug pa med cing dbyings la bri ba med//chos kyi rol pas nyam phag lhag pa'i gnas
30a1(rnying rgyud): med de/rang byung che ba'i yul la kun tu ji bzhin gnas//ngo mtshar mthong ba'i yul med de la mthong ba'i mig//gang la thos par mi rigs bsnyad ba kun dang bral//chos dang chos min rtag tu 'dres shing mtshungs//don dam chos zhes lhag pa'i gnas zhes pa gang dang
30a2(rnying rgyud): med/'khrul dang byang chub lam de rtog pas thob pa'ang med//rang byung ye shes nyid kyang tshig gi mtha' dang bral//ye nas rgyas pa'i bdag nyid mngon sum gnas pa la//grib ma gzugs kyi dpe bzhin de la rtog pa 'byung //meng de med pa ma yin med pa'i snying po 'byung //stong ste stong pa
30a3(rnying rgyud): ma yin stong pa'i yul la gnas//na mkha'i rang bzhin de las 'byung ba'i dran pa skye//ba bral zin pa'i bde ba 'dod pa med pas len//gza' ba'i yul med de las ye shes 'byung bar rtsom//sngon gyi drang srong rje su chags pa'i yid kyang 'jog/rnam rtsol 'bad pa'i gdu bas
30a4(rnying rgyud): kun tu zin ta re//rang bzhin la ma der zhugs pas thams cad mkhyen pa'i//ji bzhin pa zhes rnam rtog de'i phyir rtog bsgom pa//bde ba chen po 'dod pas chags pa'i nad yin te//mi g.yo mnyam par gnas pas sman chen ma byas na//mtho ris bgrod pa'i rgyu des nyon mongs zin pa
30a5(rnying rgyud): 'gyur//lam med lam du zhugs pa rnams kyi nad che ba//phyin par 'dod pas ri dgas smig rgyu snyeg pa 'dra//rnyed pa'i yul med 'jig rten gsum las mi 'byung zhing //sa bcur ltos pa'i gnas kyang byang chub sgrib pa yin//shin tu myur ba'i ye shes bsam pa kun dang bral//
30a6(rnying rgyud): bshes gnyen kun las 'byung ba'i nor bu rin chen bzhin//dmigs pa med cing 'gyur ba'i gnas la mi ltos pas//rang gi rang bzhin legs pas re ba thams cad skong //brtags na med la bzhag na legs par 'byung ba che//dengo su mi snang chags pa'i cha lugs kun la ston///
30a7(rnying rgyud): dang gzhan dubul ba'i ston mkhan rin chen mdzod//thams cad grub pa'i yul zhes bdag med snying rjes bstan//na nas ma g.yos nang du brtsal ba'i gnas med//yul phyir chags la yul la bsngo ba'i dmigs pa med//mi 'byung mi 'jug bdag med snying rje de yis ni//gzhan gyi/
30b1(rnying rgyud): min mi 'ong ye nas gnas pa yin//bde ba 'dod pas bde la rgyab kyis phyogs//bde bas zin te bde bas bde ba tshol//byang chub 'khrul pa ye rngam chel za//'di lta'i yul can sangs rgyas mthong ba med//sangs rgyas med pas sangs rgyas mi sang med//sangs rgyas ston pas
30b2(rnying rgyud): ming du btags pas nor//sangs rgyas gzhan nas thob byed nor pa'i lam//gzugs med chos kun bshad pa rdul tsam med//zin dang chags dang bral dang ni//dngos po med dang rnam par spong //bdud rtsi chen po'i rang bzhin pas//bza' ba'i thabs la rag pa med//yangs che'o chos
30b3(rnying rgyud): chen po//chung ngu rnams kyi gnyen po yin//che la dmigs pa'i cha bsnyams nas//chung che de rnams dmigs dang bral//gsungs dang rgyas dang dmigs dang snang //rgyu mkhan ri mo ri rnam pa bzhin//'byung 'jug ye shes rmugs pa yis//de'i dbang las skye bar 'gyur//'di ni theg pa
30b4(rnying rgyud): kun gyi rgyal//gta bzhago rang bzhin te//'dod pa med cing len pa med//brod pa rdul tsam mi bskyed//khyung chen na mkha' ldings pa bzhin//spro ba med cing bsdu ba med//stor bar mi dogs bza' ba'ang med//rgya mtsho bzhin du ye gnas la//chos rnams sna tshogs 'byung bar byed//ston maka'i/
30b5(rnying rgyud): mtha' dang mnyam//bsdu ba'i gan su nges pa med//byang chub snying po do ma thag/ting 'dzin rgyal po chen po 'byung //snang ba rgya mtsho chen po bzhin//mi rtog na mkha'i mtha' ltar yangs//kun tu bzang po'i spyod yul la//skye zhing 'pho ba'i chos med//rgyu rkyen yan lag bcu gnyis po//
30b6(rnying rgyud): skur bas btags te bshad pa yin//'khrul pa rnams kyi sgo tsam du//mkhas pas de bzhin shes par gyis//'gro ba rgyud drug snang ba yang //dang po'i lam du shes par bya//'dod spyod snying rjes brlan pas na//gang du dga' bar byang chub spyod//shan pa smad 'tshong mtshams med lnga
30b7(rnying rgyud): kha na mtho 'jig rten spong //yongasu rdzogs pas chos kyi ches//bde ba che las gzhan medo//yong chos thams cad de lta bas//chos kyi rang bzhin de yin pas//chos kyis chos nyid btsal ba dang //na mkhas namakha' btsal ba dang //gzhan la ltos pa'i chos nyid ni//me yis mo gas/
31a1(rnying rgyud): @dang 'dra ste/shin tu dka' ba'i las byed pa'o//rnam par mi rtog snying po 'di//yongs kyi rgyud la sbas pa med//mi len byang chub spyod rnams la//ram pa kun tu rang bzhin gnas ces gsung so//bya med rdzogs pa'i lung bcu las//mi gnas yul gyi le'u sa
31a2(rnying rgyud): nyi shu rtsa gnyis pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po ngas//lta ba yul med kyi lung 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams ni//rang gi byang chub sems yin pas//de la blta ba'i yul ma mchis//mi rtog pas na mnya
31a3(rnying rgyud): pa nyid//de bzhin na mkha'i dgongs pa ste//rna myor zhes ni de la bya//dbang po lnga la snang ba rnams//rang gsal de la mi rtog pas//de yang na mkha'i dgongs te//rnal 'byor de bzhin nyi du gnas//tshig dang yi go don brtags pas//tshig yig don la brtags med pas//rtog med mnyam pa de bzhi
31a4(rnying rgyud): nyid//de yang na mkha'i dgongs par gnas//bsam du med pa'i don 'di ni//na mkha' bzhin du ye gnas ces gsung so//bya med rdzogs pa'i lung bcu las//lta ba yod med kyi na mkha' dgongs pa bstan pa'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa gsum pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyan
31a5(rnying rgyud): bo ngasadub med rdzogs pa'i lung phyag rgya bzhugs tshul gyi le'u 'di bstan to//kye sems dpa' chen po//sems nyid kun tu bzang po yi/bzhugs tshul bstan pa 'di lta ste/byang chub semasu bzhugs tsam na//chos rnams kun gyi snying por bzhugs//de rtags kun byed rgyal po yi na/
31a6(rnying rgyud): byed rgyal po 'gyur ba med//des na 'gyur ba med par bstan//chos kyi sku ru bzhugs tsam na//gzung 'dzin spros dang bral nas bzhugs//gzung 'dzin bral la 'gyur ba med//long spyod rdzogs skur bzhugs tsam na//'dod pa'i ston lnga ldan pas//'dod po phun sum tshogs/
31a7(rnying rgyud): bzhugs/sprul pa'i sku ru bzhugs tsam na//gang gis 'dul ba'i dus tsam na//gang la gang dgos skur sprul nas//dgos pa phun sum tshogs par bzhugs//dus gsum 'gyur ba med par bzhugs//chos kun phyag rgya na mkha'i mtshan nyid de//na mkha'i mtshan nyid de bzhin nyid/
31b1(rnying rgyud): sku gsum mtshan nyid de bzhin gnas//thams cad de bzhin nyid du gnas//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams ni//ji bzhin pa la bco su med//brtsal zhing bsgrub pa thams cad kyis//snga ma bgrod pas phyin pa gcig kyang med//snga ma byas pas zin pa gcig kyang med//sgrubs shing brtsal bas
31b2(rnying rgyud): snga ma grub pa med//thams cad ji bzhin pa las 'gyur ba med ces gsung so//ba med rdzogs pa'i lung bcu las/khyu med rdzogs pa phyag rgyar 'gyur med kyi le'u ste rtsa bzhi pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po des//khams gsum gyi seMn la
31b3(rnying rgyud): rang gi ston pa'i ston pa yin par bstan pa 'di gsung so//kye khams gsum sems can thams cad kun//rang gi byang chub sems de ni//ston pa yin par mngon bstan na//bskal pa brgya phrag stong du ni//rang sems ston pa ma mthong bas//kun bang rgyal po nga yis ni//rang sems stonapa
31b4(rnying rgyud): mngon phyung bas//rang sems ston pa'i chos nyon cig/kun byed rgyal po nga yis ni//kun rgya bang chub sems las ni//sems kyi ngo 'byung chen lnga//ston pa byang chub sems lnga phyung //kuM ni long spod rdzogs pa'i sku//chos ni rang gi ngos 'chad //ston ni rang gi rang bzhin ston//ston
31b5(rnying rgyud): ba long spyod rdzogs sku de//dgos pa nyid la nyid mi rtog/yul chos gzhan la'ang rtog mi byed//byang chub sems kyi ston pa lngas//kun kyang chos nyid de bzhin ston//byang chub sems kyi ye shes ngos//ston pa sa'i skur byung nas//tshig dang yi ges mi ston te/rang gi rang bzhin ston par/
31b6(rnying rgyud): baT bdag tu mi rtog gzhan mi rtog/mi rtog mnyam pa'i dgongs pa ston//khams gsum sems can thams cad kyis//de rtogs sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam//rtsol ba'i chos nyid ma brtsal grub//byang chub sems kyi ye shes des//ston pa chu'i skur byung nas//tshig dang yi ges mi ston
31b7(rnying rgyud): rang gi ngos ston byed pa//bdag tu mi rtog gzhan ni rtog/mi rtog mnyam pa'i dgongs pa ston//khams gsum sems can thams cad kyis//de rtogs sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam//rtsol ba'i chos ni ma brtsal grub/byang chub sems kyi ye shes des//ston pa me'i skur byung nas//tshig
32a1(rnying rgyud): mi shes ma stan / ga ngas chos ston ga/padag tu ga gzhan rtog/ tog mnyam pa'i dngas pa rjod//khams gsum sems can thams cad kyis/rtogs sangs rgyas kun dang maHsa/rgyal ba'i chos nam cal phrug byang chub sems kyi yas
32a2(rnying rgyud): ston pa rlung gi skur byung nas//tshig dang yi ges mi ston par//rang gi ngos chos ston pa//bdag gzhan gnyi su mi rtog par//mi rtog mnyam pa'i dgongs pa ston//khams gsum sems can thams cad kyis//de sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam//rtsol ba'i chos ni ma
32a3(rnying rgyud): brtsal 'grub//byang chub sems kyi ye shes nges//ston pa na mkha'i skur byung nas//tshig dang yi ges mi ston te//rang gi ngos chos ston pa//bdag gzhan gnyisu mi rtog par//mi 'byed pa'i dgongs pa ston//khams gsum sems can thams cad la//mngon
32a4(rnying rgyud): phyung ston pa nye rnams kyis//rang gi ngos chos 'chad pa//'di yis kun dang rig par gyis//kye khams gsum sems can thams cad kun//kun byed rgyal po nga yis ni//khyed rnams nga yis byas pa'i phyir//nga yi sras yin pa dang mnyam//da dang mi gnyis nga yin/
32a5(rnying rgyud): phyir/nga yis khyed la mngon phyung ba//nga yi ngo ston pa lngas//rang bzhin rnam lnga gcig tu bstan//gcig ni kun byed nga yin gyis//de bzhin khyed kyang yid ches kyis//kye khams gsum sems can ma lus rnams//nga med tsam na khyed kyang med//khyed med tsam
32a6(rnying rgyud): gyi dus chod na//ston pa lnga yang 'byung mi 'gyur//mi rtog chos kyang ston mi 'gyur zhes gsungaso//bya med rdzogs pa'i lung bcu las//rang sems ston par phyung ba'i le'u ste rtsa lnga pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//
32a7(rnying rgyud): sems las byung ba'i chos chen po lnga//ston par phyung ba'i rtag su//ma skyes pa'i byang chub kyi sems ston par zhal gyis bzhes pa 'di gsung so//kyo sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/bsam 'das brjod med pa'i byang chub sems//ston pa'i sgron mar gyu
32b1(rnying rgyud): pas gzhan gyis rab bsngags pa//chos kyi snying por gyur pas/'jam dpal gzhonu nyid//ba bral lhun rdzogs bde ba'i lhun la gnas//tshul khrims la sogs spyod pa rgya mtsho'i gzhir gyur pa'i//ji snyed rnam par grol ba'i lam ston pa//bde gshegs yuM
32b2(rnying rgyud): 'gyur kun gyis mtshungs lam ste//de med mi 'byung de phyir nga ni rnam grol mchog gi lam//phra zhing shes dka' kun gyi lam ste mi rtog rtog las 'das//mi gnas mi dmigs spros med bsam pa kun dang bral//tshig gi maM phebs dbyibs dngape kha dog spyod yul me/
32b3(rnying rgyud): /brtan zhing brtag par dka' la brjod rdul tsam med//sngon gyi drang srong lam du gang zhugs pa//sgom chags lam gyi nad kyis zin ta re//ston pa'i lung bzhin tshig gi mtha' spyod lam du de mthong na//de ni rtog pa'i rgyun 'brengs smig rgyu sgeg pa 'dra//yang dag lam ni tshig gi
32b4(rnying rgyud): mtshon du med//yang dag nyi du bstan pa nga yis 'khrul//dag dang ma dag gnyis med 'dres shing mtshungs//rnam pa cir yang mi 'byed ye shes gti mug nga//thabs med gsal ba'i mar me bsam pa kun dang bral//ngang gis mi g.yo rmugs shing ting 'dzin rgyal por gnas//
32b5(rnying rgyud): mngon gsum mthong bas mngon med mngon sum mthong ba'i mig/de phyir thams cad mkhyen pa'i spyan zhes de la bya//mtha' dang dbus med yangs pa'i rang bzhin shes//mi len mi 'dor mnyam pa'i rgyal por gnas//sems dang bag chags med 'dres shing mtshungs//'dzin pa//
32b6(rnying rgyud): rtags shing snang ba'i chos rnams ni//rang gi rgyan du snang bas mi 'dor spong mi byed//cir yang mi dgongs thabs kyis de la rol//kun dang mi mthun yongs kyis spong ba'i chos//nyon mongs lnga dang mtshams med lnga po yi//rnam dag lam der zhugs nas mnyam pa'i rgyal
32b7(rnying rgyud): po thob/bung med la sogs kun kyang spong mi byed//lo rgyus don gnyis tshad ma'i blor bzhag nas//ting 'dzin gsum po brtan nas grub pa'i mtha' sbyong pa//rtsol bral lung las gol zhes 'khrul pa yin//bya bral lhun rdzogs bde ba'i lhun la gnas//ye shes rang byung che'i
33a1(rnying rgyud): snying po nyid mi doyo mi 'gyur bsnyad pa kun dang bral//zin pa'i bdud rtsis rtsol ba'i ba sdug bsngal 'joms//bya brtsal zin pa'i yul la ji bzhin gnas/kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/chos rnams thams cad thig le chen po'i rang bzhin pas na//spros pa
33a2(rnying rgyud): med cing bsdus pa med//skye ba med cing 'gag pa med//'gog pa med par de bzhin gnas//rnam por mi rtog snying po 'di//mamakha' bzhin du laM gnas pas//rtog pa'i sgra bsam yul las 'das ces gsung so//bya med rdzogs pa'i lung bcu las//bya med rdzogs pa bsgom du me
33a3(rnying rgyud): ba'i le'u ste rtsa drug pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byod rgyal po ngas//nyid kyi gdam ngag phog pa dang mthun pa'i lung chen po rims kyis bstan te//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/chos thams cad nga las byung bas na//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thaMd kun/
33a4(rnying rgyud): /ye nas rnam dag dbyings kyi lung du bstan//phyi dang nang kun ye nas chos kyi dbyings//ye nas rnam par dag pa'i spyod yul la//sangs rgyas dang ni sems can gnyisu med//lam la gnyen pos bco su ga la yod//bsgrub med rtsal sbugs smon pa med pas na//bya med lhun rdzob
33a5(rnying rgyud): snga ma de nyid don//rtog dpyod rnam dag gnyis med chos dbyings la//log rtog byis pa'i spyod pas ci zhig bslu//gnyis med bde chen sems can kun spyod la//'khrul pas nor pa'i lam du brtags pa yang //gong du bstan pa'i lam chen de las mi gnyis pas//mnyam par shes pas sangs
33a6(rnying rgyud): kun gyi rje//nga dang bdag tu rtog pa mu stegs 'khrul pa'i lam//byis pas bslus pas chos spyod rtog pa'i lam la btsud pa ni//phyin pa'i lam med rtogs pa'i dus med//chos nyid chos tshol rnyed par ga la 'gyur//tshad mar ma gyur slob dpon dpe 'dra'i lung //rten dang lam nor rtog pas
33a7(rnying rgyud): zin ta re/de bas gser la nag tshur gtong ba yis//slob dpon yang dag ston pa rin chen mdzod//rin thang dpag tu med pas bslu bar ros ces gsung so//bya mad rdzogs pa'i lung bcu las/bya med rnam dag dbyings kyi lung bstan pa'i le'u ste rtsa bdun pa'o////de nas bya
33b1(rnying rgyud): chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po nyid kyi rang bzhin 'gyur ba med pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga yi rang bzhin dbyings las 'gyur pa med//nga yi ye shes na mkha' 'gyur ba med//nga yi chos nyid rang bzhin 'gyur pa med//nga sems chos kyi snying po 'gyur pa med//de bzhin /
33b2(rnying rgyud): 'gyur ba med pa la//nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//rgyu dang 'bras bur chos la ming btags nas//sprul pas lung bstan dbang po yul spong 'gyur//rang rkyen chos lnga 'chang ba'i sems can la//rang byung chos nyid yin par lung ma bstan//ngan 'gro'i sdig sgrib che zhes lung
33b3(rnying rgyud): bstan nas//lus ngag yid gsum sdig spong dge bcu spyod//rang byung chos la blang dor spyad pa'i phyir//rang byung ye shes skal mang phrad mi 'gyur//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/sprul pa rang la 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs la//rang byung ye shes lnga'i chos spangs la//ye shes
33b4(rnying rgyud): nga dgra lnga choms lung bstan//dgra lnga rang byung ye shes ma rtogs te//rang sems spong 'gyur nga yis lung ma bstan//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/rang gi sems las byung ba'i chos nyid la//don dam kun rdzob bden pa rnam gnyis kyis//spangs nas rgyu las 'bras bu 'byung
33b5(rnying rgyud): zer nas/bskal pa gsum gyi bar du sbyong byed pa//sdom dang pha rol phyin bcus rang mi 'gyur//sems nyid 'gyur bar nga yis lung ma bstan//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/rang sems chos nyid ting 'dzin byin rlabs nas//dag pas chu zla bzhin du gsal byas nas//rjer
33b6(rnying rgyud): lngas de la mnyes pa'i mchod phul nas//de la re 'dod dngos grub thob ba/chu Ta med pas rang sems ma rtogs pas//skye ba bdun gyi bar du rang byung gi ye shes med ces nga yis lung ma bstan/kye sems dpa' tshon po nyon cig/rang sems byin brlabs tshe 'phrul bsgom pa ts+li/
33b7(rnying rgyud): ting 'dzin ye shes sems dpa' grogs mnyam des//bdag 'dod pa'i dngos grub ster re ba//rang byung chos nyid de la 'dod len med//rang sems bya med mi tshe gsum mi 'phrad//kun byed nga yis lung bstan ma yi mo//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/rang las byung ba'i chos nyid lta
34a1(rnying rgyud): byas nas/phyag rgya bzhi dang snying po 'gro 'du yis/mi 'gyur ba'i chos nyid sgyur sgrub byed//sems kyi ting 'dzin dbang gis thob sems pa//des kyang thob par nga yis lung ma bstan/kyesems dpa' chen po nyan cig gnyis dbyis las 'gyur ba med pa'i byas
34a2(rnying rgyud): dbyings de 'gyur ba rang yis lung ma bstan/dbyings la dbang sgyur byed pa'i sku gsum gyis//rab 'khor zhi gnas tshol ba'i ting 'dzin bstan//des na 'dren pa'i lung du bstan pa yin//de bzhin namakha' 'gyur ba med pa de//rang nyid namakhas namakha' 'grub mi 'gyur/de ba'i
34a3(rnying rgyud): la 'gyur ba med pa de//de bzhin chos kyi chos nyid 'grub mi 'gyur//de bzhi sems nyid 'gyur ba med pa de//sems nyid rang gi sems nyid 'grub mi 'gyur//mi 'gyur sbyar bar byed pa'i ting 'dzim ni//dus dang smon lam re ba 'jog pa'i//dus la ltos pas ting 'dzin chi chad 'gyur
34a4(rnying rgyud): kun bye nga yis nges lung bstan pa med//ces gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//bya med rdzogs pa 'gyur med btsal mi rnyed pa'i le'u ste rtsa brgyad pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//sems rnal du bzhag pa 'di gsung so//kye sedar
34a5(rnying rgyud): chen po nyon cig/nga ni kun byed byang chub sems//chos rnams thams cad nga yin te//nga yi rang bzhin thams cad ni//ye nas rnal du bzhag pa yin//nga'i rang bzhin bstan pa ni//rnam pa lnga ru shes par bya//ston pa bstan pa 'khor gnas dus//rnam lnga'i rang bzhin rnal du bzhag/sto
34a6(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin 'di ltar bzhag/chos sku ma bcos rnal du bzhag/rlung bzhin de bzhag yin//long spyod ma bcos ngo bzhag//nga yi rang bzhin bzhag pa yin//sprul pa ma bcos thugs rje bzhag/nga yi rang bzhin bzhag pa ste/rang byung ye shes bzhag pa yin//gnas ni gang na gnas pa'i
34a7(rnying rgyud): gnas desang rnal du banag pa yin//dus nis byi gang tshogs rdus//rnal pa na ga dus tshogs pa yim/kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/phun sum tshogs pa rnam pa lnga/ma bcos rnal du bzhag pa las/chos zhes bya ba gzhan yong min/kye sems dpa' chen po/phun sum tshogs
34b1(rnying rgyud): ma 'dus pa'i//chos ni gcig kyang yod ma gsungs//kye ni rnal du bzhag pa yis//thams cad chos nyid grol ba yin//ston pa ma bcos chos nyid grol ba yin//bstan pa ma bcos chos nyid grol ba yin//'khor yang ma bcos chos nyid grol ba yin//kye thams cad grol
34b2(rnying rgyud): bar bzhed pas na//rang lus ma bcos lha ma bsgom//smra ba'i tshig dang ngag ma 'chos//ting 'dzin ma byed sems ma 'chos//bcos pas rnal du 'byor med pas//rnal du ma 'byor grol mi 'gyur//ma grol chos nyid mnyam mi thob//kye sems dpa' chen po 'di lta ste//bang chub sa/
34b3(rnying rgyud): kyi kun pang rgyal po ni//ye nas 'das pa'i rnal du bzhag pa las//lta ba rnams ni rnal du 'jog mi shes//rdzogs chen kun byed ye nas rnal bzhag la//nyan thos rang rgyal rnams kyis thos mthong na//drug skye zer nas rang sems spong 'gegs 'gyur//des kyang lta ba rnal du 'jog ma the/
34b4(rnying rgyud): bskal pa grangs med 'jog mi shes la gnas//theg chen mdo sdes rdzogs chen thos gyur nas//rang sems de bzhin nyid du ma rtogs te//ji bzhin du 'jog pa ma shes te//bgrod cing sbyong bas bskal pa gsum du gnas//rje su rna myor sems dpas thos gyur na//'di ltar snang ba'i chos rnaMs
34b5(rnying rgyud): de bzhin la//rang sems de bzhin nyi du ma rtogs par//ji bzhin rnal du 'jog pa ma shes pas//byang chub yan lag cho 'phrul sna tshogs kyis//byin rlob sems la mi tshe bdun pa gnas//rnal 'byor chen po rnams kyis thos gyur na//rgyu dang 'bras bu'i bdag nyid yin pas na//yen
34b6(rnying rgyud): yin la gdod rtsol sgrub par byed//stong phrag gnyis dang brgya phrag dag tu//de bzhin rnal du 'jog pa mi shes te//ting 'dzin gsum gyis sa la gnas par byed//yongasu rnal 'byor rnams kyis thos gyur na//ye nas yin la rgyu 'bras ming btags nas//ji bzhin rnal du 'jog pa ma shes te//
34b7(rnying rgyud): rgyu ni dag pa chos kyi dbyingasu bsgrub//'bras bu dag pa ye shes dkyil 'khor bsgrub/khar mchod kyi mi tshe gcig tu gnas//shin tu rnal 'byor rnams kyis thos gyur na//ye nas sangs rgyas sa la gnas par 'gyur//ba med bde ba chen po de yis thob//bsgrubs pa med par sangs rgyas do'i/
35a1(rnying rgyud): khong rgyam sems can bang chom mthun te/la la dbang po sbyangs pa'i 'gro can yong/la la dbang po yinas ci gachod yang//del dgos med do ces bstan pa min/mnya mo gardzogs can shes byag sa tinas sgra dag tu stan/marnam sa
35a2(rnying rgyud): nyid rnal du bzhag ti ni ma brtsal lhun gyis grub par bstan//yo ga shin tu rnal 'byor rdzogs chen la/rgyu dang 'bras bu yod zer smra byed pa//de ni rdzogs chen rtog pa'i don mi ldan//don dam kun rdzob gnyis smra byed pa//sgo dang skur ba 'debs pa'i tshig yin te//de yis gnyis med pa
35a3(rnying rgyud): rtog pa med//dus gsum sangs rgyas rnams kyi rtogs pa yang //gnyi su ma mthong rnal bzhag gcig tu rtogs//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//dgongs pa rnal du bzhag pa'i le ru ste rtsa dgu pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun
35a4(rnying rgyud): bye rgyal po des//nyid kyi snying po rdo rje sems dpa' la//sems dpa' rdo rje nyid kyi rang bzhin bya med rdzogs pa'i lung mi nub rgyal mtshan 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' rdo rje khyod nyon cig/song 'i rdo rje khyod nyid la//nyid kyi rang bzhin bstan par bya//nyid ni kun byed nga yin te//nga ni ye nas byang
35a5(rnying rgyud): chub sems//bang chub sems ni 'di lta ste//rdo rje sems dpa' namakha' che//kun bzang yangs pa chos kyi dbyings//rnam dag lam chen kun sgrol phyir//mi skye mi 'gag cir dgongs byams pas don nyid rnam sbyang phyir//snying rje chen po cir mi mdzad//che bas che ba'i zab mo yis//ston/
35a6(rnying rgyud): cir yang bsngags pa med//don rnams ji bzhin mi bskyod de/bya ba med par grol bas grol//rang byung ye shes brtsal med pas//grol nas grol ba'i lam yang ston/'byung ba chen po bac+doMs/'gro ba kun la rang bzhin gnas//byin ci log tu rnam brtags kyang //grol ba rang byung gzhan
35a7(rnying rgyud): las min//che ba'i ye shes rnyed dka' ba//shes rab thabs la brten pas 'grub//ming tsam gzhan la rten 'dra yang //mngon sum bde ba rang las byung //cho 'phrul chen po dka' ba min//lton kun dang stobs rnams kyi//ji bzhin rtogs pa phra ba yis//de ma thag tu rang las sbya
35b1(rnying rgyud): snang ba med pa'i chos nyid 'di//ma brtsal bzhag pas bsgom pa yin//de dang der ni rnam brtsal na//de las de bzhin de mi 'byung //mchog tu gsang ba'i chos nyid ni//rna dbang gzhan la thos mi 'gyur//de bzhin lce'i dbang pos kyang //de la brjod rdul tsam med//'gro ba'i sdug bsngal bya/
35b2(rnying rgyud): chub sems//kun du chub pas rnam par rol//de la bskyod pa med bzhin du//na mkha'i mtha' ltar mnyam par gnas//khyad par cir yang mtshungs pa la//la so zhes ni rnam par brtags//ci ste las kyi dbang 'gyur na//rang byung ye shes yod ma yin//rgyu nyid rdo rje rkyen dang 'dra//ma skyes
35b3(rnying rgyud): pas na 'jig pa med//gdod nas snying po byang chub la//brtsal ba'i bsam pas dbyings mi bskyod//ston chen po'i bsam gtan ni//bsam gtan nyid bas bsam du med//ma bsams ma sbyangs chos bzhin du//rnam rtog nyid las ye shes skye//phra ba'i sgo mor ming btags te//sems kyis
35b4(rnying rgyud): dben pa'i lam tshol zhing //dgon pa'i rgyu du dben 'dzin zhing //brtags na rnam par rtog 'gyur bsgom//rgyu dang 'bras bur ming btags te//dge sdig gnyis ka rnam par sel//'jig rten 'di las 'byungango zhes//blang dor brod pa mchog tu bskyed//chags dang ma chags tshig gi lam//dbu
35b5(rnying rgyud): ma bzhin te blag cha 'dra//bde dang sdug bsngal rgyu mthun zhes//'gro ba'i mgon po sems dpa'as gsungs//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug kyang //byang chub chen po'i lam khyung //kun spyod ston rnam lnga yang //chos nyid dbyings kyi rgyan zhes gsungs//na mkha'i rtog pa skye med cing //rtog pa de/
35b6(rnying rgyud): nyid na mkha' 'dra//mi chags na mkha'i bsngo ba las//rang don chen po na mkha' 'byung //rtog med mnyam nyid chos kyi sku//bzung bas mi zin chu zla 'dra//kun tu bzang po'i rol pa yis//A li kA li zab tu bstan//'di ni a dang mdzes pa'i ta//pa dang yan lag spros pa bzhin//'jig rten yongs

36a1(rnying rgyud): @bgrong 'gyur na//na mkha'i mtha' bzhin thob pa med//de ltar de bzhin de'i phyir//de la de bstan de yang thob//de ni snying po de bas ma//de las de byung ngo mtshar che//sngon gyi de dang da ltar de//de bzhin de'i gan su che//de ltar de'i lam 'dra bar//de ni de'i rang bzhi no//de dang
36a2(rnying rgyud): 'dra ba yongs kyi lam/zla ba las byung rten dang bcas//kun gyi mnyam nyid yin pa la//phyogasu bltos pas 'grub pa med//da ltar bde dang phyi mar bde//mngon sum pa dang rgyab nas byung //de yang rnam pa'i skyon yin pas//de la brten par mi bya'o//dus gsum gcig ste khyad par med/

36a4(rnying rgyud): der 'byung bar mi 'gyur te//ma bral smon pas spyod pas na//stong pa'i mtshan nyid gsungs pa bzhin//gcig ste rnam pa yongs kyis med//rnal 'byor na mkha'i bya la ma gnas//ma byung ma skyes snying po la//sgros btags chos kun ga la yod//phyi nang gnyis ka phyi nyid nang //zab mo'i cha shas

36a6(rnying rgyud): pas na bdag med cing //mi 'dor tshig bral mnyam nyi //gang dang gang gi gang du yang //kun 'khol kun spyod bdag las byung //'di la skyes pa bud med ces//mnyam pa'i rgyal pos yongs ma gsungs//'di la brtul shugs drag shul gyi//gnas bya ba'i gnas med de//a dang par ni rnam ldan na/

36b4(rnying rgyud): zhes bstan par ni//rdo sengs mtshon du med//ngo mtshar rmad byung rol pa 'di//bya bral na mkha' bzhin du gnas//cir yang mi dmigs gti mug las//de ma thag tu rang las byung //'di ni thad mtshungs pa'i lam//'gro ba kun la rang bzhin gnas//bus pas bslang bas 'khrul pa'i phyir//sman nyid
36b5(rnying rgyud): sman pa tshol ba bzhin//go ba'i yul na bde ba che//'di ni rnam dag 'jig rten yin//de la phyogs kyi 'od 'dus pas//phyogs bzhi mtshams dang bla 'og 'grub//ma nges 'ja' tshon kha dog las//rigs kyi khyad par mngon par snang //de bzhin g.yo rdul mi g.yo ba//'byung ba lnga pas gtso chen yin//da

36b7(rnying rgyud): gdod nas dag pas bdud rtsi yin//'du mched bcus khyad par du//lhag pa'i bsam pas gzung ba med//yid kyis bsams pas yon bdag ste/bltas pas tshogs ni bkram pa'o//mthong bar gyur pas dngos grub la//de nyid mnyam gzhag rdzogs pa'o//yud tsam gzung bas sbyor ba yin//dga' bar gyu
38a1(rnying rgyud): #/ngas dang tshig ste/thabs kyi ga thabs bskyod pa yis//gnyis med sbyor ba phul ba yin/mi gzung gtong bas gta ma yis khya ba med pa'i las rnams te/ma rtag ye shes bgegs ngas la nas mi gsung ma nyam gzhag sngags che go//zla ma mtsha dang gtong ba dang /
38a2(rnying rgyud): bsod nams zad kyang //ma chags mi g.yo'i stobs med na//bas na 'ching ba chen por 'gyur//de bas de lung de nyid la//de las de sbyar sgrib par 'gyur//de ltar de la de rtog na//de la de nyid grub pa med ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byang rgyal po las/ji se de'i rang bzhi
38a3(rnying rgyud): mi nub pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi le'u ste sum cu pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//kun byed nyid kyi rang bzhin pa med rdzogs pa lhun gyis grub pa 'di gsungaso//kye sede' chen po nyon cig/sna tshogs rang bzhin mi gnyis kyang //cha shas nyi du spros dang bral//ji zhin
38a4(rnying rgyud): pabazhes mi rtog kyang //rnam par snang mdzad kun tu bzang //zin pas rtsol ba'i nad spangs te//lhun gyis grub pas bzhag pa yin//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//rdo rje tshig drug pa'i le'u ste sum cu gcig pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun deng rgyal podi
38a5(rnying rgyud): seder rdo la ma byas don grub kyi lung nges pa tshig bzhi'i le'u 'di gsung so//kye se dr-i' chen po 'di ltar don rig gis//'di ltar snang ba de bzhin nyid gcig/'di la su yang 'chos par ma byed cig/ma bcos mnyam pa'i rgyal po de la ni//mi rtog tshe sku'i dgongs pa de la gnas//ces gsungs//
38a6(rnying rgyud): byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//nges pa'i tshig bzhi'i le'u ste sum cu rtsa gnyis pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//nyid las byung ba'i chos nyid //nyid kyi rig pa'i yis sengar rdo zhes bya ba chos thad rig par byed pa'i rgyal po rang byung ba'i las/
38a7(rnying rgyud): de la nyid kyi rang bzhin rtog par dka' ba 'di bstan to//kye seMnga' chen po//yid kyi dbang pos sgra rtogs shig/sgra yis don de rtogs rig byos//kun gyi gnas ni chos kyi dbyings yin na//dbyings kyi sgra don ji ltar rig par byed//kun gyi lha yul na mkha'i khams 'di la//na mkha'i sgra
38b1(rnying rgyud): rig par byed//mngon du gsal la 'byung ba lnga yin na//mngon du snang ba'i don ni ji ltar rig/de las byung ba'i khams gsum sen ni//de'i don ni ji ltar rig par byed//rig pa'i rgyal pos nyid phyir nga la nyon ces gsung so//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//khyod kyi rang bzhin zhi
38b2(rnying rgyud): tu rtogs dka' ba//rnam bzhi bstan pa'i sgra yis bdag gi rtogs//dbyings kyi sgra yis 'byed pa med par rtogs//na mkha'i sgra yis ma bsgribs gsal bar rtogs//'byung ba'i sgra yis kun gyi snying por rtogs//se ni sems kyis sgra don de rig byed//de rig kun byed shin tu rtogs dkar//

38b4(rnying rgyud): pa'i rgyal po kun byed byang chub sems//khyod kyi rang bzhin 'di ltar chos nyid 'di//sems dang ye shes chos nyid rig pa de//de'i rang bzhin bdag gis 'di ltar mthong //sems nyid spros bral sgra ni chos nyid sgra//sems nyid ma bsgribs sgra ni na mkha'i khas//

39b1(rnying rgyud): sde ston pakun byed rgyal po lags//chos kyi sku dang ma myur rdzogs kun zang rjes dang dang /rnam gzigs gtsug tor la sogs te//khas bdun shAkya thub pa dang /stong rtsa gnyis kyi sangs rgyas dang //lhun gyis grub dang rdul rnyed kyi/dgongs pa khyod la 'as la Nas/

39b7(rnying rgyud): ma lus pa'i//snying po rtags ni semasu 'dus//des na dpe don gsum du 'dus/kun byeng rgyal po nga la ni//spyi 'dus sgo 'dus dgongs pa 'dus//spyi 'dus dpe don rtags 'dus la//sgo 'dus rnam pa lnga 'dus te//chos sku dgongs pa 'dus pa ni//mi rtog mnyam pa nyid de 'dus///
40a1(rnying rgyud): mi rtog mnyam pa nyid 'dus la/mi g.yo cho 'phrul 'byin par nus//long spyod sku'i dgongs 'dus pa//mngon sum rang bzhin snang ba dang //'dod pa tshogs pas sbyin pa 'dus//kun bzang dgongs pa 'dus pa ni//ji ltar snang ba thaMd kun//bzang ngan blang dor med pa ru/ku

40b1(rnying rgyud): chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal pos//nyid kyi bstan pa gzhan gyi spyod yul ma yin pa/rdzogs pa chen po'i gzhung 'angadi //rgyu 'bras rtsol sgrub las 'das pa 'di bstan te/kye seM de chen po//nga'i rang bzhin 'di ni dga' ba ste/sgo dang lta ba dang ni dmatshig dang /'phrin las dang ni bgrod lam ga/

42b6(rnying rgyud): nas//rang rgyud dag pa dkyil 'khor la/bsnyen bsgrub rnam bzhis bsgrub par byed//a ti yo ga beng chen ni//rtsol sgrub 'das pa'i byang chub sems//rtsol sgrub 'das la ma hAs bsgribs//rdzogs chen rtsol sgrub byed pa ni//ma hA yo gar gel bar bshad//ar+tha
42b7(rnying rgyud): dbye mings 'dring ba//dbyings dang ye shes sgor zhugs nas//'di ltar snang ba'i chos rnams la//rgyu ni ngag pa'i dbyings lta ba//'bras bu ye shes dkyil 'khor lha//a ti yo ga bang chen ni//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i byang chub sems//rgyu 'bras 'das la a nus smrig
43a1(rnying rgyud): rdzogs chen rgyu 'bras gnyis ldan//a ru yo gang gel bar bshad//lha skyong gnyis kyi go ba sgrib bstan//kye sems dpa' chen po//rdzogs pa chen po'i lha spyod maM//rgyu 'bras bsgrub dang mi 'dra ste//lha spyang byang chub sems dang 'di ni//nam par lta bu
43a2(rnying rgyud): /rang bzhin te//namakha' la ni rtog dpyod 'das//gang zhig rtogs dpyod byed pa des//byang chub na mkhara' bzhin mi 'grub//rtogs dpyod gnyis byung gol sgrib pa//lha skyong na mkha'i rang bzhin la//gang zhig gzung dang 'dzin byed pas//nam mkha' byang chub de mi 'grub//
43a3(rnying rgyud): gzung 'dzin gnyis byung gol sgrib bo//lha spyong rnam pa gnyis byung nas//na mkha' gnyisu meng pa bzhin//byang chub la ni gnyi su med pa//gang zhig gnyi su byed pa des//byang chub rnam par de mi 'grub//lha skyong gnyis byung gal slob bo//byang chub rnamakha'i rang bzhin te/
43a4(rnying rgyud): namakha' la ni blang dor med//grang zhig blang dor byed pa des//byang chub na mkha' de mi 'grub//blang dor gnyis byung gi la sgrib pa//byang chub rnam pa'i rang bzhin te//namakha' la ni rtsol sgrub med//gang zhig rtsol sgrub byed pa nges//byang chub rnam par de mi 'grub/rtsol zhing bsgrub
43a5(rnying rgyud): pa gol sgrib po//byang chub rnam pa'i rang bzhin te//nam mkha' la ni rgyu 'bras gnyis lta bas//byang chub na mkha' nga mi 'grub//rgyu 'bras lta ba gol sgrib bo//kye sems dpa' chen po//chos kun namakha'i rang bzhin te//namakha' la ni rang bzhin med//namakha' la ni dpe yang
43a6(rnying rgyud): med//na mkha' la na tshad kyang med//chos rnams thams cad ma lus pa//don ni 'di ltar shes par bya//kye sems dpa' chen po 'di rtogs shig/theg pa re le lta ba re//lta ba'i yul ni gzhan med//chos nyid byang chub sems 'di la//gzhan du lta ba'i yul med pas//lha
43a7(rnying rgyud): bas byan pa gang lag par ladag chag pa khyang dub las ma stsal pa khyab khyang chung badrung 'i 'bral bar bya /ga'ang du 'asrung du bang bar bya/dam chag la yang bsrung du med/ngas chog des gsungam chong bar bshang /theg pa ma 'phrin pas ro/
43b1(rnying rgyud): byung rgya gyis grub par byab mas ca byed sa pam/sa gyis gyu gyis zug gyu da/'phrin las nas ma ba papa/ga tha la gas nga /rdzogs byung du sems//sa gams sa rnas pa sa dang /
43b2(rnying rgyud): sbyin grags pa dang //mas gsum mam mam lus dang/nga srung srod byung bas pa'i bye//sa la sbyang shing grong dbyed//sa las kyang slong rlo gyu /ga par ma shes cing pa rdzogs kyi cu mas//sems pas pa
43b3(rnying rgyud): chos med pas//reg pa'i rjes kun byang chub sems//ches kyi zhes dmigs med par bshad//rdzogs chen da byung me shes ma//um la rtog par mi byed//mi rtse mi bskyod ye shes ces gsungs//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las/khwa sbyong
43b4(rnying rgyud): gol sgrib bstan pa'i le'u ste/sum cu rtsa brgyang ba'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po nges/nyid kyi rang bzhin thams cad la//ro la sa dang sgrib pa med pa rgyal ba'i snying po yin pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa'
43b5(rnying rgyud): chen po nyon cig/nga ni kun gyi byang chub sems//des na kun gyi snying por bshad//sku gsum ston pa rnam gsum dang //dus gsum sangs rgyas bzhugs pa dang //rigs 'dzin byang chub sems dpa' dang //khams gsum sems can thams cad dang //sngang
43b6(rnying rgyud): srid sdod bcud thams cad la//'byung ba'i snying po nga las med//de yang ji lta bu zhe na//mi rtog mnyam pa de nyid las//skye ba med pa'i chos su byung //rang gi ngo bo de nyid las//'dod pa longs spyod rdzogs sku byung //thuzugs rje byang chub chen po las//sprul
