43b7(rnying rgyud): pas 'gro ba'i nga mdzang byung //dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad kyang //snga 'das pa'i sangs rgyas kyis//rang byung ye shes sems cad ngo//mi cho rtogs nas sngar sangs rgyas//da ltar mi rtog dang la bzhungs//da ltar bzhubs pa'i sangs rgyas cing /
44a1(rnying rgyud): rang byung ye shes sems cid de//mirtogs pas ni 'gro da mdzad//srang nas 'byor pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//byang su la byung ye shes pa//mi rtog pa'i lung bstanas//'gro ba'i ngos pa byung bar nges//rig 'dzi dang ris pa rnams
44a2(rnying rgyud): rang byung byang chub sems ni ni//bltas pas mthong ba ma yinan//rang sems snying po nga ru grang //khams gsum sems can thams cad kyi//rang byung ye shes bkag med pas//dbang po yul la rig gsal ba//ma bcos pa'i byang chub sems//
44a3(rnying rgyud): de nyid snying po yang dag bshad//snang sring snod bcud thams cad kyang //nga yis ma bskyed gcig kyang med//kun rgyu las ma byung med//des na kun gyi snying por bshad//nga ni kun las rgyal ba ste//sku gsung las kyang nga rgyal la//sangs rgyas gsum las/
44a4(rnying rgyud): nga rgyalo//rig 'dzin byang chub sems dpa' dang //khams gsum gnas pa'i sems can dang //snang sring snod bcud thams cad kun//byed pa nga yin kun las rgyal//kun byed byang chub sems pa yis//bstan pa kun gyi sngon rol du//byang chub sems kyi
44a5(rnying rgyud): bstan pa bkod//byang chub sems kyi bstan pa 'di//kun gyis dgod par mi nus te//de yang ji ltar bkod zhe na//chos la bya ru med pa'i phyir//brtsal ba med pa'i sgo nas 'jug/byang chub sems las med pa'i phyir//byang chub sems kyi chos nyid la///
44a6(rnying rgyud): sems kyis bsgom du med pa'i phyir//lta ba bsgom du med pa bkod//gzung 'dzin 'das pa'i chos nyid la//dam tshig bsrung du med pa bkod//ye nas lhun gyis grub pas na//'phrin las brtsal du med pa bkod//byang chub sems la kun gnas pas//
44a7(rnying rgyud): sa la sbyang du med pa bkod//byang chub sems ni bgrod pa'i gnas//sems kyis sa la bgrod med pas//lam la bgrang du med par bstan//chos nyid byang chub sems yin pas//chos nyid gzhan du lha mi byed//ye shes rang byung ye shes te//yul la/
44b1(rnying rgyud): rtsom par ye shes mis//kun bye byang chub sems pa ni//lha sbyo kun las rgyal ba'i byed//byed pa'i snying po med pa'i byi ba rgyal para'i snying po zhesu bshad//ces gsu so//byang chu gyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//myu ma pa'i snying po 'i le ru ste/'am
44b2(rnying rgyud): nu rtsa dgu pa le//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po ngas//byang chub kyi sems nyid kyi rang bzhin bstan pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/bdag gi bstan pa 'di bkod pas//dus gsum sangs rgyas
44b3(rnying rgyud): thams cad dang //rig 'dzin byang chub sems dpa' dang //khams gsum sems can thams cad kun//rtsol dang bsgrub ma byas par//ye nas sangs rgyas lung bstan pas//'khor gyi ston pa rnams gsum gyi//rtsol sgrub byed pa'i bstan pa gsum//gol/
44b4(rnying rgyud): sa sgribs zhig par ra'agyur ces gsung so////de nas sems dpa' rdo rjes slar zhus pa//kye ston pa kun byang rgyal po lags/khyed kyi bstan pa byang chub sems//byang chub sems 'di sangs rgyas na//khams gsum sems can sangs rgyas sam/
44b5(rnying rgyud): khams gsum sems can sangs rgyas na//khams gsum gnasu ci ste 'khor zhes zhuso//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos slar gsungs pa/kye sems dpa' chen po 'di nyon cig/byang chub sems las byung ba'i//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams
44b6(rnying rgyud): kun ka chos nyid mngon du phyung ba ste//de bzhin nyid mi spong bas//khams gsum gnasu 'khor ba yin//de bzhin nyid du gcig pa yi//chos nyid yul ni lnga snang la//'dod mi 'dod ni lnga lnga las//nyon mongs sdug bsngal skye zer nas//chos nyid yul lnga
44b7(rnying rgyud): sposlo byed pa//rang byung ye shes sdang ba de//bskal pa yung du mi spong bas//khams gsum gnasu 'khor ba yin//de bzhin nyid du gcig pa yis//chos nyid yul ni lnga sdang la//'dod dang mi 'dod lnga snang la//'khor ba'i rgyu ni yin zer nas//chos nyid lnga
46a1(rnying rgyud): @#/po 'gegs par byed//rang byung ye shes snang ba de//bskal pa du mar mi khegs pas//khams gsum gnasu 'khor ba yin//de bzhin nyid du gcig pa yi//chos nyid yul ni lnga snang la//'dod dang mi 'dod lnga yul byung //chags pa'i gnas
46a2(rnying rgyud): ni yin zer nas//chos nyid lnga po sbyong bar byed//rang byung ye shes snang ba de//bskal pa yun du mi spyod pas//khams gsum gnas 'khor ba yin//chos nyid mngon du phyung ba yi//de bzhin nyi du gcig pa yi//chos nyid lnga ni yul snang la//
46a3(rnying rgyud): 'dod dang mi 'dod lnga byung bas//byin brlabs gnas ni yin zer nas//phyi nang dag par byin gyis rlob//chos nyid lnga po dag par byed//rang byung ye shes snang ba de//mi tshe bdun du byin mi rlobs//khams gsum gnasu 'khor ba yin//byang chub/
46a4(rnying rgyud): sems las byung ba yi//chos nyid mngon du phyung ba yin//de bzhin nyid gcig pa yi//chos nyid lnga ni yul snang la//'dod dang mi 'dod lnga phyung ste//mtho ris ngan song rgyu zer nas//de bzhin nyid la blang dor byed//rang byung ye shes snang ba de//rang
46a5(rnying rgyud): gi rkyen gyis ngor mi btub//mi btub dor bar byed pas 'khor//byang chub sems las byung ba yis//mngon du phyung ba'i chos nyid//de bzhin nyid du gcig pa las//chos nyid yul ni lnga snang ste//'dod dang mi 'dod yul lnga ste//sku gsung
46a6(rnying rgyud): thugasu shes byas nas//sku gsung thugasu gdod sgrub byed//ye nas yin la sgrub byed pas//sgrub pa nyid kyis 'khor ba yin//kye sems dpa' chen po de bas na//byang chub sems spangs pha rol na//rang byung ye shes ye nas med//'khor
46a7(rnying rgyud): ba spangs pa'i pha rol na//mya ngan 'das zhes bya ba med//chos can spangs pa'i pha rol na//chos nyid bya ba ye nas med//thams cad de bzhin nyid du gcig/de bzhin nyid du gcig pa la//de bzhin nyid la bcos pas nor//de ni ma nor pa yi ming //bzhin
46b1(rnying rgyud): ni bcosu med pa'i don//nyid ni rang gi mtshan nyid bstan//de bzhin nyid la 'chos ma byed//de bzhin nyid la 'chos byed pa//byang chub sems la su 'chos pa//des ni 'khor ba'i snying po bsgrub ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las/byang chub kyi
46b2(rnying rgyud): /sems kyi rang bzhin bstan pa'i le'u ste bzhi bcu tham pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin nga yin pa ni bstan to//kye sems dpa' dam pa rdo rje khyod//nga yi rang bzhin 'di ltar shes
46b3(rnying rgyud): par gyis//nga mi stor pa'i slon ma byang chub sems//dus gsum sangs rgyas kun gyi snying po yin//khams gsum sems can kun gyi pa dang ma//snang sring sdong bcud kun gyi rgyu yang yin//nga las ma byung ba ni gcig kyang mang //nga ni mi gnas kun tu khyab pa'i
46b4(rnying rgyud): phyir//bani e nas dus gsum sangs rgyas yin//nga ni mi rtog mnyam par gnas pa'i phyir//rtog med mnyam nyid chos sku yi sangs rgyas//nga yi ngo bo kun tu longs spyod phyir//nga nyid longs spyod rdzogs skur ye sangs rgyas//nga nyid rang byung ye shes snang ba
46b5(rnying rgyud): byod+yo gyi dbugs rje sgru ba sgyur ma nas sa rgyus//des nasangs rgyas kyi snying por bshad//nga s+t+rai s+ye shes byang chucu sems snang gA/l+r-ing+ga sa snang bas sa rgyas yin// la gnyis me ga ga tu 'as pas rgya//sbya ba byed pas sems stong
46b6(rnying rgyud): tshu /# mam pas sa/ ma nas sa gsas 'a mas ma/ ma sa
46b7(rnying rgyud): masapa brgyas pa sa bya pa sa byas 'gyu ming sa 'jus ma sa ma pa 'ja bya ma/ga sa tham mas pa/thams sa lus ma
47a1(rnying rgyud): @#g.yo ba rlung du gsal//ma bsgribs gsal bas namakha'i rang bzhin te//gcig la nyi shu rtsa lnga tshang ba ni//byang chub sems kyi che ba'i yon tan bstan//chos la gnyis med byang chub semasu gcig/gcig gis kun byas kun byed rgyal
47a2(rnying rgyud): bo byas ces gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las/ngo bo gcig pa'i le'u ste/zhe gcig pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//chos thams cad byang chub kyi sems kho nar sangs rgyas pa 'di
47a3(rnying rgyud): gsungs//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/mchis kyi sku ni byang chub sems//byang chub sems las chos kyi sku//rngub tsam sus kyang bcosu med//des na sangs rgyas sems las med//longs spyod rdzogs sku byang chub sems//byang chub
47a4(rnying rgyud): sems la longs spyod sku//sems las byung ba'i gzugs rnams las//longs spyod rdzogs las sku gzhan med//sprul pa'i sku ni byang chub sems//byang chub sems kyi sprul pa las//'gro ba'i don mdzad gzhan ma med//dus gsum sangs//
47a5(rnying rgyud): rgyas thams cad kyang //byang chub sems las gzhan med//snga rabs 'das pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//rang sems ma bcos sngar mthong rtogs na//da ltar bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//ma bcos pa'i rang sems do//ma bcos ji bzhin
47a6(rnying rgyud): rtogs gyur nas//da ltar 'gro ba'i don yang mdzad//smad nas byon pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//bcos shing bsgrubs pas 'grub mi 'gyur//ma rtogs bcos pas grub par byed//bskal pa du ma'i grangs bgrangs kyang //mi rtsol bde dang phrad
47a7(rnying rgyud): mi 'gyur//kye sku gsum sems las ma bcos phyir//sku gsum ston pa'i mdo lung las//'chos par byed pa'i don 'byung ba//de ni ston pa sku gsum yang //nyid kyi rang bzhin ston pa min//de bzhin 'chos par byed pa yi//ston pas
47b1(rnying rgyud): bden tshul ci bstan yang //nges lung ma yin zhes gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po las/bcosu med pa'i le'u ste/zhe gnyis pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byedang rgyal pos//chos thams cad kyi ston pa nyid yin par
47b2(rnying rgyud): /zhal gyis bzhes pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga ni chos rnams kun gyi ston pa ste//nga yi rang bzhin rnam pa gsum las ni//ston pa yang rnam pa gsum du bstan//bstan pa yang ni rnam gsum pa yis bstan//nge 'dzin 'khor tshogs
47b3(rnying rgyud): gsum yang nga yis bstan//ston pa gsum yang gang dang gang zhen//chos kyi rang banam ston pa'i ston pa dang //chos kyi ngo bo ston pa'i ston pa dang //don gyi sgra tshig ston pa'i ston pa ni//skye ba med las byin gyis brlabs phyung ste//ji ltar snang ba'i chos
47b4(rnying rgyud): rnams thams cad kun//chos kyi de bzhin nyid du ston par byed//chos kyi ngo bo ston pa'i ston pas ni//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//ston pa nyid kyi sku ru phyung nas ni//chos kyi ngo bor de las med par ston//chos kyi sgra tshig ston pa'i
47b5(rnying rgyud): ston pas ni//chos rnams kun gyi don ni sgrar bsdus nas//mi rig mi shes khams gsum sems can la//don de sgra dang tshig gis bsdebs nas ston//des na ston pa'i rang bzhin bstan par bshad//de 'dzin 'khor ni rnam pa gsum bstan te//
47b6(rnying rgyud): shin tu rnal 'byor rtsol sgrub 'jas pa'i 'khor//sgor zhugs yid ches sangs rgyas sa la gnas//rig 'dzinas dang dang po bcu pa'i 'khor//ma bor sde reg bsgoms pa'i sa la gnas//sems bskyed mos pas sbyong ba'i 'khor rnams ni//rgyu sa
47b7(rnying rgyud): 'bras bu drug 'joms sa la gnas//de ni ston pa gsum gyi 'khor du bstan//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//ston pa gsum dang 'khor tshogs de ltar na//bstan pa rnam gsum ji ltar bstan par bgyi cis zhus pas//kye sems
48a1(rnying rgyud): dpa' chen po nyon cig/chos sku'i bstan pa de bzhin nyid bstan te//de bzhin nyid la rnam par dbye ru med//ji ltar sdang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//sems kyi ngo bo yin pas de bzhin nyid//de la gang su bsgom zhing chos byed pa//de yi chos
48a2(rnying rgyud): sku bstan pa rtogs pa med//snang ba med pa'i chos nyid byang chub la//ma nor de bzhin nyid du ma rtogs te//de la gang du 'chos cing bsgom byed pa//de yis rang sems bcos med de ma rig/snang srid chos dang sdang byed byang chub sems//sems kyi de bzhin nyid 'byung de
48a3(rnying rgyud): bzhin nyid//de bzhin nyid du ma rtogs so sor mthong //de bzhin nyid ni dbyings nas tshol byed pa//dus gsum rgyal bas btsal yang mi rnyedado//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/longs spyod rdzogs pa'i bstan pa longs spyod de//longs spyod lnga ni chos nyid longs spyod
48a4(rnying rgyud): cig/gzugs la sogs pa'i longs spyod lnga la ni//gcig la'ang lnga lnga nyi shu rtsa lngar spyod//rtsa lnga longs spyod de ni su lnga'i tshul//tshul ni chos nyid don gyi ngo bo nyid bshad//sangs rgyas sems can kun gyi longs spyod //longs sku nga'i gzugs la spyad
48a5(rnying rgyud): du med//rig 'dzinas bcu rnams la nga yis bstan//ces gsungs//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/sbrul sku bstan pa thugs rje don 'tsham ste//khams gsum sems can bsam pa tha dad la//sku dang gsung dang thugos na rnam gsum gyis//bsam pa
48a6(rnying rgyud): dang sku gsung thugs rjes bzlog/dbang po yul zhen pa de bzlog nas//dben pa'i yul ni gang 'thad de bstan pas//des ni nga yis thugs rjes 'dren lung byed//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga ni chos rnams kun gyi ston yin phyir//sangs rgyas la yang nga yi
48a7(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin bstan//sems can la yang nga yi rang bzhin bstan//rang bzhin bstan pas ston par zhal gyis bzhes//rang bzhin 'di ltar rig par gyis//ston pa dang ni bstan pa 'khor rnams dang //nga yi rang bzhin gsum las gzhan med//ston pa rang byung ye
48b1(rnying rgyud): shes 'gag med yin//bstan pa nga yi rang bzhin ma bcos yin//ngar 'dzin 'khor ni nga'i ngo bos 'dzin//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po las//ston par zhal gyis bzhes pa'i le'u ste/zhe gsum pa'o///de nas byang
48b2(rnying rgyud): chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//nyid la bya ba med pa'i don 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po/bya ba med pa'i don 'angadi ni//kun byed nga yi rang bzhin te//nga la bya ba med pa ni//nga ni ye nas byas zin pas//bya ba med pa'i chos nyid yin//chos nyid
48b3(rnying rgyud): /rang bzhin pas//rang bzhin de la bya ru med//rang bzhin rang bzhin bya ba de//bco su med pa'i rang bzhin yin//bcos med byang chub de la no//sems dpa' rdo rje khyod ma 'chos//sems dpa' rdo rje de 'tshos nA//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//kun byed nga'i rang bzhin
48b4(rnying rgyud): yin//kye sems dpa' chen po khyod nyon cig/nga yi rang bzhin 'gyur med la//bsgoms na 'chos shing sbyar ba yin//da ni ye nas lhun grub la//bsgrubs na nga la btsos pa yin//nga la bgrod pas phyin pa maud//de la brtsal bas grub pa med//nga la sbyangs pos byung ba med//
48b5(rnying rgyud): nga la ma lha yul med kyis//nga la ma bgrod lam med kyis//nga la ma sbyong sgrib med kyis//mi gnas dmigs pa'i yul dang bral//spros med bsam pa'i yul las 'das//'dzin pas brtags pa'i chos rnams ni//nga yi ngo bo bstan pa yin//de la spong
48b6(rnying rgyud): 'gegasu byed pa//bskal pa gsum gyi bar du ni//kun byed nga dang phrad mi 'gyur//spang bar bya ba'i chos de ni//rang gi ngo bo yin par bshad//kye sems dpa' chen po//byang chub sems ni kun byed rgyal po ste//kun byed byang chub sems kyis byas pa ni//
48b7(rnying rgyud): byang chub sems las gzhan du byas pa med//byang chub sems ni ye nas ma nor yin//ye nas yin pas ye nas nor ba med//nor ba med la bden brdzun spros mi 'dogs//rgyu rkyen 'bras bu rtsol sgrub kun las 'das//byas zin rtsol sgrub
49a1(rnying rgyud): @#'das pa ma rigs nas//rtsol sgrub pa byed bsgoms nas grung lha na//byang chub gegs su de las che ba med//mi gnas mi rtog cing yang dgongs med pas//mi rtog mnyam pa'i yul la rang bzhin gnas//ye nas byas zin rtsol
49a2(rnying rgyud): ba'i sems mi 'byung //bde chen de la gang su gnas pa ni//chos rnams kun gyi snying por de 'gyur ro//nga yis bdag gzhan kun gyi don mi byed//gcig 'dus thams cad 'dus pa'i snying po nges//ma bltas dus gsum sangs rgyas thugs kyang gsal//
49a3(rnying rgyud): ma bsgribs sems can kun gyi sems kyang snang //kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/kun byed nga yis sems can don pas bzhin//nga yi don byas gzhan gyis bya mi dgos//gzhan la ltos pa'i chos ni nga lung min//nga la kun rdzogs ye nas byas zin
49a4(rnying rgyud): rdzogs//ye nas byas zin lta ba bsgom mi dgos//ye nas rnam dag dam tshig bsrung mi dgos//ye nas byang chub sa la kun gnas pas//rig 'dzinas dang sa bcu sbyang mi dgos//ma brtsal ye nas lhun gyis grub pa'i phyir//brtsal ba'i 'phrin las
49a5(rnying rgyud): ye nas bya mi dgos//rang byung ye shes yul la mi rtog pas//rig pa'i ye shes yul gyis gsalo mi dgos//bde chen chos sku sems kyis gcig pa'i phyir//snga phyi dus gsum med de khyad par gcig/phun sum tshogs dang dus gnas kun
49a6(rnying rgyud): gcig dang //thams cad gcig phyir nga la kun rdzogs te//rdzogs pa chen po nga la kun rdzogs pas//lha spyod 'phrin las dam tshig sa lam ni//gong bshad bzhin du btsal zhing bsgrub mi dgos//de ltar ma rig rtsol sgrub byas gyur na/
49a7(rnying rgyud): 'bras 'das pa'i ngen dang 'gal gyur nas//bya med bde chen de dang mi phrang de//rtsol sgrub na kyis ma rig de ziran 'gyur//de bas rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i rdzogs chen la//skal med rnams kyi spyod yul ma yin pas//rgyu dang 'bras bu'i chos la sbyang/
49b1(rnying rgyud): par bya ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las/rdzogs pa chen po skal med don med kyi le'u ste/zhe zin pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos/rdzogs pa chen po la lta ba bsgom med kyi mdo
49b2(rnying rgyud): lung 'di bstan no//kye sems dpa' chen po//rang gi lta ba bsgom med ni//kun byed nga yin nga la bsgom du med//kun byed nga ni bsgom pa'i yul ma yin//nga ni ma bcos kun la rang bzhin gnas//rang bzhin gnas pa'i bsgom du med pa de//nga las
49b3(rnying rgyud): ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//nga las mngon du byung ba'i chos nyid la//brkam chags lnga'i sdug bsngal yin zer nas//ma bcos mi 'jog brtul zhugs spong 'gegs bsgom//de lung bstan pas ma bcos don dang 'gal//mu stegs sde
49b4(rnying rgyud): bzhi sprul pa'i thugs rje yi//byin brlabs 'khor du bkod nas lung bstan pas//bya bral bde ba che nyid thob mi 'gyur//kye sems dpa' chen po//nga lus mngon du phyung ba'i chos nyid la//brkam chags lnga'i sdug bsngal skye zer nas//dang
49b5(rnying rgyud): /po'i sa bcu par sems dpa' la//bden pa rnam pa gnyis kyi rtog dpyod kyis//don dam med la kun rdzob yod pa ru//sprul pas lung bstan rtog dpyod bsgom par byed//ma bcos ji bzhin pa'i don dang 'gal//kye sems dpa' chen po//nga
49b6(rnying rgyud): las mngon du phyung ba'i chos nyid /ma bcos ji bzhin pa'i chos nyid la//longs spyod rdzogs pas 'khor la lung bstan pa//ji bzhin bcos pa med par ma rtogs te//phyi nang sdong bcung dag par byin brlangs te//byang chub yan lag che ga kun
49b7(rnying rgyud): sgo nas//bsgom pa'i ye shos lha ni sdang byed//skang cig mchod nas gshegs gsol ting 'dzin bshig/yang bsgoms yang bshig byis pa'i 'dag khang 'dra//ji bo zhin ma bcos pa'i don dang 'gal//kye soms dpa' chen po nyid cig/
50a1(rnying rgyud): nga las byung ba'i ston pa'i lung ston gyis//nga yis byung ba'i chos nyid mngon phyung la//ting 'dzin mtshan ma yod med sgor zhugs nas//chos nyid cig la blang ngor byas nas ni//ye shes dam tshig so sor bsgoms
50a2(rnying rgyud): /nas ni//grogs po dag gis dngos grub ster re ba//gcig la gnyisu bltas pa'i bsgom pa nor//ngo bo gcig la gnyis kyis thob pa med//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga yi rang bzhin mngon du phyung ba la//phung po 'byung ba rnam shes dngade
50a3(rnying rgyud): po yul//rang rgyud dag pa rdo rje lhar byas te//bsnyen bsgrub rnam bzhi'i cho gas bsgom byas nas//sems kyi 'phro 'dus 'phags mchod 'gro don byed//'phro 'du bsdus nas dngos grub thob 'char ba//rang gis rang gi dngos grub theb par bsgom//
50a4(rnying rgyud): de yang sems kyi ting 'dzin che chad 'gyur//rdzogs chen lnga pa bsgom du med pa ni//kun byed pa'i sems kyi yon tan te//byang chub sems kyi che ba'i yon tan gyis//brtsal zhing bsgrub pa'i dka' ba spyad mi dgos//rgyu rkyen med
50a5(rnying rgyud): pas brtsal ba'i las mi dgos//'bras bu rang bzhin gzhan nas bsgrub mi dgos//chos nyid rang yin bsgom pa bya mi dgos//ma skyes pas na 'jig pa'i dngos po med//gzhan la ma ltos bsgom gnas tshol mi byed//nga la gang zhig bsgom
50a6(rnying rgyud): par byed pa des//bsgoms pa nyid kyis nga dang phrad mi 'gyur//chos nyid mngon du phyung ba nga yin pas//sdug bsngal mi skye rnam par spang mi dgos//rang byung yin pas skye 'jig med pa'i phyir//ma rig rten 'brel dbang po dgag mi dgos//rang byung
50a7(rnying rgyud): nam dog chos nyid sbyangs mi dgongs//ye nas dag pas gtsang sa byami dgos//si nas lhun drub krol sgrub bya'i dgos//bsdus pas ma dag pa'i chos med//de ban nyid dang a snga kun tu bzang //dbang po drug la snang ba'i chos rnams kying/
50b1(rnying rgyud): ye nas de yin nga las gzhan ma med//de las gangazhig bsgom dang sgrub byed pas//de ni mangs pa rnam par sgeg pa 'dra//nga yi rang bzhin spyod yul kun las 'das//nga yis mngon du phyung ba'i chos rnams ni//nga yi ngo bo kun gyis mthong bya'i phyir/sna tshogs
50b2(rnying rgyud): dbang po ngo la bstan pa yin//de la nga yi rang bzhin kun rtogs shig/de la nga'i rang bzhin ma mthong na//kun byed nga yi theg pa bstan mi bya//rdzogs pa chen po'i don dang skal med pas//rgyu 'bras theg pa drang ba'i lung la gzung //da ni ye nas spyod yul 'das
50b3(rnying rgyud): ba 'di//rgyu 'bras dga' ba'i skal ba med rnams la//bstan na spros dang skur ba 'ong ba'i phyir//bstan na rang gi sems la skur btab pas//drug tu skye zhing gtan du 'khor bar gnas///kye sems dpa' chen po ngaden rtogs//nga ni
50b4(rnying rgyud): spyong yul kun las 'das pa'i phyir//tshig gis mi phebs dbang po spyod yul 'das//nga yi rang bzhin bsdus te thig ler gnas//ma spos gnyis med ye nas gcig tu bstan//dam tshig bsang ba'i bsgom pa bya mi dgos//'phrin las brtsal ba'i bsgom pa bya ma
50b5(rnying rgyud): dgos//lam la mi bgrod sa la mi sbyong ste//rang las chos nyid gzhan du tshol mi byed//rang byung ye shes mi rtog g.yos pa med//gzhan gyi lung men kun be dang yi lung //rgya 'bras 'das pa brtsol dang sgrub mi byed//ma byung bas na dngos po bsgom mi byed//
50b6(rnying rgyud): ma skyes pas na 'jig pa'i dogs pa med//nga mi snang bas dmigs pa bsgom dang bral//bde chen gdal ba chen po'i klong dkyil na//mi rtog mnyam pa chen po'i rang bzhin gnas//chos nyid snang ba med pa'i rang bzhin la//rig pas mi rtog ting 'dzin ma byed cig/
50b7(rnying rgyud): kyi sems dpa' chen po//bsgoms ma gang bsgoms nga yi nang yin gyis//kun gyi snying po byang chub sems yin na//byang chub sems ni gzung 'dzin dral ba'i phyir//bsgom par bya ba rdul tsam yong ma yin//gang bshad bsgoms pas 'bras bu ma thob bzhin//
51a1(rnying rgyud): @#/yang bsgoms pas thob par lung mi ston//kye sems dpa' chen po nga la ltos//ba ni kun byed byang chub sems yin te//kun byed byas pa kun byas nyid du byas//kun ni ma lus pa'i sgrar bshad//byed pa'i snying po de yis thams cad byed//byang
51a2(rnying rgyud): ba gzung du yod pa ma yin la//chub pa kun gyi snying pos snying por chub//sems ni rang byung ye shes kun tu sems//ces gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//lta ba bsgom du med pa'i le'u ste/zhe lnga pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi
51a3(rnying rgyud): sems kun byed rgyal po des//rdzogs pa chen po dam tshig bsrung du med pa 'di gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/rdzogs po chen po dam chag bsrang du med pa 'ang gsungs//kya sams dpa' chen pa nyan cag/rdzogs chen dam tshig bsrung du med bstan pa//kun
51a4(rnying rgyud): byed byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin ni//ma skyes spros dang bral ba'i rang bzhin gyis//skye ba'i cho 'phrul sna tshogs yul nas byung //yul de rang gi rang bzhin yin shes byas//de la mi rtog rtog pa'i yul las 'das//yul la mi 'dzin slong dang len med pas//med
51a5(rnying rgyud): pa phyol ba gcig pu lhun grub ste//bsrung bar bya ba'i sdom khrims kun dang bral//bsrung med nga las byung ba'i ston gsum gyis//mu stegs sde bzhi thugs rje bini brlabs nas//'dul ba phog pa'i 'khor ni sde bzhi la//bsrung bar bya ba'i khams 'di lta ste/
51a6(rnying rgyud): rtsa ba dang ni yan lag bsrung ba'i grangs//nyis brgya lnga bcu lnga brgya lnga bcu khrims//bsrung ba'i dus na grangs dang don mi shes//des na sdom pa mi thub bsrung thabs med//rdzogs chen ci spyod rtog pa kun las 'das//sdom pa rgya mtsho grangs mang mtha' yas pa/
51a7(rnying rgyud): yus ngag yid la bsdus pa rnam pa gsum//dbang po'i yul las sdug bsngal byung nas ni//lus kyi 'gro 'dug gom pa bongs med//ngag gres tshig brjod don gyis bden par dka'o//yid kyi bsam pa bden pa kun las 'gal//ma nor bsam pa kun
51b1(rnying rgyud): kyang bsgrub tu med//lus ngag sdom pa rgya mdud dam po des/bden gnyis don dang phrad pa shin tu da/ra//bsrung med nyams pa med dang phrad mi 'gyur//bsrung med nyams pa med pa kun byed lung //kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/'a las byung ba'i ston pas lu
51b2(rnying rgyud): phog pa//gza' dang rgyu skar dus tshigs bzang sgo nas//phyi nang gtsang ma'i khrus kyis dgu byas te//sdom pa tshug khrims 'gro don zhal 'chas pa//gang yang dam bcas tshul bzhin mi 'grub ste//dam bcas ma grub bshags pas sems mi 'dag/bsrung me dang /
51b3(rnying rgyud): nga dang yul du thag rings nyams//kye rdo rje gsang ba gsum gyi dam tshig ni//rtsa ba gsum dang yan lag lnga rnams te//rtsa ba gsum ni sku gsung thugs 'dus te//sku ni lha'i skur bsgoms lus kyang 'ching //ngag ni 'dzab dang ting 'dzin zhi ba min//yid kyis ting 'dzin
51b4(rnying rgyud): 'phro 'dus bzung nas ni//rtsa ba mi rtog dam tshig bde dang bral//yan lag rnam pa lnga ni 'di lta ste//dang por shes par bya ba'i dam tshig ni//phung po lnga dang 'byung ba rnam pa lnga//rnam shes dbang po yul la sogs pa rnams//lha dang dkyil 'khor rang bzhin shes
51b5(rnying rgyud): par bya//shes bya shes byed med pa des ma mthong //spyad par bya ba'i dam tshig rnam lnga ni//ta na ga na ma byin blang ba dang //mi tshangs spyod dang brdzun du smra ba dang //rnam pa lnga ni thabs mkhas spyod byed pa//ma spyad nyams mthong rtogs dang bcas nas spyod//de yang
51b6(rnying rgyud): mi rtog mnyam dang bral ba yin//dang du blang ba'i dam tshig rnam lnga ni//dri chen dri chu la sogs ngam rdzas lngas//rgyal ba'i sku lnga 'di la grub zer nas//dpa' bo 'bru lngas 'phro 'du byas nas ni//rgyal ba'i sku lnga dang du len byed pa//dang du ma blangs sku lnga mi grub rtog
51b7(rnying rgyud): b+h+r-ing dor bral ba'i rtog 'das de ma grub//mi spong ba'i dam tshig rnam lnga ni//'dod chags zhe sdang la sogs dug lnga po//gsang ba rdo rje dam tshig chen po ru//nyon mongs lnga bsgyur ye shes rnam pa lngar//dug lnga mi spong ye shes rnam lngar len//blang dor med pa'i bde
52a1(rnying rgyud): ba des ma 'grub//grub par bya ba'i dam tshig rnam lnga ni//phung po lnga dang 'byung ba rnam lnga dang //rnamaHshes yul lnga dkyil 'khor grub byed pa//ting 'dzin gsum dang cho ga lnga rdzogs nas//rgyal ba rigs lnga'i dkyil 'khor grub byed
52a2(rnying rgyud): pa//ting 'dzin cho ga lnga yis sems rtsol byed//mi rtog ma brtsal lhun gyis grub dang 'gal//bsgom pa'i sku ni mi rtog 'jar tshon 'dra//cho ga bzlas brjod 'jab kyang rtsi skya 'dra//rtsel sgrub spyod par lhun gyis grub mi 'gyur//med par bsgrubs pas shin tu
52a3(rnying rgyud): grub pa med//kun byed nga la bsrung ba'i dam tshig med//rgyu rkyen med pas rtsol zhing bsgrub mi dgos//lhun gyis grub pas spyad par bya mi dgos//ye shes yin pas shes par bya mi dgos//rang byung yin pas rgyu dang rkyen mi dgos//bzang ngan med pas blang dor bya
52a4(rnying rgyud): mi dgos//dngos po med pas med pa zhesu bshad//dngosu mi spyod ye shes ma 'gags pas//de la/sa phyal pa zhesu btags pa yin//semasu kun gcig gcig par bstan pa yin//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//yid ches byang chub sems
52a5(rnying rgyud): su rdzogs pa'i phyir//lhun gyis rdzogs pa zhesu nga yis bshad//tsha bgrang bkres skom blun lkugs chos nyid la//sku gsum 'khor bsdangs 'bros pa'i cho ga drug/gong bshad bzhin du brtsal zhing bsgrub mi dgos//rgyu gnyis rang byung ye shes yin pa'i phyir/
52a6(rnying rgyud): las kyi rkyen gyi dbang du de mi 'gyur//chos nyid rang bzhin cir yang 'gyur med pas//brtsal bas grub par nga yis lung ma bstan//rang byung ye shes kun dang mtshungs med pas//rgyu rkyen las kyis rang 'byung de mi 'grub//bden pa gnyis kyis gtan
52a7(rnying rgyud): la dbub tu med//nges paM gcig la bded pa rnam pa gnyis//ma nges po com can gyi lung de la//nges pa gcig tu bstan pa ga la yod//rnal 'byor gang zag chos la gros byed ni//sbyin gsum rgyu 'bras lung la rten smra ba//de la bden gnyis chang ma
52b1(rnying rgyud): bzhi yang dgos//kun byed nga yis ches kun byas pa'i phyir//ngas byas chos la bden pa gnyis mi dgos//ngas byas chos la bden pa gnyis dgosu//ngas byas thams cad byang chub sems byas//byang chub sems su byas//byang chub sems la don dam kun
52b2(rnying rgyud): rdzob med//ces gsungs//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//dam tshig bsrung du med pa'i le'u ste zhe drug pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po des//rdzogs pa chen po la 'phrin las brtsal du med pa 'di gsungs//
52b3(rnying rgyud): kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/kun byed nga ni rdzogs pa chen po ste//rdzogs pa chen po lhun gyis grub pa'i phyir//'phrin las ye nas btsal du med par bstan//'phrin las brtsal du yod par ston pa//nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//rang
52b4(rnying rgyud): rere 'khor la brtsal ba'i las phog pa//brtsal ba'i las ni rang res so sor phog/sprul pas brtsal ba'i rgyu la las pho ga pa//lus ngag yid gsum tshad med sgor zhugs nas//lus kyi sdom pa srog gcod rku g.yem spangs//ngag gi sdom pas
52b5(rnying rgyud): ngag 'khyal tshig rtsub dang //brdzun dang phra ma la sogs ngag gis spangs//yid kyi brnab sems gnod sems log lta spangs//dge bcu spyod pa'i las de rgyur bkod nas//sbyin pa tshul khrims pha rol phyin bcu las//spyad nas sa dang kun 'od thob
52b6(rnying rgyud): 'dod pa//tshe chad las ni bskal pa gsum du spyod//de yang kun byed nga'i lung phog min//longs spyod rdzogs pas 'khor la lung phog pa//rgyu dang 'bras bu'i sgo nas las phog pa//sems kyi ting 'dzin las ni 'di lta ste//khrus dang gtsang sbras phyi nang dag
52b7(rnying rgyud): byas te//tshe 'phrul las kyis byin gyis brlang sa nas ni//ye shes sems dang dam tshig gnyis bltas nas/ting 'dzin las kyis mchod cing gshegs gsol byed//las nges mi lo bdun du ci chad 'gyur/chos kyi sku las longs spyod skur ba
53a1(rnying rgyud): nas//rnal 'byor chen po 'khor la lung phog pa//bdag nyid chen po yin par lung bstan nas//rang rgyud dag pa'i dkyil 'khor de rnams la//kha dog rigs dang sbyar ba'i lha bsgoms nas//zhi lhas zhi bar byed pa'i las byed pa//rgyas
53a2(rnying rgyud): lhas rgyas par byed pa'i lngas byed pa//dbang lhas dbang du sdud pa'i las kyang byed//drag lhas spongs par byed pa'i las kyang byed//tshe la gnas dang phyag rgya rigs 'dzin 'grub/'phrin las rnam bzhis chi chad grub pa med//mi rtsol bya med las ni
53a3(rnying rgyud): des ma grub//rdzogs chen rgyu 'bras ye nas 'das pa'i phyir//brtsal zhing bsgrubs pas bya med grub pa med//rgyu 'bras lte ba'i ston pas lung bstan pa//'jig rten mtshan ma'i chos la tshod btsugs nas//rgyu las brtsal nas 'bras bu grub par
53a4(rnying rgyud): byed//sems la rgyu med yin 'bras bu med//byang chub sems la skye ba med pa'i phyir//'jig rten skye ba'i chos la dpe blangs nas//skye las 'jig pa 'byung bar spros mi gdags//rang byung rgyu 'bras 'das pa ma rtogs par//'jig rten mtshan ma'i chos/
53a5(rnying rgyud): la tshod btsugs nas//rgyu las brtsal bas 'bras bu 'byung zer ba//de ni rgyu 'bras theg pa drang lung yin//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/ye shes ye shes zhesu bstan pa ni//rang byung ye shes ma 'gags kun dang mtshungs//rgyu las ma byung mtshungs med
53a6(rnying rgyud): ye shes de//thams cad bskyed pas gzhan nas chos 'byung med//'jig rten rgyu 'bras chos la tshod btsugs nas//brtsal bas med pa'i 'bras bu 'byung mi 'gyur//byang chub sems ni rgya rkyen med mi skye//'jig rten skye 'gag chos la chod mi gzugs//byang chub
53a7(rnying rgyud): sems ni rkyen gyis ma bskyed pas//'jig rten rkyen bskyed chos la dpe ma len//kye sems dpa' chen po//che ba 'dod nas bsgom bsgrub col byed pa//bsgom bsgrub brtsal bas che ba de mi 'grub//che ba rang byung ye nas che ba yin/6/bsgoms na sangs rgyas
53b1(rnying rgyud): dgongos pa 'chos pa yin//sangs rgyas dgongs pa gang zhig 'phos byed pa//da las sangs rgyas thams cad spangs pa ya//de yis sangs rgyas thams cad sbangs pa yin//de yis sangs rgyas spos pa ma yin te//de yis rang gi byang dub sems spangs pas/
53b2(rnying rgyud): rang gi rang bzhin bskal par phrad mi 'gyur//kye sems dpa' chen po//sems kyis ting 'dzin bsgom pa ma byed cig/ye shes yul la rtog par ma byed cig/ngag gis 'dzab dang snying po bzlas mi dgos//lag gi 'du byed phyag rgya bca' mi dgos//sems kyi ting
53b3(rnying rgyud): 'dzin 'phro 'du las mi byed//ngad la gnas pas g.yos med lhun gyis grub//rang bzhin gnas pas sus kyang bco su med//ma brtsal ji bzhin pa ru gnas pa des//bya ba med pa las kyi mchog tu bstan//don de rtogs pas las rnams mi byed ka las ma
53b4(rnying rgyud): byas pa de bzhin nyid du gnas//de bzhin nyid las gang zhig su gnas pa//de yis ma nor ma bcos 'grub par 'gyur//de ni ma nor bzhin ni ma bcos yin//nyid ni ma nor ngo bo nyid la bya//de bzhin nyid las sangs rgyas chos medo ces gsung so//byang chub kyi
53b5(rnying rgyud): sems kun byed rgyal po las//'phrin las brtsal du med pa'i le'u ste zhe bdun pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po des//chos thams cad kyi gzhi ma//kun byed yin par zhal gyis bzhes pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa'
53b6(rnying rgyud): chen pa nyon cog/kun byedang ni kun gyi byang chub sems//byang chub sems ni chos kun gan ma ste/kun byedang mi rang bzhir rnam gsum las//phus su mtshogs pa'i dgos pa kun kyang chang //rang bzhin gsum ni 'di ltar shes par bya//skye ba med dang gag
53b7(rnying rgyud): pa med pa dang //mi 'gag skye ba'i cho 'phrul 'byung ba gsum//gsum ni phun sum tshogs par bston pa yin//ma skyes skye ba med pa'i rang bzhin las//chos kyi gzhi ma bstan pa phun sum tshogs//ma bsgribs ma bkag pa'i rang bzhin las//stong
54a1(rnying rgyud): pa gsum gyi rang bzhin phun sum tshogs//ma skyes skye ba'i tshe 'phrul ngo bo las//'khor gyi rang bzhin rnam gsum phun sum tshogs//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga yi rang bzhin chos nyid gcig pu la//nga ma 'gags pa'i ston
54a2(rnying rgyud): ba rnam gsum gyis//skye ba'i cho 'phrul phyung ba'i 'khor gsum la//nga ni chos nyid de bzhin nyid gcig la//rtogs par bya ba'i thabs kyi gzhi ma drug/lta ba chos nyid mthong ba'i gzhi ma byed//ngas ni mthong bar bya ba'i thabs yin te//ma skyes dmigs par
54a3(rnying rgyud): bya ba'i yul med pas//bltas pas de bzhin nyid la mthong ba med//dam tshig rang skyon sel gzhi ma byed//des ni bar chad med pa'i thabs yin te//de bzhin nyid la bsrung ba'i yul med pas//bsrungs pas de bzhin nyid la thub pa med//las ni
54a4(rnying rgyud): grub par byed pa gzhi ma byed//brtsal bas grub par byed pa'i thabs yin to//de bzhin nyid la btsal ba'i yul med pas//de bzhin nyid la las kyis grub pa med//lam gyis bgrod pas byin pa'i gzhi ma byed//da ni gnasu phyin pa'i thabs yin
54a5(rnying rgyud): te/de bzhin nyid la lam gyis bgrod med phyir//bgrod pas phyin pa'i de bzhin nyid ma yin//sa ni gnas par byed pa'i gzhi ma byed//de ni spang mi dgos pa'i thabs yin te//de bzhin nyid la thams cad kun gnas pas//gnas par bya ba'i
54a6(rnying rgyud): sa la sbyang du med//ye shes rtogs par byed pa'i gzhi ma byed//des ni chos nyid rigs pa'i thabs yin te//de bzhin nyid la rtogs pa'i yul med pas//rang byung ye shes yul la rtogs pa min//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/byang chub
54a7(rnying rgyud): sems na chos kyi gzhi yin pas//theg pa so so lnga skyong drug drug gis//rgyu 'bras ltos pa'i 'khor la de bstan pas//de ro rang 'thang bsam pa re ra yis//lha skyong gzhi ma drug las brtsal byas kyang //de bzhin nyid las brtsal ba'i yul
54b1(rnying rgyud): med pas//brtsal bas de bzhin nyid la bcos pa'i phyir//rang sems gzhi ma bcos med de mi 'phrang //kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/lta ba bsgom du med ces bstan pa ni//chos kyi rtsa ba rang las med pa'i phyir//rang gis rang la blta ru med pa'i phyir//
54b2(rnying rgyud): rang med yul ni nga yis bstan pa med//des na lnga ba bsgom du med ces bstan//dam tshig bsrung du med ces bstan pa ni//bsrungs pas mi thub rang gi sems mi khebs//rang byung ye shes yin pas bsrung du med//kye 'phrin las brtsal du med ces bstan pa ni//
54b3(rnying rgyud): rdzogs chen rgyu 'bras ye nasu 'das pa'i phyir//'phrin las rgyu las bsgrubs nas 'bras bu 'dod//rdzogs chen 'bras bu 'dod pa med pa'i phyir//'phrin las brtsal med lhun gyis grub pa'i phyir//'phrin las brtsal du med ces bstan pa yin//lam la
54b4(rnying rgyud): bgrod du med ces bstan pa ni/kakye dus gsum sangs rgyas khams gsum sems can dang //bgrod pa byang chub sems kyi lam la bgrod//sem su sangs rgyas sems can gnyi su med//des na sems kyi sems la lam bgrod med//sa la sbyang du med ces bstan/
54b5(rnying rgyud): pa ni na/ chos dbyings byang chub sems kyi sa 'di la//sdang bcas pas bslab lung ni ngas ma bas na//sbyangs pas thob par 'dod pa rgyu 'bras can//chos dbyings byang chub sems kyis 'di la//sbyang bcos pas bsgrub lung ni ngas ma bstan//ye shes yul ni mi rtog
54b6(rnying rgyud): zhes bstan pa//yul de ye nas rang byung ye shes te//ye shes la ni ye shes yul ma mchis//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//chos thams cad kyi gzhi ma kun byed nyid yin par zhal gyis bzhes par bstan pa'i le'u ste/ni
54b7(rnying rgyud): brgyad pa 'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des/thams cad pal gnas pa sa 'odi bstan to//kye sems dpa' chen po kun byang rgyal po nga nyid ni//kun gyi byang chub snying por gnas//byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin ni//chos kyi/
56a1(rnying rgyud): dbyings kyi phro brang ste//dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad dang //khams gsum sems can thams cad kun//klong chen sems kyi gnas de na//mi gnas pa ni gcig kyang med//kun gyi gnas dang sa yin te//'du 'bral med par de na
56a2(rnying rgyud): bzhugs//de la sbyang zhing bgrod mi dgos//de la sbyangs te bgrod gyur na//rang gi sems de 'chos pa ste//bcos pa rang sems 'byings mi 'gyur//de ni spyi sa kun gyi gnas//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nga las byung ba'i sku gsum gyis//rgyu 'bras
56a3(rnying rgyud): dga' ba'i 'khor rnams la//sa mdo rnam pa gsum bstan te//bskyed pa'i sa dang spyod pa'i sa//bsgrub pa'i sa dang gsum bstan ste//bskyed pa'i sa ru sems bzhi bskyed//spyod pa'i sa ru sems spyod bcus//bsgrub pa'i sa ru grub 'dod pas//
56a4(rnying rgyud): de ni drang po'i lung du bstan pa'i phyir//yang dag nges par yod par lung ma bstan//yang dag nges par nga yi lung 'di nyon no//byang chub sems la rgyu rkyen med pa'i phyir//bskyed pa'i sa ru byang chub sems mi bskyed//bskyod pa'i sa ru byang
56a5(rnying rgyud): chub sems bskyed na//chos dbyings na mkha' byang chub sems mi shong //dge sdig las la dran pas dbang bsgyur bas//phyi yul chos nyid spangs pas ma spongs te//rang 'byung ye shes mi khegs bzhin du 'gegs//rang 'byung ye/
56a6(rnying rgyud): shes yul la mi rtog bzhin//gzung dang 'dzin pa yod ces 'joms par byed//des na byang chub sems la skyes pa med//spyod pa'i sa ru byang chub bcu spyad pas//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//byang chub sems las g.yos pa med pa
56a7(rnying rgyud): ni/na mkha'i lta bu'i chos nyid snang bar zad//na mkha' la ni g.yos pa rdul tsam med//de bzhin byang chub sems la g.yos pa med//grub pa'i sa ru byang chub grub 'dod pa//kun tu 'od dang ma chags pad+ma'i spyan//rdo rje 'dzin dang stug po bkod pa dang //'khor
56b1(rnying rgyud): lotshogs ches rdo rje 'chang ba dang /rgyu 'bras kun parang bye ba sangs drug byang chub sgrub pa'i sa chu ming btags pa//sems kyi rang ba'i drurgya rkyen bral ba'i phyir/bsgrung pas bru pa'i 'bras bungs ma bstan//sems kyi mang bzhin drug sdang bstan pa yin//sems
56b2(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin drug ni 'di lta ste//rang byung ye shes ma 'gags pa'i rtags//dbang po sgo lnga kun tu gsal ba la//kun tu 'od kyis zhes btags pa yin//yul la ma chog chags pa med pa la//ma chags pad+ma spyan du btags pa yin//skye 'jig med pa'i byang chub sems pas
56b3(rnying rgyud): rdo rje 'dzin pa zhesu nga yis btags//rang gi rang bzhin chos nyid dpag med la//rang byung ye shes mi rtog mnyam par bkod//stug po bkod pa'i sa zhes btags pa yin//byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin dkyil 'khor ste//rang la gnas pa'i byang chub chos rnams la//yi
56b4(rnying rgyud): 'a'akhor lo tshogs chen sa rnams te//sku gsung thugs la skye dang 'jig med pas//rdo rje 'chang gi sa zhes bshad pa yin//byang chub sems lu rgyu 'bras gnyis me de//sems las byung ba'i chos rnams thams cad la//bzang ngan blang ngor ye nas med pa'i phyir//
56b5(rnying rgyud): dbye ba med pa'i sa zhes nga yis bshad//des na byang chub sems kyi chos dbyings la//dus gsum sangs rgyas khams gsum sems can kun//de las 'byung zhing de ru gnas pa'i phyir//chos dbyings byang chub sems ni kun spyod sa zhes gsungs//sangs rgyas bsgrub
56b6(rnying rgyud): pa'i sa zhes btags pa de//byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin de las ni//gong bshad bzhin du byang chub sems la gnas//ces spyad du med pa'i sa bstan to//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//rdzogs pa chen po sa la sbyang du med pa'i le'u ste/zhe dgu pa'o///
56b7(rnying rgyud): /de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//byang chub kyi sems rang 'byung ye shes la sgrib pa med pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/ye shes ye shes zhes bya ba//ye shes ye nas shes pa yin//rang byung ye shes ye nas shes//yul
58a1(rnying rgyud): las rtog pa'i ye shes de//yul las byung bas rang byung min//yul med tsam na gsal mi byed//des na rang byung ye shes de//ye nas shes pa'i ye shes yin//ye shes zhes ni de la gdags//'khor gyi ston pa'i ye shes ni//yul la
58a2(rnying rgyud): btags pa'i ye shes de//gang su yul la rtog byed pa//de ni rig byed yin shes bya//de ni rig byed ye shes yin//rig par bya ba'i yul de ni//byang chub sems las byung ba'i phyir//de ni yul med byang chub sems//ye shes rang las byung ba'i phyir//de la ti
58a3(rnying rgyud): 'dzin bsgom mi byed//ting 'dzin sgom pa mi byed pas//ting 'dzin bag chags sog mi 'gyur//bag chags med pa'i chos nyid//dus gsum sangs rgyas dgongs par bshad//dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//dgongs pa'i yul la mi rtog ste/
58a4(rnying rgyud): mnyam nyid du ye nas gnas//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//rang byung ye shes thugs de la//ye nas rtog pa med pa'i phyir//sangs rgyas dgongs pa mi mdzad//rtog pa'i yul ni kun dang bral//shin tu rnal 'byor
58a5(rnying rgyud): gang zag gang //su yang rung ba de yis ni//rtog pa med la gnas gyur na//de ni sangs rgyas dgongs pa mdzad//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/rang byung ye shes sangs rgyas dgongs pa mdzad//rang byung ye shes yul la mi rtog pas//rnam par rtog pa'i
58a6(rnying rgyud): bag chags ges mi 'gyur//las ni ti Sha 'dzin brtags pa'i bag chags yin//mi rtog pa la bag chags yod ma gsungs//mi rtog pa'i rang byung ye shes lnga//sems kyi rang bzhin dbang po sgo lngar shar//rang byung ye shes de la 'gag pa med//bya
58a7(rnying rgyud): nub sems kyi ngo bo mngon sdang la//rang byung ye shes mi 'gag so sor snang //rang gi rkyen gyis sdang ba'i ngor mi spong //ting 'dzin mi rtog bag chags sog mi 'gyur/'di yang dus gsum sangs rgyas dgongs pa ste//gang du mi rtog 'di la su gnas pa//
58b1(rnying rgyud): maM gdas 'gro ba'i do mi gnas/chos su byang chub sems la 'di gnas te//dusu gsungs sa rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa ngas//kye sems dpa' chen po tshagadon 'ang/rig panyam su mngon sum rtogs par gyis//rang thung me shes yul la thar gnas pa//ya
58b2(rnying rgyud): lnga ni yul 'dod ma yin te//yul 'dod lnga ni ye shes lnga la snang //yul de ye nas ye shes yin pa'i phyir//yul la ye shes gnyisu ngas ma bstan//des na yul yang rang byung ye shes bshad//rang byung ye shes gcig las med pa la//nga las byung ba'i
58b3(rnying rgyud): 'khor gyi ston pa yis//yul la brten nas ye shes ming btags la//rnam pa grangs kyi ye shes byung ba ni//rnam pa btags pa'i ye shes gcig gis ni//yul cig rang bzhin so sor snang ba la//yul can la ni yul gyi ming btags pa//de ni nges pa ma
58b4(rnying rgyud): yin 'khrul pas btags//kun byed nga yi rang byung ye shes min//kun byed nga yi rang bzhin gcig pu la//'khor gyi 'dod pa rnams kyis ming btags pa//la las byang chub semasu ming btags la//la las chos kyi dbyingasu ming yang btags/la
58b5(rnying rgyud): las na mkha'i khamasu ming yang btags ka la las rang byung ye shes ming yang btegs//la las chos kyi sku ru ming yang btags//la las longs spyod rdzogs par ming yang btags//la las sprul pa'i sku ru ming yang btags//lo las sku
58b6(rnying rgyud): gsung thugasu ming yang btags//la las thams cad mkhyen par ming yang btags//la las rnam pa thams cad ming yang btags//la las ye shes bzhi dang gsum du btags//la las ye shes lnga ru ming btags la/ka la las dbyings dang
58b7(rnying rgyud): yeshes bstan btags pa//rang byung byang chub sems ni gcig la btags//ranga'abyung nga la mthong chang smras pa yin//zhes gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po las//rang byung ye shes bstan pa'i lo ru ste/lnga bcu pa'o//
59a1(rnying rgyud): de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//rdzogs pa chen po'i lam la bgod du med pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/kun byed rgyal po nga ni byang chub sems//byang chub sems ni kun gyi lam chen te//
59a2(rnying rgyud): gnas dang yul dang sangs rgyas sa dang gsum//gzhan na mod de 'di na rang bzhin gnas//chos kyi dbyings dang chos nyid 'di ni gnas//na mkha'i khams dang gzhal yas 'di na gnas//sangs rgyas gnas dang yul ni byang chub sems//gzhan du bgrod pa'i
59a3(rnying rgyud): lam dang gnas med//bgrod pas na mkha'i lam la phyin pa med//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/lam bgrod pa yod par lung ston pa//nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//bgrod ba'i lam ni lnga dang gsum bstan te//de la bgrod nas phyin par bstan pa
59a4(rnying rgyud): /nirte/ rgyu las 'bras bu thob par 'dod pa yin//rdzogs chen bgrod pa med dang mi mthun te//tshogs dang sbyor ba bsgom mthong thob pa lngas//tshogs gnyis bsags nas gzung 'dzin gnyis choms te//bsgom pa chos mthong thos pa'i 'bras bu de//bgrod du med pa'i
59a5(rnying rgyud): lam dang ma phrad pas//bde chen rtsol sgrub 'das pa ming mi grag/'bras bu gsang bar bstan pa'i lam gsum ni//de bzhin nyid dang kun du snang la sogs//sku gsum lam zhes goms par bsgom pa des//rang byung chos la rgyu 'bras bsnyen bsgrub bzhis//bgrod
59a6(rnying rgyud): pas rtsol sgrub 'das pa'i sar ma phyin//rang sems spros bral chos kyi dbyings yin pas//spros bral de na sangs rgyas thams cad bzhugs//de bas lam la bgod pa ma byed cig/rang sems ma bsgribs na mkha'i khams bzhin gsal//de na dus gsum sangs
59a7(rnying rgyud): rgyas thams cad bzhugs de bas lam la bgrod par ma byed cig/rang sems ma bcos chos nyid ji bzhin pa//de la dus gsum sangs rgyas lhun gyis grub//de bas lam la bgrod par ma byed cig/gal te ma rig lam la 'grod gyur ni//byin pa'i dus
59b1(rnying rgyud): med rtogs thob dus med//bgrod pas sangs rgyas sa ru phyin pa med//sangs rgyas rang sems rtog pa bral ba yin//bgrod pas rang sems rtog bral de mi byin//lta ba bsgoms pas ti ring yul dang phrang //ting 'dzi de yis rtog bra la mnyam dang bral//ngas
59b2(rnying rgyud): /tshig bslus pas ma thams 'dzin la gnas//ma nyams 'dzin pas rtog meng mnyam dang bral//'phrin las brtsal bas grub pa'i ting 'dzan byed//grub po'i ti bar dzin mi rtog mnyam dang bral//sa la sbyangs pas gnas pa'iri ting 'dzin byed//'byungs pa'i
59b3(rnying rgyud): ting 'dzin rtog med mnyam dang bral//chos nyid brtags pas gsal ba'i ting 'dzin byed//ti 'dzin gsal bas rtog med mnyam dang bral//ye shes gsal bar rtogs pa ri ti 'dzin byed//gsal bar rtogs pa'i ting 'dzin mi rtog gro la//kye sems dpa'
59b4(rnying rgyud): chen po nyon cig/'dod pa'i ting 'dzin ji ltar gsol ba nges//'dod pa med pa bde ba grub pa med//bya bral zin pa'i bde ba chen po de//mi rtog 'dod pa med pa de yis blangs//ting 'dzin mi byed bag chags mi sogs pas//rang byung ye shes las kyi dbang mi
59b5(rnying rgyud): 'gyur//rang byung ye shes chos sku snying po de//brtsal bas mi 'grub rang bzhin gnas las 'grub//grub par bya ba'i don rnams kun dang bral//don zhes bya ba'i ming tsam btags pa yin//yang dag nyid du sangs rgyas ming tsam yin//sangs rgyas ming du 'dog
59b6(rnying rgyud): par byed pa ni//nges pa'i lung med drang ba'i lung du bstan//de las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//chos dang sangs rgyas dge 'dun dkon mchog gsum//yod par ming btags de gnyen sgrub pa ru//nga las 'byung ba'i 'khor ni sems yin la//bde'i skying
59b7(rnying rgyud): da sdug bsngal der spangs nas//'di las ma gtogs pa'i bde ba ni//gzhan med zer nas lha skyong sna tshogs pas//spyad pas grub pa'i lung ring bstan pa yin//bya med kun byeng nga yi lung ma yin//zhes gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po wa/
60a1(rnying rgyud): lam la bgreng du med pa'i le'u ste/nga gcig pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//bya med rdzogs pa 'dod pa med pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po//chos rnams thams cad
60a2(rnying rgyud): grub 'dod pa//'dod pa'i sa ni ma sgrub gcig/'dod par byed pa'i sems de ni//mi len thams cad spong ba yin//de ni rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i phyir//'bras bu 'dod nas rgyu mi sgrub//mi rtog rang bzhin gnas pas grub//'dod pa'i ting 'dzin thams cad spong //'dod
60a3(rnying rgyud): me thams cad len par byed//ting 'dzin las 'dod pa 'dzin//'dod pa de ni yang dag min//yang dag gzung du yod ma yin//byas pa'i las ni thams cad kyis//ma brtsel lhun gyis grub pa bsgrub//ma brtsal lhun gyis grub pa de//byas pas grub pa ma
60a4(rnying rgyud): yin te//ma byas ye nas lhun gyis grub//'dod pa thams cad kun spangs pas//grub par byed pa'i gegasu che//'dod pa'i ting 'dzin bag chags nges//bag chags de ni sems 'dzin yin//'dzin pas bde ba de spong byed//mi 'dzin bde ba kun len pa//
60a5(rnying rgyud): ji bzhin pa'i rang bzhi no//ji bzhin bcos pa med pa de//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs par gcig/bde ba che las ma gtogs pa//'dod dang 'dzin pa ga la yod//mi gnas pa la dmigs pa med//mi rtog pa la ting 'dzin med//med pa don gyi snying po de//'dod
60a6(rnying rgyud): pa med pa thob par 'gyur//gang la 'dzin chags med pa des//ting 'dzin nge 'dzin pa'i mchog/kun 'dzin ye nas grub zin pas//gtang gnag mi rtog grub pa'i rgyal//kye sems dpa' chen po//sna tshogs bsgoms pas gcig mi gsal//gsal ba ting
60a7(rnying rgyud): 'dzin gang byed pa//de la rang ti nga'i dzin yong ma yin//de bzhin nyi du gcig pa la//sangs rgyas sems can gnyis phye//bde dang sdug bsngal gnyis phye nas//bde ba dang du thang lung bstan//sdig pa rgyal du bor lung bstan//ma nges nga yaM lung las 'gal/
60b1(rnying rgyud): kye sems dpa' chen po//gcig la gnyisu ma 'byed cig/bde ba sdug bsngal byang chub gcig/sangs rgyas sems can semasu gcig/snang srid snod bcud chos nyid gcig/bden brdzun gnyis kyang chos nyid mnyam//bde ba mi len sdug ma spong //rang bzhin
60b2(rnying rgyud): gnas pas kun kyang grub//bde la chags pa sdug bsngal yin//kun gsal bde la rtog med pas//de ni rang byung ye shes yin//ye shes bag chags mi sogs pas//las kyis gos par ga la 'gyur//rgyu 'bras ye shes ting 'dzin rtog/rtog pa'i ye shwa
60b3(rnying rgyud): bag chags can//ye shes bag chags dbang bsgyur bas//rtog pa'i ye shes bsgom mi bya//rtog pa'i ye shes bsgom byas pas//mi rtog mnyam dang bral bar 'gyur//ting 'dzin rtsol ba'i nad kyis zin//kye sems dpa' chen po//kun byed nga ni byang chub sem/
60b4(rnying rgyud): thams cad byang chub sems yin la//byang chub sems las thams cad med//sems las sprul pa'i chos ni sna tshogs te//sna tshogs sprul pa ji yang snang ba ni//sems can gzugs dang dbyibs dang kha dog dang //ji ltar snang ba de la dad pa re//dad pa
60b5(rnying rgyud): de yis 'thad pa'i chos ston pa//de ni ye shes thugs rje sprul par bshad//de yang rang bzhin chos las g.yos med par//sangs rgyas rang bzhin chos las g.yos med pas//dus gsum rgyal ba rnams kyi chos nyid yin//chos nyid las ni
60b6(rnying rgyud): sangs rgyas gud na med//sangs rgyas las ni sems can gud na med//sems can mi rtog sangs rgyas dgongs pa yin//byang chub sems ma byung gi bar du ni//sangs rgyas dang ni sems can gnyis ka med//de bas sangs rgyas kun pas sems nyid
60b7(rnying rgyud): bsngags//sems nyid mi 'gyur 'gyur ba ye nas med//chos nyid bsgyur bas g.yos pa rdul med par//gcig pa rim pa dgu dang brgyad khri stong //'byed pa'i ston pas gcig gi don ma rtogs//gcig ma rtogs pas kun don rtogs mi 'gyur/
61a1(rnying rgyud): des na sems gcig don de rang du dag pa//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//ma g.yos 'dod pa med pa'i le'u ste/nga gnyis pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po
61a2(rnying rgyud): /des//chos thams cad g.yos pa med pa 'di gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po nga rtogs shig/brtogs chos rtogs g.yos pa med//kun byeng rgyal po ston pa nga nyid ni//byang chub sems la g.yos pa med//kun byed rgyal po ston pa nga nyid ni//byang chub
61a3(rnying rgyud): sems la g.yos pa med pa de//thams cad byang chub sems las g.yos med zhi//rang byung ye shes rgyu rkyen med pa de//rgyu dang 'bras bu kun las shin tu rgyal//rgya 'bras theg pa can la shin tu gsang //ma skyes rdo rje chos kyi dbyings na rgyal//rgyu 'bras can
61a4(rnying rgyud): la mi smra shin tu gsang //sems nyid ma 'gags na mkha'i dbyings na rgyal//rgyu 'bras can la mi sgra shin tu gsang //snang srid snod bcud ma lus chos nyid rgyal//rgyu 'bras can la mi smra shin tu gsang //nam mkha' rlung chus ma snang ba lnga//byang chub sems//
61a5(rnying rgyud): kyi ngo bor shin tu rgyal//rgyu 'bras can la mi smra shin tu gsang //khams gsum dug gsum thugasu ye shes rgyal//rgyu 'bras can la mi smra shin tu gsang //byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin kun byed la//ma yal ma zhi rang bzhin zhi ba med//chos dbyings
61a6(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin spros med zhi ba da//ma yal ma zhi rang bzhin spros pa med//rang byung ye shes ma bsgribs gsal ba de//mkha' la ma yal ma zhi zhi ba med//snang srid snod bcud chos nyid rang bzhin de//ma yal ma zhi rang bzhin zhi ba med//chos rnams
61a7(rnying rgyud): thams cad ma yal ma zhi ba//rang bzhin gsal te ngo bo g.yos pa med//g.yos med chos la rgyu 'bras sgrub byed pa//de ni nga yi lung min 'dod lung yin//nga ni 'dod pa med pas kun grub te//mi 'dod 'dod pa med pa rang bzhin grub//grub ces bya ba
61b1(rnying rgyud): sa spras pa med//rgyu 'bras grub 'dod rnams kyi tshig du'o//nga ni sgo dang skur pa'i dgra ru Ne//yod dang med dang grub dngam grub dang //spros dang sgyur ba btags pa thams cad kun//mi rtog pas ni mnyam pa nyid du gnas//mnyam pa nyid las ma gtogs
61b2(rnying rgyud): dgongos pa ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi mes 'ongs ma gsus//ma gsungs dgongs pa sangs rgyas kun mi ston//gsungs pa thams cad nga yi ngo bo gsus//ngo bo rang bzhin chos rnams thams cad yin//rang bzhin rnam dag mnyam pa'i rgyal po de//mi 'gyu
61b3(rnying rgyud): /rang bzhin chags pa kun dang bral//gzung ba'i yul med sems kyi gnas kyang med//de la mngon sum thob par su 'dod pa//'dod pa de la chags pa'i gnas yin te//de la mi rtog mnyam pa'i dgongs pa med//chos nyid ye nas rang bzhin gnas pa
61b4(rnying rgyud): la//de brtsal bsam pa 'dod sgrub byed pa ni//rang sems gzhan du tshol bar byed pa yin//de yis chos kyi dbyings dang na mkha'i khams//khams gsum gnas dang srid pa'i chos bshig kyang //rang sems rang bzhin gnas pa de mi rnyed//rang gi sems ni/
61b5(rnying rgyud): namakha'i rang bzhin te//mtha' la thug pa med pas dbyings la bri ba med//rang sems na mkha'i rang bzhin rnyed mo rigs//chos kyi rang bzhin kun kyang de lta ste//ji bzhin pa la kun kyang bcos pa med//chos kyi rol pa ting 'dzin ci byas kyang //ji bzhin pa la/
61b6(rnying rgyud): ngo bor 'gyur ba med//'gyur te lhag pa'i stobs cig cer 'gyur na//ting 'dzin cig gis 'khor ba lhag ma med//sgra yang ji bzhin pa la 'gyur ba meng //rang bzhin che ba'i yul la rang bzhin gnas//rang bzhin ma bcos pa'i ming yin la//che ba ma bcos pa
61b7(rnying rgyud): la che zhes brjod//sangs rgyas rnams kyi sku gsung thugs dang ni//sems can rnams kyi lus ngag yid gsum la//bzang ngan che chung khyad par yod ma gsungs//mi rtog chos nyid mnyam pa nyi du mnyam//mnyam pa nyid la chos dang chos min cig kyang
62a1(rnying rgyud): med//sems can rgyu med sangs rgyas 'bras bu med//rgyu 'bras ston pa ston pa nyid lung nor//de bas nga las byung ba'i chos mnyam pas//nga las byung ba bzang ngan med mnyam gcig/ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed
62a2(rnying rgyud): rgyal po las//chos thams cad rang bzhid g.yas pa med pa'i le'u ste/nga gsum pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po ngas//nyid kyi rang bzhin par byung gi ye shes yon la//rang byung gi ye shes rdo rje
62a3(rnying rgyud): gyi ngo bo lnga dang ldan te//de gang zhe na/don chos thams cad 'byung ba'i snying po dang //don ma nor nges pa'i ngo bo dang //don rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i ngo bo dang //don byar med pa'i ngo bo dang //chos thams cad gtan la 'bebs pa'i ngo bo dang lnga'o//rang byung gi ye
62a4(rnying rgyud): shes de la//bsdus pa'i mdo lung ni lnga//phye ba'i mdo lung ni lnga bcu rtsa bdun po 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po don 'di nyon cig/kun byed rgyal po byang chub sems nga ni//rang byung ye shes bsdus pa lnga las ni//phye bas mdo lung lnga bcu rtsa bdun
62a5(rnying rgyud): te//ngaM ngo bo lnga bcu rtsa bdun yin//de'i ngo bo ji ltar yin zhe na//ye shes ngo bo chos kyi snying po las//mngon du phyung ba'i mdo lung bcu yang bstan//ye shes ma nor nges pa'i ngo bo las//ma nor nges pa'i mang lung bcu yang bstan//ye shes rgyu 'bras 'das
62a6(rnying rgyud): pa'i ngo bo las//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i mdo lung bcu yang bstan//ye shes bya ba med pa'i ngo bo las//bya med rdzogs pa'i mdo lung bcu yang byung //ye shes gtan la 'bebs pa'i ngo bo las//gtan la 'bebs pa'i mdo lung bcu yang byung //sems
62a7(rnying rgyud): nyid ye shes ngo bo rnam lnga las//mdo lung bcu tshan rnam pa lnga byung ni//rang bzhin ngo bo yid ches lnga bstan yin//bdun ni nges par bstan pa'i le 'dun gyis//gcig gis gzhi bstan gcig gis gdams pa bstan//lnga yis so so dgos pa lnga
62b1(rnying rgyud): yang bstan//de'i don gyi bre brag bstan pa ni//dang por chos nyid mthong bar bya ba'i phyir//mngon du phyung ba'i mdo lung bcu yang bstan//yin min yid ches rtog pa bsal ba'i phyir//ma nor nges pa'i mdo lung bcu yang bstan//kun byed rgyal po'i rang bzhin ye shes
62b2(rnying rgyud): deg/rgyu rkyen dag la rag ma las pa'i phyir//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i mdo lung bcu yang bstan//kun byeng rgyal po rang byung ye shes la//bya byed rtsol sgrub bya mi dgos pa'i//bya mengad rdzogs pa'i mdo lung bcu yang bstan//kun byeng rgyal po rang byung ye shes des//
62b3(rnying rgyud): nyid kyi rang bzhin gtan la 'bebs pa'i phyir//gtan la 'bebs pa'i mdo lung bcu yang bstan//de la don gyi dgos pa lnga yod//chos nyid mngon du phyung ba'i mdo lung la//chos nyid mngon du mthong ba'i dgos pa yod//chos nyid ma nor mngon du mthong ba/
62b4(rnying rgyud): des//lta spyod dam tshig lam la bgrong mi dgos//ma nor nges par ston pa'i mdo lung la//ma nor yid ches pa'i dgos pa yod//sems nyid ma nor yid ches mngon pa des//sangs rgyas bsgrub mi dgos pa'i dgos pa yod//rgyu rkyen rab ma las pa'i
62b5(rnying rgyud): /mdo lung la//rtsol sgrub bya mi dgos pa'i yon tan yod//rtsol zhing bsgrub pa bya mi dgos pa des//mi rtsol ba de la gnas pa'i dgos pa yod//bya med rdzogs pa'i mdo lung bstan pa la//ma byas rang bzhin rdzogs pa'i dgos pa yod/ bya me/
62b6(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin rdzogs pa'i dgos pa des//ye nas lhun gyis grub pa'i dgos pa yod//gtan la 'bebs pa'i mdo lung bstan pa las//dus gsum sangs rgyas khams gsum sems can dang //snang sring snod bcud bsdus pa thams cad
62b7(rnying rgyud): kun//rgyu med nges par rang byung bya med par//mdo lung lnga dang mthun par gtan la 'bebs//gtan la 'bebs pa'i mdo rtsa bstan pa ni//dus gsum sangs rgyas dang por lung bstan pa//chos nyid na mkha' lta bu'i rang bzhin
63a1(rnying rgyud): @snga phyi dus gsum lta ba'i sems can rnams//phyi ba dran med snga pa bag chags 'gags//des na sems can na mkha'i chos nyid ma shes mthong //sangs rgyas sangs rgyas zhes bya'i nges tshig ni//chos nyid byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin
63a2(rnying rgyud): pa//spros med dbyings dang dbang po'i spyod yul sangs//na mkha' bzhin du kun la khyab cing rgyas//sangs rgyas ming du btags pa nga yis med//khams zhes bya ba'i gtan tshigs bstan pa ni//mang zhing rgya che ba'i mtshan nyid//khams gsum sems can gcig gi
63a3(rnying rgyud): dbang po yis//dpag gis mi lang bas na kham su btags//yang dag nges par ming du btags pa med//sems zhes bya ba'i nges pa bstan pa ni//rgyu rkyen med pa'i rang byung ye shes te//ma 'gegs kun la dbang po sgo lngar gsal//de la sems zhes bya ba gsal badra
63a4(rnying rgyud): bshad//snang sring snod bcud bsdus pa thams cad ni//skye ba med las cho 'phrul snang ba de//sna tshogs srid pas snang srid zhe su btags//snod dang bcud zhes bya bar bstan pa ni//'byung lnga snod kyi nang na rgyud drug bcud//gnas pas snod bcud zhesu btags pa yin//kun
63a5(rnying rgyud): /zhes bya ba lhag ma ma lus pa//bsdus pas kun byed rgyal po'i rang bzhin lngar//mdo lung lnga bcu rtsa bdun phyung nas bsdus//kun byed rang bzhin lnga ru 'dus par bstan//snying po mngon du 'byin pa'i rang bzhin dang //ma nor nges par ston pa'i rang bzhin dang //rang byung
63a6(rnying rgyud): rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i rang bzhin dang //bya byed rtsol sgrub 'das pa'i rang bzhin dang //mthun par gtan la 'bebs rang bzhin lnga'o//ces gsungs pas//kye ston pa kun byang rgyal po lags//sems dpa' rdo rje bdag gi rig pa yis//kun byed rang byung ye shes gcig
63a7(rnying rgyud): de lan dgos pa phun sum tshogs pa lnga byung ste//rang byung ye shes chos kyi snying po las//sdang zhing srid pa'i chos rnams thams cad kun//mi sdang ba la mngon du 'byin nus pas//chos kyi snying po yin par yid kyang ches//rang byung yau shes gcig gi ngo bo nga/
63b1(rnying rgyud): don dam kun rdzob bden brdzun gnyis med par//ma nor nges pa'i byang chub sems ston pas//be tsom som nyi med par yid kyang ches//rang gyur ye shes gcig gi ngo bo de//rgyu la mi rten rkyen la mi sgrub pas//gzhan la rag ma lus pa'i rang bzhin gyis//rang byung ye shes
63b2(rnying rgyud): yin par yid kyang ches//rang byung ye shes gcig gi ngo bo de//rgyu rkyen med par lhun gyis grub pa'i phyir//'phrin las brtsal zhing bya med pa mi dgos//ma brtsal lhun gyis grub par yid kyang ches//rang byung ye shes gcig gi ngo bo de//rang byung yang shes ye nas ma
63b3(rnying rgyud): bsgribs pas//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad gsal//chos rnams thams cad gtan la 'bebs pa'i phyir//ston pa'i sgron ma yin par yid kyang ches//ces gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//mdo lung thams cad kun byed nyid kyi rang
63b4(rnying rgyud): bzhin du bsdus pa'i le'u ste/nga bzhi pa'o////de nas byang chub kyi sems kun bde rgyal po des//chos thams cad byang chub sems las gzhan med pa'i mdo 'di gsungaso//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/ji ltar snang srid snod bcud bsdus pa dang /
63b5(rnying rgyud): don dang tshig du bsdus pa thams cad kun//byang chub sems yin pa ni 'di lta ste//tshe skyi dbyings dang na mkha'i khams 'di yang //ma spros ma bsgribs pa'i byang chub sems//'di las gzhan yin bdag gis gsungs pa med//na mkha' rlung chus ma snang ba yang //
63b6(rnying rgyud): /byang chub sems kyi ngo bo bstan pa yin//'dod khams gzugs khams gzug so med khams gsum yang //byang chub sems kyi ngo bo gsum sdang yin//de ston pa'i ston pa sku gsum yang //byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin nam gsum yin//sems/
63b7(rnying rgyud): nyid ye shes ma 'gags 'dod khams sdang //sems nyid ngo bo sdang ba gzugs kyi khams//sems kyi rang bzhin ma skyes gzugs med khams//rang bzhin ma skyes spros bral chos kyi sku//sems nyid nyid la nyid spyod longs spyod rdzogs//sems las sems
64a1(rnying rgyud): sbrul sems kyi don mdzad sprul//dmyal ba la sogs 'gro ba ris drug gis//tsha gang bkres skom blun lkugs ltung 'gyur rnams//mi rtog mnyam pas ji bzhin chos nyid grol//rang byung ye shes yul la mi rtog pas//las kyi bag/
64a2(rnying rgyud): chags skyon yon kun dang bral//yon tan mi len skyen rnams mi 'dor bas//bde ba mi sgrub sdug bsngal spang du med//ji bzhin pa la g.yos pa ye nas med//de bzhin bsgyur bas 'gyur pa'i chos kyang med//'dod pas len pa'i chos kyang thob pa med//chos rnams
64a3(rnying rgyud): /rang bzhin 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid ni//sems las rang byung ye shes 'byung ba 'dra//'dra zhes maung gdags rang byung ye shes yin//rang byung ye shes chos nyid yul gcig la//chos gzhan mthong nas ye shes de spong byed//rang byung ye shes de dang phrad mi 'gyur//spong
64a4(rnying rgyud): 'gegs yul can dag la ming mi grag/don dam kun rdzob bden pa rnams gnyis kyis//sbyangs kyang skal pa gsum gyi bar du thogs//ngag gsum spyod dang mtshan med sgor zhugs nas//byang chub yan lag kun gyi sgor zhugs nas//cho ga rdzogs pa'i ting 'dzin rim
64a5(rnying rgyud): pa yis//rang byung ye shes tshe bdun bar du spangs//thabs dang shes rab gnyis med sgor zhugs nas//skye med shes rab thabs kyi bskyod gyur nas//snang la rang bzhin med par rang bsgoms nas//cho ga lnga dang yo byad yan lag dang //dam tshig tshul dang rab tu brtson
64a6(rnying rgyud): gyur na//rigs 'dzin sa ste lo stong drug brgya ru//longs sku 'dod pa'i ting 'dzin yengs med byed//longs sku thob nas rang sems gdod tshol lha//stong phrag gcig na mi rtog mnyam bzod rnyed//kye sems dpa' chen po rdo rje khyod nyid kyang //nga lung ma nor khyod kyis
64a7(rnying rgyud): nga ston cig/kye sems can lha mi rnams ni shes pa rtsing //dbang po yul la yengs pa'i rang bzhin gyis//sems can mi shes gang bstan tshim pa la//ma nor nges pa'i lung chen ma bstan par//rgyu 'bras drang lung don dam kun rdzob bstan//bden brdzun gnyis kyis
64b1(rnying rgyud): shes pa de tshim nas//bskal pa yun rings bden brdzun gnyis la gnas//de bas dang por rang sems ma nor bstan//bstan pas ma nor brtsal med lam phrad nas//da lta nyid nas mi rtsol sa la gnas//brtsal du med pa'i lam dang de phrad 'gyur//ting 'dzin
64b2(rnying rgyud): bsgom mi dgos dang de phrang 'gyur//mi rtsol bde la gnas pa de yis thob//ma nor nges par rang sems de thob pas//ma brtsal lhun gyis grub pa'i yon tan ni//sems bskyed mi dgos lam la bgrod mi dgos//sa la mi sbyong lta ba bsgom mi dgos//sdom
64b3(rnying rgyud): /pa tshul khrims dam du gnyer mi dgos//kye sems dpa' chen po lung ston cig/'jig rten mtshan ma'i chos 'di bsgrub pa bzhin//lus kyi lasu bya byed lha mi bsgom//ngag gi lasu sngags dang tshig mi brjod//yid kyi la su ting 'dzin gsal mi bsgom//zhes
64b4(rnying rgyud): gsungs so//byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal po las//chos thams cad byang chub kyi sems las med pa'i le'u ste/nga lnga pa'o///de nas sems dpa' rdo rje des//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po de chos thams cad kyi ngo
64b5(rnying rgyud): bopo dang byed pa po yin par//nyid kyi 'khor thams cad la tshig bsgrags pa dang //rtog pa phul ba 'di bsgrag so//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byeng rgyal po lags//bdag gis khyod kyi rang bzhin rtogs pa ni//chos rnams thams cad khyod kyi rang bzhin gcig/
64b6(rnying rgyud): chos nyid chos kyi dbyings kyi pho brang ste//kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//na mkha'i khams kyi gzhal yas khang 'di yang //kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//kye skye med chos kyi sku'i skyon yang //kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//
64b7(rnying rgyud): 'dod spyod longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku 'di yang //kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//thugs rje ye shes sprul pa'i sku 'di yang //kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//'dod pa'i khams kyi 'dod chags khams 'di yang //kun byed rgyal po yin/
66a1(rnying rgyud): @#//par rab du grags//gzugs kyi khams kyi nga rgyal khams 'di yang //kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du glegs//gzugs med khams kyi yid kyi lus 'di yang //kun deng rgyal po yin par rab du grags//'das pa'i sangs rgyas nyid la gnas pa yang
66a2(rnying rgyud): kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//da ltar bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas don mdzad kyang //kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//slad kyis byon pa nyid las byung ba yang //kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//rnal 'byor rnam bzhi nyid la lta ba yang //kun
66a3(rnying rgyud): byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//snang srid snod bcud je byung ba lngas bsdus pa//kun byed rgyal po yin par rab du grags//kun byed rgyal po las ni ma gtogs pa//sangs rgyas sems can snang srid snod bcud dang //chosu grags pas bsdus pa thams cad la//
66a4(rnying rgyud): kun byeng rgyal po las ni gzhan mi snang //ces sems dpa' rdo rjes bsgrag so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//sems dpa' rdo rjes kun byed rgyal po'i rang bzhin rtogs pa'i sgo nas bsgrags pa'i le'u ste/lnga bcu pa drug pa'o//
66a5(rnying rgyud): de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//sems dpa' rdo rje la bstan pa gtad pa'i le'u 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' rdo rje 'di zung gcig/khyod ni rang byung ye shes te//khyod ni nga yi snying por byung //nga yi rang bzhin 'di zung gcig/nga yi rang bzhin
66a6(rnying rgyud): ma gsum las//sku gsum ston pa byung ba yang //de'i rang bzhin khyod kyis zung //de'i bstan pa rnam gsum yang //ston pa sme ba'i seng ge gyis//dus gnas 'khor la sogs pa yang //nyid kyis byas par khyod ston cig/kun byed nga yi rang bzhin nam//
66a7(rnying rgyud): kun byed nga yi mtshan bshad bzhin//khyod kyis nga yi rang bzhin zung //nga ni kun gyi rang bzhin yin//sangs rgyas mes po nga yin gyis//sangs rgyas kun gyi mes po gyis//kun byed rgyal po nga nyid ni//sku gsum ston pa'i pha yin gyis//khyod kyis sku gsum
66b1(rnying rgyud): pam gyis//dus gsum sangs rgyas mes po byis//sems can kun gyi yang mes po//smra ba'i seng+ge smra ba'i tshe//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//sems dpa' chen po nyid dang ni//kun byeng rgyal po nga las ni//gnan yin smra bar ma byed cig/sku gsum
66b2(rnying rgyud): bstan pa'i nang nas ni//rgyu dang 'bras bu rnam gnyis las//rgyu las bsgrubs nas 'bras bur 'dod//sems can sangs rgyas ming btags nas//sems kyis sangs rgyas 'bras bu sgrub//'di ni nga yi bstan pa yin//kun bad nga yi rang bzhin no//rgyu dang /
66b3(rnying rgyud): 'bras bu gnyis las 'das//sems can sangs rgyas gnyis med pas//sangs rgyas sems kyis sgrub ma byed//kun byed rgyal po nga nyid ni//sems nyid rang byung ye sheso//zhes gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//bstan pa gtad
66b4(rnying rgyud): pa'i le'u ste//mdo lung lnga bcu rtsa bdun pa'o//rdzog s+ho///bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshalo//dus gsum rgyal ba'i sku gsung thugs kyi bcud//nam mkha'i mtha' mnyam snying po'i don can nyid//kun byang
66b5(rnying rgyud): rgyal po rdzogs chen rgyud 'di la//gsang ba mchog gi mdo lung lnga bcu bdun//dang po gzhi las phye ba'i mtshan nyid ni//mngon du phyung ba'i mdo lung bcu dang gcig/nges par bstan pa'i mdo lung bcu dang gnyis//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i mdo lung bcu dang
66b6(rnying rgyud): gsum//bya med rdzogs pa'i mdo lung bcu dang ba ni//gtan la 'bebs pa'i mdo lung bcu dang lnga'o//dang por mngon par mthong ba'i dgos pa dang //gnyis pa yid ches ston pa'i dgos pa dang //rtsol bsgrub bya mi dgos pa gsum pa ste//ma byas rang
66b7(rnying rgyud): bzhin rdzogs pa bzhi pa yin//gtan la 'bebs pa'i dgos pa nyid dang wa//gzhi dang gdams pas lnga bcu bdun du bstan//dang por kun gzhi lnga bcu bzhi par bcas//mngon du phyung ba'i mad lung bcu pa la//dang pos rgyud 'byung ba'i rgyu bstan
67a1(rnying rgyud): phyir//gleng gzhi mngon du byung ba'i mdo yang bstan//de nyid gsal bar sems la 'dus bstan phyir//phun sum tshogs pa mngon du phyung nas bstan//de nyid chos nyid dang du 'dus pa'i phyir//chos nyid mngon du phyung ba'i le'u
67a2(rnying rgyud): bstan//chos nyid kun byed rtsa ba byang chub sems 'dus pa//rtsa ba kun byed mngon phyung le'u bstan//de nyid chos kun kun 'byung gzhi yin pas//thams cad byung khungs mngon du phyung ba bstan//de dag kun byed nyid las bstan pa'i phyir//kun byed las
67a3(rnying rgyud): byung byung khungs mngon phyung bstan//de nyid phun sum tshogs par rab byung bas//sku gsum phun sum tshogs par phyung nas bstan//de nyid rang sems ston par 'dus pa'i phyir//rang sems ston par sngon du phyung bar bstan//rang sems ston pas gnyis med
67a4(rnying rgyud): de'i phyir//bstan tshigs mngon du phyung ba'i lung yang bstan//de yis rtsa ba semasu gcig rig pas//rtsa ba semasu cig pa mngon phyung bstan//de ltar mngon phyung mdo lung bcu po la//gdams ngag phog pas chos nyid mthong ba'i phyir//gdams ngag phog
67a5(rnying rgyud): pas dgos pa dang po bstan//mngon du phyung ba'i don la des rnyed pas//nges par bstan pa'i mdo lung bcu po la//chos kun kun byed de nyid nges rtogs phyir//mtshan nyid nges par bstan pa'i lung yang bstan//de'i rgyud dang mngon sum thabs rtogs pa//
67a6(rnying rgyud): lung gnyis nges par bstan pa'i lung yang bstan//lung de nges par rtogs par bya ba'i phyir//sems nying ngas par bshad pa'i lung yang bstan//bshang po de nyid yid ches bya ba'i phyir//lung khungs nges par bstan pa'i lung yang bstan//de yis don nyid
67a7(rnying rgyud): bstan la nges 'bebs pas//don 'grel nges par bstan pa'i lung yang bstan//don 'grel bstan phab nges pa'i gdeng rnyed pas//tshig bzhi nges par bstan pa'i lung yang bstan//nges pa sku gsum thug su 'dus pa'i phyir//sku gsung thugasu nges pa'i lung
67b1(rnying rgyud): yang bstan//sku gsum dgongs pa gcig tu gardal ba'i phyir//dgongs pa nges par bstan pa'i lung yang bstan//dgongs 'dus don la ma rtsal rang bzhag phyir//dgongs pa nges par bzhag pa'i lung yang bstan//nges par bstan pa'i don la mi 'da' bas//
67b2(rnying rgyud): sku gsum nges 'chang lung 'di nges par bstan//nges par bstan pa'i lung bcu brjod pas ni//ma nor yid ches nges pa'i dgos yod pas//thams cad byang chub sems las med par bstan//nges par rang byung gcig las me pa'i phyir//rgyu 'bras
67b3(rnying rgyud): gnyis kyi zhen pa bzlog pa'i phyir//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i lung bcu bshad pa ni//nges par rab byung rig pa kun cig pas//ngo bo gcig pas rgyu 'bras 'das par bstan//gcig la rang byung rdzogs par rtogs dka' ba//bskal med gsang ba rgyu 'bras
67b4(rnying rgyud): 'das mi rtogs//ma rtogs de nyid lta spyod gol sgrib pas//gol sgrib rgyu 'bras sgrib pa'i lung yang bstan//de nyid sems nyid la bzla rgyu 'bras 'das//rgyu 'bras gol sgrib skyon yon sel bar bstan//gol sgrib skyon med rdzogs chen sems rtog phyir//rgyu
67b5(rnying rgyud): 'bras 'das pa'i rdzogs chen nges par bstan//rdzogs chen gzhung rtogs ma rtsal lhun grub pas//ma rtsal lhun grub rgyu 'bras 'das par bstan//ma rtsal rtsal bas grub par mi 'gyur phyir//chos nyid rtsal bas mi 'grub 'dres pa bstan//rtsal med rang
67b6(rnying rgyud): bzhin yongs la bkod pa'i phyir//rgyu 'bras kun 'das chos nyid bkod pa bstan//chos nyid byang chub sem su bstan pa'i phyir//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i sems kyi rang bzhin bstan//de nyid ma rtsal lhun grub rgyu 'bras 'das//ma g.yos 'dod pa med pa'i rang
67b7(rnying rgyud): bzhin bstan//rdzogs chen rgyu 'bras 'ongs pa'i lung bcu yis//rtsol bsgrub bya mi dgos pa'i dgos pas//rnam grangs cig tu bsdus te rtsal med bstan//rtsal med rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i rang bzhin la//bya med lhun gyis rdzogs pa'i lung bcu ni//sems kyi
68a1(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin mi gnas yul med pas//bya med rdzogs pa mi gnas yul med bstan//de nyid ma 'dres yongs rdzogs kun khyab pas//mi nub rgyal mtshan pa med rdzogs pa bstan//de nyid ye shes phyag rgya mi 'da' bas//bya med rdzogs pa phyag
68a2(rnying rgyud): rgya 'gyur med bstan//de nub rgyal mtshan bya med rdzogs bstan//de nyid ye shes phyag rgya mi 'dar bas//bya med rdzogs pa phyag rgya 'gyur med bstan//de nyid brtsal bas 'bras bu mi rnyed pas//bya med rdzogs pa rtsal mi rnyed pa bstan//brtsal bral byang chub sems
68a3(rnying rgyud): rnam dag pas//bya mad rdzogs pa rnam dag dbyingasu bstan//rnam dag dbyings la brtsal ba'i nad med pas//bya med rdzogs pa rdo rje tshig drag bsta ni//rtsol bral ji bzhin mkha' ltar gnas pa'i phyir//bya med rdzogs pa na mkha'i dgongs pa bstan//nam mkha'i
68a4(rnying rgyud): dgongs pas dgongs pas kun las 'das//bya med rdzogs pa bsgom du med pa bstan//bsgom med sgom pas grub par mi 'gyur phyir//bya med rdzogs pa bskal med don med bkod//rdzogs chen bskal ldan 'bras bu rang gnas pa//kun byed rtogs bsgrags
68a5(rnying rgyud): bya med rdzogs par bstan//bye med rdzogs pa'i lung bcu bstan pa la//ma byas rang bzhin rdzogs pa'i dgongs yod pas//ma phyed rang bzhin rdzogs par zhal gyis bzhes//ma bcos de nyid gtan la dbab pa'i phyir//gtan la 'bebs pa'i mdo lung bcu
68a6(rnying rgyud): bstan pa//chos kun gzhi ma gtan la dbab pa'i phyir//gzhi ma kun byed gtan la 'bebs par bstan//de nyid dus gsum rgyal ba'i snying po bas//rgyal ba'i snying po gtan la 'bebs par bstan//rgyal ba'i snying po bsam brjod yul las 'das//lha
68a7(rnying rgyud): bsgom med gtan la 'bebs par bstan//lhar med skom bral nyid ni ma yengs pas//bsgom du med par gtan la phab te bstan//bsgom bral bde chen dbyings las mi 'dar bas//dam tshig bsrung du med par gtan la phab//mi 'dara' chos nyid pa
68b1(rnying rgyud): rtsal lhun grub pas//'phrin las rtsal du med par gtan la bag/rtsal med nyid la dus cig 'jug pa'i phyir//sa la 'jug tu med par bstan la pab//ma sbyangs 'od gsal ye shes la ma mchog pas//lam la bgrong du med par gtan la bab//
68b2(rnying rgyud): de phyir rgyu rkyen ma rtsal rang byung bas//rang byung ye shes sgrib med bstan la phab//ye shes ma rtsal rang la lhun grub pas//ma g.yas 'dod med 'bras bur gton la phab//gtan la 'bebs pa'i lung bcu bstan pa la//ma bcos gtan la 'bebs pa'i
68b3(rnying rgyud): dgos pa'i dgos yod pas//bco su med pa'i don gyi dgos pa bstan//de rnams skal ldan don du bstan pa'i phyir//thams rgyud de gtad cing gdams pa bstan//dang pos rgyu bstan gnyis pas ngo bo bstan//tha mas der gnas gdams pa 'bras
68b4(rnying rgyud): bu bstan//dang por lhun grub gzhi'i don nyid las//gdul bya'i dbye ba lnga bcu lnga 'byung bas//mngon du phyung ba'i lho yul thig les btab//don la nges pa'i gtan tshigs nges bskyed pas//nges par bstan pa'i mdo lung bcu ru bstan//des rnyed grub
68b5(rnying rgyud): mtha' rgyu 'bras kun 'das pas//rgyu 'bras 'das pa'i mdo lung bcu ru bstan//de nyid ma byas rang bzhin rdzogs 'jog pas//bya med rdzogs pa bsgom pa'i lung bcu bstan//rang byung bstan la 'bebs pas 'das rdzogs pas//gtan la
68b6(rnying rgyud): 'bebs pa'i 'bras bu bcu bstan to//gzhi lam 'bras bu klong dgu bstan pa bkram//gdam ngag lung gi dngos po bstan la 'bebs//ma yengs gnas pas lhun grub rang la 'char//skal ldan lung bstan rgyal sras sems la thums//kun byed
68b7(rnying rgyud): rgyal po'i skabs sbyor/lnga bcu rtsa bdun bshad pa/rdzogaso///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//rang byung ye shes skye ba med par rtogs nas//cang mi gsung bar bzhug so//de nas sems dpa'/
69a1(rnying rgyud): rdo rje dam pa des//'di skad ces nuso//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//legs par gsungs pa'i mdo dag mthar phyin na//ston pa gsum po nyid kyis ji ltar rtogs//bstan pa gsum po nyid kyis ji ltar ro/
69a2(rnying rgyud): 'khor gnas dadus kyi rang bzhin ji ltar rtogs//nyid kyis ji ltar rtogs pa'i dgongs pa'i don//bdag cag rnams la legs par bstan du gsol//zhe zhus//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byang rgyal pos rtogs pa bshad pa zhes bya ba'i
69a3(rnying rgyud): rgyud phyi ma 'di gsung so///kye sems dpa' rdo rje don 'di legs par nyon//ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po yis//nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//so sor 'dul ba'i don du gang gsungs pa//ston
69a4(rnying rgyud): pa de dang gnas dang 'khor dag dang //bstan pa sdad pa so sor gnas par rtogs//kun byed nga yis rang bzhin rtogs pa min//kye kun byed pa'i rtogs tshul bstan pa ni//nga nyid skye ba med pa'i rang bzhin te//de las byung ba'i chos ni thams cad
69a5(rnying rgyud): kun//rang byung skye ba med pa'i dbyingasu rtogs//kye sems dpa' chen po legs par nyon cig/nga nyid kye ba med par ngas rtogs pas//ston pa gsum yang skye ba med par rtogs//bstan pa gsum yang skye ba med par rtogs//'khor gnas dus kyang skye ba med par
69a6(rnying rgyud): rtogs//'das pa'i sangs rgyas skye ba med par rtogs//da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bzhugs pa yang //skye ba med pa nyid du rab tu rtogs//ma byon slad kyis 'byon pa'i sangs rgyas yang //skye ba med pa nyid du rab tu rtogs//kye ston pa kun byed nga yis rtogs pa
69a7(rnying rgyud): ltar//sems dpa' rdo rje khyod kyis rtogs par gyis//sems dpa' rdo rjes de ltar ma gtogs na//kun byed pa'i rang bzhin rtogs mi 'gyur//nga'i rang bzhin khyod kyis ma gtogs na//snying po'i rang bzhin bcu dag rtogs mi 'gyur//rang bzhin bcur don la rmongs
69b1(rnying rgyud): pa yis//bsgom du med pa'i lung bcu ji ltar rtogs//kye nga yis chos rnams ji ltar bstan pa ltar/sems dpa' rdo rje khyed kyis rtogs par byos//nga yi rang bzhin skye med rtogs pa ltar//kun gyi rang bzhin skye med rtogs par gyis//ston pa gsum yang skye ba med par
69b2(rnying rgyud): rtogs//bstan pa gsum yang skye ba med par rtogs//'khor gnas dus kyang skye ba med par rtogs//dus gsum sangs rgyas skye ba med par rtogs//rgyud drug sems can skye ba med par rtogs//sa chu me rlung namakha' lnga po yang //skye ba med pa'i ngang du rab tu rtogs
69b3(rnying rgyud): khams gsum srid gsum skye ba med par rtogs//'dod lnga yul lnga skye ba med par rtogs//des na chos kun byang chub sems nyid//byang chub sems ni kun byed rgyal po nyid//kun byed rgyal po skye ba med par rtogs//chos kun rtsa ba kun byed de nyid//nga nyid ma
69b4(rnying rgyud): skyes dbyingasu rtogs pas ni//kun kyang skye med dbyingasu rtogs par 'gyur//zhes gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//rtogs pa gleng gzhi'i le'u ste/nga brgyad pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//
69b5(rnying rgyud): lta ba bsgom du med par rtogs pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' rdo rje legs par rtogs shig/chos rnams kun gyi rtsa ba kun byed la lha dang lta bar byed pa yod ma yin//gang la ltar med snying po gang rtogs pa//kun byeng nga yis rang bzhin rtogs pa yin//
69b6(rnying rgyud): ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams ma lus kun//kun byed skye ba med par kun gnas pas//su zhig snying po don la rtogs 'dod pas//kun byed nga yis rang bzhin rtogs par gyis//kye kun byed nga yis rang bzhin ma rtogs par//so sor bstan pa'i lung dang yid 'thad cing //
