69b7(rnying rgyud): sku gsum ston pa'i don la gang 'jug pa//kun byed pa'i don dang shin tu 'gal//kye ston pa kun byed pa nyid ma gtogs par//lta ba'i rang bzhin kun mkhyen skye med 'di//'das dang ma 'ongs da ltar sangs rgyas kyi//ma rtogs mi rtogs par lung mi ston/ma/
70a1(rnying rgyud): @rtogs mi rtogs par lung mi ston//ma skyes skye ba med pa'i sangs rgyas kyang //kun byed nga'i lung dang phrad nas ni//des kyang lta ba skye med rab tu rtogs//sangs rgyas kun gyi ngo bor byin gyis brlabs//kye kun byed rgyal po ston pa'i
70a2(rnying rgyud): lung med na//dus gsum sangs rgyas khams gsum sems can kyang //ma skyes lam la lta ba'i rnal 'byor dang //la dgu lung dgu rgal ba'i sa bcu yis//rang rang dngos po'i don la rtog dpyod pas//ma skyes snying po'i don la sgrib pa'i phyir//rgyu 'bras
70a3(rnying rgyud): bral ba'i byang chub snying po 'di//kun gyi lta ba'i don rtogs pa med//kye de bas ston pa kun byed nga yis kyang //'khor gyi dang po sems dpa' rdo rje la//sku gsum ston pa gsum gyis bshad pa'i lung //de la ma gnas shig par nga lung ston//de ltar nges pa'i
70a4(rnying rgyud): lung 'di ma bstan na//dus gsum rgyal ba kun gyi yang me so//kun byed rgyal po nga'i gdung chad nas//kun kyang 'khor ba'i lam la gzhol bar 'gyur//kye de phyir sems dpa' rdo rje khyod kyis kyang ke/lha dang mi lha yul las 'das pa la//gang lhas don la
70a5(rnying rgyud): rnam rtog 'tshed pa'i phyir//rgyu 'bras can gyi lta ba khyod ma rtogs//shin tu rnal 'byor mi gnas theg pa la//ltas shing brtags pas mthong ba'i don 'gags pas//ma bcos snying po kun byed rang bzhin la//lhas pas mthong ba med pa'i don du rtogs//kye kun
70a6(rnying rgyud): byed rgyal po byang chub sems nga ni//chos rnams kun gyi lta ba'i me long ste//gsal la rang bzhin med par kun 'char bas//ma bltas rang bzhin gsal bar lung bstan to//ma skyes de bzhin nyid kyi yul nyid la//lta ba'i kun med mthong ba chen por rtog/
70a7(rnying rgyud): zhes gsungs//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//skye ba med pa'i le'u ste/nga 'du b.ro///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal pos//ngam tshig bsrung du med par rtogs pa 'ang gsung so//kye ston pa'i stong
70b1(rnying rgyud): pa kun byed rgyal po yis//nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//dbang po'i khyad par rim par lung bstan pa//kun byed bsrung du yod pa'i don du rtogs//la las sdom dang 'dul khrims dag par rtogs//la las rig 'dzin sdom pa dag par rtogs//la las grangs
70b2(rnying rgyud): dang rtsa ba yan lag rtogs//ji ltar brtags kyang rtogs par lung ma bstan//kun byed rgyal po nga yi dam tshig 'di//bsrung dang mi bsrung phyogs ris med par bstan//gang gi bsrung dang mi bsrung med rtogs pa//kun byed pa'i dam tshig rtogs pa yin/kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed
70b3(rnying rgyud): /rgyal po nga//rtsa ba byang chub semasu kun gcig pas//kun kyang snying po'i dam tshig yin pas na//bsrung dang mi bsrung don las 'das par rtogs//kye kun byed rgyal po lta ba'i dam tshig ni//skye med na mkha' lta bur phyogs 'das pas//gang gis de dag legs par rtogs
70b4(rnying rgyud): pa de/ kun byed rgyal po'i dam tshig rtogs pa yin//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po ngas//dam tshig bsrung du med par lung bstan pa//tshig du rtsa ba'i rgyud las gang thos pa//'dis ni don de legs par rtogs pa yin//kun byed rgyal po lnga par lung bstan ltar//
70b5(rnying rgyud): dus gsum chos nyid ra gyur pa med pa'i phyir//kun byed rgyal po nga'i dam tshig ni//dus gsum kun tu bsrung du med par ston//kye chos rnams rtsa ba sems su gcig pa ltar//dam tshig rtsa ba bsrung du med par gcig/de yang rang sems skye med rtogs pa yin//phyal ba
70b6(rnying rgyud): dang ni med pa gcig pu dang //lhun gyis grub par bsrung ba kun las 'das//kun byed rgyal po ri dam tshig rnam grangs te//skye ba med par rtogs pa'i rnam grangs yin//snying po bsrung du meng bar don gcig la//rtogs dang ma rtogs khyad par la dgongs nas//drang
70b7(rnying rgyud): dang nges pa gnyis rnam phye nas//bsrung du yod dang med par lung bstan te//kye 'dis ni dam tshig bsrung med rtogs pa'i phyir//dus gsum rgyal ba'i dgongs pa rtogs pa yin//gang gis 'di ma rtogs pa'i rnal 'byor pa//rang sems snying po'i don dang shin
71a1(rnying rgyud): @#tu 'gal//kye kun byed nga'i dam las gang 'das shing //sku gsum ston pa'i dam las gnas pa dag/bskal pa brgya phrag du ma rnamasu yang //snying po'i don dang 'phrad pa shin tu dka'//de phyir kun byed nga'i lung thob pas//dam tshig bsrung du med
71a2(rnying rgyud): par rtogs par byos//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//bsrang med dam tshig gi le'u ste/drug cu pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po des//'phrin las rtsal du med pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa'
71a3(rnying rgyud): rdo rje legs par da rtogs shig/stor ba'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po b.ri//rang bzhin las byung ston pa sku gsum gyis//so sos ji ltar 'dul ba'i skur bung nas//'brin las brtsal bas don dang phrad par rtogs//lus kyis la suM phyag rgya bca' bar rtogs//ngag gis
71a4(rnying rgyud): sngags dang bzlas pa byed par rtogs//sems kyis tingange 'dzin 'phro 'du bsgom par rtogs//sgo gsum yal bar gnas pas grub mi rtogs//kye ston pa kun byed nga'i 'phrin las ni//brtsal bar bya med 'phrin las lung bstan pas//don dang mi 'gal bsam pa//
71a5(rnying rgyud): mthar byung bas//gzhan don dbyingasu gcig pas rtsa la med rtogs//de ltar khyed kyis nges par rtogs par gyis//kye kun bde bang chub semasu kun byas pas//byang chub sems la bya mi dgos par rtogs//ma byas pa bral snying po byang chub la//ba dang mi bya tha
71a6(rnying rgyud): snyad gang gis gdags//kye kun byed rgyal po nga'i 'phrin las ni//lus kyis lasu phyag rgya bcar med rtogs//ngag gis bzlas brjod bya med snying por rtogs//sems kyis 'phro 'du bsgom dang bral bar rtogs//'das dang ma byon da ltar bzhugs pa yang //
71a7(rnying rgyud): 'phrin las bya med rtogs nas mngon sangs rgyas//dus gsum rgyal ba rtogs la rag lus pas//sems dpa' rdo rjes thos nas rtogs par gyis//kye nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//zhi rgyas dbang dang drag pos lung bstan pa//rnam rtog mtshan
71b1(rnying rgyud): blangs la dkar ba la//'dus pa'i rtogs pa grang su bstan pa yis//kye dpyir ku bye dang yis lung bstan pa//thos pa'i do la ma nong brtag par byos//byum la kun dang bral ba'i 'dzin las kyis//ma byas dod kur rdzogs pa rtogs par 'ar /kye sems dpa' ngo/
71b2(rnying rgyud): rje don 'di legs par rtogs//snying po bya ma la bral ba'i don rtogs pas//bya dang mi bya med par don rdzogs pas//kun byed rgyal po'i 'phrin las 'di rtogs shig/kye ston pa'i rgyal po kun byed nga yis ni//byas pa thams cad mtshan ma'i lam du bstan//bya dral
71b3(rnying rgyud): 'phrin las nges don 'di rtogs pas//'gro ba kun byed rgyal po nyi du 'gyur//snying po bya bral sgo gsum ma nor bas//bya rtsal 'das pa'i don la rtogs pa ni//dus gsum rgyal ba'i dgongs pa gcig pa'i phyir//ma nor lam la rtogs pa nyid mchog yin//ces gsungaso///
71b4(rnying rgyud): byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//sgo gsum bya rtsal med par rtogs pa'i le'u ste/drug cu gcig pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//dkyil 'khor bskyed du med par rtogs pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' rdo
71b5(rnying rgyud): rje 'di rtogs shig/nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//mtshan ma grangs la dga' ba'i gdul bya la//tshogs dang dkyil 'khor bskyed du yod par bstan//gang zhig sku gsum lam la rten byas nas//mtshan ma grangs kyi sgo nas rtogs byed pa//bskal pa
71b6(rnying rgyud): /bye ba brgya phrag yun ring su//dkyil 'khor skyed du med pa'i don mi rtogs//kye de bas ston pa kun byed rgyal po ngas//tshogs dang dkyil 'khor rang bzhin lhun rdzogs pas//mau byas rang bzhin grub po'i chos nyid la//skyed cing bsag tu med pa'i don rtogs na//kye
71b7(rnying rgyud): kun byed pa'i dkyil 'khor bstan pa ni//rang las byung ba'i dkyil 'khor rdzogs pa'i lung //ma lus snying po rdzogs par skye 'bri med//ma bskyed dus cig rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor rtogs//kye dkyil ni ma nor don gyi snying po la//'khor ni 'khor 'das
72a1(rnying rgyud): @bde chen rdzogs par ldan//de ni dkyil snying po kun gyi rdza//dkyil 'khor ma lus 'di la 'dus par rtogs//kye kun byed rgyal po nga'i dkyil 'khor ni//ma lus snying por rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor te//gang gis de dag rdzogs par gang rtogs pa//
72a2(rnying rgyud): dkyil 'khor bskyed pa med pa'i don la mkhas//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po ngas//skye med byang chub sems kyi dkyil 'khor gyis//bgro dang 'od med kun gyi khyab par gdal//rtogs pas skye ba med pa'i don la 'jug/kye rang byung ye shes lnga'i
72a3(rnying rgyud): dkyil 'khor ni//rtogs dang ma rtogs don la rag lus pas//bskyed dang mi skyed gnyi sasu mthong ba yin//dam pa rtogs na bskyed dang mi skyed med//do byar sems dpa' rdo rje khyed kyis kyang //sgra la ma gnas don nyid rtogs par gyis//gang gis de la rtogs pa skyes
72a4(rnying rgyud): /ma thag/kun byed rgyal po don la 'byor par 'gyur//kye rgyal ba ma lus 'dus pa'i snying po ste//rang bzhin khyab par gnas pa'i dkyil 'khor 'di//ma skyod rang bzhin gnas pa'i don 'di la//rtogs pas ci bder gnos la ma rtsol cig/kye rgyal po kun gyi rang bzhin kun
72a5(rnying rgyud): byed des//dkyil 'khor bskyed du med par don rtogs nas//de'i rang bzhin sgra thos ma yin par//sems dpa' rdo rje khyod kyis rtogs par gyis//kun byedang rgyal pos chags sdang rnam pa lnga//skye med byang chub sems gsum bstan pa ltar//sems dpa' rdo rje khyod
72a6(rnying rgyud): kyis rtog nas kyang //de bzhin 'khor la khyod kyis lung ston cig/ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//dkyil 'khor bskyed du med par rtogs pa'i le'u ste//drug cu gnyis po'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun
72a7(rnying rgyud): byeng rgyal pos//dbang la bskur du med par rtogs pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' rdo rje don 'di nges par rtogs shig/nga ni ston pa kun byed rgyal po ste//nga las byung ba'i stor ba sku gsum gyis//dbang la bskur thob yod par lung phog pa//mtshan ma'i rtog
72b1(rnying rgyud): ba'i lam la zhen chags pas//pi'iri rang bzhin mi mthong bskal par 'gyur//kye nga ni ston pa kun byed rgyal po ste//nga'i rang bzhin kun byed khyab rtogs pas//nga la gnyis pa'i don ni med pa'i phyir//bskur dang mi bskur don dang bral bar rtogs//gang zag mtshan ma grangs
72b2(rnying rgyud): la dga' bas la//dbang gi grangs dang bskur ba'i rim pa bstan//ma bcos don la gnas pa'i gang zag la//ma bskur rang bzhin gnas pa'i rim par bstan//kye kun byed rgyal po'i rang bzhin kun khyab pa//de lhor 'jug cing rtogs pa'i rnal 'byor pa//ma bskur
72b3(rnying rgyud): bzhin gnas pa'i don rtogs pas//dbang bskur thob med par lung ston cig/kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po ngas//bskur thob med par rtogs nas lung ston pa//skye med rnam dag sems kyi dbang thob nas//rang bzhin bcu'i dbang la rtsol mi 'gyur//sngags dang
72b4(rnying rgyud): phyag rgya'i dbang la re mi 'jog//rdzas dang mtshan ma'i dbang la sems mi chags//rang rgyud dag pas mtshan ma'i lha mi sgom//gza' dang rgyu skar dus tshigs rim pa med//kye ston pa'i rgyal po nga yis rtogs pa ltar//sku gsum ston pa gsum gyis ma rtogs pas//rang bzhis
72b5(rnying rgyud): bcu'i don la sgrib pa'i phyir//kun byed nga'i rang bzhin rtogs pa med//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po ngas//'khor gyi dang po sku gsum ston pa la//rang bzhin ma bcos ji bzhin lung phog pa//mtshan ma'i 'jig rten lha la rten mi 'cha'//tshogs la mi rten dbang la
72b6(rnying rgyud): rag mi lus//skye med rig pa'i dbang la kun rdzogs pas//ma rtsal bde bar bzhag pas don kun rdzogs//kye nga ni ston pa kun byed rgyal po ste//sku gsum ston po nga yis bkod pa yin//des ston chos kyang nga yis bkod pa yin//rang nyid rang la bgrod par bya mi dgos//
72b7(rnying rgyud): kye de ltar kun byeng rang bzhin gang rtogs pas//thams cad kun byed pang bzhin de rtogs pas//kun byed nga yis rang bzhin rtogs kyis la//mtshan 'dzin nan pa'i dbang la ma rten cig/ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//rang byung rtogs pa/
73a1(rnying rgyud): @/dbang gi le'u ste//drug cu gsum pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal pos//lam la bgrong du med par rtogs pa 'di gsung so//kye ston pa'i sten pa kun byed rgyal po yis//rang bzhin ma skyes
73a2(rnying rgyud): gcig tu gnas pa la//skye med mngon du rtogs par dka' ba'i phyir//'khor gyi dang po ston pa sku gsum gyis//rang rang dga' ba'i lam la gzud pa'i phyir//lam gyi khyad par bgrod du yod par bstan//blo chung rgyud nas don la 'dren pa'i phyir//drang ba'i lung du
73a3(rnying rgyud): nga yis rtogs pa yin//kye tshogs dang sbyor lam mthong lam bsgom pa'i lam//mthar phyin rim pa bskal pa brgya phrag dang //tshe gsum bdun stegs dus der grub par rtogs//de yis nga yi lam 'di rtogs mi 'gyur//kye kun byed rgyal po nga yis lam bshad pas//
73a4(rnying rgyud): nga nyid skyo ba med pa'i rang bzhin//'jug dang mi 'jug tshig las shin tu 'das//de phyir lam la bgrod du med par rtogs//lam lngar snang ba rnam rtog mtshan ma'i grangs//nga nying mtshan ma'i yul las 'das pa'i phyir//mtshan ma'i lam gyis bgrod du med par
73a5(rnying rgyud): rtogs//kye kun byed nga yi lam la rim med te//skye med thig le chen por gcig pa'i phyir//gcig gi rang bzhin rtogs pa de'i phyir//lam la bgrod med gcig du rtogs pa yin//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po nga //nga la 'jug pa'i rim pa'i lam med//
73a6(rnying rgyud): rang byung ye shes dus gcig rdzogs pa'i phyir//ma bgrod rang bzhin bzhag pas phyin par 'gyur//de phyir sems dpa' rdo rje khyod nyid kyis//nga la rim pa bgrod par ma byed cig/kye nga ni mtha' bral na mkha'i rang bzhin te//nam mkha'i mtha' la bgrod cing chi chad ltar//
73a7(rnying rgyud): pa'i rang bzhin lam gyis sgro 'dong ba//tshe tshad nang du de las che ba med//de phyir sems dpa' rdo rjes rtogs nas kyang //khyod kyi 'khor gyi mang du lung ston cig/de ltar ba'i rang bzhin lung bstan pas//kun kyang ba'i rang bzhin nyid rtogs 'gyur//'jig rten mtshan
73b1(rnying rgyud): ma'i lam la zhen mi 'gyur//rnam grangs tshig gi lam la rtsod mi 'gyur//ma bcos bder bzhag rtogs pas pha rol phyin//kun gyis snying po'i lam dang phrad par 'gyur//kye nga ni kun byed chos kyi gzhi ma ste//lam lnga sa bcur ston pa'i sku gsum yang //nga'i rang bzhin
73b2(rnying rgyud): mngon du bstan pa yin//de ltar khyod kyis legs par rtogs par gyis//ma bcos bgrod med kun byed kun gyi lam//su zhig bgrod pas byang chub don mi rtogs//kun byed nga yi rang bzhin rtogs mi 'gyur//de bas bgredang du med pa'i don la rtogs//ces gsung so//byang
73b3(rnying rgyud): /chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//lam la bgrod du med par rtogs pa'i le'u ste//drug cu bzhi pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//sa la sbyang du med par rtogs pa 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po don
73b4(rnying rgyud): 'di legs par rtogs//ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po yis//sa la sbyang du med par bstan pa ni//kun gyi yul min brtag par rab tu dka'//kye nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//sman pa'i rim par bgrod cing sbyang ba'i phyir//rnam rtog grangs la dga'
73b5(rnying rgyud): ba'i gang zag la//sa'i rnam grangs bcu dang drug tu bstan//bstan pa de bzhin 'khor gyis de ltar rtogs//rnam rtog blo'i grangs la snang bar zad//kye sa drug grangs kyi rang bzhin bstan pa ni//kun tu 'od dang rdo rje 'dzin pa dang //rdo rje 'chang dang stug po bkod pa dang //pad+ma
73b6(rnying rgyud): spyan dang rdo rje sems dpa' yis//rim grong rnal 'byor don du gsungs pa de//lha dang ting 'dzin khyad par re re gsungs//kye ston pa kun byed rgyal pos sa bstan pa//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams ma lus pa//skye med ches kyi dbying su gcig pa'i phyir//sa'i khyad par/
73b7(rnying rgyud): mimomaung de gcig tu rtogs//kun byed nga yis la ma sbyar na//rtogs pa'i sa'i rnam grangs kun thob kyang //so so rang gi rnam rtog snang ba la//kun byed nga dang phrad par lung mi ston//kye kun byed rgyal po nga'i rang bzhin ni//ma btsos ma sbyangs rig pa lhun grub sa//
74a1(rnying rgyud): smras shing bgrong kyang don da ma 'bradrang ky-i//rang bzhin rtongs pa dus gcib rdzogs par byed//bye theg gcis kun byed byang chub sems kyis//skye med khyab pas rtogs pa'i rang bzhin ni//gang myur dgrong ba nam pa'i mtha' rol 'dra/sa
74a2(rnying rgyud): bgrod rang bzhin bzhag pas grol bar ston//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po //sa'i ngo bor nga nyid shar ba'i phyir//nga'i sa la mtho dman rim po mod//kun byed nga'i sa 'di rtogs par gyis//nga nyid kun gyi mdun du mngon phyung yang //sku gsum 'khor gyi rnam rtog grangs
74a3(rnying rgyud): su rtogs//kun byed rgyal po'i dgongs pa zab mo yang //gang ltar ltos pas mthong bar lung mi ston//kye nga'i 'khor gyis dang po sku gsum gyis//rang rang mos pa'i 'khor la chos bstan pa//sku gsum thugs dang theg pa re re dang //sa re lam re 'bras bu re re
74a4(rnying rgyud): bstan//kun byed nga'i sa las ma rtogs na//rnam grangs sa des nges pa'i lam mi rnyed//kye ba'i 'khor gyi dang po sku gsum gyis//rang ba mos pa'i 'khor la chos bstan pa//sku gsung thugs dang theg pa re rI dang //sa re lam re 'bras bu re re bstan//kun byed pa 'i sa la/
74a5(rnying rgyud): ma rtogs na//rnam grangs sa nges nges pa'i lam mi rnyed//des na sems nyid kun theg sems kyis//rtsa ba'i sa la bstan pas rtogs par gyis//sa'i rang bzhin snang med 'di rtogs na//kun byed byang chub sems kyi sa la gnas//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun
74a6(rnying rgyud): byed rgyal po las//sa la sbyang du med pa'i rtogs pa'i le'u ste/drug cu lnga pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//spyod pa blang dor med par rtogs pa 'di gsung so//kye nga ni ston pa kun byed rgyal po ste//nga las byung ba'i ston po sku
74a7(rnying rgyud): gsum gyis//nga las dge dpyad sdig pa dor bar rtogs//la las gtsang rma blang dor gnyisu rtogs//la las gtsang rmi gnyis ka dpyad par rtogs//dpyad dang ma dpyad med pa'i don ma rtogs//gang dag spyod pa 'di la gnas pa ni//rang rang gnyen po'i rtog pa byang ba yis//dmigs
74b1(rnying rgyud): ba lings smras brjonas pa phyag ma ma pa'i byang phyogs pa phrul//thegs 'i spyo smra na/mas ThA skye rlung ste/ rgyu ngas su byug pas//thag sa nga yang ba nga mas/s+yA nga sa mam me pa tshu/
74b2(rnying rgyud): dbyang dang mi drod dganyi su med par rtogs//mtha' dang dbusu 'byed pa med par rtogs//ba'i tshe skye meng byang chub sem su chags//kye kun byeng rgyal po'i srung ba bsnyen po ni//gang phyir dbyung ba de ltar mi 'gal haM//byas dang ma byas gnyis ka skye med par//shes pas gang ltarang dpyad kyang /
74b3(rnying rgyud): med don/gya kun byed rgyal po ngar spyod pa la//mapar ltar gnas pas grangs lhang chad bzungs medado//gan su med pa yin min phyogs las 'das//kun byed byang chub sems kyi spyod par bstan//'dod pa lnga yang sems kyi spyod par rtogs//chags sdang lnga yang sems kyi skyeng pa
74b4(rnying rgyud): rtogs//rgyan gyi rgyu lnga sems kyi spyod par rtogs//khams gsum srid gsum sems kyi spyod par rtogs//skye med ma rtogs pa'i spyod pa ni//rgyal ba kun gyi mes po ngas ma bshad//kye de phyir sems dpa' rdo rje khyod kyis ni//kun byed pa'i spyod pa 'di rtogs pa//blang dor med pa kun gyi
74b5(rnying rgyud): rtogs pa'i phyir//nga'i 'khor gyi nang du lung ston cig/kun byed nga'i spyod pa ma gtogs par//sku gsum ston pa'i 'khor la gang zhen pas//'jig rten log pa'i lha spyod la 'chel nas//blangs dor med pa'i spyod pa rtogs mi byed//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po yis//'khor
74b6(rnying rgyud): gyi nang du spong ba'i lung bstan pa//ma tshogs blang dor med po'i 'khor waM gang zhen pas//'jig rten lag pa'i lha spyad la 'chal//spyod pa yis//thams cad skye med mnyam par rtogs lung bstan//sems dpa' rdo rje 'khor lo lung bstan pa//'khor kyang blang dor med par rtogs par
74b7(rnying rgyud): gyu 'i nga bya ba 'ad mas ma 'di/skye meng nganag pa ya zhin lhag nga gas pa de lta ba khyo ma lam canang du sung gyi cag sa ga nga pa pa la nya /khyos pas su bye byed pa gas par gyur sa pargya'o rgya
75a1(rnying rgyud): kyisems su byed rgyal po las/spyong ba blang ngo med pa rtogs pa'i le'u rtse/b+hu zhu drug par//denas bya chub kyi sems su byed rgyal zes/ye shes ba ba sgrib pa med par rtogs pa 'di gsung so// tsun pa'i skyon pa dun byed rgyam po
75a2(rnying rgyud): ngas//ji ltar 'dul ba'i thab su bstan pa'i phyir//nga las byung bar stong pa sku gsum gyis//sgrib pa bas pa zhing ye shes bsgrub par rtogs//sku gsum ston pas 'khor la de ltar bstan//'khor gyis go zhing rtogs pas de bzhin te//rang rang mos pa'i don la zhen pa'i phyir//kun byed pa'i
75a3(rnying rgyud): 'khor du 'du mi byed//kye kun byed rgyal pos 'khor la lung phog pa//ji ltar snang grags gnas pa'i chos rnams kun//ma skyes de bzhin nyid du kun gcig phyir//sgrib dang mi sgrib chos la gnas mi byed//de phyir ye shes sgrib par ngas mi byed//sgrib dang mi sgrib cho suM ris mi lho//mthar
75a4(rnying rgyud): thug med pa'i don la grangs mi grang //rgyu dang 'bras bu snying po'i don gyis skyed//kye ston pa kun byed rgyal po nga'i bkar//sku gsum ston pa'i lung dang mi 'thun pas//skye med cig la mtshan ma'i grangs mi bgrang //yul bshes mi lha gnyen po'i tshogs mi rten//rig dang ma rig skye med
75a5(rnying rgyud): dbyings cig pas//gcig la gnyis 'dzin log pa'i grangs mi 'debs//rnam par mi rtog 'du shes nad mi 'jog/res 'jog ma yin dus cig kun mkhyen pa//de ltar sems dpa' rdo rjes rtogs par gyis//kye ston pa kun byed pari ye shes nyid//bgrang du med la bgrangs na mtha' las 'das//
75a6(rnying rgyud): mob pa la ye shes bras bstan pa brjod papa de ma rdzogs nga sa to/un byed rgyam pos pa sa /mamapa mdza sa ban sus/yang dru medapa par sa gas pa ste//sgru ba med ba rnyen pabyung ba yin/dargyu sags gtsug la phrul gsong
75a7(rnying rgyud): rnam pa'i khyad par ma bcos so sor gas pa/de ltar gang gis ri gcing rtogs pa nyid//kun byed rgyal po'i srasu lung bstan to//nga yis ye shes rtogs pa ji lta bar/sems dpa' rdo rjes de ltar rtogs par gyis//kyai ba ni la shes kun byed rgyal po ste/ma rig pa lta ba sel bar byed/
75b1(rnying rgyud): sgrib pa'i dra ba ma lus bcod byed cing //ye shes snang ba rgyas pa 'tshed par byed//kun byed nga ni rang byung ye she ste//nga las ma byung rang byung ye shes gcig med pas//ye shes ma byung nga yis bkod pas na//nga ni ye shes kun byed rgyal po zhesu bshad//nyon mongs ye shes gsal
75b2(rnying rgyud): bar mched 'dod na//kun byed pa'i lung 'di rtogs par gyis//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//ye shes sgrib pa med par rtogs pa'i le'u ste/drug cu bdun pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byeng rgyal pos//lhun grub rtsal
75b3(rnying rgyud): dusnang bar rtogs pa 'di gas so//kye ba ston pa kun byeng rgyal ba ste//ngani lhas myu rnal du med pa yin/ nga banang ngas bstar par bya//khyod ces pa dod la lags rloms shes me ni ston pa ku byed rgyal po ste//ngalas byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum gyis//nga ma da/
75b4(rnying rgyud): shin tu rtogs dka' bas//so so'i thabs kyis rtsal du yod par bstan//de yang ji ltar nges par bstan zhe na/la la bden pa bzhi'i lam zhugs nas//nyon mongs ye shes spong len rtsol ba ltar//la la rten 'brel lam la zhen pa yis//nyon mongs ye shes bgag bsgrub rtsol bar
75b5(rnying rgyud): bstan//la la bde gnyis lam la 'jug byas nas//sbyang ninang rtsol bas 'bras bu thob par bstan//la la bzung 'dzin bya ba'i stsol ba yis//grub pa lus gsal tshul du gnas par 'dod //la la las dang ting 'dzin rtsol ba yis//glang ngor sgonas 'bras bu thob par
75b6(rnying rgyud): 'dod /ka la la ting 'dzin rnam gsum rtsol ba yis//blang de byas pas 'bras bu thob par 'dong //la la ting 'dzin 'phro 'dra'i rtsol sgrub gyis//blang dong med pa'i 'bras bu thob par 'dod //la la rgyu 'bras ting 'dzin rtsol ba yis//sku gsum rdo rje sems dpa'i 'bras bu/
75b7(rnying rgyud): thog/ca 'di dang ma la zhing bsgrub pa'i 'bras bu yis//sa bye 'i rang bzhin rtogs mi 'gyur//kye gu pa/ kyang mi rtog sa chog tugs // bani dam ma thong gsang ba'i phyir//chol sgrub can gyi theg srur btab pas//bskal pa phyag khrag khrig yun ris//
76a1(rnying rgyud): bgrob ma gdas skye nas/dengs brem dpa rdang pomatshan pa/zhing po dong phras dang brnas par gyug/kye na tho ba pa byed byang chu sems/'depa la ltar mos khyad par byes/kun dang brel zhing yongs kyi da bye sa/
76a2(rnying rgyud): rtogs pa khyed la lung bstan pas//khyad kyis chos kun da ltar rtogs par 'gyur//de nas rtsol sgrub med par dbugs phyin pas//kun byed rgyal po nyid du lung bstan to//de bas sems dpa' rdo rje dam pa yis//ma bcos bskal par sbyangs pa'i skyes bu la//'bad rtsod med
76a3(rnying rgyud): bangs la bzhag pa'i phyir//rtsal bsgrub med par lung ston cig/ces gsung sa//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//lhun grub bo rtsal du med par rtogs pa'i le'u ste//drug cu brgyang ba'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po la/sem
76a4(rnying rgyud): dpa' rdo rjes 'di skad ces bsgrag so//kye khyed ni kun byed rgyal po ste//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//byed pa'i rgyal po khyod kyis byas//ma lus khyod kyis kun byas kyang //byed pa'i rgyal po khyod nyid la//bya mi dgos te gdod nas grub//dus gsum sangs rgyas
76a5(rnying rgyud): khyod kyis byas//khams gsum sems can khyod kyis byas//rgyung drug lam khru khyod kyis byas//skye ba'i 'gro lnga khyod kyis byas//skor pa gsum yang khyod kyis bkod//bstan pa gsum yang khyod kyis bkod//srung ba gsum yang khyod kyis bkod//dus gsum 'ar
76a6(rnying rgyud): yang khyed kyis byas//rang byung lnga yang khyod las byung //rgyan gyi rgyu lnga khyod kyis bkod//rang bzhin bcu yang khyod kyis bstan//kun byed rgyal po byang chub sems//mdo lung kun gyi ngo bo khyod//bstan pa'i me long mun sel sgron//'khor gyi mun pa sel mdzad pa//khyod ni
76b1(rnying rgyud): rgyu rgya log/ cu ba sa yogs kyilag/'gyu shes pa rgyas ga/sa med/[ऽ]nga byed/2/lag sa sbyong sas /ku kyi rtogs pa bar yug/blo gub pas bcos kyi gyings/
76b2(rnying rgyud): tshogs pas pas las 'as//'kho lus gyu snang po sa/ sa brjod byung pos ga/yang 'khri tsham pa med pa'i su//theg par srung po ngag gis bzlam/sku bsung drugs la mnan mong /bdag gi rtogs pa' ngo rtogs pas//rgyal bkun gyi yang mas
76b3(rnying rgyud): bo kun byed rgyal po khyod la 'dud//ces ston cing skrag so//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos//sems dpa' rdo rje la 'di skad ces bsgrags pa mdzad//a ma ho//sems dpa' rdo rje'i sku//rgyal ba kun gyi sku gsung thugs//gsang ba'i mdzod la mabar brnyes/
76b4(rnying rgyud): shing //smra ba'i seng ge bla na med//dam pa'i gsang ba rtogs pa'i thugs//gsang ba rgya mtsho sgrog pa'i gsung //yon tan lhun grub rdzogs pa'i sku//khyod ni rgyal ba'i sras po ste//kun byed rgyal po de nyid ltar//rgyal ba kun gyi mes po gyis//sa khams kun gyi mun sel
76b5(rnying rgyud): gyis//rnal 'byor rnams kyis bla ma gyis//gsang ba kun gyi rgyal po ste//bstan pa phyi ma gsang ba 'di//rnal 'byor pa la lung ston cig/ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//rtogs pa bsgrags shing //bstod pa'i le'u ste/drug cu rtsa dgu pa'o//
76b6(rnying rgyud): rgyud phyi ma zhes bya ba//mdo lung bcu gnyis pa rdzogs///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byedang rgyal pos//chos thams cad skye bo med par mnyam pa la gzigs shing //dgongs nas cang mi gsungs par bzhug so//de'i tshe sems dpa' rje/
77a1(rnying rgyud): par dus la bab bo//zhes bskul bas//sems dpa' rdo rje dgar ba'i mangs dang bcas te//nga ning mos pa'i mngangs phyung ngas//stan las 'das te//stod pa'i mdun du 'di skad ces zhuso//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//khyod kyi 'khor du
77a2(rnying rgyud): bdag 'dus 'khod pa de//bdag la 'tshal ba'i don ni ci zhig mchis//bdag 'tshal don ni khyod kyis bstan du gsol//ces zhus pa las//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po nyid ci yang mi gsung bar bzhugs te//sems dpa' rdo rjes 'di skad ces zhuso//
77a3(rnying rgyud): kye ston pa kun byed rgyal po lags//chos kun skye med thig le'i rang bzhin la//ston pa gsum po nyid kyis ji ltar sgom//bstan pa gsum du nyid kyis ji ltar bsgom//bsdud pa gsum du nyid kyis ji ltar bsgom//'khor gnas dus kyi rang bzhin ji ltar sgom//chos
77a4(rnying rgyud): rnams kun gyi don la ji ltar sgoms//bsgom pa'i lung bcu bdag la bstan du gsol//zhes zhuso//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos 'di skad ces gsungs//kye sems dpa' rdo rje/rang sems skye med thig le nya gcig ni//rtogs pa'i don la nam yang
77a5(rnying rgyud): ma yongs shig/bsgrom du meng pa'i rjes nyid bya bdub la//bsgom dang m+bu pa bya pa gnyis med na//mi sgoms rang bded pa ga na bsgos pa yin//da ni kun nga skye ba med pa ste/rnam cog mtshan ma de badzin ris byas nas//drang bsam yid la 'grub ma lum
77a6(rnying rgyud): kun/skye ba med pa'i dang los ma yengs pa//ga'i ltar dasams kyang bsgos po yon shes na//ma bsgoms gang kyang bzhag kyang yings pa med//kyeng las byung ba'i sto pa sku gsum gyis//mchar bsgom du dga' bar gang dag la//rang rang gang snang
77b1(rnying rgyud): ltar 'thad pa'i lung bzhin du//bsgom du yod ces kun la khung du bstan//kye nga ni ston pa kun byed rgyal po la//ston pa rang byung rig pa'i ye shes las//ma bsdus ston pa gsum yang de la 'dus//ston pa de la sgom du med par bstan//bstan pa brjod bral rig pa
77b2(rnying rgyud): mtshan las la//bstan pa gsum yang ngal 'dus te gnas//ma bcos rig pa'i gzhal las 'od min//gan pa yas gsum yang del 'asus par bshad//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun//snying po byang chub sems la kun lasu pa/lo

78a1(rnying rgyud): /mtshan ma grangs kyi ting 'dzin te//ston pa kun byed rgyal pos lung ma bstan//kye ston pa kun byed nga yis lung bstan pa//ma lus skye med rnam dag ti nga 'dzin te//bsgom dang mi sgom rkyen la rag ma lus//bsgom bya ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kum/

79a1(rnying rgyud): byang chub sems//ngal sgro dang skur ba'i yul med pas//gang la sems dpar ma byed sbom ma byed//sgo gsum bcos par ma byed lhug par bzhag/gza' dang rgyu skar tshes grangs dus tshod med//yid la mi byed sngags dang phyag rgya bral//de

83a7(rnying rgyud): chos rnams thams cad la//rtog sa goms don du phyung nas gang bstan pa//rang bzhin skye ba med pa'i don du rtog so//ma yengs re dogs med pas dgongs pa yin//kye sems dpa' rdo rje rtogs pa'i don la legs par dasgoms shig/ji ltar snang ba'i chos la/
84a1(rnying rgyud): kun/ma skyes rnam par dag pas lung bstan pas//ji ltar skye ba snying po nyi du tshogs//kye nga las byung ba'i ston pa sku gsum la//sems nyid skye ba med par kun smra zhing //rang bzhin med pa'i don la kuran rtsod kyang //skye med
84a2(rnying rgyud): mngon du kun gyis rtogs pa med//de phyir kun byed rgyal po'i lung 'di la//shes nas ma yengs don la gnas pa las//rtsol bas mi 'jug gnyen pos sems mi spyod//yul la mi 'jug dran pa'i sems mi sdad //gang ltar 'byung ba de nyid don yin
84a3(rnying rgyud): pas// kun byed pa'i don la 'jug par gyis//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po yis//ma bcos sems kyi 'khor la lung phog pa//ma lus skye med rtogs pa'i rnal 'byor pa//rang bzhin bcu las rtsol bar mi byed cig/kun byed byang chub sems
84a4(rnying rgyud): kyiyang bzhin ga/shes pa gzung 'dzin mi gas spangs med pas//gos pas dbang bsgyur dzin la gnas pa yis//kun byed rgyal po'i dgongs po lhun gyis grug//ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems ku byed rgyal po las//lhur chub donas
84a5(rnying rgyud): bcang pa'i ler ste/brgyad du pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po la//sems dpa' rdo rjes 'di sad ces buso//kye skyon pa'i spyos pa kun byang rgyal po las//ji ldang spang ba'i chos ni khyod myos byas//bcu
84a6(rnying rgyud): pa'i ngam chos ni khyod kyis byas//bstan pa'i dam chos ma lus khyod kyis bkod//dam pa'i rang bzhin khyod kyis bstan pas na//khyod kyi rang bzhin rtogs na chos kun rtogs//de bas kun byed nyid kyi rang bzhin 'di//'dus pa'i cho gos kyis
84a7(rnying rgyud): grasal bar ma rig pas//don du ji ltar gnas pa bsha du gsol//zhes zhu so//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal pos mdo lung rgyad la bsdu ba 'di gsung so//kye sems dpa' rdo rje legs par nan tan gyis//ston pa'i ston pa kun
86a1(rnying rgyud): 'das bcol sgrub bya mi dgos//bdun pa nyid nges par bstan pa yin//kye ston pa'i smon pa kun byed rgyal po yis//bya med rdzogs pa'i lung bcu bstan pa ni//nyi shu gnyis dang sum cu pa dal dang //nyi shu bzhi dang nyer brgyad nyi shu bdun//sum nu gcig dang nyer
86a2(rnying rgyud): gsum ci shu drug/na dzin pa dang rba drung drug pas bzlas//ma byas rang bzhin rdzogs par bdun pa bya//ngan phu gsus pas der yongs pa bstan//kye spod pa'i spyon pa kun byed rgyal po yis//gtam pa 'bengs pa'i lung bcu bstar 'a ni//de brkyang ba dang sum cu dgu ba dang /
86a3(rnying rgyud): bzhi bcu lnga dang so bdun zhe drug pa//zhe bdun pa dang zhe dgu bcig pa//lnga bcu pa dang lnga bcu gsum pas rdzogs//gtan la phebs na ma bcos bcos med pas//bzhi bcu gnyis pas dgongs pa bstan pa yin//lnga bcu bzhi pas rtsa ba'i gzhi bstan nas//lnga bcu bdun pas gtang
86a4(rnying rgyud): tsing gang bas pa ste//ma bor rgyud de thos par bkres pa yin/kye ston pa'i sun pa kun byed rgyal bo ngas//bsam pas gtan la lab te bstan pa mi/rtsa ba'i rgyur du gsungs pa'i don rnams kyang //rdzogs pa'i rang bzhin bcu ru bstan pa yin//rtogs pa'i mdo thung bcu ru bstan pa li/
86a5(rnying rgyud): kyi ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po yis//bsgom pa nges par 'byin par bshad pa ni//skye ba med pa'i don tu bsdus te bstan//skye med rtogs pa'i don las ma yengs pas//bsgom pa mdo lung bcu ru sdus te bstan//'di las 'bros shing thams cad 'dir 'dus pas//kun
86a6(rnying rgyud): gyi rtsa ba don gyi 'grel pa'i lung //kun byed pa'i srasu gyur pa la//ming gi don mtshon skye med rtogs phyir bstun//kye sems dpa' rdo rje legs par nan tan gyis//theg pa'i rtsa ba byang chub sems kyi lung //kun byed rgyal po bsdus pa'i rtsa ba 'di//sems dpara' rdo rjes legs
86a7(rnying rgyud): par rtogs gyur nas//rgyab pa'i rgyal ba kun byed rgyal po 'gyur//dpyir kun byed nga 'bor rnams la/sems dpar rdo rjes don 'di lung ston cig/skye med byang chub semasu lung ston cig/'ber 'das dbying su cig pa lung ston cig/rgyu 'bras dusu gcig rdzogs par/
87a1(rnying rgyud): @chos kyi snying po khyod la phyag 'tshal 'dud//kye ston pa'i ston pa kun byed rgyal po khyod//ma rig pa'i lam ni bsal nas kyang //ye shes snang ba kun tu mched par byed//ston pa kun byed rgyal po khyod la 'dud//kye ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams
87a2(rnying rgyud): cad kun//rang byung nyid pas rgyu rkyen kun dang bral//ma bcos snying po ji bzhin gsal bas na//rang byung ye shes kun byed rgyal po nyid//ji ltar brtags kyang gting mtha' dbus med kyis//kun gyi 'byung gnas bsam pa'i yul las 'das//skye 'gag dmigs 'jug las
87a3(rnying rgyud): 'das rnam par zhi su zhig 'jug cing dmigs dang dad pa dag/'di la sman dang dam par dmigs med pas//mi rtsol bde la gnas shing dbugs 'byin 'gyur//chos kyi rtsa ba kun byed rgyal po las//ji ltar gnas pa rtsol sgrub kun dang bral//ces gsungaso//byang chub kyi sems
87a4(rnying rgyud): kun byed rgyal po las//rjesu bstod pa'i le'u rtse/brgyad cucu gnyis pa'o///de nas byang chub kyi sems kyang byed rgyal po la//sems dpa' rdo rjes legs par 'du nas zhus pa//kye ston pa'i ston bkun byeng rgyal po lags//ji ltar/
87a5(rnying rgyud): snang zhing grags pa'i chos rnams kun//rang byung ye shes cig tu bstan pa'i mdo//chos kyi rtsa ba kun byed rgyal po'i rgyud//phyi rabs rnal 'byor gang dang gang dag la//bstan cing gdams pa legs par bshadu gsol//zhes su so//de nas byang chub kyi sems kun
87a6(rnying rgyud): byad rgyam pas//skra ga ldan la bsdams pa'i den 'jig smras so//kye sems dpar rdo rje legs par rtson cig/skyon pani ston pa kun byed rgyal po ngas//ji ltar snang ning grags pa'i chos rnams kun//ngas byas byed pang byeng 'gyur snying po'i don//gan tha kun
87a7(rnying rgyud): 'das steng po byang chub rgyung //bangs pa mngon du zung ba'i mdo lung 'di//dang dang satshig 'byor 'grus cha ba dang /blong rje mid mo skye dang ming rjeg med //lus dang bu 'dung ma bran ngan pa/gang la'ang ma chags do dang dag bab 'jal/'ang dag nga
87b1(rnying rgyud): dam tshig brtags yin pas//snying po'i don dang ldan pas sbyin par bya//grags pa spangs shing rgyal bral ba dang //dam pa snying po'i don phyir lus srog 'phangs pa med//bka' las mi 'da' rtags dang ldan pa la//snying po don dang skye med bstan pa sbyin//gang gis snying
87b2(rnying rgyud): bo'i snyan pa dag thob na//'jig rten mtshan mas bdag la ci bgyi ste//thob na bdag gi dka' bas bsgrub po zhes//dam bcol legs par gnas nas bstan pa sbyin//ji srid bdag dang slob dpon 'tsho ba'i bar//lus dang srog gi 'brel pa ma mchis par//khyod kyi bka'
87b3(rnying rgyud): bden bsgra ces mam shong la//bstan pa srung po la byed rgyal ba spyon//mdzanarna mas pa srog kyang sa rgya dang //rdus da shi sul ma 'gros smras tshang/gros//gal te dag la dgos par mgyur kyang //ngas pas bras te pon mchog gsus la ma/ ston pa'i sngon pa /
87b4(rnying rgyud): byed rgyal po yis//snod min log pa'i gang zag bstan pa ni//grags dang 'jig rten chos la dga' ba dang //nga rgyal dam pa mi bkur yid thung ldog/'phyar zhing rjes dang dad par mi ldan te//rang tshod nyams len brtsan thabs 'dod pa dang //bstan pa yongs la 'phel zhing mi 'thun zhing //sgro skur
87b5(rnying rgyud): ga gzhan gnod sems bsgom pa min//'di la mi bstan mchog du gsang bar bya//sngon du brtags dang dad pa brtag bya'i phyir//rin chen dbyig dang chas pa gud nod gyis//chags sems nga rgyal gzhan sems ma nges la//de la kun byed rgyal po bstan mi bya//bstan pa'i don la blang dor mi
87b6(rnying rgyud): gnas kyang //mi ma yin dang 'byung pos bar chos cing //mkha' 'gro grub pa can gyis gnyi ga la//rus min 'chi dang 'jigs pa'i dbang gyur nas//sgo skur dbang gis snying po'i theg pa sbubs//de bas rigs ngan gang zag spang bya//'jig rten zhen pa dpral phyir rgyud
87b7(rnying rgyud): brtag phyir//lus dang longs spyod bla mas kun blangs la//nus dang mi nus khyad par brtag ldan nas//snying po don ldan kun byed rgyal po sbyin/ces gsung so//byang chub kyi sems kun byed rgyal po las//bstan pa gang la bzhag pa'i le ru ste/brgyad cu ma gsum pa'o//
88a1(rnying rgyud): pas byang chub kyi sems gur byed rgyal po ma/sems dpa ngas legs par mangs byubs 'dug sa zhus pa/byergud pa'i ston pa du byed rgyal po lags/nyid kyi thugs gcig pang byung chen pa las/rgal ldan du cig grol ba
88a2(rnying rgyud): nga byung ba da pa bshad dus // nus sa/ sa pa gyu/ ma grus dam pa bye ba 'angasas so// ga pa'i rlo pa ma thams/ma gyi 'ang sa bya pa sha pa sa/
88a3(rnying rgyud): chos rnams thams cad kun//ma lus kun byas byed cing byed 'gyur te//nyid la bya mi dgos te lhun grub pas//kun byed rgyal po zhesu bstan pa yin//snying po dang la dri ma med pa dang //ma rtsal lhun gyis grub pa'i don ldan pas//'di la rtsal zhing bsgrub pa mi mnga' ste//byang chub
88a4(rnying rgyud): sems zhes nges par bstan pa yin/kye ma byas gdod nas grub pa'i chos nyid la//rgyu dang rkye myed rtsal med snying po ni/ma sgribs kun la phyogs ris med gsal bas//rang thung ye shes zhesu bstan pa yin/kye ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kum/ma bcos rdzi bzhin nga la kun snang bas//
88a5(rnying rgyud): phyogs ris gnyi su tshong pa'i ngor med//bka'i 'grel pa chos kyi me long yin/kye ji bzhin ma bcos snying po rnal ma la/'khor dang mya ngan las pa 'byung sdung pas//blang dor 'dzin chags rgya las rnam grol te//snying po don gyi theg pa bstan pa yin/kye 'das dang ma byon da ltar rgyal ba la//skye med 'di
88a6(rnying rgyud): 'di la ma gzigs mi dgongs pas//ma bcos thig le bzung 'dzin las 'das pas//lta ba'i me long zhesu bstan pa yin//kye nga ni ma bcos ji bzhin snying po la//sgro 'dogs skur 'debs don las kun 'das te//dus gsum rgyal ba nga las byung bas na//rgyal ba'i yum zhes nges par bstan pa yis///
88a7(rnying rgyud): kyem bcos skye med kur byed rgyal po la//ma thal dgongs pa'i yon tan tshangs rdzogs pas//bsal zhing bsgrub ba kun las 'das pa na//rgyud kyi rgyal po zhesu bstan pa yin//kye ma bcos don las ma nor blo 'jog pas//snying po'i don la sgo dang skur ba bral//gnyi ga mi 'gal don du ga/
89a1(rnying rgyud): dza wA d+hIm ThAs s+thA ya swAt+ma/rtsub skung du/s+yAHrmA/ sa d+d+has Ar+tha/'ong du/chos pa sa rdzogs pa po byang chub kyi sems sa ngas pa mang//
89a2(rnying rgyud): ldan radas dpal kum tu bzang po la phyag 'tshalo//snga phyi mang bar 'di skad bshad pa'i dus na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa chos thams cad kyi snying po//rdo rje rdzogs pa chen po'i bdag nyid chen po de//'og min chos nyid kyi mkha' dbyings nyid kyi klong //sems
89a3(rnying rgyud): nyid+hi gnas//rang byung ma sgribs pa'i gzhal yas khang na/dus gsum cig pa'i sangs rgyas dang /mi skye lam la lta ba'i rnal 'byor pa rnams dang /sa la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang //nges pa ma thob pa rnams dang /snying pos bsdus pa'i srid pa gsum yan

89a5(rnying rgyud): ba zhes bya ba'i chos nyid la gnas par mdzad//mdzad pas 'khor rnams yong su rtogs pa skyes nas yongasu 'grog so//yo su sems 'phrog par gzigs nas/de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu bzang po des//'khor rnams la nyid kyi rang bzhin 'di bstan to//e ma/
89a6(rnying rgyud): nga byang chub sems ni kun tu bzang //rgyu rkyen med pa'i rang byung sems//thams cad 'byung ba'i snying po nges//dga' mgu rangs pa kun 'dod na/'dun pa ma skyes sems 'grogs pas//pa 'i rang bzhin ma mthong de//nga ni kun gyi rang bzhin te/chos kun snying po mtho
89a7(rnying rgyud): ba medan ces gsung so//de nas 'khor de rnams kyang sems kyi bsam pa so sor mthong ste/la la ni rang bzhin tu gyur to//la la ni ye shes skye so//la la ni dgar bso 'dun to//la la ni mos pas 'dun to//la la ni ma dang pa'i sems byung //la la ni dga' bar gyur na
89b1(rnying rgyud): ya//'byed pa mi mnga' ba'i thugs rjes zabs gcig/de nas sems pa rdo rje nyid kyi rang bzhin des/kun tu bzang po la 'di skad ces gsol to//e ma ho//rdo rje snying po khyod/byang chub sems ni kun tu bzang //yin par thams cad yongasu rtogs/rgyu rkyen med par 'byung mi
89b2(rnying rgyud): sems ces zhuso//de nas kun tu bzang po de nyid kyis//mi dmigs pa'i dod la zhugs nas thugs rje ye shes bskul bsgos pas/dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyes nas//sems dpa' rdo rje la/dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad
89b3(rnying rgyud): kyis//'od zer bsdus te bskul bar mdzad//don gyi tshig gis kyang 'di skad ces bsku lo//rdo rje snying po sems pa khyod//kun gyi rang bzhin khyod yin pas//sems nyid kun tu bzang po 'di//khyod kyis kun la bstan du gsol/ces bskulo//de nas rdo rje sems dpa' nges//
89b4(rnying rgyud): bde bar gshegs pa kun gyis bskul bar rig nas//mi rtog pa'i tingange 'dzin gyi sgo nas/'od zer phyogs bcur bgyi nas slar bsdus te/nyid kyi sems kyi sku bzhengs nas/kun la snang bar mdzado//kun gyi rang bzhin du yang bzhug so//rang bzhin de kun la yang snang //
89b5(rnying rgyud): sems kyi rang bzhin yang snang bar mdzad//de nas lus 'dus pa'i tshogs kyis//sems dpa' rang bzhin kun tu rdo rje la zhus pa/e ma ho//rdo rje snying po khyod/byang chub sems ni kun tu bzang //nyon mongs lnga'i sdug bsngal gang //khams gsum gnas pa'i sdug
89b6(rnying rgyud): bsngal gang //rgyud drug 'khor ba'i sdug bsngal gang //mtho ris ngan song bde sdug bsngal gang /ces buso//de nas sems dpa' rang bzhin kun tu rdo rjes/'dus pa'i tshogs la chos thams cad rtsa ba gcig las byung ba'i don 'phags pa' cha lnga stan//gal sbal pa/
89b7(rnying rgyud): drag tu spyan yan sa la/bsdus pa 'ong gsung so//chos kun rtsa ba sems gcig/za ba gcig la dod gsum ni//bde dang sdug bsngal theg pa gsum/phun sum rnam pa gsum tshogs pas/yo ga bani bye brag dbye//khyad par 'phags pa'i tshe lnga ston/
91a1(rnying rgyud): ge la zla drug drub stod men se /gas parnas pa bdus kyis 'das/sa ma smra myu stso/ sa susu na chu sa yong sa rdzogs pa'i mdo tsun//gcig bani gyon par byed/'ag pa/

91b1(rnying rgyud): chos dang srid pa'i chos//mi rtog pa'i rgyu rkyen gyis//skyed nas ji ltar 'byung ba yang //don dam skye ba med pas na//spros pa med pa'i don du bstan//'bras bu gcig pa'i don 'di yang //rgyu gcig pa'i tshul bzhin du//chos rnams kun gyi 'bras bu gcig/
91b2(rnying rgyud): yang ji ste bu zhe na/dab na byi nang chos med kun//la de las grub pa n+ya//'bras bu ga pa'i dzis yin//'gro ba'i sems kyi byin tshe log/banas chags lnga'i sdug bsngal rgyas//'bras bugs la byed sgyu b+ha 'khor//de la 'bras bu mang mi
91b3(rnying rgyud): gdags//sdug bsngal kun 'byung 'gog pa'i lam//spong dang 'gegs dang sbyor ba dang //lam la mngon du byed pa dang /nyon mongs lnga bcom 'bras bu de//mi skye mi 'gag 'bras bu min//bden pa gnyis kyi rtogs brjod kyis//mi rtag sdug bsngal

92a1(rnying rgyud): pa'i dang kyis sa/kud kyi snying pos ma rgyas sku//ma slabs ye shes chos kyi phyings//mi 'gag sa kyi byong ducu gsung //ma skye ma 'anag ye shes thugs//myang ba bras cu gnyis ma mchis//6 pa gcig pa'i byu
92a2(rnying rgyud): chung mas//thams ca kun pa rgyu spang //tshong byang gyi yang bas//oM ma mtshan 'bras zung bachog/'bras bu nas de lago ba de ni mtshan pa min//rtag 'a dor mtshon pa ste//mtshan po'i dzin gyis gdags med mtshan//dem
92a3(rnying rgyud): sa 'a ga chu ma smra bye ma nus // la myu me // skye la sa/klu drag sang ba byang chu tham
92a4(rnying rgyud): byang chub sems la skye ba med//ma mtshon skye med de mi mthong //skye ba med pa'i don tshig gis//skye med de mtshon mthong bar bya//thams cad chos ni sgro skur bral//sgro skur bral ba'i chos nyid ni//brjod med pa'i byang chub sems//

99b6(rnying rgyud): de nas sems dpa' chen po ngas//'dus pa'i tshogs la bstan pa//phun sum tshogs pa 'di stan to//kye 'dus pa'i tshogs chen da nyon cig/dus gsum ston pa gang gis kyang //bstan pa'i rang bzhin 'di yin zhes//tshig dang yi ger brjod pa med//ma gsungs
99b7(rnying rgyud): mi gsung gsung mi 'gyur//khyed kyi rang bzhin bstan pa'o//ye shes thugs kyi rig pa yis//rang gis rang bzhin ngo bstan te//de mthong bstan pas de 'dus pas//de la de bstan de yang mthong //de bzhin bstan pa zhesu bshad//ston pa chos kyi sku yis/

100a2(rnying rgyud): rtsol mi byed//ston pas theg pa rnam pa gsum//bstan pa gsang ba rnam gsum ni//spros pa med pa'i rdo rje ste//gsang ba rang rgyud dag pa yang //spros pa med pa rdo rjer bshad//gsang ba rgyu 'bras dag pa yang //spros med ye shes chen por bshad ka rdzogs pa chen po'i
100a3(rnying rgyud): gsang po yang //spros bral rtsol sgrub bral bar bshad//ston pa long spyod rdzogs pa yang //ye shes sku'i rang bzhin gyis//nu zla snang shes me long bzhin//ma sgrubs ston pa'i ngo bo la//snang ba'i sku mthong yang na gsum//gzung 'dzin blang dang dor byed bka phyi
100a4(rnying rgyud): pa rnam pa gsum du bstan//bya ba dag gau kr-i ya ni//phyi nang rtogs pa gsum dag par//gzung 'dzin rdo rje 'dzin du bstan//gnyis ka bya ba'i lha spyod ni//gnyi su med par sgrub 'dod la//nges pa med pas khe be la 'dra//dang du mi len phyi ba ni//mtshan ma med pa'i gtan tshigs

100b1(rnying rgyud): gis brjod par bya//ston pa chos skur bzhugs ca na//na mkha'i mtha' klas chos kyi dbyings//mtha' dang dbus med gnas kyi mchog/spros med gnas kyi kun tu khyab//kun bzang chos sku'i gnas chen po//ston pa longs spyod gnas chen ni//'og min 'og min gnas

103a1(rnying rgyud): tshegs chen nga nyon cig/khyad par 'phags pa'i mdo 'di las/khyad par 'phags pa'i tshe lnga ru/chos rnams thams cad 'dus par bshad//khyad par 'phags pa'i tshe lnga ni//mngon par sangs rgyas che ba dang //chos dbyings sangs rgyas che ba dang /bdag nyid sangs rgyas che ba dang /de yin sangs rgyas che ba dang /thams cad sangs rgyas med pa'i tshe/
103a2(rnying rgyud): bldog de yang 'i lta 'phags shes /che balnga'i byang chub sems/sems nyid ce lngas 'phags te/mngon par sangs rgyas che ni/'dus ma byas pa byang chub sems su spring kun las khyang par dmag sa/ma hi lo ga ma ma shad/chos dbyas sangs rgyas che ba ni thams ca 'byung ba 'chub sems 'byung gang kun lus bya

111b2(rnying rgyud): pa chen po yis 'gyur med ma byung yis la/yenas sgrib pa mi mngar bas/snong len med pa'i les/dbas spong len med par nges/sa bstar/sgyid na phyogs ris med pa ni/ma pa gsum du shes par bya//cin mi mdzad pa'i snying rje dang /bam+b+ha khyab pa'i snying rje dang /phyogs 'tshams 'dzin pa'i snying rje'o//gnyis la phyogs
111b3(rnying rgyud): ris med pa ste//phyogs 'tshams 'jim la phyogs yod//de yang gang dang gang zhe na//rnam pa gsum du shes par bya//cir mi mdzad pa'i snying rje ni//sems nyid rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems//spos dang bral ba'i rang bzhin gyis//dngos po mtshan ma cur mi mdzad//des na snying rje phyogs ris med//kun la khyab pa'i snying rje ni /
111b4(rnying rgyud): sems nyid rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems//blang dor med pa'i rang bzhin gyis//spang+yas seM ni kun la khyab//des na snying rje phyogs ris med//phyogs 'tshams 'dzin pa'i snying rje ni//yang na brgyad kyi chos nyid//rgyu dang 'bras bu phyogs 'tshams 'dzin//lha dang bdag tu phyogs 'tshams 'dzin//yul dang dbang por phyogs
111b5(rnying rgyud): 'tshams 'dzin//kun rdzob don dam phyogs 'tshams 'dzin//phyogs 'tshams 'dzin pa'i snying rje de//snying rje chen po ma yin ste//snying rje chu ngu yin par bshad//sems nyid rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems//tha~Md rdzogs pa'i rang bzhin gyis//snying rje phyogs ris med par nges//ces bstan//'byung lnga ye nas sung+yas
111b6(rnying rgyud): ni zab mo lnga'i sangs te//de yang gang dang gzhen/sa chu me rlung nam pa'i lnga//gcig la rang bzhin lnga lnga ste//nyi shu rtsa lngar byung ba'o//de yang rang bzhin rnam lnga ste//swa bA sa'i rang bzhin lnga//sdug pa cu'i rarwang bzhin yin//yang na rlung gi rang bzhin yin//snyoms pa me'i rang bzhin la//gsal ba nas
111b7(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin yin//de yang ye nas sabyas ste//sa ni sra byed rdo rje dbyings//skye 'jigs med pa'i sang+yaso//chu ni rin chen gtan gyi dbyings//thaMd sdud pa'i sangs rgyas//me ni pad+ma rnam dag dbyings//gzung 'dzin sreg pa'i sang+ya so//rlung ni yang zhing g.yo ba'i dbyings//kun du bskyod pa'i sangs rgyas//
112a1(rnying rgyud): /na mkha'i snang byed khyab pa'i dbyings//mi sgrib gsal ba'i sangs rgyaso//des na 'byung chen rnam pa lnga//ye nas sangaso yin pa ru//sems nyid rdzogs pa'i lung gi bshad//ces bstan//sems la gnyen po med des ni//rnam pa gsuM gyi gtan tshigs kyis//gnyen po med par
112a2(rnying rgyud): bstan pa yin//de yang gang dang gang zhe na//da pe la gnyen po med pa dang //don la gnyen pa med pa dang //rtags la gnyen po med pa ni//dpe la gnyen po med pa ni//na mkha'i dpe la rang bzhin med/don la gnyen po med pa ni//chos nyid don la gnyen po med//chags la gnyen/
112a3(rnying rgyud): bo med pa ni//byang chub sems la gnyen po med//de yang ji lta bu zhe na//dpe dang don rtags gsum pa la//rgyu dang sa bon ma mchis pas//rkyen dang gnyen po kun dang bral//des na sems nyid rdzogs pa la//gnyen po med pa zhes bya'o//nges pa bzhi'i man ngago//mi rig rig par bshad gsum ni//sems las
112a4(rnying rgyud): byung sangs rgyas mi/mi rig rig par bshad pa yin//rgyu las ma byung 'bras bu yang /mi rig rig par bshad pa yis/lung las ma byung man dag kyang //mi rig rig par bshad pa yin/de yang ji lta bu zhe na/sems las ma byung sangs rgyas ni//chos nyid mkha' la sems nyid glong //sems las ma byungs ng+yas yin/rgyu
112a5(rnying rgyud): las ma byung 'bras bu ni//byang chub sems kyi 'bras bu ste//rgyu las ma byung 'bras bu yin//lung las ma byung man ngag ni//chos nyid byang chub sems kyi don//sprul pa gang zag skra ldan gyis//ji ltar rtogs shing rig pa de//lung las ma byung man smin//mi rig rig pa 'di gsum yang //man ngag dag gis r-i//
112a6(rnying rgyud): bar bshad//mi rig rig par bshad pa'o//skabs dang phyag rgya'i man ngag ni//rnam pa gnyisu dbye bar bya//skabs ni bcu pa gnyis dang bdun//phyag rgya bcu gnyis lnga las med//gnyis ni rtsol zhing bsgrub par byed//skabs zhes bya ba'i nges pa ni//sems nyid rdzogs pa'i snying po de//sgrib med rang byung ye shes las//
112a7(rnying rgyud): kun dus gsum sgrib med pas//chos nyid don la 'gags pa med//sau dag gis go skabs 'byed//de na skabs zhes bstan pa yin/phyag rgya dag gi nges pa ni//sems nyid rdzogs pa'i snying po de//ye nas byang chub sku yin pas//skye ba med cing 'gyur ba med//ye nas yes chen po'i sku//'gyur ba med pa'i rang bzhin la//phyag rgya zhes ni/
112b1(rnying rgyud): bstan pa yin//sems nyid rdzogs pa chen po las//yis don gyis bye brag ni//skabs dang phyag rgya nyi shu lngas//sau dag gis skabs phye nas//yo ga bzhi'i don phyung ste//che ba lnga'i khyad par gyis//rim pa dgu dang 'jig rten pa//rang re dag gis bden pa des//nges par gtan la ma phebs pas//me long smigs bu zin pa ltar//ye shes don gyi
112b2(rnying rgyud): byi brag 'dis /nges par gtan la 'bebs par byed/de yang ji lta bu zhe na/ji ltar snang ba'i rnams krun//de nas 'dus byas ma yin/chos nyid dag du gnas pa ni/chos nyid skabs zhes bstan pa yin/'dus ma byas pa'i chos nyid de/'gro ba rnams kyi rang bzhin gyis//ye nas ma bye gnas pa'i phyir/rang bzhinas/
112b3(rnying rgyud): ba'i skabs zhes bya/'dus ma byas pa'i chos nyid//ma bcos dang la gnas pa yis//sang+yas che ba thob pa'i phyir//rang bzhin che ba'i skabs kyis bstan//'dus ma byas pa'i chos nyid//btsal ba med pas 'grub pa'i phyir//tsal dang bral ba'i skabs kyis bstan//'dus ma byas pa'i chos nyid//tshig dang yes brjod med phyir//brjod
112b4(rnying rgyud): dang bral ba'i skabs kyi bstan/'dus byas pa'i chos nyid/skye ba med pa'i rang bzhin gyis//khams gsum dag du mi 'khor bar//las kyis 'khol ba'i skabs zhes bla//'dus ma byas pa'i chos nyid/byang chub sem su gcig pa'i phyir//gnyis med skyon bral skabs zhes bya//chos kun rang gi sems la 'dus//sems kyi rtogs pa
112b5(rnying rgyud): bshad pa'i phyir//ye shes rang byung skabs zhes bya//chos kun rang gi sems la 'dus//sems la 'du 'bral med pa'i phyir//gzungs kyi sbyor thabs skabs zhes bya//chos kun rang gi sems la 'dus//sems la chos kun rdzogs pa'i phyir//yongasu rdzogs pa'i skabs zhes bya//chos kun rang gi sems la 'dus//sems
112b6(rnying rgyud): la gzung 'dzin med pa'i phyir//chags dang bral ba'i skabs zhes bya//chos kun byang chub semasu 'dus//sems nyid ye nas phyi ba'i phyir//gdod nas che ba'i skabs shes bya//chos kun byang chub semasu 'dus//sems la skyon yon med pa'i phyir//smon lam bral ba'i skabs zhes bya//chos kun byang chub sems
112b7(rnying rgyud): 'dus//sems ga thams cad 'dus pa'i phyir//lung chen ston pa'i skabs zhes bya/chos kun byang chub semasu la 'dus//sems la bzung ngan med pa'i phyir//mnyam pa nyid kyi skabs zhes bya//chos kun byang chub sems 'dus kyang blang dor med pa ma rtogs phyir//bde ba spong ba'i skabs zhes bya//chos kra/
114a1(rnying rgyud): bying kyis rnal dun pa zla bya na mas dud min mas papa skye kyi nga la shang/chung ba 'gyu ma shes su ga/snab nga na med pas mi yin ma rgyad cing mi de gyur ma snyings gas pa ni sem sa so/
114a2(rnying rgyud): nyid yin//bdag nyid chen po grub pa ni//ngo bo dag gi de nyid//sems dpa'i rnal 'byor de nyid kyang //rnam pa bzhi ru bgrang bar bya//gang zhig ting 'dzin sdom gnas nas//sdom de yid la gang 'dzin pa//sems kyi de nyid yin par bshad//ting 'dzin chags pa med pa ni//chos kyi de nyid yin par bshad//rigs dang kha dog sor gsal///
114a3(rnying rgyud): ye shes dag gi de nyid yin//rigs bzhi phyag rgya chen po bzhi//ngo bo dag gi de nyid yin//de nyid rnaMpa bcu drug ni lam gyi lung 'du shes par bya//zhes bya//ngo bo bzhi'i man ngag la/byang chub sems kyi mang 'ag ni//dus gsum spang+yas thaMd dang //khams gsum se ni thaMd kun//byang chub sems las kun byung ste//sang+yas sku dang ye shes
114a4(rnying rgyud): dang sa naM lus dang bag chags dang //snang zhing srid pa thaMd kun//byang chub sems kyi blang dor med sangs ye shes thugs rjes kyang //bri skol blang dor yongs mi mnga'//sems can 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal yang //byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin te//snang srid snod bcud kun la khyab//gnyen po dag gis skyong med//na mkha'i mtha'
114a5(rnying rgyud): mnyam pa das des ni snying bye chub de gang 'a sgros kun krugA/sma dAn snang gas la sa rdzi ga dang migs spyod 'azug/sgyu du ma nas rdi ngos po song la migs pa'i yul 'phral/b+hU la 'byung snying po 'di /smos kyi bags paku pral//'os pasnan sbyor
114a6(rnying rgyud): med man dag ni//byang chub sems kyi sku mchog 'dis//chos kun yongasu khyab pa'i phyir//gcig dang gnyisu byar med pas//de la gnyis kyi sbyor ba med pa/chos kun sems las byung ba'i phyir//chos sems gnyisu sbyararu du med//rgyu las 'bras bu btsal med pas//rgyu 'bras gnyisu sbyar du med thubs lnga
114a7(rnying rgyud): shi 'ang sul byung sa/thabs dang shes byar bye sa pa myom gsal bas/daskyes pa migs sbyar du myed/dbang po dag la yul snang bas/yus dang ngar ba gyur rdzo mas kun pa su gci'i pa'i phyir/gcig la gnyi sbyar byed par gsungs ces bya/sa gsum mar
115a1(rnying rgyud): sku gsung thugs la chu banas na rnams kyi lus ngag yid la khyag /dus gsum sang+yas sku dang ye shes rol//khams gsum san dag lnga rang khar spyod//byang chub sems kyi snying po 'di la ni/dngos po mtshan mar spyod pa med bzhin du/dzi ni mamatha' lta bu'i rang bzhin du/kun la khyab pas
115a2(rnying rgyud): mtha' ltar gnas ces bya//byang chub sems kyi man da'o//de bzhin sbyor ba dag pa'i man ngag ni//chos nyid skad ci gnyis med gzung ba ni//gnyi su med pa'i sbyor ba chen po yin//rgyu rkyen med pas bde zhing dga' ba de//skye ba med pa'i dam tshig yin par bshad//shes rab yul de rig pas mngon gyur nas//thabs kyi gang thabs
115a3(rnying rgyud): rig pas myong bas yis/chos rnams sa skyes bdag dang gnyis meng spyor//skyo ba med pa'i mtshar ba bral ba mi/ sbyor ba ma ga sasum/dus gsum gtan nin ma dag mi sam rgyas dgyu nga/khyim ma 'os dus gsum ba stang 'di/gsum du mi gnas sa phyi tha snyad mis/dgyur mi yas su byi skyen
115a4(rnying rgyud): med//dus gsum skye 'gag med pa'i don 'di ni//snga phyi med pas dus gsum chen por sbyor//ces dus gsum gtan tshigs kyi man ngag bstan//lung chen khungs kyi man ngag ni//btsal na 'ching bar 'gyur bas de bas na//thaMd yong su rdzogs pa'i lung 'di la//btsal du med pa de las btsal 'gyur ni//tshe chad pa'i nang sgrib
115a5(rnying rgyud): byar 'gyur//bya ru med pa de la de rtogs na//chos nyid la ni chos nyid grub pa med zhes gsung so//gtan la 'bebs pa'i mdo dang don gyi 'grel pa bstan pa'i le'u ste/'dun pa'o////de nas yang sems dpa' chen po des//'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la nges par ston pa 'di bstan to//blo nges par gzha'o//kye 'dus pa'i tshogs chen
115a6(rnying rgyud): cig/nges par rtog pa'i mdo 'di las//med pa'i man ngag rnam pa brgyad//nges pa lnga dang ma nges gsum//brgyad pa gnyis kyi man ngag gis//khyod blo nges par gzhag par bya//de yang gang dang gang zhe na//sems la skye 'jig med pa dang //don la sgom du med pa dang //spyod pa blang dor med pa dang //rnam pa bgrad du med pa dang //sems la ngo bo me
115a7(rnying rgyud): bdang /chos nyid mchon du med pa dang /sgro slar gdag su med par brgyad/nges pa lda ni gang zhen/sa la khyad par med par nges//rnam mchod nga mnyam par nges//mchong ba sngon med par res/bka' slad tshon du med pa nges/ dang sdug bsngal rgyu 'thun so//mdzes gsum ni gang zhe ma chos kyi rang bzhin ma sa
116a1(rnying rgyud): yod du sems la ngo bo med pas ni//dus gsum sang+yas thaMd kyis//sems kyi ngo bo 'di yin zhes//tshe chung mtshan ma'i ngo bo dang //dbyib dang kha dog spyod yul du//ston pa nga yis bstan pa med//ngo bo yod par ngas ma mthong //des na ngo bo med cis bya//chos nyid mtshon du med pa na//chos nyid rang bzhin

116a4(rnying rgyud): med pa'i gtan tshigs kyis//dus la khyad par med par nges//bla ma mchod dang mnyam pa ni//blang dor med pa'i sems nyid kyis//thaMd chos nyid gcig pa'i phyir//mnyam pas gong 'og rim pa med//gcig la gnyisu dbye ru myed//sems la blang dor bya ru med//sang+yas sems can gnyis med pas//bla ma
116a5(rnying rgyud): mchod pa gnyis med mnyam//des na bla ma mchod pa mnyam//mchod pa bsngo ru med nges ni//sems nyid rdzogs pa chen po la//thaMd lhan gyis grub pa'i phyir//sngo bar bya ba yong ma yin//ye nas bdud rtsi'i rang bzhin pas//ting 'dzin dbang pos sbyang du med//bka' sla mtshon du med pa ni//chos nyid byang chub

116a7(rnying rgyud): balba zhes bya bar bshad ma nges pa ni rnam gsum ste/de yang gang dang gang zhe na/chos kyi rang bzhin gcig la yang //ji ltar bltas pa der snang bas/nga la 'di min ngas pa meg byis pa'i blo la nges med pas//rang sems gzhan du tshol bar byed/des na nges pa med pa yis yis rang bzhin ma /
117a1(rnying rgyud): /dmigs pa'i yul dang bral/tshang mkhyen pa'i spyar zhes bya/mtha' dang bus med spangs pa ni/rang bzhin chos kyi gzhal laso//sgo skur gangag su med pa ni//thang ngor med pa'i rgyal po ma thams cad snying rje byang chub sems//sems dang bag chags 'dres shing mtshungs
117a2(rnying rgyud): sems kyi spadabang po sgo lnga yi/snang ba'i chos ni rnams pa lnga//dbang po sgo lnga rang rkyen gyis//sbyong zhing dor bar yong mi byed/cir yang mi dgongs thabs kyis rol//kun dang mi 'thun yongs kyis sbyang ba'i chos//nyon mongs lnga dang mtshams med lnga//de ni rnam ngasag chen po snre

117a4(rnying rgyud): bzhin gnas/le nas byas zin bdud rtsi yis/rtsol bdag gis sdug bsngal 'joms//ye nas mi g.yo mi 'gyur te//'gyur med bsnyad pa kun dang bral//bya bral zin pa'i yul de la/bcos pa med par ji bzhin gnas//btags pas chos ni tha~Mng kun/theg le chen po'i rang bzhin pas/sprob med cing sdu ba med/

118a1(rnying rgyud): med gzhan nas thob pa med//rdul phran don dam rang bzhin gcig pa la//tha dad btses shing phyogs cha rnams dang bral//gar mi gnas pa'i don nyid yis legs pa che//'dus ma byas pa'i yis rang las 'byung ba ste//mngon sum rnam par mi rtog yangs pa'i snying po 'byung //rnam par dag pa'i lam chen 'di/

118a3(rnying rgyud): gnas shing sgom par bya/skom pa'i rol chags pa'i dang ni bde ba'i nang /de lta su la mnyam pa'i don chen ye mi 'byung /byang chub sems kyi sku gcig kun tu khyab gangs pas/gzhan nas gdas pa'i tsha sgra senas med 'a byud ye shes mtha' 'thug pa med pas ma chos kyi dpyis la bri nga nga 'phel ba med/chos ci

118a5(rnying rgyud): yin paso/brjod pa'i tha snyad spros pa kun dbral/chos ni snang ba chos min de nyid men//rtag du 'dres pas dbyar med zhes ni brjod pa la/don dam stong pa'i chos zhes lhag par smra ba de/gang ltar bshad pa ltar ni dbyibs la grub pa med/'phrul par gyur pa'i byang chub lam des ni//ldog pa 'gyur bas 'bras bu thob pa med/rang byung

119a1(rnying rgyud): phyogs par 'jog pade la chags pa'i ma gyis zin te de bde bachol khyang chub los nas 'dod pas 'khrul ba'i ngas la gnas/des na le brngam chom las zhing 'chig de lta bu'i yum can ga zung 'dzin bcas pas na/bang+yas zhes ni log ras 'thong ba med/'dus zhes ning gis gnas pa med de na
119a2(rnying rgyud): sangs ming du smos pa bral//sang+yas zhes mi ston pa so ming btags nor//sangs rgyas zhes ni gzhan nas brong byed pa//de nyid smig rgyu bzhin te nor ba'i lam/kha dog dbyibs kyi gzugs ni yod min pas/chos kun bshad pa rdul tsa rtsa ma yang med//zin pa'i nang dang chags pa'i sdug bsngal ye nas ni//dngos po med la zhen pa ye

171b4(rnying rgyud): mdzod kyi sgo 'byed cing //dad dgu'i nor bu snang bar mdzad//gang 'di rnam par 'phral por mdzad//chos nyid byang chub sems rdzogs pa//rang byung sku mchog 'gag med par//byin rlabs kun du bzang po'i rtsal//

173a6(rnying rgyud): ye shes chen po'i sgo//rigs kyi dkrol 'khor rdo rje las/bde gshegs rin chen pad+mo'i chos//las rab rdo rje kun spyod pa'o//dag pa'i 'khor chags pade gshegs tshogs//rang snang gzhan las 'ongs po rnams//gnyi su med kyi bkri shes brgyad/

176b4(rnying rgyud): la sogs chos//ming tsam bde ba chen po'i dbyings//thams cad snying po 'bras bu'i mchog/ye shes ma skyes 'jog med gnas//tha snyad 'dzin pa'i chos gnyis kun//ma byung ma skyes rang gi dngos//'pho 'gyur 'jig pa'i rkyen dang bral//rdo rje snying ba
176b5(rnying rgyud): rgyan ldan pa//gang gi gang la gang du gnas/gang phyir gang zhig gang brtag cir//'jigs tshad tshul la mi gnas pa'i//lhun gyis grub pa'i chos 'di ni//byang chub sems kyi chos nyidaka thaMd rdzogs pa'i yon tan rgyal//e ma yi mtshan rmad byung
176b6(rnying rgyud): chos//de las sna tshogs cir yang snang //shes pa rgyun chags 'khor lo'i gzhi//mi mthun gnas ris gcig pa dang //bde sdug longs spyod bdag gzhan kun//cha mnyam mi mnyam stobs rgyas pa//gnyis gcig sna tshogs snang ba'i tshul//yun
176b7(rnying rgyud): rings goms shugs las skyes kyang gi des na rgya mtsho'i rba rlabs bzhin//'od gsal kun snang yo shes rgyan//slar nyid rol pa mi 'gags pas//kha dog dbyibs bcas 'byung ba'i rtsa la/yul dus bye brag phun sum tshogs/khang gshegs dkyil/
177a1(rnying rgyud): /la bar snyoms 'jug pa'i//zhing khams spyong yul yongs dag/dag ga sgrub med pa'i mthu las snang //sku yang bzang mo'i b+ha ga las//kun 'byung sgyu 'gag la sogs rgyun//gnyis med de bzhin nyid kyi sku/zung 'jug bdeb chen po
177a2(rnying rgyud): sbyor//zhen pa'i mtha' rnams 'gag pa'i phyir//mtshan pa'i tha snyad dpag med kyang //de bzhin nyid sku tshig dang bral//kun 'byung kun gyi mtha' bral ba'o//khams gsum skye 'gro'i gnas rnams kyis//phung khams skye mched phye nang tshogs//yas
177a3(rnying rgyud): don snang sangs rgyas sku//rang byung lhun gyis grub par gnas//ye shes chen po gsal byas pa'i/phyogs bcur bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas sku//dus gsum sgrib pa med mkhyen pa//rims bzhin yon tan phrin las rtsal//thad byang chub mchog
177a4(rnying rgyud): gi dbyings//sems nyid bde ba chen po'i sku//chos can ji snyed ma 'gags pa'i//chos nyid zab mo'i don snang ba'i//rang nyid rtogs pa'i bye brag gis//chos dbyings me long lta bu dang //mnyam nyid so sor kun tu rtogs//bya ba grub po'i ye shes
177a5(rnying rgyud): sku t+yA mi gnyis so sor gsal ba yi//nyi shu rtsa lnga la sogs rgyun//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ji lta bar//gnyis med gnyisu rnam par snang //shes bya'i dkyil 'khor ji snyed kun//gsang chen de bzhin nyid kyi dbyings//rnam par spros pa kun spyod/
177a6(rnying rgyud): pa'i//tha snyad yul las rtags pa yi//de ltar rang rig gsal ba ni//rang bzhin mkha' la snang ba'o//ma rtsal yon tan rgyan snang ba//bde gshegs rgyal ba kun gyi dngos//gcig dang gnyis min dkyil 'khor tshug/sna tshogs rnam par nges
177a7(rnying rgyud): pa'o//gti mug chan 'a das snang mdzad/'did chags chen po mtha' yas su//zhe sdang chen po mo bskyod pa/nga rgyal thub chen rin 'byung ste//pra dag chen pa den yod grug//'ang rigs kyi dkyil 'khor che//de dag 'kha lo padro nga //rdong ba /
177b1(rnying rgyud): /sna tshogs kyi//rigs mchog gtso dang sprul bar bcas//thabs dang shes rab snyoms 'jug pa'o//gang du dmigs par bya ba'i yul//phyi rol 'byung chen mthu rtsal stobs//thad nang gi snang ba'i tshogs//rgyu nyid zab mo'i byang chub sems//
177b2(rnying rgyud): de dag la rten 'gro kun dang //spyod yul ye shes chen po'i rgya//rang bzhin kun tu bzang mo'i mkha'//rang gi cho 'phrul sna tshogs 'char//kun bzang bkod pa rol pa'i chos//rgyu rkyen don bzhin rnam dag pas//gnyis med 'bras bu bdag nyid che//'das
177b3(rnying rgyud): dus la sogs 'gyur ba med//thad mi tshig rtag pa'i chos//de dag tha snyad las 'das te//lus rtog phyi rol don gyi rgyun//phyogs chus snang ba ris ma grub//rtog dus brtag pa'i bo'i mchog/nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor zer dang bcas//bar snang
177b4(rnying rgyud): gsal mkha' khyab pa yi//chos kun bde ba chen po'i dbyings//snying po mchog gi mchog gyur pa'i//ye shes 'jigs pa med pa'i don//rab tu mi gnas sangs rgyas kun//sgyu ma lta bur 'tshogs pa'o//mi zhig snang ba'i 'bras bu kun/
177b5(rnying rgyud): rnam grol ye shes zag pa med//de dag skye 'jig rgyun 'byung ba'i//blo bur chos la dben pa'i don//rang byung rgyan gyi 'khor lo che//ming tsam tha snyad dad pa'i gzhi//phyogs pa cu'i rgyal ba kun gyis kyang //snying po'i don las 'da' ma
177b6(rnying rgyud): yin/zhes gsung so//rdzogs pa chen po chos nyid byang chub kyi sems bkra shis mi 'gyur bar gnas pa'i rgyud las//da ltar gyi dus byung ba ste le'u drug pa'o///de nas kun bzang ye shes mchog/tha
177b7(rnying rgyud): snang ba rnam rtog cing //zab mo byang chub dkyi la 'khor rdzog so//gnyis med khril ba sbyor ba las/gdul bya'i rtog tshogs lhun grub sku/rmang byung bkra shis mi 'gyur ba'i//ngo mtshar yul 'dir ston par byed//ma 'ongs bskal pa
178a1(rnying rgyud): gzi brgyan gyis//dus bdun mthong ba bde ba can//lha mdzes mi g.yo nyi shu lnga/rgyal po grags pa khang bzangasu//'dod pa'i yul kun spyod pas rgyas//sngon tshe rigs tshogs don brtson pa'i//dpa' bo me tog gzugs
178a2(rnying rgyud): /'dzin pas//dang por rgyal ba 'byung byed cing //tshul gnyis sprul pa sangs rgyas pa//mi mchog seng ge yang dag skyes//las kun rnam par 'byed byed cing //bde ba'i bde chen rgyud gsal ba//yon tan phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor gyi//sangs rgya/
178a3(rnying rgyud): ting 'dzin mnga' bsres pa'o//de tshe bde gshegs rigs kun dang //byang chub seMnga'i grags chen kun/rang gi snang ba ji lta bar//chos kyi nyams dga' longs spyod mdzad ga blo gros grol ba'i mkhas pa dag/bstan pa'i gzugs kyis bsu/
178a4(rnying rgyud): 'gyur zhing //dus ring rgyud pa'i sgo gsal bas//sangs rgyas pha rol phyin pa mchog/phyi ma'i gsum brtsegs gangs ri can//'od rgyal grong khyer stobs duso//bu ram shing pa'i rigs las skyes//sngon dus sangs rgyas mnyes byas
178a5(rnying rgyud): bas k+Sha rin chen 'khor lo mtshan mnga' ba/yang dag sangs rgyas sgrol pa'i tshog so//rgyal ba kun gyi gsang ba'i mdzod//dag pa'i 'khor la ston par byed//shes rab thugs rje nyi zla'i 'od//srid gsum rang rgyud rnam dag chos/
178a6(rnying rgyud): ngo bo nyid kyi don ston pa//theg pa chen po gsang ba'i rgyud//dus dor bsten pa'i glegs pa ma las/snod ldan rnams kyi don 'byung 'gyur//byang chub se de'i mkha' khyab pas/de dag bsdu zhing bzung byed//dus kun bkod pa'i mchod
178a7(rnying rgyud): pa dang /sr-i zhus gus par rab tu ston//mi mchog sangs rgyas sprul pa'i sku/bya ba thams cad mthar phyin pa'o//bskal pa bcu las 'das pa'i 'og/sprul pa kun 'byung phyogs rnamasu /gsal ba nyid dga' rgyal po kha/
178b1(rnying rgyud): rol mo thams cad tshogs pa'i dus//sngon ldan snying po rgyal ba'i sras//bde ga shegs grong khyer zhi ba la//sems bskyed tshogs mang bsags pa'i mthus//rang byung sems kyi bde ba rtogs ka rin chen btsun mo la sogs stobs/
178b2(rnying rgyud): chags bcas pa/thub chen pos gnas//ji tsham pa d+ma las rgyud mya ngon/rgyag pa'i byin rlabs ston par 'gyur//dmas cad bad chen rang las byung /ye shes 'di nyid dag pa mad/kun 'byung gnas kyi rgya mtshalas/'bras
178b3(rnying rgyud): don kun rnam par mdzes//de nyid 'od dpal sgra dbyangs kyis stobs can sangs rgyas go tso bo mchog/grogs bcu'i rgyal ba kun byon nas//byin rlabs dbang bskur bstod pa'i gnas/rdzogs pa chen po chos nyid rgya//byang chub sa/
178b4(rnying rgyud): kyi don ston pa'i//gsang ba'i rgyud sde mi gnyis mchog/sku gsung thugs kyi tshul kun snang ka theg chen snying po la dga' bas/bstan pa yun rings bzung bar byed//skad cig gcig las rnam grol mchog/thams cad mtshungs/
178b5(rnying rgyud): par sa mchog sbyor/srad kyi bskal pa gnyis 'das par//gyad gyi rgyal po dmago ldan pa'i//stobs sde yul mchog 'jigs med rgyal//sangs rgyas bstan pa'i gzhung med pa res dpa' bo gzhu 'dzin ral pa can//sngon dus yun
178b6(rnying rgyud): rings sbyangs pa'i mthus//snying rje chen po dashes rab stobs/g.yul ngo'i 'jigs dus mtshungs par dran//rang bzhin mtshan nyid rnams brtags te//thugs rje ye shes nyi shor lha ra byang chub sems nyid zla ba'i zer//nges par
178b7(rnying rgyud): rang byung ngang tshul brtag/gang du phyogs 'dzin mtha' brel ba'o//gnyis rgyud bde ba gsal po'i mthus/skad cig sangs rgyas sa chen 'dir/ngo mtshare ya mtshan chos kun 'byung //phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas byang chub sems/kun/
180a1(rnying rgyud): don//dus rings byang chub sems dpa' spyod//theg pa'i mchog 'di zab mo nyid//yul kun skye zhing 'gag med rgyun//de dag de las de 'bras bu//gang zhig gang gis gang ba den 'jug nga las ma byung
180a2(rnying rgyud): chos med phyir//kun 'byung rgya yi ye shes dngos//phyogs chos ri su ma grub pa'i//bde ba byang chub sems kyi rgyan//rgya chen snang ba'i bye brag kun//der snang der 'jug de'i chos//ji srid thog mtha'i bar gnas tshogs//nyid las
180a3(rnying rgyud): 'phrul pa'i dkyil 'khor che/smig rgyu snang ba'i dus bzhin du/ma bcos gdod nas dngos mo grub//zla ba'i gzugs kyis khyab pa'i dngos/ma 'gags sngon du gsal ba'i don//rgyu rkyen las grub rgyu med pa//sgyu ma'i tshogs 'dzag
180a4(rnying rgyud): zhen pa med//de dag 'ja' tshon ris kyi chos//so sor ma 'gags dbyer med cing //rnam gsal de la bstan pa'i mthus//me longs gsal ba'i yul las 'das//res 'jug dri za'i grong khyer rgyu ni//rang bzhini mi gnas 'gyur
180a5(rnying rgyud): ba'i dngos//mig yor sna tshogs dngos gcig bdag/mtshon byed brag cha'i rgyu las byung //gnas skabs dgos ched las skyes pas//mi mthun ris rnams bdag grub pa'i//sprul pas yul rnams rag tu slod//dus gsum ma

180a7(rnying rgyud): /bad pa chen po la shes rgyun/dbang po drug gi gnas 'jug/de dag nang gi rdol 'byor gyis//stan cig skyes pa'i skyabs rgyas shing gi/khams kyi gsum ches kyi phyag rgya mas/gnyis med bsgrub pa'i ngan 'jug pas//rab tu zhib bdas
180b1(rnying rgyud): med pa'i//gnyis med thugs kyis gsal bar 'gyur/chos kun rang gi dngos 'dzin paSh+T+rI//chos nyi dngos bral byang chub sams/dza ltar gnas pa'i'adar banag pas sa ram pa khams dngas thob pa meng /gang dag phyag nagazhan nas 'jug
180b2(rnying rgyud): rgyu thas byas pas rnam par na//gzhag pas bdag kyi rab tu gas// mda' mo 'o na gog pa meng/sa lus srung ba'i rgyas dag par/bsam mtha' rgyam mthun pa'i dru//ji skar byung canarnam pa'i dbyings//snang bam gams
180b3(rnying rgyud): rdzogs pa'o//rnam par rtog tshogs ye shes rgyun//de mtha' de rgyun nga yis chos/ma lus byang chub sems kyi rgyan//nyon mongs sdug bsngal kun du bzang //de nas 'jug pa'i yan lag bzung //A li kA li la spyod cing //bdud rtsi bcud rgyas
180b4(rnying rgyud): de dag las//rtag tu mi gnyis mkhas pa'i sgo//kun spyod tshul mang bdag spyod pa'i kla ma lus de don snang ba rgya//spang blang med pas 'dzin chags bral//re dogs bcad pa'i skyon bral ba'oka thams cad dkyil 'khor snang ba'o//sku mchog ye
180b5(rnying rgyud): shes bzang po nyid//ji ltar stobs kyi yan lag kun//ma 'dres chos kyi rgyun las byung //don kun gsal ba'i bdag nyid kyis//phyag rgya chen pos kun ston cing //gser 'gyur rtse'i rnam pa ltar//stong gsum rin chen rgyan gyis mdzes//
180b6(rnying rgyud): rnal 'byor rdzogs pa'i rgyud chen 'di ka sangs rgyas kun gyis bshad pa'i mdo//thams cad de las byung ba dang //yang dag gnas pa nyid kyi phyir//mchog tu sngags pa'i dbang ba kun//bla na med pa'i de bzhin nyid//rgyul bas bstod

181b3(rnying rgyud): don 'jug tshogs kun bral//de bzhin nyid dbyings rtogs pa las//dam pa'i shes pa skye bar 'gyur//gang gis gang la gang ltar 'jug/de dag de nyid de mtshungs chos//med dang yod rtog tha snyad rgyun//ji bzhin pa yi don 'jug pa'i//dam/

183a5(rnying rgyud): 'dus pa rnam grol chos//byin rlabs snang cher 'phags pa yi//rang gi ngo bos don ston pa//thams cad nyi zla'i dkyil 'khor bzhin//ma lus rgyud chen kun bsdus pa//sems dpa' chen po khyed kyis bzung //gnyis med ye shes tshul rgyun che 'gas pa yi//rang gi ngo/'brel//don 'di kun du snang byed
183a6(rnying rgyud): la thabs dang shes rang gnyis med pa'i//gsang ba rgya mtsho kun gyi gnas/de dag dga' dang dang ba'i shugs//yang dang yang du phyag 'tshal bdud//mchod pa rgya chen rnams bkod pa//ma yus mnyes pa chen por spyod//dam tshig chen po gas sngags che//gsang ba'i sgo kun g.yo ba'i tshul//rgyal ba'i bkar
183b1(rnying rgyud): bzhin gzung byed cing//dam pa'i yi ge rnam gsum bsrel//rgyal ba thams cad mkhyen pa'i gtso//bkris yi ge bzang po'i gnas//don kun phyag rgya'i tshogs bcas ston//rnam grol ye shes 'gyur ba med//thabs mchog rigs kun grangs med bzhin/byang chub sems nyid bstod pa'i gnas//
183b2(rnying rgyud): t+yag tu rang gi gnas lang sa spyong //sbrul babangs me gun gzugs mchog thog ma'i sangs rgyas sus bya bang /khyod kyi mtshan nyid mchen par dag pa/rtog pas nga grol ba'i 'bras/rdo rje nam pa'i mtshan nyid can pa/'astod bkun du zhi ba'i dbyings/'ang gshegs yon tan bsdegs pa

184a1(rnying rgyud): byungs rjes kun tu zang po la dga' ba'i sems kyis nga pa'i yangs byung sa lakho po nas c+doMs kun tu bza pos rdo rje sems dpa' la smras pa rdo rje sems dpa' e ma'o//dga' ba'i sems skyes e ma'o/dang po'i mangs byung ma mgo/
184a2(rnying rgyud): las phyung ba'i a ma'o//khyed 'dud dga' ba a ma'o//ces gsungs pas//rdo rje sems dpas zhus pa/ston pa'i ston pa kun tu bzang po lags//ston pa nyid kying theg le spros pa med//'khor rnams kun kyang thig le spros pa med//bstan pa kun kyang thig le spros pa/

186a1(rnying rgyud): bzhin nyid bstam//de bzhin nyid la rnam par dbye ru med/ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams thams cad kun ce/sems kyi ngo bo yin pas de bzhin nyid//de la gang zhig 'chos shing sgrom byed ba//de yis chos sku'i bstan partogs pa med/snang ba med pa'i
186a2(rnying rgyud): chos nyid byang chub sems//ma nor de bzhin nyi du ma rtogs te//de la gang du chos shing sgom byed pa/de yis rang sems bcos med ma rig/snang srid chos dang snang med byang chub sems///sems kyi de bzhin nyid phyung de bzhin nyid /de bzhin nyi dus rtags sor mthong //de bzhin nyid ni

186a4(rnying rgyud): lnga lnga nyi shu rtsa lngar spyod/rtsa lnga'i longs spyod ni sU lnga'i tshul/chos nyid don gyi ra ba nyi du bshad /s+phul m+bu'i di sbyin ba krugs rjes dong mdzad/kham so gsum son bas patha dang la/sru dang gsung dang thu ba'i rnams gsum gyis/bas pa thaMng sku gsung grub rjes bzlog de 'din la po ni rje pa gsum bstan te/shi tu/

186b1(rnying rgyud): dpa' khyod ma 'chos na//kun tu bzang po 'chos pa yin//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun kyang nga yi rang bzhin yin//mi gnas dmigs pa'i yul dang bral//spros med pasaM med yul las 'das te//de la spong gegasu byed pa//bskal par btsal yang rnyed par dka'//nga la btsal/
186b2(rnying rgyud): bas grub pa med//nga la sbyangs pas byang ba med//nga la ma lha yul med kyis//nga la maM grong lam med kyi//nga la ma sbyong bsgrib med kyi//nga la bgrod pas phyin pa med//bde chen chos skyong semasu gcig pa'i phyir//snga phyi rus gsum med khyad par med//rang byung yes yu ba'i la mi rtogs pas//

186b4(rnying rgyud): gnas mi rtog cir yang dgos med pas//mi rtog mnyam pa'i yul la rang bzhin gnas//rdo rje sems dgyun rnamakha' me bram de'i rgyas pa'i rgyud mas bya bla med pa'i le'u skye bAzhi pa'o///rdo rje sems dpas yang zhus pa/bcom ldan pade ba che kun ba

187a1(rnying rgyud): rnams//rang sems ma bcos sngar mthong rtogs//da ltar bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//ma bcos pa'i rang bzhin te//ma bcos ji bzhin rtogs gyur nas//da ltar 'gro ba'i don yang mdzad//slad nas 'byon pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//rang byung sems nyid 'di//

187a3(rnying rgyud): pa du ma'i grangs bgrangs kyang //mi rtsal bde ba dang mi phrad//sku gsum sems las ma bcos phyir//sku gsum ston pa'i kun bzang las//de bzhin 'chos par byed pa yi//ston pas bden tshul ci bstan yang //nyid kyi rang bzhin ston pa yin//rdo rje sems dpa' na mkha' che bram ze rgyas pa'i

187a6(rnying rgyud): ni 'jig pa'i gnyen po med//gzhan pa mi ltos sgoms nas 'chol mi dgos//dam tshig bsrung ba'i bsgom pa bya mi dgos//phrin las rtsal ba'i sgom pa bya mi dgos//lam la mi 'grongs la mi sbyong ste//rang las chos ni gzhan du tshol mi byed//ngan spyod yul lngas 'das pa'i phyir/chog
187a7(rnying rgyud): gis mi phebs dbang po'i spyod yul 'das//rang gi rang bzhin bsdus te thig ler gnas//ma spros gnyis med yo nas gcig tu bstan//rang byung ye shes mi rtogs g.yos pa med//rgyu 'bras 'das pas rtsol dang sgrub mi byed//ma byung bas na dngos po bsgom mi byed//ma skyes pas na 'jig
187b1(rnying rgyud): pa'i dgos pa med//bde chen brdal ba chen po'i klong dkyil na//mi snang bas ni dmigs pa'i yul dang bral//chos nyid snang ba med pa'i rang bzhin la//mi rtog mnyam pa chen por rang gis gnas//chos nyid spros pa med pa'i rang bzhin la//rig pas mi rtogs ting 'dzin ma byed cig/

191a7(rnying rgyud): thams cad len par byed//ting 'dzin las 'dod pa 'dzin//'dod pa de ni yang dag min//yang dag gzung du yod ma yin//byas pa'i las ni thams cad kun//ma rtsal lhun gyis grub pas bsgrub//ma rtsal lhun gyis grub pa de//byas pas grub pa ya yin te//ma byas ye nas lhun gyis grub//'dod pa thams cad kun spangs pas//grub
191b1(rnying rgyud): par byed pa'i bgegasu che//'dod pa'i ting 'dzin bag chags des//bag chags de ni sems 'dzin yin//'dzin pas bde ba de spong byed//mi 'dzin bde ba kun len pa//ji bzhin pa'i rang bzhi no//mi gnas pa la dmigs pa med//mi rtog pa la ting 'dzin med//med pa don gyi snying po de//'dod pa med par thob par 'gyur//ji/
191b2(rnying rgyud): /bzhin bcos pa med pa ste//sangs rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa gcig/bde ba che las ma gtogs pa//'dod dang 'dzin pa ga la yod//gang la 'dzin chags med pa des//tingange 'dzin de 'dzin pa'i mchog/kun 'dzin ye nas grub zin pas//btang gzhag mi rtog grub pa'i rgyal//zhes gsung so//rdo rje sems dpa' namaSha
191b3(rnying rgyud): tshe bram ze rgyas pa'i rgyud las bya med lhun rdzogs kyi le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o///rdo rje sems dpas yang zhus pa/bcom ldan padade chen kun bzang po//'dod med ma g.yos rtsol med gang //kun tu bzang pos bka' stsal pa//nyon cig pa la 'dus pa'i 'khor//mos nyid 'gyur ba ye nas med//sgyur bas g.yos pa/
191b4(rnying rgyud): rgyas med//gle sdug bsngal byang chub gcig/sangs rgyas sems can sem su gcig/sngang sring sdong rlung chos nyid gcig/na nu gnyis kyang chos nyid mnyas//de la chags na sdug bsngal yin//rab nin gnas pas kun kyang 'grug kun gas pa de la rtog med pas//de ni nga byung mi shes yen/ye

209a2(rnying rgyud): dro 'jam rtsubs cha snyoms pa//rtog pa'i lte ba de ltar ston//sangs rgyas zhing khams rab 'byams na//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dkyil 'khor dang //sprul pa bsam yas 'byung ba yang //lta ba'i byin gyis brlabs las byung //phyogs bcu dus bzhi thams cad kyang //rtog hUM
209a3(rnying rgyud): mtshan ma sor snang //yang na dam tshig lta bar 'dus//dam tshig lta ba'i don rtogs na//sku dang ye shes bsam mi khyab//rang rig ye shes don rtogs na//skye shi med pa'i g.yung drung thob//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug kyang //byang chub chen po'i lam las byung //kun spyod ston/
