209a6(rnying rgyud): du gsol//zhes gsol ba btab pas//bcom ldan 'das kun tu bzang pos dkyil 'khor rnam pa gnyis gdon pa'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//de bzhin gshegs pa nyid kyis sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las yi ge bzhi bcu rtsa gnyis po 'di phyung //a ka khag g+ha/

209b7(rnying rgyud): gnyis dang ldan pa'i//rdo rje rgyal po rgyal po dang /g.yas na 'khor bas snying dang //nam pa'i snying po spyan ras gzigs/phyag na rdorje ban po dang /la s+ye mA le la sogs pani//sems ma g.yon gnas 'khor bar bskor/chu chang la ni bams pa dang /kun bzang smi sem
210a1(rnying rgyud): /'jam dpal bani//mchod pa'i lha mo bzhi rnams ni//sa snying g.yas nas gong bzhin dgod//ba par khyams thub drug dgod byas nas//rdo rje 'khor lo'i rgyab mdun du//kun tu bzang po yab yum dkod//sgo ma khyud la ni khro bo bzhi//gas su mo la g.yon du 'o//khro bo lab yum/
210a2(rnying rgyud): gnyis bar du//dbusu sgom bzhi yang dgod//bzang mo mdun la bzang po rgyab//gtso bo dkar po rdo rje 'dzin//me long ser po 'khor lo drili//ye shes sngon po rin chen 'bar//rtog pa'i ye shes pad+ma dmar//grub pa'i ye shes rgya gram ljang //dkar ser myu gu rin po che//'thing ka ral gri pad ma

212a2(rnying rgyud): sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' ma//'byung ba'i tshul du ldan pa'i//gzung 'dzin gnyis med mnyam pa'i klong //thams cad mnyam pa'i rang gyur pas//byi nang dkyil 'khor thams cad dang //sgra dang dngos po ji steng pa/thams cad tshogs kyi snying por rdzogs/dper na gar mkhams/

212a4(rnying rgyud): gsal ba nyid na 'ja' tshon bzhin//rang bzhin med par kun tu bzang //de nyid chos sku yin par bshad//kun gyis sku de rigs mtha' yas//gsang ba'i dur khrod brgyad zhi zhing //dngos po med las dngos po'i sgra//sna tshogs rnal 'byor zhi zhing stong //sems la sna tshogs chos rdzogaso//chos
212a5(rnying rgyud): kyi phung po khams rdzogs te//na mkha' mi dmigs mtha' yas la//nyi zla gza' skar kun rdzogs nas//mkha' dbyings dag par rdzogs pa ltar//phung po khams nyid chos yin te//chos kyi dkyil 'khor chos la rdzogs//'di zhes mtshon du med pa la//mtshon par byed pa rnam rtog ste//
212a6(rnying rgyud): ji snyed rnam rtog ma zad paR+YA//byang chub lam mi brnyed//rtog med mnyam med chos kyi sku//bzung bas mi zin chu zla 'dra//kun tu bzang po'i rol pa yis//a li ka li zab tu bstan//ji ltar rtogs pa'i 'byung 'jug nyid//mi rjes spre'us gnon tshul du//bya byed mang pos bdag med par//rang
212a7(rnying rgyud): rig stobs kyis dbang bskur nas//mtshan ma'i rnal 'byor rtog pa nyid//mnyam pa chen po'i klong du gan+da//'du shes 'du byed sna tshogs pa//mi g.yo rang byung thig ler bstan//de la nga dang bdag med cing //rkyen gyi rdul gyis mi 'go ste//rang bzhin rnam par dag pas na//thams cad
212b1(rnying rgyud): bde gshegs snying po nyid//gang du 'gro zhing gang du skye//'jig rten skye ba'i dngos po med//khyud chen mkha' la 'phyo ba de//rgya mtshor gnas pa'i klu rnams kyis//lus nyid ma rungs 'jig pa ltar//rdo rje theg mchog bla med pa//bya bral ma nyams thams cad bsdud//rnam rtog gzhig pa/
212b2(rnying rgyud): sa rnams 'joms//theg pa ma lus lto bar bza'//bsam dang bral ba'i dbyings nyid//'di zhes dpag tu med pa'i phyir//yi ge'i rgyud med sgrom du med//ba bral na mkha' ji bzhin du//mi btsal mi sgrub mi g.yeng bar//bsgom tshul zad pa spyod tshul gzhom//rang rig snying po mi g.yo bar/
212b3(rnying rgyud): 'i lam du su 'dod pa//glang po zhon nas rjes spyod tshul//mnyam dbyings dag la ldan pa la//bor nas sangs rgyas 'tshol bar byed//oM ma'o sangs rgyas spyod yul 'di//btsal bas rnyed pa'i gnas me de//drug gi chos bzhin yul med pas//long pas na mkha'i brlabs pa bzhin//gong
212b4(rnying rgyud): nas gong du tshangs pa'i lam//bya bral chos dang mthun pa min//ci ste lam la bgrod gyur na//na mkha'i mtha' bzhin mthong ba med//ye nas mnyam rdzogs snying po la//bsgrub dang bsrung bar bya ba med//bsgral dang ma bsgral gnyis med cing //dge dang mi dge bsam las 'das//rgyu dang
212b5(rnying rgyud): 'bras bu dbyer med//nyon mongs lnga po spangs byas nas//thar pa byang chub bsgrub tu med//ye nas de bzhin nyid kyi klong //dka' thub bgrod skyong 'bad rtsol gyis//yang dag sangs rgyas laM mi rnyed//sdom pas rtsa ba byang chub ring //dge rtsas sdug bsngal skye gzhir 'gyur//ngan song du yang
212b6(rnying rgyud): skye bar byed//smad 'tshong ma dang sbyar byas shing //sems can kun gyi srog gcad na//kun tu bzang po'i sa nyi du//'jol rnyog spyod cing shin tu bde//bde ba chen po'i rgyud las/man ngag thams cad ma lus kun rdzogs pa'i lhun gyis gnas pa'i le'u ste nyi shu pa'o//
212b7(rnying rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das kum tu bzang pos lta ba sna tshogs pa bltar meng pa'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/tshe du brjod pa 'di che du brjod//phyi dang nang med ye shes chen po'i mkha'/kun na kun nyid dpal gyi sku/rang bzhin dkyil 'khor yang dag nyid/de bzhin mnyam thom/
213a1(rnying rgyud): @#lhun grub kyis//yul dang rig pa gnyis med par//snga phyi med dbyings de bzhin nyid//rdo rje sems dpa' 'dra ba yang //blta bar snang ba 'bras bu min//'dus dang ma 'dus gnas mi gnas//rtsa ba min te ltung ba'i rgyu//sa yang bgrang nge 'jug phyag 'tshal//skyed/
213a2(rnying rgyud): /dang bsgrub dang mchod pa rnams//ngang gis dag pas dang gnas pa//dka' thub dka' ba spyad mi dgos//sa dang rtsol bas btsal mi dgos//'di ni ye shes bla med pa//bgrang zhing 'jug phyag bya mi dgos//'di ni byar med pa las 'das//bskyed dang bsgrub pa bya mi dgos//'di ni mi skye bde ba'i
213a3(rnying rgyud): klong du mchod pas rgyal po mchod med//'di ni rgyal mchog rdzogs pa'i sku//yi ges mtshon par bya mi dgos//'di ni rang bzhin gsal ba'i 'od //bstod dang bkur ba bya mi dgos//'di ni phung po'i dbyibs las 'das//ye shes lnga'i 'od dang bral//'di ni kha dog dbyibs ma yin//
213a4(rnying rgyud): 'di la blta bas blta ru mdo//lta ba nyid kyi tshig las 'das//'di la rgyu dang 'bras bu med//gang yang 'byed pa med pa'i mchog/'di ni 'dus pa zhes bya ste//rgya mtsho'i klung bzhin thams cad 'dus//gang yang dngos yod ma yin te//dngos med de nyid sna tshogs snanga'o//snang ba nyid na/
213a5(rnying rgyud): gang yang min//theg pa sna tshogs kun spyod kyang //dngos med de nyid yang dag nyid//dpa' bos mi tshugs 'bras bu'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' nyid kyi tshul//da lta'i bde ba phyi ma yi//mngon sum pa dang rgyab nas byung //de yang rnam pa'i skyon yin pas//de la rten par mi bya'o//gdong/
213a6(rnying rgyud): snying po yin pas na//dus gsum gcig ste khyad par med//byang chub sems dpa' dbang phyug dang //zhing khams rgyal ba ma lus kun//rdzogs pa chen po rdzogs chen po//bsam 'das brjod med mchog gi lam//pha rol phyin pa bcu'i bzhi//grub pa mchog gi tha snyad la//
213a7(rnying rgyud): spro dang skur dang rnal 'byor ba ni//ye rgyas btab pa'i rnam dag sku//rgya so rgyaso khams gsum ye sangs rgyas//brjo du brjod ston dbang phyug 'di/brjod pa'i tha snyad las 'das brjod med//sgom pa nyid na rtog pa'i rtog pa ste/mi sgom mi btsal rang rig nyid kyi sku//de bzhin de nga ni
213b1(rnying rgyud): de bzhin gyi nar gnag/gzhag tu gzhag tu'ang gzhag pa med pas na//gzhag du med pa'i gzhag sar gzhag la gzhag/gzhag pa'i dngos po rdo rje sems dpa'i thugs//'di zhes bstan du sangs rgyas nyid kyis med//bde ba chen po'i rgyud las//gsang ba man ngag ltar med pa'i
213b2(rnying rgyud): leru ste nyi shu rtsa gcig pa'o///gsang ba'i bdag po rdo rje 'dzin pas gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin nyid ji ltar lags pa bshadu gsol//zhes nus pas/bcom ldan 'das bde dbang kun tu bzang pos//ji bzhin nyid sna tshogs pa'i ting 'dzin pa
213b3(rnying rgyud): snyoms par zhugs nas//che du brjod pa 'di che du brjod//e ma'o sna tshogs kun tu ngo nyid//gdod nas ma skyes rnam dag byang chub sems//rang bzhin dbyar med gdod mtha' mi phyed cing //ye nas mnyam pas rgyal ba rgya mtsho yin//dus gsum srid gsum mnyam hA/
213b4(rnying rgyud): snying po nyid//ye nas kun bzang shes rab mchog gi dbyings//dbye gsal meng phyir bzang ngan blang dor med//'gro 'ong med par dus gsum cig car rdzogs//thams cad mnyam nyid spangs pa'i dkyil 'khor 'di//rnam rtog nyid na yis
213b5(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin te//rnam rtog mi skye ye shes brjod med//bdag dang gzhan du mi 'byed snying po ye shes nyid//snying po ma mthong shes rab ma spangs pas//gzhan du 'tshol zhing rmongs pa'i sems can gyis//bstan kyang ma thos mtshon kyang
213b6(rnying rgyud): ma mthong ba//bdag nyid gzhan du 'tshol bas sang mi rgya//de dang ma bral ston pa mi mthong zhing //lam du 'dod pas lam la bgrod 'gyur pa//bgrod pas 'dod pa phyin par mi nus te//don nyid sbas pa sus kyang mi mthong ba//shes shing bsgrub rgyu ci yin gang gis
213b7(rnying rgyud): bya byed las 'phags kyi nar 'dug byed pa//gang gis thol spyod pa byang chub spyod par 'gyur//rtog spyod tho'i mtshan ma ma spangs par/dran med mi spong 'khor rgyu mi 'byed de//ma stegs mi 'byed mngon sum sangs rgyas la/'di zhes rin lo mtshon du med pa'i

214a2(rnying rgyud): mnyam gnas rnam dag cing //mi rtogs ler gnas na rtogs mdzad dang //bdag med bdag chen thams cad 'byung ba'i phyir//ma bkag pa las rgyu zhing 'byung med pa'i//shes rab bla med rgyal ba ma bcos lam//dpyad med spyod bral bdag 'dzin thabs stobs bral//mkhyen/

214a4(rnying rgyud): bstan par dka' zhing don du phebs mi rung //me long gzugs brnyan tshul du sus snang ba//dam pa nyid ni 'di 'drar bstan du med//gti mug tshul du shin du gsal rig med//lcags kyi sgrom bu 'theb rgyas btab pa bzhin//nor bzung dam pa khyad par bstan par dka'/
214a5(rnying rgyud): phyi nang mi sgrib gsal ba'i snying po nyid//thams cad ma nor lta ba ji bzhin nyid//'khor ba ma spangs sdug bsngal byang chub lam//lha dang lta bar bya spyad yul las 'das//ye shes sgron ma 'dzin 'khrul las 'das pa//thugs rje chen po rol pa'i ye shes 'byo/
214a6(rnying rgyud): bsgom dang bsgrub dang bsam dang brjod las 'das//ji bzhin pa nyidang sa sa rgyas sangs rgyas pas/ji bzhin po la de bzhin de bzhin no/rtan zhing mi 'gyur dam tshig chen po yin//yenas yangs pa'i snying ba stan ma 'bar/ngo mtshar rjor 'byung rom pa na

214b1(rnying rgyud): ba'i phyir//sman nyid sman pa 'tshol ba ji bzhin te//rang rig ye shes mi 'gyur bde ba'i klung //phyi nang gnyis na mi gnas ji bzhin nyid//'di zhes bstan pa dka' bas bar na'ang med//bde ba chen po'i rgyud las gsang ba'i man ngag ji bzhin pa'i le'u ste rtsa gnyis pa'o//

216b7(rnying rgyud): thams cad dang bral ba'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas je du brjod pa 'di che du brjod//e ma'o mtho ris bgrod ba'i rgyu des byang chub lam mi rnyed /migaso rang bzhin gnas pas sman chen ma byas na/dus gsum nad kyis 'tsho ba'i grags yongs med/'phrul rto las sa/
217a1(rnying rgyud): @#//re bsgrib pa'i rgyun//btsal nas byang chub 'dod pa rtog pa'i gzhi//bde ba'i mchog ni bsam brjod yul dang bral//bang chub 'khrul pa ye rngam chos la za/de lta'i yul can sangs rgyas mthong ba med//byang chub 'dod pa skyon rtogs sangs rgyas med//yang dag brdeg
217a2(rnying rgyud): cir yang mi dmigs pas//sangs rgyas ston pas ming du btags pas nor//kun rdzob tshig gi spyod pa rtog pa'i rgyun//nang dang phyi rol chos rnams rnyed pa'ang //ston pa'i zhal nas gsung zhes bya ba'ang 'gal//ston pos bstan pas byang chub lam mi rnyed//mi dmigs mi rtsa gnas
217a3(rnying rgyud): skye 'gag med pa'i don//brjod cing bsam du med pas gsung du'ang med//gal te ston pas gsung pas byang chub lom brnyed na//ston pa'i ston pa rim par brgyud pas mnga'//sangs rgyas sangs rgyas bskal par thob mi srid//rtsol ba'i nad spangs ye nas ba bral med//rtsol ba'i
217a4(rnying rgyud): kyis zin pas 'khrul pa'i snying po 'gegs//nam mkha' lta bur rang bzhin med pa la//gza' skar lta bur tshig gis tshegs byang ba//zad med mi zad zad mtha' mang pa ste//cir yang smra zhing cir yang snang ba la//'dul ba mdo sder gdags pa ye nas nor//theg pa'i rim pa bya ba gting na
217a5(rnying rgyud): med/sangs rgyas yab yum sras med chos kyang med//dkon mchog gsum med dbang po'i spyod yul min//gzugs med chos skur bshad pa med kyang gting //me long chu zla bzhin du lha tshogs kyis//bshad par snang zhing theg pa bsam mi khyab//chos skur bshad pa sor bzhin ltar/
217a6(rnying rgyud): snang /gzhan nas bshad pas thob re 'khrul pa'i chos//sangs rgyas gzhan nas thob re nor ba'i lam//dka' thub sdug bsngal 'di la spyad mi dgos//tshogs bsags rgyu la ltos pa 'khrul pa'i lam//yangaso tsha'o chos chen snying po'i chos//chung ngur bgrod skyong 'dod pa'i gnyen no/
217a7(rnying rgyud): yin che rang mi dmigs chung ngu'i cha snyam na//chung che de rnams bgrod pa'i lam dang bral//gsungs dang ma gsungs rgyas dang dmigs dong snang //ye nas ltos pa'i chos min lhun gyis rdzogs//'di zhes bstan du gang yang 'bral ba med//bstan pas go bar bya min gsung tshig min//
217b1(rnying rgyud): skye zhing 'pho ba'i chos min gcig pur snang //gcig pu de yang bsam yas rtsa ba'i rtsa//snying po'i rang bzhin 'byung ba chos sku gzhan na med//mngon sum kun tu mi rtog yangs pa'i snying po la//gzung ba'i yul med sems kyis gnas kyang med//chos sku kun tu khyab pas bstan pa'i
217b2(rnying rgyud): chos med//rang byung mi dmigs yul la ji bzhin gnas pa'i phyir//rdul phran gcig las g.yengs shing phyogs bcu kun dang bral//gong du rdul sogs rung min dus pa ni nyid kyang med//ngo mtshar mthong ba'i yul med de la mthong ba'i mig /rnam pa'i rnam pa med pas snying /
217b3(rnying rgyud): /po'i yul yang med//sku med sku ru snang yang yan lag med pa'i sku//mtha' la mtshon du mod phyir dbyings la bri ba med//chos 'di ro la nyan phog bstan par dka' ste slar yang med//rang bzhin che ba'i yul 'di kun tu ji bzhin yul//stong pa'i stong pa nyid stong pa nyid kyang
217b4(rnying rgyud): med//mkha' 'dra chos kyi dbyings te dbyings med ye shes bral//bsgom par gzung ba'i yul med yid kyis spa du med//gang la'ang mi bstan snying po'i snying po med//sna tshogs chos 'di ston pa gol ba'i lam//na mkha'i rang bzhin yin par bsam pas gzhag du med//shin tu myu/
217b5(rnying rgyud): bsam pas snyeg par dka'//nyon mongs lnga nyid ye nas snying po'i mchog/snying po'i snying po yin te chos kyi snying //dngosu mi snang chags pa'i cha lugs kun skong mdzad//cha lugs 'od 'bar 'byung zhing slar 'gro yul la khyab//gzhan gyi yul min snying po'i yul te bstan par dka'
217b6(rnying rgyud): sngon gyi drang srong bsgom mkhan gyis kyang bzung du med//ma chags chags pa'i yul med chags pa'i gzhi//dam pa yin zhes lhag par bshad pa med//gang zhig bshad pas sangs rgyas mtshan re 'khrul pa che//'khrul dang byang chub lam ste rtog pas thob pa med//mtho ris
217b7(rnying rgyud): thob par 'dod pa ji bzhin snyed mi srid//de bzhin nyid las chos kyang mong mi 'gyur//yod pa'i chos ni thob pa med cing rtog pa'i sku//de la snying po'i yul med grub pa'i mtha' dang bral//spyod pa'i rjesu steg pa thar pa'i gegs//brtsal bas thar pa 'dod pa bus pa'i pa las/
218a1(rnying rgyud): kun tu bzang po'i chos nyid chen po 'di//rgyu rkyen yan lag bcus mi spyod tshogs//gang durang ma song gang du'ang mi gnas te//rang rig ye shes rang gi sems te mchog/shan pa smad 'tshong mtshams med spyad pas kyang //rang rig shes pas gang gis sgrib mi 'gyur//
218a2(rnying rgyud): ji ltar sdig pa byas pa tshogs kyi mchog/slob cing skyong bas bsnyad cing bskrun du medo//che zhing yangs pa'i bde ba chen po la//chos dbyings kun shong yangs pa'i mkha' de bya bral med//shes rab thabs la rag med de bzhin pas//mi g.yo rdul med don las nyams pa min//
218a3(rnying rgyud): drang srong bsgom mkhan spyod pa'i rjesu 'breng //tha snyad tshig la lta ba'i mchog tu 'dzin//yin zhes bzung nas rtsol ba'i don la 'jug 'di zhis chos 'di bstan du med cing yi sangs rgyas//dam pa'i don la bstan pa'i snying po med//kun pas dbang phyug tshul du nyid kyis dbang //sA du mad pa
218a4(rnying rgyud): bskrun du yod ma yin//don dang mi don bde dang mi bde nyid//'di la yongs kyis med pas bsgom mi bya//mi bsam mi yengs mi lta mi bsgom par//ji bzhin thig le ji bzhin de bzhin bzhag/chus gdungs glang pos 'thungs las mi 'gyur ltar//rang rig snying po gzhan nas yod 'ong
218a5(rnying rgyud): min du rang nyid ji bzhin de bzhin mi 'gyur gnas//sangs rgyas kun gyis dbus na ye shes gsal//spyod pa'i tshogs bral bsam pa'i rjes bral bas//mchog gi mchog nyid snying po phro ba'i sku//sangs rgyas mi 'dod de bzhin gshegs mi 'dod//tshig la mi spong yi ge nyid
218a6(rnying rgyud): /kyang med//rdo rje sems dpa' khong nas ma shar na//yi ge bstan pas yin pa'i don mi brnyed//bsam pa'i yul med dmigs par rung ma yin//'grub pa'i lam med tshig gi spyod mtha' med//sgom bsgrubs la sbyong bas don mi mthong //ting 'dzin gsum po sbyong bas mtha' mi sel//
218a7(rnying rgyud): 'bras bu don du bsgrub pas don mi rnyed//dpag bsam shing las rang gis kun 'byung ltar//yul phyogs 'di la rang gis kun 'char bas//drang srong bsgom 'dod rnams kyis brtsal mi bya//mi gsung mnyam par bzhag pa sngags kyis tshig ci yang bzlasu med pa bzlas pa'i
218b1(rnying rgyud): mchog gang yang bsgom du med pa bsgom pa yin/mchog gi snying po 'di ni kun gyi yul//bsam med thob med de bzhin de bzhin gzhag/de nas rdo rje 'dzin pas/bcom ldan 'das 'od mi 'gyur ba//rgyud kyi don ji ltar lags zhes gsol pas/bcom ldan 'das rgyud la bstod ni
218b2(rnying rgyud): titi 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/me du brjod pa 'di che du brjod do//ye nas mnam ste mthig le yangs pa yin/gang la'ang mi rten thig le'i ngo ste//rgyal ba sangs rgyas nyid de yang dag ma yin/Ni la'ang mi rtog mchog gi snying po nyid//yangs shing rgya me gting khung ring ba yin/
218b3(rnying rgyud): /cung zad 'di la sus kyang dpogs mi rung //na mkha' yin te sus kyang bsnyad pa med//sku gsung las 'das thugasu gdags pa'ang min//nges pa yin te 'di zhes bstan du med//ye nas sangs rgyas 'di zhes bco su med//khyud chen mkha' lding bya lam rjes med 'dra//bya bral lung gi
218b4(rnying rgyud): /rgyal po 'di ni kun gyi smad//slob mar mi gnas ye nas 'bras bu'i mchog/cir yang 'di la mi dgos ye sangs rgyas//spu gri pad+ma ral gri 'khor lo te//kun 'byung snying po rgyud kyi rgyal po'o//rgyas pa chen po'i rgyud//rgyud thams cad kyi rgyal po//rgyud kyi khog pa'i le'u ste/nyi shu rtsa drug pa'o//

218b7(rnying rgyud): me dang /rlung dang /na mkha' dang /snang ba thams cad dang /yongasu rnal 'byor ces bya ba dang /rnal 'byor chen po zhes bya ba dang /sems dpa' rnal 'byor zhes bya ba dang /shin tu rnal 'byor zhes bya ba dang /'das pa'i sangs rgyas dang /ma 'ongs pa'i sangs rgyas dang /

221a7(rnying rgyud): de'i 'khor du bshad/chos sku dag la 'gyur ba med/ma bcos rang 'byung rtag pa yin//chos sku rang byung spros med pas/ye shes sku la spros med gsal/des na byin gyis brlabs kyis bshad/long spyod rang rig nyid yin te/gzugasu snang ba rnam pa yin/'dod pa'i bdeb/
221b1(rnying rgyud): /thams cad dang /rgyud kun ma lus thun mong gi bde ba dngos 'dzin rang rig kyang /rig pa nyid nas 'dod nas rdzogs/bsam gtan bzhi pa'i bde ba yang /gzugs khams lha'i long spyong ste/sems kyi bde ba rang bzhin tshad/skye mched mu bzhi'i ting 'dzin pa de/rang byung byang chub sems
221b2(rnying rgyud): la rdzogs/'dzin gnyis ldan pa'i mthar phyin dang /ma sgrubs bde ba'i rang bzhin rdzogs/dag pa gsum dang gnyi su blta/rgyud pas byin rlabs bde ba yang /sems nyid rtogaso pas nyid la gnas/gsal bya rim pas don 'dzin cing /'dod pa'i stobs kyis nye bar bsgrub/baspod

227a1(rnying rgyud): par phyag rgya can/mi g.yo rnam dag sman chen gyis/gcong rong ye shes nyi du slar/'khor ba rnam par dag pa ste/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes gyis/dugu chen sman du btul ba ltar/'khor ba ye shes nyi du gzigs/snying po don gyi de nyid ni/gang ltar/
227a2(rnying rgyud): ci yang ma yin pas/thams cad mkhyen pa'i dgongs pa la/bden par gzigs pa mi mnga' la/de bzhin rdzun par mkhyen pa min/thams cad gang yang mkhyen pas na/snying po don gyi ngo mkhyen/ma bcos thugs kyi phrin las can/cis kyang ma bcos cis mi g.yo//gdod nas
227a3(rnying rgyud): dag pa'i chos nyid la/dran pa rkyen gyis de bzhin gnas//lhun grub sems la rtsol ba med/rang byung ye shes rgyu rkyen med/mi gnas thugs la phyi nang med//stong pa'i ye shes dngos po med/me long lta bu'i ye shes ni/shes bya kun la mngon phyogs med//mnyam nyid /
227a4(rnying rgyud): che chung med//saur rtogs pa ma 'dres gsal/bya ba nan tan thugs rje'i dngos/rang byung ye shes nyag gcig la/ston cha re ru mar bgrangs/rang rig nyid la yul med pas//yul gyi bskyed pas ye shes min//rang byung nyid la rgyu med pas//rgyu yis bskyed pa ye shes
227a5(rnying rgyud): min/lhun grub nyid la rtsol med pas/rtsol bas bskyed pa ye shes men/nam mkha' lta bu'i ye shes la/phyogs ris med par ye gdal ba/lhun gyis grub pa'i phrin las yin//rnam dag dbyings la gnas bzhin du/yod la snyoms pa'i thugs rje 'byung //'gro ba
227a6(rnying rgyud): gang gis ngo dag tu/gang la 'tshams pa'i thugs rje 'byung /skal ldan gdul bya gnas pa na/thugs rje gdul bya bzhin du 'byung /gdul bya gang yang mi gnas na/thugs rje'i mdzad pa gang la 'tshor/gang gi skal ldan gdul bya la/thugs rje gzhan zhig 'dod pa na//
227a7(rnying rgyud): rang nyid thugs rjer ma rtogs te/gzung 'dzin 'khrul pa'i blo yis brtags//thugs rje gzhan la 'char ba na/sdug bsngal drag po'i 'khor ba la/skyob pa'i thugs rjes lam ci/nam zhig rang rgyud tsam na/thugs rje gzhan gyis ci zhig bya/rtogs pa 'phra rag gdul bya/
227b1(rnying rgyud): ga ye nas rig pa'i thugs rje 'char//'gro 'dul thugs rje'i phrin la so//dam tshig yid bzhin rin po che/rgya mtsho'i pha rol sgrol ba'i sa/ma bcos rig pa'i chos nyid la/mi 'gyur ye shes bsal bar gnas/rtsol bral rig pa'i dam tshig//dam tshig mi ldan rdo rje ni/bka'i rgyud la
227b2(rnying rgyud): mkhas gsal yang //sgrol ma g.yang du 'dril ba bzhin/bdag gzhan rdo rje'i khab du 'gro//des na dam tshig gzhung ldan ni/'jig rten grogs ngor chos mi gtong /'brel pa rdzas kyi ched ma yin/bdag gzhan gnod pa'i las mi byed/kun la snyoms pa'i thugs kyi gnas/
227b3(rnying rgyud): 'jig rten 'brel pa'i ched mi byed/ma bcos dbyings la mi 'gyur bstan/ye shes gdeng gis ma zin par/chags sdang zhen pa'i las mi byed/sems nyid rdo rje rnam dag la/gnang bkag chos yod ma yin yang /zhen pa ye shes ma rtogs pa ra/lha'i spyod pa gtso cher 'dzin
227b4(rnying rgyud): de nyid don ldan legs pa che/slob ma bye brag gnyis dag la/bdag don spyod pa'i chos nyid la/snod ldan dam tshig gtsesu 'dzin/sems nyid dag pa'i gdengs byas na/dam tshig mi bsrungs tha mal sems/rang rgyud ngan pas dregs pa dang /thos pa chung ba'i blo sbram dang /man ga/
227b5(rnying rgyud): bcung chen 'dzag pa dang /se nas blo 'phangs dma' ba dang /'jig rten bya ba'i rgyud 'grub dang /mu tshor smra zhing mkhas 'dod dang /slob dpon srog du mi 'dzin dang /man ngag bsgrub byar mi sems pa/rdzogs chen rna rgyud bstan byas na//dam shod gling gi dur khrod du//mkha 'gro'i rna mnyan 'dus
227b6(rnying rgyud): bzhin du zhi ba'i dus kyis sngon rol du/sha khrag sngon du za bar byed/gzhung dang mi ldan slob ma ni/grib bzhin 'khor yang spangs par bya/lus srog bral yang khar dbyung min/'du 'bral byung yang las kyis byung /yon de ldan slob ma ni/stong gsum btsal kyang gcig 'ga' dkon/mang yang /
227b7(rnying rgyud): gsum las 'da' mi bya/man ngag rgyal 'theb gcig la bstan/bskal pa grangs med sngon rol nas/rdzogs chen don la sbyangs sbyangs pa/ye shes gsang ba'i snod bsngags/sgo nga'i nang gi khyung brug ltar/'khor ba nyid du yi sangs rgyas/sgong chag mkha' la khyud 'phur ltar/rjo bram
228a1(rnying rgyud): @/bcos bla ma'i sa//rgya mtsho'i bcud 'dus bdud rtsi bzhin/bka'i bcud sdud rdzogs chen 'di//snod ldan dang tshul bzang la bstan/dbang dang snod brtag cho ga dang /gsol ba gdab dang gnang ba sbyin/sngon du 'gro ba'i rim pa'o//bde gshegs ma lus gsang ba'i thugs/
228a2(rnying rgyud): srid pa gsum la gdod nas gnas/gang gis de rig rtogs gyur na/dbang rnams kun gyi mchog yino//srid pa gsum nas ye dbang bskur/rang dbang dam pas snod byas/gzhi nas dbang thob bskur mi dgos//rtags thabs sgra khrid thabs kyis shes/bcos ma'i sbyor ba'i rnal ma nyid//
228a3(rnying rgyud): gang mos 'thun pa'i sgo nas bstan/thabs sbyor bcos pa'i sgo nas ni/'phrul gyi me long bzhin du bstan/rtog don rnal ma'i don du ga/don rtogs bcos ma'i sbyor ba spang /tshig don khyad par bstan pa ste/dgongs don gong ma'i don la 'jug/sgra brjod thams cad semasu bstam//
228a4(rnying rgyud): rnal ma ma bcos gzhi nyid bzhin/lhun grub gsum gyi don la 'jug rim par 'jug pa'i bzhi yi so//bya ba med pa'i dkyil 'khor gsum/gdod nas rtsol med lhun gyi grug ye shes nyi ma'i snying por shar/rang gsal mun pa'i ye nas dag/ma lus thig le chen po nyid/mi g.yo rma
228a5(rnying rgyud): dag yul 'das la/rgyu dang 'bras bur rtogs 'dzin cing /mi shes rgyu ma'i blo yis bcings/rang snang mo bcos nyi ma dag/ma btsal ji bzhin snang bas na/snying po'i sems la gol ba med//thams cad gsal ba'i snying por 'bar/log rtog blo'i dbang gis na/snang srid 'khor
228a6(rnying rgyud): ba'i kha rgyud kyang /ye shes dbyings la g.yos pa med//sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes ni/bdag du rtog pa nyid las byung /rtog pa'i blo la mtshan mar snang /mi rtog ye shes grol ba'i thugs//ye shes mi rtogs me long gsal/mkha' la 'ja' tshon bzhin du snang /rang snang yis ngo la/
228a7(rnying rgyud): rol pa'i khyad par snang ba ni/snang la ngos zung ma yin te/chu'i zla ba lta bur snang /gar yang ye shes rol pa la/gang yang ma yin ji bzhin pa/rang byung ma bcos de bzhin nyid/dngos nyid mi g.yo bad chen dang /btsal zhing g.yo ba ci yang med/dbyings dang ye shes tha snyed de nyid ma yiH
228b1(rnying rgyud): zhen pa'i gnas rmad byung gzhal yas spros bral na/'bar ba'i ye shes bde gshegs gsum/zug phrang nyid gzhag pa ste/bla ma'i zhal nas shes par bya/spyod pa'i sgo nas sna tshogs pa/dang ldan bla ma shes par bya/'phrul tshig sgra yis shes pa rta ga/gang la bdag gi de yin na/de nyid
228b2(rnying rgyud): ye shes rig pa 'char//ma yin de nyid log pa na/rig dang ma 'thun rang bzhin log/rlung dang mkhris pa'i chos bzhi ni//skrod pas phyag stong bye brag shes/de'i drag dal gnyug ma'i don/de nas rdo rje slob ma yis/rig don 'dod pa'i gsol ba gdag/gnang na de nyid rgyal tshab bya/stsol dral zin dang ma/
228b3(rnying rgyud): bzung bar//gsang ba'i 'khor lo ye shes gter/gsang sngags gter gyi mdzod 'dzin cing /rig pa'i bstan pa sgrogs pa'i bdag gzhi cha'i rig pa rang gsal te/dus gsum bde gshegs snying po yin/gsum ldan 'khor ba'i rang bzhi no//dus tshod bla ma'i zhal nas shes/gzhan du'ang de
228b4(rnying rgyud): bzhin kho na yin/shes rab gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor ni//dgu ru ma rnas sa dag yin yang /gsal byed sgron ma de yis mthong /ye shes gsal ba'i phyogs med gdal//phyi nang med par kun la gsal/'khor ba rag pa'i cha yis ni//de nyid phra ba'i don mi mthong //bde don mthong bas 'chang ba
228b5(rnying rgyud): grol/rdzogs pa'i ye shes de nyid go//nyams blangs goms pas mthar thug ste/ji ltar 'dzin pas bzung ba'i blo/ma lus par ni zad 'gyur na/mi rtog ye shes gsal mthar phyir/'gyur med sems kyi g.yung khrung la/ye shes rang snang gyi nar gzhag/dran pa rkyen gyis ma shor te//
228b6(rnying rgyud): de nyid tsho drang lkug par zhog/ma nor sems kyi slob dpon la/lung nod dran pas thag gtsad bzhin/bsam med dran pa mi g.yo la/mi spyad legs pa'i don mi zung /sdu bar ma yin spro bar yin/mi rtogs gsal ba ma bcos pa/rdul ma yin ni 'jam pa'i ngang /rig pa de nyid dang la gnas/sems/
228b7(rnying rgyud): nyid rang gzhag lhag pa'o//glang chen lta bu'i sems la ni/ma skyod rang lugs nyi du bde/btsal na glang chen myos 'drar 'gyur/na mkha'i do ma bcos pa/sems nyid rang bzhin ma bcos bde//khyung chen mkha' la lding ba bzhin/mi btsal rang bzhin gyi na bde/thams cad gtang bas/
229a1(rnying rgyud): @'dzin pa med/khu ba med phyir rtogs pa'i ngangag sems kyis sgris par mi bya zhing /lus dang ngag dang btsal mi b+ha/shes pa mi sku san du khe/snang ba dag gzhen mi ga mtshan ma'i spros pa 'phel ba na/sna tshogs rtogs pa'i sel byung ma/lus sems glong la gzer chen gangag shin du
229a2(rnying rgyud): /dran pas g.yo ba na/stong nyid bde ba'i man ngag sbyin/ye shes gsang ba'i dgongs pa la/gang yang ma yin de ltar yin/zhes gsung so//ye shes gsang ba'i rgyud las/rang gis gsal ba'i le'u ste lnga pa'o///de nas rdo rje sems dpa' des/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad
229a3(rnying rgyud): la 'di skad ces gsol to//rin chen 'khor lo gsang ba'i thugs/'phrul tshig rig pa'i sgron ma yis/dgu rdum sbas pa'i don gsal na/de nyid rang gis gsal bar gyis//zhes gsol pa dang /de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyis bka' stsal pa/sems dpa' chen po
229a4(rnying rgyud): chur nyon cig/man dag de bzhin gsang ba ni bka' rgyud ma lus mtha' dag gis/brjod bya'i snying po da yin te/mi tshig rnam grangs sdud pa'i bdag//kun brjod gsung gi 'khor lo tsam dus mtha'i skad cig nyid la ni glong dgu bam po nyi khri nyid/rin chen gsal bya'i tshe du gsungs/de nyid/
229a5(rnying rgyud): dgu lda na sems kyis gzhi/rin chen lam gyis nyam su blang /ma sgrubs 'bras lhan gyi grug lta ba sems la blang dor med/mtha' dbus med pa'i snying po len/ye shes ma 'gag gsal bar snang /gnang bkag med par spong ba'i rgyan/mi bsam mi yangs dgongs pa'i klong //rgyal thabs spyi
229a6(rnying rgyud): blugs rdzogs pa'i dbang /bgrod med mthar phyin sems kyi lam/bcos med dam tshig gdod nas dag/rang snang 'bras bu lhun grub klong //chos nyid ma bcos rnal ma'i gzhi/don dang nges tshig de yis bstan//gzhi don lhun grub rnam gsum la/rnam pa rang bzhin mtshan nyid rnams/bde ba chen
229a7(rnying rgyud): bair lhun grub pa/dus gsum bde gshegs ye shes dang /'gro drug sems kyis ma bcos pa/tha dad ma yin rang bzhin gcig rnam pa lcu dgrub sgyu sa ni/rol par snang la ngos zung med/rang rig mtshan nyid lhun grub la/gzung 'dzin brtags pa dang gis med/de nyid de'i rang bzhin ni/ma lus
229b1(rnying rgyud): /don gyi snying po bzhi/ma shes rtogs pa'i rang bzhin te/rtog tsam nyid na mi rtog pa/thams cad rol pas 'khor ba yang yin/'jig rten rtog med pa sangs rgyas yin/'ching ba bral bas lta ba yin/rang 'bar gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor dag/ma lus 'dus pa'i lus chen po/gcig ni kun gyi
229b2(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin te/lhag ma med par yongasu rdzogs/rig pa'i 'khor lo 'bum phrag yas/bzhi mtshan dpe'i rang bzhi no//mun pa chu dang nyi ma bstan/sems nyid rig pa dka' rag la/zhen pas brtag pa'i snang med pas/smag gi mun rum lta bur bshad/dngos med rig pa rang snang la/yas
229b3(rnying rgyud): rol pa mi dgag pas/chu'i gnyer ma lta bur bshad/ma bcos sems nyid mtshan nyid la/rtsol bas bkag pa ma yin yang /gdod nas dag la dri med la/nyi ma shar ba lta bur bshad/tha dad rtag par 'dzin pa dang /rol ba'i snang ba bkag pas chang//'tha' bral dbu ma'i don 'dzin pas
229b4(rnying rgyud): mtha' gsum gsal ba'i dgongs pa'o//rang snang lhun grub ye gnas la/bgrod pa'i lam gyis btsal mi dgos/mtha' med na mkha' lta bu la/bgrod pas phyin par ga la 'gyur/sems la bgrod pas phyin 'dod na/mkha' la tshad kyis 'jal ba 'dra/lam bstan pa dang lam gyi dpe/nges sti
229b5(rnying rgyud): don ldan de nyid yin/don gyi lam dang tshig gi lam/don ni thug phrad gsum yin la/de'i brda' phrad tshig gi lam/ye shes bgrong med rdzogs pa'i lam/rnam grol 'bras bu gyi rgyu/ye shes gsang ba'i don rtogs na/mi g.yo sems kyi dkyil 'khor nas/theg pa kun gyi 'bras bur 'cha
229b6(rnying rgyud): des na kun grol dgongs pa'i thug/lam la rim med cig char 'jug/rnam rtogs phra rags rigs kyi shes/tshig lam rkyen gyis ma mtshon na/ma bcos sems kyi don mi shes/tshig gi lam gyi don la sbyor//gsang don mthong bas bde ba'i sa/'dzin par rags pa'i rtog pa dag/rang byung
229b7(rnying rgyud): ye shes nye du gsal/mi rtso la bde ba'i sems la gnas/mi 'gyur spros bral chos nyid la/'dzin bral btang snyoms rnal 'byor gyis/skyon nyid byang chub semasu mthong //gnyis med 'od gsal chen po la/skyon dang ston blang dor med/bsam pa ma lus semasu shes//mthar phyin byang chub sas/
230a1(rnying rgyud): kyai lag mang pa'i bad+ma'i rab nu sogs tshig dang don la skabs kyi sgra lus dang ngag gi rtsol ba dag ting 'dzi sems kyi rim med pa pa 'das kyis sgrol pas rdzogs pa'i las rig pa'i gnasu rtog pa'i phyir des nam par byin 'bras bu'i lam/ye nas yong mang da ga la/
230a2(rnying rgyud): skyon yon rnam rtog kum tu bzang /dbyer med thams cad mtshungs lem yin//byang chub sems la rgyu med pas/rgyu yis sgrub pa'i 'bras bu min/dngos po ye shes rgyu med pas/rang gsal nyag gcig dri ma med/rtsol med rnamakha' bzhin du gnas/sangs rgyas sems can kun la khag/de nyid rtog
230a3(rnying rgyud): bas 'bras bu'i sa/'das dang ma 'ongs da ltar yang /rtog pa snga phyi'i khyad par las/dus gsum gcig ste khyad par med/bcos thugs kyi rig pa la/sngon med phyis med gnyi ga gsal/rang byung 'od gsal gdod nas spong //rgyu med 'bras bus byang chub sems/'bras bus kun gyi bdag nyid
230a4(rnying rgyud): /yin//chos sku 'bras bu rang la gsal/rin chen 'bar ba lhun grub ltar/thugs rje'i byin rlabs 'dod dgur 'char/skal ldan rang rkyen nyi ma ban/sems can dus na skabs kyis mthun/lhun grub dus na phyogs ris med/'od gsal rig pa'i dkyil 'khor las/rang snang 'bras bus rgya mtshor
230a5(rnying rgyud): 'khyil/gsal 'bar 'bras bu bdag gzhan med/bdag gzhan 'bras bu rang snang ba/dngos dang bsam pa skabs kyis shes/gsal 'bar rig pa'i byin rlabs ni/chos nyid rig pa'i gzhi la rdzogs/snying po don gyi gzhi rtob na/rang bzhin tshogs rdzogs sgrib pa med/gnyis kyis mi sgrub spangs pa/
230a6(rnying rgyud): med/spang med thig le nyid kyi dbyings//mtshon med sems kyi 'bras bus ni/rig pa'i bdes la mtshon du med/'od gsal rgyu 'bras gnyis med la/rgyu 'bras gnyis rtog pas min//gsal ba rdzogs pa'i 'bras bu ni/'byung ba lnga la rag ma las/sku gsung thugs la rten mi 'cha'/zhes gsu/
230a7(rnying rgyud): so'u ye shes gsang ba'i rgyud las/rin chen gsal byed pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o///de nas sems dpa' rdo rjes/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad la 'di skad ces gsol to//'od gsal thugs rje lhun grub khyod/rol pa'i mdzad pa 'bum phrag pas/'odagasal
230b1(rnying rgyud): dbyings la bzhugs bzhin du/thugs rje'i mdzad pa phyogs bcur gangadal/'gro ba'i bsam bzhin re bskong /yid bzhin nor bu lta bur shar/khyod ni snang gsal sgron ma ste/ma rig mun pa dngas par byed//sman chen bdud rtsi byams mgon gyis/rdzogs chen sems kyi rang bzhin la/
230b2(rnying rgyud): med spyod pa ji ltar gyis//zhes gsol to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyis bka' stsal pa/sngags kyi spyod pa spyod pa'i mchog/sems kyi 'phro 'du'i las mi byed/rang gsal klong la ye nas gnas pas/na mkha' rdo rje ma bcos sems/sgo gsum rdo rje ma/
230b3(rnying rgyud): btsal ba/lus kyi phyag rgya'i lam mi byed/tha ma ma lus spyod de bzhi no//ngag gi snying po bzlas brjod med/ma bcos gsung gi spyod pa la/zhen pa'i ngag sogs gang yang min/sems kyi 'phro 'du'i las mi byed/rang gsal klong la ye nas pas/dran bsam rgyu ba gang yang min/spros
230b4(rnying rgyud): bral na mkha' lta bu la/rang gsal ye shes ma bcos pa/rtsol med rang bzhin lhag pa'o//gang du'a'am gzhag 'od gsal mkha'//dus gsum dran pa rkyen gyis zung //tha snyad mtshan mas g.yos gyur na//nam mkha' rdo rje'i spyod pa la/'od gsal lhag pa'i ye shes kyis//rnam rtog dran
230b5(rnying rgyud): papas g.yos pa na/rin chen 'phrul gyi snying po bsgom/dran pa rkyen gyis zung bar bya/lus ngag gtso ru 'jog byed na/de'ang spyod nyid spyod pa min/spyong med rig pa'i spyod pa la/rtsol spyod 'bras bu lhun gyi grug bcos ma'i sems kyi rang bzhin la/bcos ma'i spyod pa phyin ci log//dngos
230b6(rnying rgyud): bral rig pa bdag med lnga/brtag pa'i bde bzhin log pa bzhin/'od gsal rig pa lhun grub la/rtsol ba'i spyong bas sems mi mthong //bsam gyi mi khyab 'od gsal sems/'gyur med dbying las ma g.yos kyang /rang snang lhun grub byin brlabs las/skal ldan bsam pa ji bzhin
230b7(rnying rgyud): du 'gro 'dul thabs kyi spyod pa mdzad/rang sems pad+ma ltar dag nas/gzhan dag ji ltar mos bzhin mdzad/g.yem par 'dod pa g.yom pa nyid/rang sems 'brang sgo dang bstun/zhe sdang mnar sems nga rgyal nyid /thabs nyid gsang ba'i lam du bstan/nyan thos la sogs 'a
231a1(rnying rgyud): pa'i sgo gang mos 'thun pa'i thabs kyis bstan gsang don rtog na gang yang rung garang byung ye shes nyag gcig lag gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor grangs med pa/mthun 'jug thab su bstan pa yin/ci yang yin gang yang rung su zhig zugs kyi dkyil 'khor du/
231a2(rnying rgyud): /rgyan dang ldano khyad par can/ma bkag sems rtogs khyad par las/sgrib par 'gyur ba gang zhig yod/ma bkag ci bder rtogs par spyod/snang gsal rig pa'i gdeng ldan na/gdul bya mos pa'i ngo dag du//sna tshogs mos pa rgya mtsho yin//gang yang ma bkag ku ru bzang //mthar phyin dus
231a3(rnying rgyud): gsum sangs rgyas kyis/dug lnga rtogs pa'i spyod pa 'og min//bya btsal kun dral lhun gyi grub/dus gsum sangs rgyas gang la'ang /spyod pa 'di las yongs ming la/rnam dag sems kyi spyod rtogs na/rtsol ba'i spyod pa log pa'i chos/lhag pa'i spyod pa 'od gsal thugs/bsam gyi mi//
231a4(rnying rgyud): khyab ye shes thugs/gang la sems nyid lhag spyod kyang /lhag pa'i spyod pa mos pas 'jug/skal ldan lhag pa'i don 'dzin kyang /che la dmigs pa'i cha mnyam la/gong 'og dmigs pa'i sems dang bral/rang bzhin na mkha'i rdo rje dang /'thun 'jug 'gro 'dul thabs kyi spyod//ces gsum
231a5(rnying rgyud): so//ye shes gsang ba'i rgyud las/bya ba dang bral ba spyod pa bstan pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o////de nas sems dpa'i rdo rjes/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad la 'di skad ces gsol to//gsang 'dzin thugs rje'i rgyal po khyod/rol pa'i 'khor lor gsu
231a6(rnying rgyud): bdag /sku'i 'khar lo ma par lji zhag ltung ba 'dan kun mo byin pa'i thugs/sngams kyi 'dun kyi kyong pa la/'dus pa'i lam tshags mas pa yis dam tshig gas ba'i rin dus gnas pa'i dam tshig pa gdun ma gzhes gamal pa dag nan gshegs pa rnam par snang /
231a7(rnying rgyud): mdzad kyis bka' stsal ga/lhun grub ye shes gsang ba la/bsgrub cing rtsol ba'i dam tshig med/rig pa gsang ba'i dam tshig ni/rin chen 'bar ba sgrol ba'i glu/rdo rje nyi ma lta bur gsal/lhun grub phyogs gcig ma yin pas//sdom dang tshul khrims phyogs 'tshams dang //
231b1(rnying rgyud): phyi nang gsang ba'i dam tshig ni/ma bcos gsang ba'i dam tshig ni/med pa phyal ba gcig pu dang /lhun gyi grub pa'i dam tshig go//rang rig gdod nas lhun grub pa/gzung 'dzin mtshan ma'i chos med pas/brjod med pa'i sems nyid kyis/bsrung du med pa'i ngang du bsdams/de yang bsrung du med na bde/
231b2(rnying rgyud): gnyis med sems kyi dam tshig la/'da' med srog gi gnas gyur na/ma bcos rgyal ba'i dam tshig yin/na mkha' lta bur gsal khyab la/mtha' dang dbus med ma bcos pas/'di yin 'di min phyogs dang bral/phyogs bral skyes bu chen po des/phyogs gcig spangs zhing bsgrub
231b3(rnying rgyud): pa med/gtad med 'dzin bral chen po la/phyogs mtshams nges pa'i chos ma yin/bde gshegs sems can thams cad la/ma bcos sems kyis rig pa gcig/rgyu yis ma skyod ye nas byung /rkyen gyis ma sbyangs gdod nas dag/rang bzhin 'od gsal rig pa la/ma lus
231b4(rnying rgyud): ngongs pa thams cad 'das/dus gsum sangs rgyas dgongs pa la/rang bzhin dgongs pa gcig las meng /rang snang mi 'gyur chos nyid la/rang bzhin rig pa mi 'da' na/sems na gcig pu'i dam tshig ste/dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad la/dam tshig 'di la yang mi mngar/rnam dag rab
231b5(rnying rgyud): byung 'od gsal la/lus dang ngag la bya brtsal med//ting 'dzin sems kyi rin btsal mang /'di ni tshigs chen bral ba ste/gyi na lhan grub dam tshig yin//lhan grub dam tshig rtsol bral la/rtsol ba'i dam tshig ye nas med//sel med rin chen gling bzhin du//rnam dag rmushes rang byung la/rkyen gyi
231b6(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin ngang gi dag/gzhi rtsa med pa'i sems nyid la/brtags pa'i 'dzin pa rnam par dag/lhun grub 'od gsal dag pa la/rnam rtog brtag pa'i sems grub na/mu stegs sangs rgyas ci la rtsod/sangs rgyas zhes ni ci la gdags/rtogs pa dngos la ma grub na/rin chen 'phrul gyi lde
231b7(rnying rgyud): mig gis/tha mal zhan pa ji ltar rdzogs/lhun grub dam tshig bya btsal med//lhun grub rin chen snying po ster/cig rdzogs rang gsal dag par rdzogs//kun rdzob rang byung semasu rdzogs/thams cad lhun grub rnam par rdzogs/de na lhun grub rdzogs pa che/cis gsung so//ye shes gsang ba'i rgyu

232a2(rnying rgyud): med khams gsum 'khor bar 'khyams/dus 'dir thugs rje'i byin rlabs kyis/kun mkhyen nyid kyi 'khor du 'dus/rtog pa semasu shes pa yin/kun mkhyen khyod kyis gsung du gsol//zhes gsol pa dang /de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyis bka' stsal pa/rol pa'i
232a3(rnying rgyud): sgra la sems gtod cing //ra'abyung ye shes sems kyis/brjod pa'i tha snyad kun dang bral/dman pa'i sgo'i rtogs pa'i phyir//rang rig gsung gi sgo nas bstan/ma bcos rang snang bla ma'i sa/sbyangs pas ma yin ye nas grub/rang bzhin rnam dag kun bzang ba/dbyer med che mchog chos kyi sku/rgyu dang

232a5(rnying rgyud): lhan rdzogs chen po la/sa'i rim pa gang yang med/rang byung rim pa rtogs gyur na/nor bu rin chen mi grub ltar/thams cad sems las byung ba'i phyir/rang snang byang chub sems kyis/'gyur med sems kyi ngo ni/rtogs pa ma yin mi rtog min/gang la'ang gnas pa ma yin pa/gnas/
